FILE: scripts\scanBulkOpsExact.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [480, 1907];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      break;
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: p,
  };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    if (sectionId === id) {
      return {
        start,
        end,
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function parseBodies() {
  const sec = findSection(10);

  if (!sec) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
  }

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      start,
      end,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return {
    section: sec,
    bodies,
  };
}

function decodeInstruction(p: number) {
  const start = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  let text = `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02:
      {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `block ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x03:
      {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `loop ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x04:
      {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `if ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x05:
      text = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text = "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `br ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x0d:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x0f:
      text = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `call FUNC${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x11:
      {
        const type = readULEB(p);
        p = type.next;

        const table = readULEB(p);
        p = table.next;

        text = `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x1a:
      text = "drop";
      break;

    case 0x20:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x21:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x22:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x23:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x24:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x37:
    case 0x3a:
      {
        const align = readULEB(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = readULEB(p);
        p = offset.next;

        text =
          `memop align=${align.value}` +
          ` offset=${hex(offset.value)}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x41:
      {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text =
          `i32.const ${r.value}` +
          ` (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x42:
      {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x45:
      text = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      text = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      text = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      text = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text = "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x6c:
      text = "i32.mul";
      break;

    case 0xa7:
      text = "i32.wrap_i64";
      break;

    case 0xfc:
      {
        const sub = readULEB(p);
        p = sub.next;

        if (sub.value === 8) {
          const data = readULEB(p);
          p = data.next;

          const mem = readULEB(p);
          p = mem.next;

          text =
            `memory.init data=${data.value}` +
            ` mem=${mem.value}`;
        }

        else if (sub.value === 9) {
          const data = readULEB(p);
          p = data.next;

          text =
            `data.drop data=${data.value}`;
        }

        else if (sub.value === 10) {
          const dst = readULEB(p);
          p = dst.next;

          const src = readULEB(p);
          p = src.next;

          text =
            `memory.copy mem${dst.value}` +
            ` <- mem${src.value}`;
        }

        else if (sub.value === 11) {
          const mem = readULEB(p);
          p = mem.next;

          text = `memory.fill mem${mem.value}`;
        }

        else {
          text = `PREFIX_FC sub=${sub.value}`;
        }
      }
      break;

    default:
      break;
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: p,
    op,
    text,
  };
}

function decodeBody(start: number, end: number) {
  let p = start;

  const locals = readULEB(p);
  p = locals.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    // valtype
    p++;
  }

  const instructions = [];

  // Function body itself has an implicit outer block.
  let depth = 1;

  while (p < end) {
    const ins = decodeInstruction(p);

    instructions.push(ins);

    p = ins.end;

    if (ins.op === 0x02 || ins.op === 0x03 || ins.op === 0x04) {
      depth++;
    }

    else if (ins.op === 0x0b) {
      depth--;

      if (depth === 0) {
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  return instructions;
}

console.log("SCAN BULK OPS EXACT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const parsed = parseBodies();

console.log(
  `CODE SECTION = ${hex(parsed.section.start)} .. ${hex(parsed.section.end)}`
);

console.log(`CODE BODIES  = ${parsed.bodies.length}`);

for (const func of TARGETS) {
  const body = parsed.bodies[func];

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func} NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  const instructions = decodeBody(
    body.start,
    body.end
  );

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
  console.log(`INSTRUCTIONS = ${instructions.length}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let bulkCount = 0;

  for (const ins of instructions) {
    if (
      ins.text.startsWith("memory.fill") ||
      ins.text.startsWith("memory.copy") ||
      ins.text.startsWith("memory.init") ||
      ins.text.startsWith("data.drop")
    ) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.text}`
      );

      bulkCount++;
    }
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`BULK OPS = ${bulkCount}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR RANGE = 0x19534c .. 0x195358");
console.log();
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

// call 8952
// 8952 decimal = 0x22f8
// ULEB128 = f8 45
const pattern = [0x10, 0xf8, 0x45];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN CALL 8952");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET FUNCTION : 8952");
console.log("PATTERN         : 10 f8 45");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i <= buf.length - pattern.length; i++) {

  if (
    buf[i] === pattern[0] &&
    buf[i + 1] === pattern[1] &&
    buf[i + 2] === pattern[2]
  ) {
    hits++;

    console.log(
      `HIT #${hits} : 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TOTAL RAW HITS :", hits);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

FILE: scripts\scanContextRefs.mjs

import fs from "node:fs";

const file = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0, shift = 0, b;
  do {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (b & 0x80);
  return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function slebBytes(value) {
  const out = [];
  let more = true;
  while (more) {
    let b = value & 0x7f;
    value >>= 7;
    const sign = (b & 0x40) !== 0;
    more = !((value === 0 && !sign) || (value === -1 && sign));
    if (more) b |= 0x80;
    out.push(b);
  }
  return out;
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[pos++];
  const len = uleb(pos);
  const payload = len.next;
  const end = payload + len.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payload;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }
  pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error("WASM code section not found");

let p = codeStart;
const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

const targets = [
  { label: "offset 36 (arg0+36)", bytes: slebBytes(36) },
  { label: "offset 44 (arg0+44)", bytes: slebBytes(44) },
  { label: "offset 48 (arg0+48)", bytes: slebBytes(48) },
  { label: "offset 72 (arg0+72)", bytes: slebBytes(72) },
  { label: "offset 136 (arg0+136)", bytes: slebBytes(136) },
  { label: "address 0x183EB0", bytes: slebBytes(0x183eb0) },
  { label: "address 0x183E98", bytes: slebBytes(0x183e98) },
];

console.log("WASM CONTEXT REFERENCE SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("CODE BODIES:", count.value);
console.log("--------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const bodySize = uleb(p);
  const bodyStart = bodySize.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

  for (const target of targets) {
    const pat = target.bytes;
    for (let i = bodyStart; i <= bodyEnd - pat.length; i++) {
      let match = true;
      for (let j = 0; j < pat.length; j++) {
        if (wasm[i + j] !== pat[j]) {
          match = false;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (match) {
        const func = 83 + ordinal;
        console.log(
          `FUNC ${func} | BODY 0x${bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
          ` | HIT 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} | ${target.label}`
        );
        hits++;
      }
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("--------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RAW BYTE MATCHES:", hits);
console.log("NOTE: matches are candidates, not yet decoded instructions.");

FILE: scripts\scanCurrentPtonTransfersV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const OP_JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;

function opHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';
    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function ton(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return frac ? `${whole}.${frac}` : whole.toString();
}

function getOpcode(body: any): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function separator(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${title}`);
    console.log('============================================================');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    separator('SCAN CURRENT pTON TRANSFERS v2.2');

    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');

    console.log('');
    console.log('PTON WALLET:');
    console.log(PTON_WALLET.toString());

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        PTON_WALLET,
        {
            limit: 20,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(`TRANSACTIONS FETCHED: ${txs.length}`);

    let transferCount = 0;

    for (const tx of txs) {

        if (!tx.inMessage) {
            continue;
        }

        const body = tx.inMessage.body;
        const op = getOpcode(body);

        if (op !== OP_JETTON_TRANSFER) {
            continue;
        }

        try {

            const s = body.beginParse();

            s.loadUint(32);

            const queryId =
                s.loadUintBig(64);

            const amount =
                s.loadCoins();

            const destination =
                s.loadAddress();

            const responseDestination =
                s.loadAddress();

            const customPayload =
                s.loadMaybeRef();

            const forwardTonAmount =
                s.loadCoins();

            const forwardIsRef =
                s.loadBit();

            let forwardPayload;

            if (forwardIsRef) {
                forwardPayload = s.loadRef();
            } else {
                forwardPayload = s.asCell();
            }

            transferCount++;

            console.log('');
            console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

            console.log('TRANSFER #' + transferCount);

            console.log('LT:');
            console.log(tx.lt.toString());

            console.log('HASH:');
            console.log(
                tx.hash().toString('base64')
            );

            console.log('TIME:');
            console.log(
                new Date(
                    tx.now * 1000
                ).toISOString()
            );

            console.log('AMOUNT RAW:');
            console.log(amount.toString());

            console.log('AMOUNT:');
            console.log(ton(amount));

            console.log('QUERY ID:');
            console.log(queryId.toString());

            if (tx.inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {

                console.log('SOURCE:');
                console.log(
                    tx.inMessage.info.src.toString()
                );

                console.log('DESTINATION ACCOUNT:');
                console.log(
                    tx.inMessage.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log('TON VALUE:');
                console.log(
                    ton(tx.inMessage.info.value.coins)
                );
            }

            console.log('DESTINATION FIELD:');
            console.log(destination.toString());

            console.log('RESPONSE DESTINATION:');
            console.log(
                responseDestination.toString()
            );

            console.log('CUSTOM PAYLOAD:');
            console.log(
                customPayload ? 'YES' : 'NONE'
            );

            console.log('FORWARD TON:');
            console.log(
                ton(forwardTonAmount)
            );

            console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD TYPE:');
            console.log(
                forwardIsRef
                    ? 'REFERENCE'
                    : 'INLINE'
            );

            console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD OPCODE:');
            console.log(
                opHex(
                    getOpcode(forwardPayload)
                )
            );

            console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD BOC:');
            console.log(
                forwardPayload
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64')
            );
        }
        catch (e) {

            console.log('');
            console.log('DECODE ERROR:');
            console.log(String(e));
        }
    }

    separator('RESULT');

    console.log('');
    console.log('JETTON TRANSFERS FOUND:');
    console.log(transferCount);

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');

    separator('END');
}

FILE: scripts\scanDispatchConstToStore.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const IMPORTED = 83;

const TARGETS = new Set([
  7697, 7698, 7699, 7700, 7701, 7702,
  7704, 7705, 7707, 7708, 7709,
  2053, 2101, 2612
]);

const MAX_DISTANCE = 48;

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    if (pos + i >= b.length) return null;

    const x = b[pos + i];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        end: pos + i + 1
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function getBodies() {

  let p = CODE_START;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  if (!count)
    throw new Error("Cannot read body count");

  p = count.end;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = readULEB(p);
    if (!size)
      throw new Error(`Bad body size at ${hex(p)}`);

    p = size.end;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      global: i + IMPORTED,
      start: p,
      end: p + size.value
    });

    p += size.value;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH CONST -> STORE(+4) SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  const candidates: {
    at: number;
    value: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    /*
       i32.const
       opcode 0x41
    */

    if (b[p] !== 0x41)
      continue;

    const r = readULEB(p + 1);

    if (!r)
      continue;

    if (!TARGETS.has(r.value))
      continue;

    candidates.push({
      at: p,
      value: r.value
    });
  }

  if (candidates.length === 0)
    continue;

  for (const c of candidates) {

    const limit = Math.min(
      body.end,
      c.at + MAX_DISTANCE
    );

    for (let p = c.at + 1; p < limit; p++) {

      /*
         i32.store = 0x36
      */

      if (b[p] !== 0x36)
        continue;

      const align = readULEB(p + 1);
      if (!align)
        continue;

      const offset = readULEB(align.end);
      if (!offset)
        continue;

      if (offset.value !== 4)
        continue;

      hits++;

      console.log("");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      console.log(
        `FUNCTION global=${body.global} body=${body.ordinal}`
      );

      console.log(
        `CONST @ ${hex(c.at)}`
      );

      console.log(
        `VALUE = ${c.value}`
      );

      console.log(
        `STORE(+4) @ ${hex(p)}`
      );

      console.log(
        `DISTANCE = ${p - c.at} bytes`
      );

      const from = Math.max(
        body.start,
        c.at - 16
      );

      const to = Math.min(
        body.end,
        offset.end + 12
      );

      console.log(
        Array.from(b.slice(from, to))
          .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL CANDIDATES = ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\scanDispatchField4Stores.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const IMPORTED = 83;

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    if (pos + i >= b.length) return null;

    const x = b[pos + i];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        end: pos + i + 1
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function getBodies() {

  let p = CODE_START;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  if (!count)
    throw new Error("Cannot read body count");

  p = count.end;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = readULEB(p);
    if (!size)
      throw new Error(`Cannot read body size at ${hex(p)}`);

    p = size.end;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      global: i + IMPORTED,
      start,
      end
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH FIELD +4 STORE SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log(`Bodies : ${bodies.length}`);
console.log(`Code   : ${hex(CODE_START)} .. ${hex(CODE_END)}`);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    /*
       i32.store
       opcode = 0x36

       Encoding:
       36 <align> <offset>
    */

    if (b[p] !== 0x36)
      continue;

    const align = readULEB(p + 1);
    if (!align)
      continue;

    const offset = readULEB(align.end);
    if (!offset)
      continue;

    /*
       We are interested in offset +4.
    */

    if (offset.value !== 4)
      continue;

    hits++;

    const from = Math.max(body.start, p - 48);
    const to   = Math.min(body.end, offset.end + 16);

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    console.log(
      `FUNCTION global=${body.global} body=${body.ordinal}`
    );

    console.log(
      `STORE @ ${hex(p)}`
    );

    console.log(
      `align=${offset ? align.value : "?"} offset=${offset.value}`
    );

    console.log(
      `context ${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`
    );

    console.log(
      Array.from(b.slice(from, to))
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGETS = [2053, 2101, 2612];

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) break;
    }

    return { value: -1, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function sectionAt(offset: number): string {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];
        const s = readULEB(p);
        const payload = s.next;
        const end = payload + s.value;

        if (offset >= p - 1 && offset < end) {
            const names: Record<number, string> = {
                1: "type",
                2: "import",
                3: "function",
                4: "table",
                5: "memory",
                6: "global",
                7: "export",
                9: "element",
                10: "code",
                11: "data",
            };

            return `${names[id] ?? `section-${id}`} [${hex(payload)}..${hex(end)}]`;
        }

        p = end;
    }

    return "unknown";
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DISPATCH INDEX RAW OCCURRENCE SCAN");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log("");
console.log("TARGETS:");
for (const t of TARGETS) {
    console.log(
        `  ${t}  LE32=${Buffer.from(Uint32Array.of(t).buffer).toString("hex")}`
    );
}
console.log("");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`TARGET ${target}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    const b32 = Buffer.alloc(4);
    b32.writeUInt32LE(target, 0);

    const b16 = Buffer.alloc(2);
    b16.writeUInt16LE(target, 0);

    const le32Hits: number[] = [];
    const le16Hits: number[] = [];
    const ulebHits: number[] = [];

    // Exact aligned + unaligned LE32
    for (let i = 0; i <= wasm.length - 4; i++) {
        if (
            wasm[i] === b32[0] &&
            wasm[i + 1] === b32[1] &&
            wasm[i + 2] === b32[2] &&
            wasm[i + 3] === b32[3]
        ) {
            le32Hits.push(i);
        }
    }

    // Exact LE16
    for (let i = 0; i <= wasm.length - 2; i++) {
        if (wasm[i] === b16[0] && wasm[i + 1] === b16[1]) {
            le16Hits.push(i);
        }
    }

    // ULEB128 occurrences
    for (let i = 0; i < wasm.length; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(i);

        if (r.value === target) {
            ulebHits.push(i);
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`LE32 HITS : ${le32Hits.length}`);

    for (const off of le32Hits.slice(0, 30)) {
        console.log(`  ${hex(off)}  ${sectionAt(off)}`);
    }

    if (le32Hits.length > 30) {
        console.log(`  ... ${le32Hits.length - 30} more`);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`LE16 HITS : ${le16Hits.length}`);

    for (const off of le16Hits.slice(0, 30)) {
        console.log(`  ${hex(off)}  ${sectionAt(off)}`);
    }

    if (le16Hits.length > 30) {
        console.log(`  ... ${le16Hits.length - 30} more`);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`ULEB HITS : ${ulebHits.length}`);

    for (const off of ulebHits.slice(0, 50)) {
        const end = Math.min(off + 8, wasm.length);

        console.log(
            `  ${hex(off)}  ${wasm
                .subarray(off, end)
                .toString("hex")
                .match(/../g)
                ?.join(" ")}  ${sectionAt(off)}`
        );
    }

    if (ulebHits.length > 50) {
        console.log(`  ... ${ulebHits.length - 50} more`);
    }

    console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN COMPLETE");
console.log("============================================================");

(async () => {
    const fs = require("fs");
    const wabt = await require("wabt")();

    const file =
        "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    const targets = new Map([
        [1659724, "SLOT_19534C"],
        [1659728, "SLOT_195350"],
        [1659732, "SLOT_195354"],
        [1659736, "SLOT_195358"]
    ]);

    const features = {
        exceptions: true,
        mutable_globals: true,
        sat_float_to_int: true,
        sign_extension: true,
        simd: true,
        threads: true,
        function_references: true,
        multi_value: true,
        tail_call: true,
        bulk_memory: true,
        reference_types: true,
        annotations: true,
        code_metadata: true,
        gc: true,
        memory64: true,
        multi_memory: true,
        extended_const: true,
        relaxed_simd: true
    };

    const mod = wabt.readWasm(fs.readFileSync(file), {
        readDebugNames: false,
        ...features
    });

    mod.validate();

    const wat = mod.toText({
        foldExprs: false,
        inlineExport: false
    });

    const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);
    let funcId = null;
    let funcLine = 0;
    let hits = 0;

    console.log("DISPATCH SLOT REFERENCES");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("Context: 4 lines before and after each reference");

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const start = lines[i].match(/^\s*\(func \(;(\d+);\)/);

        if (start) {
            funcId = Number(start[1]);
            funcLine = 1;
        } else if (funcId !== null) {
            funcLine++;
        }

        const match = lines[i].match(/i32\.const\s+(-?\d+)/);
        if (!match) continue;

        const address = Number(match[1]);
        if (!targets.has(address)) continue;

        hits++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(
            `${targets.get(address)} | FUNC ${funcId} | WAT LINE ${i + 1}`
        );

        const from = Math.max(0, i - 4);
        const to = Math.min(lines.length - 1, i + 4);

        for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {
            console.log(
                `${j + 1}: ${j === i ? ">> " : "   "}${lines[j].trimEnd()}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("TOTAL CONSTANT REFERENCES:", hits);

    mod.destroy();
})().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x1295b;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 CONTROL-FLOW RAW SCAN V2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`Function range : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    let text = `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  op=0x${op
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(2, "0")}`;

    switch (op) {

        case 0x02:
            text += "  block";
            p++; // blocktype
            break;

        case 0x03:
            text += "  loop";
            p++; // blocktype
            break;

        case 0x04:
            text += "  if";
            p++; // blocktype
            break;

        case 0x05:
            text += "  else";
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            text += "  end";
            break;

        case 0x0c: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            text += `  br depth=${r.value}`;
            p = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            text += `  br_if depth=${r.value}`;
            p = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const count = readULEB(p);
            p = count.next;

            const depths: number[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                depths.push(r.value);
                p = r.next;
            }

            const def = readULEB(p);
            p = def.next;

            text +=
                `  br_table count=${count.value} ` +
                `depths=[${depths.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            text += "  return";
            break;

        case 0x00:
            text += "  unreachable";
            break;

        case 0x01:
            text += "  nop";
            break;

        case 0x10: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            text += `  call func=${r.value}`;
            p = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const t = readULEB(p);
            p = t.next;

            const table = readULEB(p);
            p = table.next;

            text +=
                `  call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x1a:
            text += "  drop";
            break;

        case 0x1b:
            text += "  select";
            break;

        case 0x20: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            text += `  local.get ${r.value}`;
            p = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            text += `  local.set ${r.value}`;
            p = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            text += `  local.tee ${r.value}`;
            p = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            text += `  global.get ${r.value}`;
            p = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            text += `  global.set ${r.value}`;
            p = r.next;
            break;
        }

        default:
            // Do not try to decode every opcode here.
            // Just show raw opcode and continue.
            break;
    }

    console.log(text);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CONTROL-FLOW OPCODES ONLY");
console.log("============================================================");

p = START;

while (p < END) {

    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x02) {
        const bt = wasm[p++];
        console.log(`${hex(addr)}  block  blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16)}`);
    }
    else if (op === 0x03) {
        const bt = wasm[p++];
        console.log(`${hex(addr)}  loop   blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16)}`);
    }
    else if (op === 0x04) {
        const bt = wasm[p++];
        console.log(`${hex(addr)}  if     blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16)}`);
    }
    else if (op === 0x05) {
        console.log(`${hex(addr)}  else`);
    }
    else if (op === 0x0b) {
        console.log(`${hex(addr)}  end`);
    }
    else if (op === 0x0c) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        console.log(`${hex(addr)}  br       depth=${r.value}`);
    }
    else if (op === 0x0d) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        console.log(`${hex(addr)}  br_if    depth=${r.value}`);
    }
    else if (op === 0x0e) {
        const c = readULEB(p);
        p = c.next;

        const ds: number[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            ds.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
        }

        const d = readULEB(p);
        p = d.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(addr)}  br_table count=${c.value} ` +
            `depths=[${ds.join(",")}] default=${d.value}`
        );
    }
    else if (op === 0x0f) {
        console.log(`${hex(addr)}  return`);
    }
    else if (op === 0x00) {
        console.log(`${hex(addr)}  unreachable`);
    }
    else if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
    }
    else if (op === 0x11) {
        const a = readULEB(p);
        p = a.next;

        const b = readULEB(p);
        p = b.next;
    }
}

console.log();

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\scanFunction3837Ops.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 3837;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(buf, pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(buf, pos, bits = 32) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < bits && (b & 0x40)) {
    result -= Math.pow(2, shift);
  }

  return { value: result, next: pos };
}

function readName(buf, pos) {
  const n = readULEB(buf, pos);
  const end = n.next + n.value;

  return {
    text: buf.slice(n.next, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end
  };
}

function skipLimits(buf, pos) {
  const flags = buf[pos++];

  const min = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = min.next;

  if (flags & 1) {
    const max = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = max.next;
  }

  return pos;
}

function skipTableType(buf, pos) {
  pos++; // elemtype
  return skipLimits(buf, pos);
}

function skipMemoryType(buf, pos) {
  return skipLimits(buf, pos);
}

function skipGlobalType(buf, pos) {
  pos += 2;
  return pos;
}

function countImportedFunctions(buf, sec) {
  let pos = sec.start;

  const n = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = n.next;

  let funcs = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const mod = readName(buf, pos);
    pos = mod.next;

    const name = readName(buf, pos);
    pos = name.next;

    const kind = buf[pos++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const typeIndex = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = typeIndex.next;
      funcs++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      pos = skipTableType(buf, pos);
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      pos = skipMemoryType(buf, pos);
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      pos = skipGlobalType(buf, pos);
    } else if (kind === 4) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;
    } else {
      throw new Error("Unknown import kind " + kind);
    }
  }

  return funcs;
}

function getSections(buf) {
  const sections = [];

  let pos = 8;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[pos++];

    const size = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = size.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + size.value;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start,
      end,
      size: size.value
    });

    pos = end;
  }

  return sections;
}

function findTargetBody(buf, codeSec, importedFunctions) {
  let pos = codeSec.start;

  const count = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const bodySize = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = bodySize.next;

    const bodyStart = pos;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

    const funcIndex = importedFunctions + i;

    if (funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {
      return {
        funcIndex,
        bodyIndex: i,
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd
      };
    }

    pos = bodyEnd;
  }

  return null;
}

function isInteresting(op) {
  return [
    0x10, // call
    0x11, // call_indirect
    0x20, // local.get
    0x21, // local.set
    0x22, // local.tee
    0x23, // global.get
    0x24, // global.set
    0x28, // i32.load
    0x29, // i64.load
    0x2d, // i32.load8_u
    0x36, // i32.store
    0x37, // i64.store
    0x3a, // i32.store8
    0x3b, // i32.store16
    0x41, // i32.const
    0x42, // i64.const
    0x6a, // i32.add
    0x6b, // i32.sub
    0x46, // i32.eq
    0x45, // i32.eqz
    0x1a, // drop
    0x1b  // select
  ].includes(op);
}

function scanBody(buf, start, end) {
  let pos = start;

  // Local declarations
  const localDecl = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = localDecl.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
    const count = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = count.next;

    pos++; // valtype
  }

  const hits = [];

  while (pos < end) {
    const addr = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    let item = {
      addr,
      opcode: op
    };

    // Control opcodes: do not try to decode their blocktype.
    if (
      op === 0x02 || // block
      op === 0x03 || // loop
      op === 0x04    // if
    ) {
      if (pos < end) {
        const blockType = buf[pos++];

        // Record it, but don't interpret it.
        if (isInteresting(op)) {
          item.extra = "blocktype=0x" +
            blockType.toString(16);
        }
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05) {
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b || op === 0x0f) {
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
      const n = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
        pos = x.next;
      }

      const d = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = d.next;

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;

      item.name = "call";
      item.func = x.value;
      hits.push(item);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const type = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = type.next;

      const table = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = table.next;

      item.name = "call_indirect";
      item.type = type.value;
      item.table = table.value;
      hits.push(item);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;

      item.name =
        op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        "local.tee";

      item.local = x.value;

      hits.push(item);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;

      item.name =
        op === 0x23 ? "global.get" : "global.set";

      item.global = x.value;

      hits.push(item);
      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x36 ||
      op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x3a ||
      op === 0x3b
    ) {
      const align = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = offset.next;

      const names = {
        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16"
      };

      item.name = names[op];
      item.align = align.value;
      item.offset = offset.value;

      hits.push(item);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(buf, pos, 32);
      pos = x.next;

      item.name = "i32.const";
      item.value = x.value;

      hits.push(item);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const x = readSLEB(buf, pos, 64);
      pos = x.next;

      item.name = "i64.const";
      item.value = x.value;

      hits.push(item);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b) {
      item.name = op === 0x6a ? "i32.add" : "i32.sub";
      hits.push(item);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x45 || op === 0x46 || op === 0x1a || op === 0x1b) {
      item.name =
        op === 0x45 ? "i32.eqz" :
        op === 0x46 ? "i32.eq" :
        op === 0x1a ? "drop" :
        "select";

      hits.push(item);
      continue;
    }

    // Everything else:
    // Do not stop. Continue scanning raw bytes.
  }

  return hits;
}

function formatHit(h) {
  let s =
    "0x" +
    h.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
    "  " +
    h.name;

  if (h.local !== undefined)
    s += " local=" + h.local;

  if (h.global !== undefined)
    s += " global=" + h.global;

  if (h.value !== undefined)
    s += " value=" + h.value;

  if (h.offset !== undefined)
    s += " offset=" + h.offset;

  if (h.align !== undefined)
    s += " align=" + h.align;

  if (h.func !== undefined)
    s += " func=" + h.func;

  if (h.type !== undefined)
    s += " type=" + h.type;

  if (h.table !== undefined)
    s += " table=" + h.table;

  return s;
}

const sections = getSections(wasm);

const importSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 2);
const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!importSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Import or Code section not found");
}

const importedFunctions =
  countImportedFunctions(wasm, importSec);

const target =
  findTargetBody(wasm, codeSec, importedFunctions);

if (!target) {
  throw new Error("Target function not found");
}

const hits =
  scanBody(wasm, target.start, target.end);

FILE: scripts\scanGlobal0TargetOffsets.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [
    0x195350,
    0x195354
];

function uleb(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("SCAN GLOBAL 0 TARGET-REACHING OFFSETS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;

let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

let q = codeStart;

const fc = uleb(wasm, q);
const functionCount = fc.value;
q = fc.next;

let global0Gets = 0;
let global0Sets = 0;
let targetArithmeticHits = 0;

console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS =", functionCount);
console.log("");

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < functionCount; ordinal++) {

    const bs = uleb(wasm, q);
    q = bs.next;

    const bodyStart = q;
    const bodyEnd = q + bs.value;

    let x = bodyStart;

    const lc = uleb(wasm, x);
    x = lc.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < lc.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(wasm, x);
        x = n.next;
        x++;
    }

    const recent: {
        at: number;
        kind: string;
        value?: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (x < bodyEnd) {

        const at = x;
        const op = wasm[x++];

        if (op === 0x23) {

            const r = uleb(wasm, x);
            x = r.next;

            if (r.value === 0) {
                global0Gets++;

                recent.push({
                    at,
                    kind: "GLOBAL0_GET"
                });
            }
        }

        else if (op === 0x24) {

            const r = uleb(wasm, x);
            x = r.next;

            if (r.value === 0) {
                global0Sets++;

                recent.push({
                    at,
                    kind: "GLOBAL0_SET"
                });
            }
        }

        else if (op === 0x41) {

            const r = uleb(wasm, x);
            x = r.next;

            const value = r.value >>> 0;

            /*
             * If a target-related constant appears near a
             * global.get 0 / global.set 0, print it.
             */
            const hasGlobal0 = recent.some(
                z => z.kind === "GLOBAL0_GET" ||
                     z.kind === "GLOBAL0_SET"
            );

            if (hasGlobal0) {

                for (const target of TARGETS) {

                    /*
                     * We are interested in small arithmetic
                     * distances between Global0 and target.
                     */
                    const d1 =
                        Math.abs(
                            (target >>> 0) -
                            (value >>> 0)
                        );

                    if (d1 <= 4096) {

                        console.log(
                            `FUNC ORDINAL ${ordinal} @ ${hex(at)}`
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `  GLOBAL0 nearby`
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `  CONST = ${value} (${hex(value)})`
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `  TARGET = ${hex(target)}`
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `  DISTANCE = ${d1}`
                        );

                        console.log("");

                        targetArithmeticHits++;
                    }
                }
            }

            recent.push({
                at,
                kind: "CONST",
                value
            });
        }

        else if (
            op === 0x6a ||
            op === 0x6b ||
            op === 0x6c ||
            op === 0x71 ||
            op === 0x72 ||
            op === 0x73
        ) {

            recent.push({
                at,
                kind: "ARITH"
            });
        }

        else {

            /*
             * Keep recent history short.
             */
            if (recent.length > 20) {
                recent.shift();
            }
        }

        if (recent.length > 20) {
            recent.shift();
        }
    }

    q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("  ", hex(TARGETS[1]));

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;
const TARGET_GLOBAL = 0;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: v, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSection(idWanted) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = start + s.value;

        if (id === idWanted) {
            return { start, end };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    return null;
}

const codeSection = readSection(10);

if (!codeSection) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let p = codeSection.start;

const countInfo = uleb(p);
const codeCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const functions = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < codeCount; ordinal++) {

    const sizeInfo = uleb(p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const funcIndex = IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal;

    functions.push({
        funcIndex,
        ordinal,
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("=".repeat(80));
console.log("GLOBAL 0 WRITER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(80));
console.log();

console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS : ${IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS  : ${codeCount}`);
console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL      : ${TARGET_GLOBAL}`);
console.log();

const writers = [];
const calls = [];

for (const fn of functions) {

    let q = fn.start;

    // Skip local declaration vector.
    const localInfo = uleb(q);
    q = localInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localInfo.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(q);
        q = n.next;

        // value type
        q++;
    }

    while (q < fn.end) {

        const at = q;
        const op = wasm[q++];

        // global.get
        if (op === 0x23) {

            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            if (x.value === TARGET_GLOBAL) {
                writers.push({
                    funcIndex: fn.funcIndex,
                    ordinal: fn.ordinal,
                    address: at,
                    kind: "GET"
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // global.set
        if (op === 0x24) {

            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            if (x.value === TARGET_GLOBAL) {
                writers.push({
                    funcIndex: fn.funcIndex,
                    ordinal: fn.ordinal,
                    address: at,
                    kind: "SET"
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // direct call
        if (op === 0x10) {

            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            calls.push({
                caller: fn.funcIndex,
                callee: x.value,
                address: at
            });

            continue;
        }

        // call_indirect
        if (op === 0x11) {

            const t = uleb(q);
            q = t.next;

            const table = uleb(q);
            q = table.next;

            calls.push({
                caller: fn.funcIndex,
                callee: null,
                address: at,
                indirect: true,
                type: t.value,
                table
            });

            continue;
        }

        // i32.const
        if (op === 0x41) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        // i64.const
        if (op === 0x42) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        // local.get / set / tee
        if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        // memory load/store common immediates
        if (
            op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2a || op === 0x2b ||
            op === 0x2c || op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x2e || op === 0x2f ||
            op === 0x30 || op === 0x31 ||
            op === 0x32 || op === 0x33 ||
            op === 0x34 || op === 0x35 ||
            op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x38 || op === 0x39 ||
            op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b ||
            op === 0x3c || op === 0x3d ||
            op === 0x3e
        ) {
            const align = uleb(q);
            q = align.next;

            const offset = uleb(q);
            q = offset.next;

            continue;
        }

        // br / br_if
        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        // block / loop / if
        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            q++; // block type
            continue;
        }

        // typed select
        if (op === 0x1c) {
            const n = uleb(q);
            q = n.next;
            q += n.value;
            continue;
        }

        // ref.null
        if (op === 0xd0) {
            q++;
            continue;
        }
    }
}

FILE: scripts\scanIndirectLoad4.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const IMPORTED = 83;

function readULEB(pos: number) {

  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {

    if (pos + i >= b.length)
      return null;

    const x = b[pos + i];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        end: pos + i + 1
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function getBodies() {

  let p = CODE_START;

  const count = readULEB(p);

  if (!count)
    throw new Error("Cannot read body count");

  p = count.end;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = readULEB(p);

    if (!size)
      throw new Error(`Bad body size at ${hex(p)}`);

    p = size.end;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      global: i + IMPORTED,
      start: p,
      end: p + size.value
    });

    p += size.value;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INDIRECT LOAD(+4) -> CALL_INDIRECT TYPE 4");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    /*
       i32.load

       28 <align> <offset>
    */

    if (b[p] !== 0x28)
      continue;

    const align = readULEB(p + 1);

    if (!align)
      continue;

    const offset = readULEB(align.end);

    if (!offset)
      continue;

    if (offset.value !== 4)
      continue;

    /*
       Look immediately ahead for:

          call_indirect
          11 04 00
    */

    const q = offset.end;

    if (
      b[q] === 0x11 &&
      b[q + 1] === 0x04 &&
      b[q + 2] === 0x00
    ) {

      hits++;

      console.log("");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      console.log(
        `FUNCTION global=${body.global} body=${body.ordinal}`
      );

      console.log(
        `LOAD(+4) @ ${hex(p)}`
      );

      console.log(
        `CALL_INDIRECT @ ${hex(q)}`
      );

      const from = Math.max(
        body.start,
        p - 32
      );

      const to = Math.min(
        body.end,
        q + 8
      );

      console.log(
        `context ${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`
      );

      console.log(
        Array.from(b.slice(from, to))
          .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL EXACT DISPATCHES = ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\scanIndirectTableConsumers.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_MIN = 4057;
const TARGET_MAX = 4188;

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {

    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {

        const b = buf[p++];

        value +=
            (b & 0x7f) *
            Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" +
        n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {

    const sections: any[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {

        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size: s.value
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * ELEMENT TABLE
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function parseElement(
    buf: Buffer,
    sec: any
) {

    let p = sec.start;

    const segs = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = segs.next;

    const flags = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = flags.next;

    if (flags.value !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected element flags ${flags.value}`
        );
    }

    const offsetOpcode = buf[p++];

    if (offsetOpcode !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(
            `Expected i32.const`
        );
    }

    const offset = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = offset.next;

    if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(
            "Invalid offset expression"
        );
    }

    const count = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = count.next;

    const tableToFunction =
        new Map<number, number>();

    const functionToTable =
        new Map<number, number>();

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < count.value;
        i++
    ) {

        const fn = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = fn.next;

        const tableIndex =
            offset.value + i;

        tableToFunction.set(
            tableIndex,
            fn.value
        );

        if (
            !functionToTable.has(fn.value)
        ) {
            functionToTable.set(
                fn.value,
                tableIndex
            );
        }
    }

    return {
        count: count.value,
        cursor: p,
        tableToFunction,
        functionToTable
    };
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * CODE BODY RANGES
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function parseCode(
    buf: Buffer,
    sec: any
) {

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = count.next;

    const bodies: any[] = [];

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < count.value;
        i++
    ) {

        const size = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = size.next;

        const start = p;
        const end =
            start + size.value;

        bodies.push({
            functionIndex: 83 + i,
            start,
            end
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return {
        count: count.value,
        cursor: p,
        bodies
    };
}

function findBody(
    bodies: any[],
    offset: number
) {

    let lo = 0;
    let hi = bodies.length - 1;

    while (lo <= hi) {

        const mid =
            (lo + hi) >> 1;

        const b = bodies[mid];

        if (
            offset >= b.start &&
            offset < b.end
        ) {
            return b;
        }

        if (offset < b.start) {
            hi = mid - 1;
        } else {
            lo = mid + 1;
        }
    }

    return undefined;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * MAIN
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

export async function run() {

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " INDIRECT TABLE CONSUMER SCAN"
    );

    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    const buf =
        fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    const sections =
        parseSections(buf);

    const elementSec =
        sections.find(
            x => x.id === 9
        );

    const codeSec =
        sections.find(
            x => x.id === 10
        );

    if (!elementSec || !codeSec) {
        throw new Error(
            "Element or Code section missing"
        );
    }

    const element =
        parseElement(
            buf,
            elementSec
        );

    const code =
        parseCode(
            buf,
            codeSec
        );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "WASM size :",
        buf.length
    );

    console.log(
        "Element count :",
        element.count
    );

    console.log(
        "Element cursor :",
        hex(element.cursor)
    );

    console.log(
        "Expected       :",
        hex(elementSec.end)
    );

    console.log(
        "Code bodies :",
        code.count
    );

    console.log(
        "Code cursor :",
        hex(code.cursor)
    );

    console.log(
        "Expected    :",
        hex(codeSec.end)
    );

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * TARGET TABLE SET
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const targetTables =
        new Map<number, number>();

    for (
        let fn = TARGET_MIN;
        fn <= TARGET_MAX;
        fn++
    ) {

        const table =
            element.functionToTable.get(fn);

        if (table !== undefined) {
            targetTables.set(
                table,
                fn
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "Target table entries :",
        targetTables.size
    );

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * RAW CALL_INDIRECT SEARCH
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * 0x11
     *
     * Then decode TWO ULEBs.
     *
     * We do NOT assume a particular type.
     * We only care whether the resulting table index
     * points to one of our 4057..4188 functions.
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const matches: any[] = [];

    let raw11 = 0;
    let validIndirect = 0;
    let targetMatches = 0;

    for (
        let p = codeSec.start;
        p < codeSec.end;
        p++
    ) {

        if (buf[p] !== 0x11) {
            continue;
        }

        raw11++;

        let q = p + 1;

        let typeInfo;

        try {
            typeInfo =
                readULEB(buf, q);

            q = typeInfo.next;

        } catch {
            continue;
        }

        let tableInfo;

        try {
            tableInfo =
                readULEB(buf, q);

            q = tableInfo.next;

        } catch {
            continue;
        }

        const typeIndex =
            typeInfo.value;

        const tableIndex =
            tableInfo.value;

        /*
         * A valid table reference must point into
         * the actual element table.
         */

        const targetFunction =
            element.tableToFunction.get(
                tableIndex
            );

        if (targetFunction === undefined) {
            continue;
        }

        validIndirect++;

        if (
            targetFunction >= TARGET_MIN &&
            targetFunction <= TARGET_MAX
        ) {

            targetMatches++;

            const caller =
                findBody(
                    code.bodies,
                    p
                );

            matches.push({
                offset: p,
                typeIndex,
                tableIndex,
                targetFunction,
                caller:
                    caller?.functionIndex ??
                    -1
            });
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " RAW / VALID / TARGET COUNTS"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "Raw 0x11 candidates :",
        raw11
    );

    console.log(
        "Valid table refs    :",
        validIndirect
    );

    console.log(
        "Target matches      :",
        targetMatches
    );

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * MATCHES
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " SEGMENT 413 TARGET CONSUMERS"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    if (matches.length === 0) {

        console.log(
            "NO TARGET TABLE CONSUMERS FOUND"
        );

    } else {

        for (const m of matches) {

            console.log("");
            console.log(
                `target function : ${m.targetFunction}`
            );

            console.log(
                `table index     : ${hex(m.tableIndex)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `type index      : ${m.typeIndex}`
            );

            console.log(
                `call_indirect   : ${hex(m.offset)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `caller function : ${m.caller}`
            );
        }
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * GROUP BY CALLER
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " GROUPED BY CALLER"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    const byCaller =
        new Map<number, any[]>();

    for (const m of matches) {

        if (!byCaller.has(m.caller)) {
            byCaller.set(
                m.caller,
                []
            );
        }

        byCaller
            .get(m.caller)!
            .push(m);
    }

    for (
        const [
            caller,
            list
        ] of byCaller.entries()
    ) {

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `CALLER FUNCTION ${caller}`
        );

        for (const m of list) {

            console.log(
                `  target=${m.targetFunction}` +
                ` table=${hex(m.tableIndex)}` +
                ` type=${m.typeIndex}` +
                ` @${hex(m.offset)}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " END"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
}

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const JETTON_LP_WALLET_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEAIwAIQgLACDZEDQhORPuUMWEyrFohQX709CnuCbVWC1Z4szTD6PedWHM=';

const LP_ACCOUNT_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEAIwAIQgLJWi7SKrUW93+dSJjcRXjnLxiiRI6PaDIzSwtL9QG8eU64SPQ=';

const POOL_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEAIwAIQgKpM47NYkyhXTfkqNm/Z33cm4Tw6Y8F8vuEx6/jMqKBtKTTrh0=';

function getBits(cell: Cell): string {
    const bytes = cell.bits.getTopUppedArray();

    let result = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.bits.length; i++) {
        const byteIndex = Math.floor(i / 8);
        const bitIndex = 7 - (i % 8);

        const bit = (bytes[byteIndex] >> bitIndex) & 1;

        result += bit ? '1' : '0';
    }

    return result;
}

function scan32(name: string, boc: string) {
    const cells = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(boc, 'base64')
    );

    const cell = cells[0];

    const bits = getBits(cell);

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log(name);
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('refs:', cell.refs.length);
    console.log('hash:', cell.hash().toString('hex'));

    const target = 0x178d4519;

    let found = false;

    for (let offset = 0; offset <= bits.length - 32; offset++) {
        const chunk = bits.slice(offset, offset + 32);
        const value = parseInt(chunk, 2);

        if (value === target) {
            console.log(
                `FOUND 0x178d4519 at bit offset ${offset}`
            );

            found = true;
        }
    }

    if (!found) {
        console.log('0x178d4519: NOT FOUND');
    }

    console.log('\n32-bit values on byte boundaries:');

    for (let offset = 0; offset <= bits.length - 32; offset += 8) {
        const chunk = bits.slice(offset, offset + 32);
        const value = parseInt(chunk, 2);

        console.log(
            `offset=${offset.toString().padStart(3)} ` +
            `0x${value.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`
        );
    }

    console.log('\nRaw bits:');
    console.log(bits);
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP CODE BYTECODE SCANNER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE CODE ANALYSIS');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    scan32(
        'JETTON LP WALLET CODE',
        JETTON_LP_WALLET_BOC
    );

    scan32(
        'LP ACCOUNT CODE',
        LP_ACCOUNT_BOC
    );

    scan32(
        'POOL CODE',
        POOL_BOC
    );

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('DONE');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}
