(async () => {
    const fs = require("fs");
    const wabt = await require("wabt")();

    const file =
        "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    const features = {
        exceptions: true,
        mutable_globals: true,
        sat_float_to_int: true,
        sign_extension: true,
        simd: true,
        threads: true,
        function_references: true,
        multi_value: true,
        tail_call: true,
        bulk_memory: true,
        reference_types: true,
        annotations: true,
        code_metadata: true,
        gc: true,
        memory64: true,
        multi_memory: true,
        extended_const: true,
        relaxed_simd: true
    };

    const mod = wabt.readWasm(fs.readFileSync(file), {
        readDebugNames: false,
        ...features
    });

    mod.validate();

    const wat = mod.toText({
        foldExprs: false,
        inlineExport: false
    });

    const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);
    const pattern = /\bmemory\.(?:copy|fill|init)\b/;

    let funcId = null;
    let total = 0;
    const counts = new Map();

    console.log("WASM BULK MEMORY OPERATIONS");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const start = lines[i].match(/^\s*\(func \(;(\d+);\)/);

        if (start) {
            funcId = Number(start[1]);
        }

        if (!pattern.test(lines[i])) continue;

        total++;
        counts.set(funcId, (counts.get(funcId) || 0) + 1);

        console.log(
            `FUNC ${funcId} | WAT LINE ${i + 1} | ${lines[i].trim()}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("TOTAL OPERATIONS:", total);
    console.log("FUNCTIONS WITH OPERATIONS:", counts.size);

    console.log("");
    console.log("COUNTS BY FUNCTION:");

    for (const [id, count] of [...counts.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0])) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${id}: ${count}`);
    }

    mod.destroy();
})().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\scanOffset4Flow.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_OFFSET = 4;
const CONTEXT = 12;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// LEB
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: result >>> 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: result | 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function skipBlockType(pos) {
  const b = wasm[pos];

  if (
    b === 0x40 ||
    b === 0x7f ||
    b === 0x7e ||
    b === 0x7d ||
    b === 0x7c ||
    b === 0x7b ||
    b === 0x70 ||
    b === 0x6f
  ) {
    return pos + 1;
  }

  return readSLEB(pos).next;
}

function readMemArg(pos) {
  const align = readULEB(pos);
  const offset = readULEB(align.next);

  return {
    align: align.value,
    offset: offset.value,
    next: offset.next
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode function
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function decodeFunction(body) {

  const instructions = [];

  let p = body.start;

  while (p < body.end) {

    const opPos = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    try {

      let text = "";

      switch (op) {

        case 0x00:
          text = "unreachable";
          break;

        case 0x01:
          text = "nop";
          break;

        case 0x02:
          p = skipBlockType(p);
          text = "block";
          break;

        case 0x03:
          p = skipBlockType(p);
          text = "loop";
          break;

        case 0x04:
          p = skipBlockType(p);
          text = "if";
          break;

        case 0x05:
          text = "else";
          break;

        case 0x0b:
          text = "end";
          break;

        case 0x0c: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `br ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x0e: {

          const n = readULEB(p);
          p = n.next;

          const labels = [];

          for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            labels.push(x.value);
          }

          const def = readULEB(p);
          p = def.next;

          text =
            `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
          text = "return";
          break;

        case 0x10: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `call ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x11: {

          const type = readULEB(p);
          p = type.next;

          const table = readULEB(p);
          p = table.next;

          text =
            `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x1a:
          text = "drop";
          break;

        case 0x1b:
          text = "select";
          break;

        case 0x20: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `local.get ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x21: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `local.set ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x22: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x23: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `global.get ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x24: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `global.set ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x25: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `table.get ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x26: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `table.set ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e: {

          const mem = readMemArg(p);

          p = mem.next;

          const names = {

            0x28: "i32.load",
            0x29: "i64.load",
            0x2a: "f32.load",
            0x2b: "f64.load",

            0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
            0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
            0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
            0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

            0x30: "i64.load8_s",
            0x31: "i64.load8_u",
            0x32: "i64.load16_s",
            0x33: "i64.load16_u",
            0x34: "i64.load32_s",
            0x35: "i64.load32_u",

            0x36: "i32.store",
            0x37: "i64.store",
            0x38: "f32.store",
            0x39: "f64.store",
            0x3a: "i32.store8",
            0x3b: "i32.store16",
            0x3c: "i64.store8",
            0x3d: "i64.store16",
            0x3e: "i64.store32"
          };

          text =
            `${names[op]} align=${mem.align} offset=${mem.offset}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x3f: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `memory.size ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x40: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `memory.grow ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x41: {

          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x42: {

          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x43:

          p += 4;
          text = "f32.const";

          break;

        case 0x44:

          p += 8;
          text = "f64.const";

          break;

        default:

          // Most numeric opcodes have no immediates.
          if (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0xc4) {

            text =
              `opcode 0x${op
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(2, "0")}`;

            break;
          }

          text =
            `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op
              .toString(16)
              .padStart(2, "0")}`;

          instructions.push({
            pos: opPos,
            text,
            unknown: true
          });

          return instructions;
      }

      instructions.push({
        pos: opPos,
        text,
        unknown: false
      });

    } catch {

      instructions.push({
        pos: opPos,
        text: "DECODE ERROR",
        unknown: true
      });

      return instructions;
    }
  }

  return instructions;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
let bodies = [];

while (pos < wasm.length) {

  const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

  const secSize = readULEB(pos);

  const size = secSize.value;

  pos = secSize.next;

  const secStart = pos;
  const secEnd = secStart + size;

  if (sectionId === 10) {

    const count = readULEB(pos);

    pos = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

      const bodySize = readULEB(pos);

      pos = bodySize.next;

      const bodyStart = pos;
      const bodyEnd =
        bodyStart + bodySize.value;

      bodies.push({
        index: i,
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd
      });

      pos = bodyEnd;
    }

    break;
  }

  pos = secEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan offset=4
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN ALL OFFSET=4 MEMORY ACCESSES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET OFFSET :", TARGET_OFFSET);
console.log("FUNCTIONS :", bodies.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let loadCount = 0;
let storeCount = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  const instructions =
    decodeFunction(body);

  for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {

    const t = instructions[i].text;

    const isLoad =
      /^i32\.load /.test(t);

    const isStore =
      /^i32\.store /.test(t);

    if (!isLoad && !isStore) {
      continue;
    }

    const match =
      t.match(/offset=(\d+)/);

    if (!match) {
      continue;
    }

    const offset =
      Number(match[1]);

    if (offset !== TARGET_OFFSET) {
      continue;
    }

    if (isLoad) {
      loadCount++;
    }

    if (isStore) {
      storeCount++;
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      `${isLoad ? "LOAD " : "STORE"} FUNC ${body.index} @ 0x${instructions[i].pos
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
      "------------------------------------------------------------"
    );

    const start =
      Math.max(0, i - CONTEXT);

    const end =
      Math.min(
        instructions.length,
        i + CONTEXT + 1
      );

    for (let j = start; j < end; j++) {

      const marker =
        j === i ? ">>> " : "    ";

      console.log(
        marker + instructions[j].text
      );
    }
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

    if (buf[p] !== 0x3c) continue;

    count++;

    const from = Math.max(START, p - 12);
    const to   = Math.min(END, p + 16);

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    console.log(`0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
    console.log();

    for (let q = from; q < to; q += 8) {
        const lineEnd = Math.min(q + 8, to);

        const hex = [];
        for (let r = q; r < lineEnd; r++) {
            const mark = r === p ? ">>" : "  ";
            hex.push(`${mark}${buf[r].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${hex.join(" ")}`
        );
    }

    console.log();

    const after = [];
    for (let q = p; q < Math.min(p + 8, END); q++) {
        after.push(buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    console.log(`FROM 0x${p.toString(16)}: ${after.join(" ")}`);

    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    if (
        wasm[p] === 0x3c &&
        wasm[p + 1] === 0x00
    ) {
        const index = wasm[p + 2];

        total++;

        counts.set(index, (counts.get(index) ?? 0) + 1);

        if (!samples.has(index)) {
            samples.set(index, []);
        }

        const arr = samples.get(index)!;

        if (arr.length < 8) {
            arr.push(p);
        }
    }

    );

        );

FILE: scripts\scanPathTo87.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [133, 148, 121, 239, 32, 3595, 1425, 91];

function readU32Leb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function getCodeBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  let codeStart = -1;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const s = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      codeStart = p;
      break;
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error("Code section not found");

  let q = codeStart;
  const count = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
  q = count.next;

  const bodies = new Map<number, Buffer>();

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const s = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
    const size = s.value;
    q = s.next;

    bodies.set(i, wasm.subarray(q, q + size));
    q += size;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = getCodeBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN PATH TO FUNC 87");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const func of TARGETS) {

  const body = bodies.get(func);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}: NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(`############################################################`);
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log(`BODY SIZE : ${body.length}`);
  console.log(`############################################################`);

  let found = false;

  for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {

    if (body[i] !== 0x10) continue;

    const r = readU32Leb(body, i + 1);

    if (r.value === 87) {
      console.log(
        `>>> DIRECT CALL 87 @ 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(4, "0")}`
      );
      found = true;
    }

    i = r.next - 1;
  }

  if (!found) {
    console.log("NO DIRECT CALL 87");
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\scanRealCallIndirectV47.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("REAL CALL_INDIRECT SCAN v47");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Code : ${hex(CODE_START)} .. ${hex(CODE_END)}`);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = CODE_START; i < CODE_END; i++) {

  if (b[i] !== 0x11)
    continue;

  try {

    const typeR = uleb(i + 1);
    const tableR = uleb(typeR.p);

    /*
       call_indirect encoding:

       0x11
       typeidx   : ULEB
       tableidx  : ULEB
    */

    hits++;

    console.log(
      `#${hits} @ ${hex(i)}  type=${typeR.v}  table=${tableR.v}`
    );

  } catch {
    // Ignore malformed byte sequences.
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL CODE CALL_INDIRECT : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END V47");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\scanS1ComputedWriters.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 0x19534c;
const BASE = 0x195348;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, end: p };
    s += 7;
  }
  return null;
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos, x = 0;
  while (p < b.length) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (-1 << s);
      return { value: v | 0, end: p };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("S1 COMPUTED WRITER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET :", hex(TARGET));
console.log("BASE   :", hex(BASE));
console.log();

let hits = [];

for (let p = 0; p < b.length - 6; p++) {

  // i32.store
  if (b[p] === 0x36) {
    const a = uleb(p + 1);
    if (!a) continue;

    const o = uleb(a.end);
    if (!o) continue;

    const offset = o.value;

    // Search backwards for:
    // i32.const BASE
    // i32.const / arithmetic
    // ... 
    // i32.store
    //
    // Focus on store offsets near +4 and +0.
    if (offset > 16) continue;

    const start = Math.max(0, p - 80);

    for (let q = start; q < p; q++) {

      // i32.const
      if (b[q] !== 0x41) continue;

      const sv = sleb(q + 1);
      if (!sv) continue;

      const val = sv.value >>> 0;

      if (val === BASE || val === TARGET || val === 4) {

        hits.push({
          store: p,
          offset,
          constAt: q,
          value: val
        });
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("CANDIDATE COMPUTED WRITERS :", hits.length);
console.log();

for (const h of hits) {
  console.log(
    "STORE", hex(h.store),
    "offset=", h.offset,
    "CONST@", hex(h.constAt),
    "value=", hex(h.value)
  );

  const s = Math.max(0, h.store - 32);
  const e = Math.min(b.length, h.store + 8);

  console.log("  BYTES:");
  for (let x = s; x < e; x++) {
    console.log(
      "   " + hex(x),
      b[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
    );
  }
  console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\scanSegment412413ConstReaders.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const SEG412_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG412_END   = 0x0018ab8d;

const SEG413_START = 0x0018ab98;
const SEG413_END   = 0x0018f512;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
    }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                value |= (~0 << shift);

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
    }
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function region(v: number) {
    if (v >= SEG412_START && v < SEG412_END)
        return "SEG412";

    if (v >= SEG413_START && v < SEG413_END)
        return "SEG413";

    return "";
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

const fc = uleb(codeStart);
const functionCount = fc.value;

let bodyPos = fc.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEGMENT 412 / 413 CONSTANT READER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM size =", wasm.length);
console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS =", functionCount);

console.log();
console.log("SEG412 =", hex(SEG412_START), "..", hex(SEG412_END));
console.log("SEG413 =", hex(SEG413_START), "..", hex(SEG413_END));

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");

let parsed = 0;
let seg412ConstCount = 0;
let seg413ConstCount = 0;
let interestingChains = 0;

for (let body = 0; body < functionCount; body++) {

    const bs = uleb(bodyPos);

    const bodySize = bs.value;
    const bodyStart = bs.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (bodyEnd > codeEnd)
        throw new Error("Body exceeds code section");

    const globalFn = body + 83;

    let q = bodyStart;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Skip local declarations
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const locals = uleb(q);
    q = locals.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
        const count = uleb(q);
        q = count.next;
        q++;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Scan instructions
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    while (q < bodyEnd) {

        const opPos = q;
        const op = wasm[q++];

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i32.const
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x41) {

            const c = sleb(q);
            q = c.next;

            const value = c.value >>> 0;
            const r = region(value);

            if (r) {

                if (r === "SEG412")
                    seg412ConstCount++;
                else
                    seg413ConstCount++;

                console.log();
                console.log(
                    `FUNCTION ${globalFn} (body ${body})`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  CONST @ ${hex(opPos)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  VALUE = ${hex(value)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  REGION = ${r}`
                );

                // Show following raw bytes
                const end = Math.min(q + 32, bodyEnd);

                console.log(
                    "  NEXT =",
                    Buffer.from(wasm.subarray(q, end))
                        .toString("hex")
                        .match(/.{1,2}/g)
                        ?.join(" ")
                );

                // ------------------------------------------------
                // Look ahead for first few memory operations /
                // call_indirect without trying to emulate stack.
                // ------------------------------------------------

                let z = q;
                let steps = 0;
                let loads = 0;
                let indirects = 0;

                while (z < bodyEnd && steps < 20) {

                    const oo = wasm[z++];

                    if (oo === 0x28 ||
                        oo === 0x29 ||
                        oo === 0x2c ||
                        oo === 0x2d) {

                        const a = uleb(z);
                        z = a.next;

                        const b = uleb(z);
                        z = b.next;

                        loads++;

                        console.log(
                            `    LOAD @ ${hex(z - 1)} offset=${b.value}`
                        );
                    }
                    else if (oo === 0x11) {

                        const t = uleb(z);
                        z = t.next;

                        const table = uleb(z);
                        z = table.next;

                        indirects++;

                        console.log(
                            `    CALL_INDIRECT @ ${hex(z - 1)} type=${t.value}`
                        );

                        interestingChains++;
                    }
                    else if (oo === 0x10) {

                        const c2 = uleb(z);
                        z = c2.next;

                        console.log(
                            `    CALL ${c2.value}`
                        );
                    }
                    else if (oo === 0x0b) {
                        break;
                    }

                    steps++;
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // immediates
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            const y = uleb(q);
            q = y.next;

            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2a ||
            op === 0x2b ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x2e ||
            op === 0x2f ||
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x38 ||
            op === 0x39 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b
        ) {
            const a = uleb(q);
            q = a.next;

            const b = uleb(q);
            q = b.next;

            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x02 ||
            op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x04
        ) {
            const x = sleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            q++;
            continue;
        }
    }

    parsed++;
    bodyPos = bodyEnd;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("Functions parsed =", parsed);
console.log("Expected functions =", functionCount);

console.log(
    "Final code pointer =",
    hex(bodyPos)
);

console.log(
    "Expected code end =",
    hex(codeEnd)
);

console.log();

console.log(
    "SEG412 constants =",
    seg412ConstCount
);

console.log(
    "SEG413 constants =",
    seg413ConstCount
);

console.log(
    "Interesting indirect chains =",
    interestingChains
);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const SEG_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG_END   = 0x0018ab8d;

const GROUPS = [
    {
        name: "GROUP_A",
        start: 0x00189dd8,
        end:   0x00189df4,
        target: 2053,
        fn: 8952
    },
    {
        name: "GROUP_B",
        start: 0x00189f88,
        end:   0x00189fa4,
        target: 2101,
        fn: 8927
    },
    {
        name: "GROUP_C",
        start: 0x0018a89c,
        end:   0x0018a8b8,
        target: 2612,
        fn: 8637
    }
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
    }
}

function i32le(pos: number): number {
    return (
        wasm[pos] |
        (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function signedLEB(pos: number) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        result |= (~0 << shift);

    return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

console.log("  end   =", hex(SEG_END));

console.log();

    console.log(
        `${g.name}: ${hex(g.start)} .. ${hex(g.end)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  ${g.target} -> Function ${g.fn}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + size;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

const fc = uleb(codeStart);

const functionCount = fc.value;

let bodyPos = fc.next;

console.log();
console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS =", functionCount);

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");

let parsed = 0;
let segConstHits = 0;
let segDerivedHits = 0;
let groupHits = 0;

const printed = new Set<string>();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Lightweight symbolic values
//
// Track:
//   const
//   local.get
//   local.set
//   local.tee
//   add/sub
//   shl
//
// We only keep expressions that may resolve to a numeric
// Segment-412 address or an address derived from it.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

type Sym =
    | { kind: "const"; value: number }
    | { kind: "local"; index: number }
    | { kind: "unknown" }
    | {
        kind: "add" | "sub";
        a: Sym;
        b: Sym;
    }
    | {
        kind: "shl";
        a: Sym;
        amount: number;
    };

function symConst(v: number): Sym {
    return { kind: "const", value: v >>> 0 };
}

function symUnknown(): Sym {
    return { kind: "unknown" };
}

function resolveConst(s: Sym): number | null {

    if (s.kind === "const")
        return s.value >>> 0;

    if (s.kind === "add") {
        const a = resolveConst(s.a);
        const b = resolveConst(s.b);

        if (a !== null && b !== null)
            return (a + b) >>> 0;

        return null;
    }

    if (s.kind === "sub") {
        const a = resolveConst(s.a);
        const b = resolveConst(s.b);

        if (a !== null && b !== null)
            return (a - b) >>> 0;

        return null;
    }

    if (s.kind === "shl") {
        const a = resolveConst(s.a);

        if (a !== null)
            return (a << s.amount) >>> 0;

        return null;
    }

    return null;
}

function symText(s: Sym): string {

    if (s.kind === "const")
        return hex(s.value);

    if (s.kind === "local")
        return `local[${s.index}]`;

    if (s.kind === "unknown")
        return "?";

    if (s.kind === "add")
        return `(${symText(s.a)} + ${symText(s.b)})`;

    if (s.kind === "sub")
        return `(${symText(s.a)} - ${symText(s.b)})`;

    if (s.kind === "shl")
        return `(${symText(s.a)} << ${s.amount})`;

    return "?";
}

function isInterestingAddress(v: number): boolean {

    if (v >= SEG_START && v < SEG_END)
        return true;

    for (const g of GROUPS) {
        if (v >= g.start && v <= g.end)
            return true;
    }

    return false;
}

function groupFor(v: number) {

    for (const g of GROUPS) {
        if (v >= g.start && v <= g.end)
            return g;
    }

    return null;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Body scan
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (let body = 0; body < functionCount; body++) {

    const bs = uleb(bodyPos);

    const bodySize = bs.value;
    const bodyStart = bs.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (bodyEnd > codeEnd)
        throw new Error("Body exceeds code section");

    const globalFn = body + 83;

    // locals:
    // params are unknown.
    const locals = new Map<number, Sym>();

    let stack: Sym[] = [];

    function pop(): Sym {
        return stack.length
            ? stack.pop()!
            : symUnknown();
    }

    function push(s: Sym) {
        stack.push(s);
    }

    function resetStack() {
        stack = [];
    }

    let q = bodyStart;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Skip local declarations
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const localDecl = uleb(q);
    q = localDecl.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
        const count = uleb(q);
        q = count.next;

        // value type
        q++;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Instruction scan
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    while (q < bodyEnd) {

        const opPos = q;
        const op = wasm[q++];

        // block / loop / if
        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            q++;
            continue;
        }

        // end
        if (op === 0x0b) {
            continue;
        }

        // else
        if (op === 0x05) {
            resetStack();
            continue;
        }

        // br / br_if
        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        // return
        if (op === 0x0f) {
            resetStack();
            continue;
        }

        // call
        if (op === 0x10) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            resetStack();
            continue;
        }

        // call_indirect
        if (op === 0x11) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            const y = uleb(q);
            q = y.next;

            // preserve symbolic index information
            pop();

            continue;
        }

        // local.get
        if (op === 0x20) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            const v =
                locals.get(x.value) ??
                { kind: "local", index: x.value };

            push(v);
            continue;
        }

        // local.set
        if (op === 0x21) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            locals.set(x.value, pop());
            continue;
        }

        // local.tee
        if (op === 0x22) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            const v = pop();

            locals.set(x.value, v);
            push(v);

            continue;
        }

        // global.get
        if (op === 0x23) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            push(symUnknown());
            continue;
        }

        // global.set
        if (op === 0x24) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            pop();
            continue;
        }

        // i32.load / load8 / load16
        if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d
        ) {
            const align = uleb(q);
            q = align.next;

            const off = uleb(q);
            q = off.next;

            const base = pop();

            const baseValue = resolveConst(base);

            if (baseValue !== null) {

                const effective =
                    (baseValue + off.value) >>> 0;

                if (effective >= SEG_START &&
                    effective < SEG_END) {

                    segConstHits++;

                    const key =
                        `${globalFn}:${effective}`;

                    if (!printed.has(key)) {

                        printed.add(key);

                        console.log();
                        console.log(
                            `FUNCTION ${globalFn} (body ${body})`
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `  LOAD @ ${hex(opPos)}`
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `  base = ${hex(baseValue)}`
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `  offset = ${off.value}`
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `  EFFECTIVE = ${hex(effective)}`
                        );

                        const g = groupFor(effective);

                        if (g) {
                            groupHits++;

                            console.log(
                                `  >>> ${g.name}`
                            );

                            console.log(
                                `  >>> target ${g.target}`
                            );

                            console.log(
                                `  >>> Function ${g.fn}`
                            );
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // A load result is unknown.
            push(symUnknown());

            continue;
        }

        // i32.const
        if (op === 0x41) {
            const x = signedLEB(q);
            q = x.next;

            push(symConst(x.value));
            continue;
        }

        // i32.add
        if (op === 0x6a) {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const av = resolveConst(a);
            const bv = resolveConst(b);

            if (av !== null && bv !== null)
                push(symConst((av + bv) >>> 0));
            else
                push({ kind: "add", a, b });

            continue;
        }

        // i32.sub
        if (op === 0x6b) {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const av = resolveConst(a);
            const bv = resolveConst(b);

            if (av !== null && bv !== null)
                push(symConst((av - bv) >>> 0));
            else
                push({ kind: "sub", a, b });

            continue;
        }

        // i32.shl
        if (op === 0x74) {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const bv = resolveConst(b);

            if (bv !== null)
                push({
                    kind: "shl",
                    a,
                    amount: bv & 31
                });
            else
                push(symUnknown());

            continue;
        }

        // Memory.store family.
        // We only need to keep stack synchronized approximately.
        if (
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x38 ||
            op === 0x39 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b
        ) {
            const align = uleb(q);
            q = align.next;

            const off = uleb(q);
            q = off.next;

            pop();
            pop();

            continue;
        }

        // i32.eqz
        if (op === 0x45) {
            pop();
            push(symUnknown());
            continue;
        }

        // i32.eq / ne / lt / gt / le / ge
        if (op >= 0x46 && op <= 0x4f) {
            pop();
            pop();
            push(symUnknown());
            continue;
        }

        // i32.and/or/xor
        if (op >= 0x71 && op <= 0x73) {
            pop();
            pop();
            push(symUnknown());
            continue;
        }

        // i32.mul
        if (op === 0x6c) {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const av = resolveConst(a);
            const bv = resolveConst(b);

            if (av !== null && bv !== null)
                push(symConst(Math.imul(av, bv) >>> 0));
            else
                push(symUnknown());

            continue;
        }

        // i32.div / rem
        if (op >= 0x6d && op <= 0x70) {
            pop();
            pop();
            push(symUnknown());
            continue;
        }

        // Loads/stores and other opcodes not modelled:
        // keep traversal safe by consuming known immediates where
        // possible, but don't fabricate symbolic results.

        if (
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2a ||
            op === 0x2e ||
            op === 0x2f
        ) {
            const a = uleb(q);
            q = a.next;

            const b = uleb(q);
            q = b.next;

            pop();
            push(symUnknown());

            continue;
        }

        // memory.size / memory.grow
        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            q++;
            push(symUnknown());
            continue;
        }

        // Drop
        if (op === 0x1a) {
            pop();
            continue;
        }

        // select
        if (op === 0x1b) {
            pop();
            pop();
            pop();
            push(symUnknown());
            continue;
        }

        // ref/null/etc. and miscellaneous immediates:
        // leave unknown rather than risking parser desync.
        //
        // The verifier at the end is authoritative.
    }

    parsed++;
    bodyPos = bodyEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Verification
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    "Functions parsed =",
    parsed
);

console.log(
    "Expected functions =",
    functionCount
);

console.log(
    "Final code pointer =",
    hex(bodyPos)
);

console.log(
    "Expected code end =",
    hex(codeEnd)
);

console.log();
console.log(
    "Segment-412 constant-derived loads =",
    segConstHits
);

console.log(
    "Target-group hits =",
    groupHits
);

console.log(
    "Symbolic/derived base candidates =",
    segDerivedHits
);

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
    {
        name: "GROUP_A",
        start: 0x00189dd8,
        end:   0x00189df4,
        target: 2053,
        fn: 8952
    },
    {
        name: "GROUP_B",
        start: 0x00189f88,
        end:   0x00189fa4,
        target: 2101,
        fn: 8927
    },
    {
        name: "GROUP_C",
        start: 0x0018a89c,
        end:   0x0018a8b8,
        target: 2612,
        fn: 8637
    }
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32(p: number): number {
    return (
        wasm[p] |
        (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow at 0x" + (pos - 1).toString(16));
        }
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEGMENT 412 READER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM size =", wasm.length);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;

let codePayloadStart = -1;
let codePayloadEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const len = uleb(p);
    const payloadSize = len.value;
    p = len.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + payloadSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codePayloadStart = payloadStart;
        codePayloadEnd = payloadEnd;
        break;
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

if (codePayloadStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

console.log(
    "CODE SECTION = 0x" +
    (codePayloadStart - 4).toString(16)
);

console.log(
    "CODE PAYLOAD = 0x" +
    codePayloadStart.toString(16) +
    " .. 0x" +
    codePayloadEnd.toString(16)
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// First ULEB inside Code section = function count
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const fc = uleb(codePayloadStart);
const functionCount = fc.value;

console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS =", functionCount);

let bodyPos = fc.next;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Targets
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log();
console.log("TARGET GROUPS");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    console.log(
        `${t.name}: 0x${t.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${t.end.toString(16)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  target ${t.target} -> Function ${t.fn}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION BODY SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");

let totalHits = 0;
let functionsWithHits = 0;
let parsedBodies = 0;

for (let bodyOrdinal = 0; bodyOrdinal < functionCount; bodyOrdinal++) {

    if (bodyPos >= codePayloadEnd) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected code end before body ${bodyOrdinal}`
        );
    }

    const bodySizeInfo = uleb(bodyPos);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    const bodyStart = bodySizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (bodyEnd > codePayloadEnd) {
        throw new Error(
            `Body ${bodyOrdinal} exceeds code section`
        );
    }

    const globalFunction = bodyOrdinal + 83;

    let hitsHere = 0;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Raw 32-bit little-endian aligned scan.
    //
    // This deliberately scans the actual instruction bytes for
    // constants equal to addresses inside Segment 412 target
    // groups. It does NOT assume a particular opcode encoding.
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    for (let q = bodyStart; q + 4 <= bodyEnd; q++) {

        const value = u32(q);

        for (const t of TARGETS) {

            if (value >= t.start && value <= t.end) {

                if (hitsHere === 0) {
                    console.log();
                    console.log(
                        `FUNCTION ${globalFunction} (body ${bodyOrdinal})`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `  range = 0x${bodyStart.toString(16)} .. 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`
                    );
                }

                hitsHere++;
                totalHits++;

                console.log(
                    `  HIT ${t.name} @ file 0x${q.toString(16)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `    value = 0x${value.toString(16)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `    target = ${t.target}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `    dispatcher = Function ${t.fn}`
                );

                const from = Math.max(bodyStart, q - 12);
                const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, q + 20);

                console.log(
                    "    bytes =",
                    Buffer.from(wasm.subarray(from, to))
                        .toString("hex")
                        .match(/.{1,2}/g)
                        ?.join(" ")
                );
            }
        }
    }

    if (hitsHere > 0) {
        functionsWithHits++;
    }

    parsedBodies++;
    bodyPos = bodyEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Verification
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    "Functions parsed =",
    parsedBodies
);

console.log(
    "Expected functions =",
    functionCount
);

console.log(
    "Final code pointer = 0x" +
    bodyPos.toString(16)
);

console.log(
    "Expected code end = 0x" +
    codePayloadEnd.toString(16)
);

console.log(
    "Functions with raw target-range hits =",
    functionsWithHits
);

    "Total raw target-range hits =",
    totalHits
);

);
console.log(

);

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

const targets = new Map([
  [0x195348,"S0"],
  [0x19534c,"S1"],
  [0x195350,"S2"],
  [0x195354,"S3"],
  [0x195358,"S4"],
  [0x19535c,"S5"],
  [0x195360,"S6"]
]);

let p=8, code=0;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;
  if(id===10){ code=p; break; }
  p+=n;
}

p=code;
const [count,q0]=u(p); p=q0;

for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const [size,q]=u(p); p=q;

  const func=83+i;
  const start=p, end=p+size;

  for(let x=start;x<end;x++){

    if(b[x]!==0x41) continue;

    const [v,qv]=u(x+1);
    const name=targets.get(v);

    if(!name) continue;

    console.log(
      `FUNC ${func} ${name} CONST @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
      [...b.subarray(
        Math.max(start,x-25),
        Math.min(end,x+70)
      )]
      .map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join(" ")
    );

    console.log("--------------------------------------------");
  }

  p=end;
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16);
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;
  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }
  p += n;
}

p = codeStart;
const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

const TARGETS = new Map([
  [0x195348, "S0"],
  [0x19534c, "S1"],
  [0x195350, "S2"],
  [0x195354, "S3"],
  [0x195358, "S4"]
]);

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
    // i32.store
    if (b[x] !== 0x36) continue;

    const [align, q1] = u(x + 1);
    const [offset, q2] = u(q1);

    // Search immediately preceding ~60 bytes for exact target const.
    for (let z = Math.max(start, x - 60); z < x; z++) {
      if (b[z] !== 0x41) continue;

      const [v, qv] = u(z + 1);
      const name = TARGETS.get(v);

      if (!name) continue;

      console.log(
        `FUNC ${func} | ${name} CONST ${hex(v)} | ` +
        `STORE 0x${x.toString(16)} | OFFSET ${offset}`
      );

      console.log(
        [...b.subarray(Math.max(start, z - 12), Math.min(end, x + 8))]
          .map(a => a.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );

      console.log("--------------------------------------------");
      break;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

FILE: scripts\scanStateFamilyWriters.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const BASE = 0x195348;
const END  = 0x195364;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);
  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function sections() {
  let p = 8, out = [];

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const z = uleb(p);
    p = z.next;

    out.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + z.value
    });

    p += z.value;
  }

  return out;
}

const code = sections().find(x => x.id === 10);

let p = code.start;
const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

const funcs = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const z = uleb(p);
  p = z.next;

  funcs.push({
    func: i + 83,
    start: p,
    end: p + z.value
  });

  p += z.value;
}

function isConstAt(pos, value) {
  if (b[pos] !== 0x41) return false;

  const x = sleb(pos + 1);
  return (x.value >>> 0) === (value >>> 0);
}

function readLocal(pos) {
  const x = uleb(pos + 1);
  return {
    local: x.value,
    next: x.next
  };
}

function readMem(pos) {
  const op = b[pos];

  // i32.store
  if (op !== 0x36) return null;

  const a = uleb(pos + 1);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    end: o.next,
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value
  };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STATE FAMILY WRITER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
  `RANGE : 0x${BASE.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`
);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (const fn of funcs) {

  for (let q = fn.start; q < fn.end; q++) {

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * CASE 1
     *
     * i32.const STATE_ADDRESS
     * ...
     * i32.store
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    let stateOffset = null;

    for (let s = 0; s < 28; s += 4) {
      const addr = BASE + s;

      if (isConstAt(q, addr)) {
        stateOffset = s;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (stateOffset !== null) {

      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log(`FUNC ${fn.func}`);
      console.log(
        `STATE CONST @ 0x${q.toString(16)}`
      );
      console.log(
        `ADDRESS : 0x${(BASE + stateOffset).toString(16)}`
      );
      console.log(
        `OFFSET  : +0x${stateOffset.toString(16)}`
      );
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      /*
       * inspect next 80 bytes for store / locals
       */

      const z = Math.min(fn.end, q + 100);

      for (let x = q; x < z; x++) {

        if (b[x] === 0x20) {
          const l = readLocal(x);
          console.log(
            `0x${x.toString(16)} local.get L${l.local}`
          );
          x = l.next - 1;
          continue;
        }

        if (b[x] === 0x21) {
          const l = readLocal(x);
          console.log(
            `0x${x.toString(16)} local.set L${l.local}`
          );
          x = l.next - 1;
          continue;
        }

        if (b[x] === 0x22) {
          const l = readLocal(x);
          console.log(
            `0x${x.toString(16)} local.tee L${l.local}`
          );
          x = l.next - 1;
          continue;
        }

        if (b[x] === 0x36) {
          const m = readMem(x);

          if (m) {
            console.log(
              `0x${x.toString(16)} i32.store ` +
              `align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
            );

            x = m.end - 1;
            hits++;
          }

          continue;
        }

        if (b[x] === 0x28) {
          const a = uleb(x + 1);
          const o = uleb(a.next);

          console.log(
            `0x${x.toString(16)} i32.load ` +
            `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
          );

          x = o.next - 1;
          continue;
        }
      }

      console.log("");
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * CASE 2
     *
     * Scan stores and look backwards for an exact
     * state-family address constant.
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (b[q] === 0x36) {

      const m = readMem(q);

      if (!m) continue;

      const backStart = Math.max(fn.start, q - 80);

      for (let x = backStart; x < q; x++) {

        for (let s = 0; s < 28; s += 4) {

          const addr = BASE + s;

          if (isConstAt(x, addr)) {

            console.log("############################################################");
            console.log(`FUNC ${fn.func}`);
            console.log(
              `STORE @ 0x${q.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(
              `NEAR STATE CONST @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(
              `ADDRESS : 0x${addr.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(
              `OFFSET  : +0x${s.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(
              `STORE OFFSET : ${m.offset}`
            );
            console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

            const a = Math.max(fn.start, x - 12);
            const z = Math.min(fn.end, q + 16);

            for (let y = a; y < z; y++) {
              console.log(
                `  0x${y.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ` +
                `${b[y].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
              );
            }

            console.log("");

            hits++;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`STATE FAMILY STORE HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\scanStaticRegion250xxx.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const file =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

const IMPORTED = 83;

const BASE = 0x250000;
const END  = 0x251000;

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
    return "0x" +
        (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(pos);
    const size = s.value;
    pos = s.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const count = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + i,
        start: p,
        end: p + size
    });

    p += size;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STATIC REGION SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`RANGE : ${hex(BASE)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("TARGET : 0x250FA0");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

    for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

        // i32.const
        if (buf[p] !== 0x41)
            continue;

        const r = readSLEB(p + 1);
        const value = r.value >>> 0;

        if (value < BASE || value >= END)
            continue;

        total++;

        console.log(
            `\nFUNC ${body.func} @ ${hex(p)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `CONST : ${hex(value)}`
        );

        const from = Math.max(body.start, p - 12);
        const to = Math.min(body.end, r.next + 24);

        const bytes = [];

        for (let q = from; q < to; q++) {

            bytes.push(
                `${hex(q)}:${buf[q]
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
        }

        console.log(bytes.join(" "));
    }
}

console.log("\n------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL i32.const IN REGION : ${total}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\scanStaticStores.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const RANGE_START = 0x195000;
const RANGE_END   = 0x196000;

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSections() {
    let p = 8;

    const sections: {
        id: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        sections.push({ id, start, end });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

const sections = readSections();

const funcSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
    throw new Error("Function/code section not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = funcSec.start;

const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.next;

const definedCount = fc.value;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSec.start;

const cc = uleb(p);
p = cc.next;

if (cc.value !== definedCount) {
    throw new Error("Function/code count mismatch");
}

const functions: {
    globalFunc: number;
    ordinal: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < cc.value; ordinal++) {

    const bs = uleb(p);
    p = bs.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + bs.value;

    functions.push({
        globalFunc: 83 + ordinal,
        ordinal,
        start,
        end,
    });

    p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Store opcodes
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const STORE = new Set([
    0x36, // i32.store
    0x37, // i64.store
    0x38, // f32.store
    0x39, // f64.store
    0x3a, // i32.store8
    0x3b, // i32.store16
    0x3c, // i64.store8
    0x3d, // i64.store16
    0x3e, // i64.store32
]);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN STATIC / COMPUTED STORES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
    `TARGET RANGE = 0x${RANGE_START.toString(16)} .. 0x${RANGE_END.toString(16)}`
);
console.log(
    `CODE SECTION = 0x${codeSec.start.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} .. ` +
    `0x${codeSec.end.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let totalStores = 0;
let interesting = 0;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// For every store, inspect previous 48 bytes.
//
// Heuristic:
// look for i32.const values in the previous 48 bytes
// that are close to our static-memory region.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (const fn of functions) {

    for (let off = fn.start; off < fn.end; off++) {

        if (!STORE.has(buf[off]))
            continue;

        totalStores++;

        const a = uleb(off + 1);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        const contextStart = Math.max(fn.start, off - 64);

        const constants: {
            pos: number;
            value: number;
        }[] = [];

        for (let c = contextStart; c < off; c++) {

            if (buf[c] !== 0x41)
                continue;

            const x = uleb(c + 1);

            // Values near the static memory target.
            if (
                x.value >= RANGE_START - 0x1000 &&
                x.value <= RANGE_END + 0x1000
            ) {
                constants.push({
                    pos: c,
                    value: x.value,
                });
            }
        }

        if (constants.length === 0)
            continue;

        interesting++;

        console.log();
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(
            `FUNC ${fn.globalFunc} | STORE @ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
        );
        console.log(
            `STORE OPCODE = 0x${buf[off].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
        );
        console.log(`ALIGN = ${a.value}`);
        console.log(`OFFSET = 0x${o.value.toString(16)}`);

        console.log("NEARBY CONSTANTS:");

        for (const c of constants) {
            console.log(
                `  CONST @ 0x${c.pos.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
                `= 0x${c.value.toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        console.log("RAW CONTEXT:");

        const c0 = Math.max(fn.start, off - 64);
        const c1 = Math.min(fn.end, off + 16);

        for (let c = c0; c < c1; c += 16) {

            const e = Math.min(c + 16, c1);

            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let i = c; i < e; i++) {
                bytes.push(
                    buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `  0x${c.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL STORES = ${totalStores}`);
console.log(`INTERESTING STORES = ${interesting}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\scanType4IndirectCalls.js
