const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("GLOBAL TYPE 4 CALL_INDIRECT SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET    : TYPE 4 / FUNCTION 8952");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = 8;

function u8() {
    return buf[p++];
}

function u32() {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return result >>> 0;

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function bytes(n) {
    const start = p;
    p += n;
    return buf.subarray(start, p);
}

function readVec(fn) {
    const n = u32();
    const out = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
        out.push(fn());

    return out;
}

function readName() {
    const n = u32();
    return Buffer.from(bytes(n)).toString("utf8");
}

function hex(x, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTION MAP
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const sections = [];

p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

    const sectionStart = p;
    const id = u8();
    const size = u32();

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: sectionStart,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd,
        size
    });

    p = payloadEnd;
}

console.log("\nSECTION MAP");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const s of sections) {

    console.log(
        `SECTION ${s.id} ` +
        `${hex(s.payloadStart)} .. ${hex(s.payloadEnd)} ` +
        `SIZE ${s.size}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TYPE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const typeSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 1);

if (!typeSection)
    throw new Error("TYPE SECTION NOT FOUND");

p = typeSection.payloadStart;

const typeCount = u32();

const types = [];

function valType(v) {

    const names = {
        0x7f: "i32",
        0x7e: "i64",
        0x7d: "f32",
        0x7c: "f64",
        0x7b: "v128",
        0x70: "funcref",
        0x6f: "externref"
    };

    return names[v] || `0x${v.toString(16)}`;
}

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount; i++) {

    const form = u8();

    if (form !== 0x60)
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported type form at TYPE ${i}: 0x${form.toString(16)}`
        );

    const params = readVec(() => valType(u8()));
    const results = readVec(() => valType(u8()));

    types.push({
        index: i,
        params,
        results
    });
}

console.log("\nTYPE 4");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const type4 = types[4];

if (!type4)
    throw new Error("TYPE 4 NOT FOUND");

console.log(
    `TYPE 4 : (${type4.params.join(", ")}) -> (${type4.results.join(", ")})`
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const imports = [];

const importSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);

let importedFunctionCount = 0;

if (importSection) {

    p = importSection.payloadStart;

    const importCount = u32();

    for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {

        const module = readName();
        const name = readName();
        const kind = u8();

        let typeIndex = null;

        if (kind === 0x00) {

            typeIndex = u32();
            importedFunctionCount++;
        }

        else if (kind === 0x01) {

            u8();
            const flags = u8();
            u32();

            if (flags & 1)
                u32();
        }

        else if (kind === 0x02) {

            const flags = u8();
            u32();

            if (flags & 1)
                u32();
        }

        else if (kind === 0x03) {

            u8();
            u8();
        }

        else {

            throw new Error(
                `Unknown import kind ${kind}`
            );
        }

        imports.push({
            index: i,
            module,
            name,
            kind,
            typeIndex
        });
    }
}

console.log("\nIMPORTS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :", importedFunctionCount);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const functionSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);

if (!functionSection)
    throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");

p = functionSection.payloadStart;

const definedCount = u32();

const functionTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {

    functionTypes.push(u32());
}

function getFunctionType(functionIndex) {

    if (functionIndex < importedFunctionCount) {

        const imp = imports[functionIndex];

        return imp ? imp.typeIndex : null;
    }

    return functionTypes[functionIndex - importedFunctionCount];
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const codeSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!codeSection)
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

p = codeSection.payloadStart;

const codeCount = u32();

if (codeCount !== definedCount)
    throw new Error(
        `CODE/FUNCTION mismatch: ${codeCount} != ${definedCount}`
    );

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// LEB HELPERS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function readVarUint32() {

    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {

        const b = u8();

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return result >>> 0;

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readVarInt32() {

    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {

        b = u8();

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        result |= (~0 << shift);

    return result | 0;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// BLOCK TYPE
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function skipBlockType() {

    const b = buf[p];

    // valtype or empty block type
    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c ||
        b === 0x7b
    ) {
        p++;
        return;
    }

    readVarInt32();
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// MEMORY IMMEDIATE
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function skipMemArg() {

    readVarUint32(); // align
    readVarUint32(); // offset
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// INSTRUCTION DECODER
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function decodeInstruction(start) {

    const op = u8();

    const ins = {
        start,
        op,
        name: `op_0x${op.toString(16)}`
    };

    switch (op) {

        case 0x00:
            ins.name = "unreachable";
            break;

        case 0x01:
            ins.name = "nop";
            break;

        case 0x02:
            ins.name = "block";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x03:
            ins.name = "loop";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x04:
            ins.name = "if";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x05:
            ins.name = "else";
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            ins.name = "end";
            break;

        case 0x0c:
            ins.name = "br";
            ins.depth = readVarUint32();
            break;

        case 0x0d:
            ins.name = "br_if";
            ins.depth = readVarUint32();
            break;

        case 0x0e: {

            ins.name = "br_table";

            const n = readVarUint32();

            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
                readVarUint32();

            readVarUint32();

            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            ins.name = "return";
            break;

        case 0x10:
            ins.name = "call";
            ins.functionIndex = readVarUint32();
            break;

        case 0x11:

            ins.name = "call_indirect";
            ins.typeIndex = readVarUint32();
            ins.tableIndex = readVarUint32();

            break;

        case 0x1a:
            ins.name = "drop";
            break;

        case 0x1b:
            ins.name = "select";
            break;

        case 0x20:
            ins.name = "local.get";
            ins.local = readVarUint32();
            break;

        case 0x21:
            ins.name = "local.set";
            ins.local = readVarUint32();
            break;

        case 0x22:
            ins.name = "local.tee";
            ins.local = readVarUint32();
            break;

        case 0x23:
            ins.name = "global.get";
            ins.global = readVarUint32();
            break;

        case 0x24:
            ins.name = "global.set";
            ins.global = readVarUint32();
            break;

        // i32 loads
        case 0x28:
            ins.name = "i32.load";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x29:
            ins.name = "i64.load";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2a:
            ins.name = "f32.load";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2b:
            ins.name = "f64.load";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2c:
            ins.name = "i32.load8_s";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2d:
            ins.name = "i32.load8_u";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2e:
            ins.name = "i32.load16_s";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2f:
            ins.name = "i32.load16_u";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x30:
            ins.name = "i64.load8_s";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x31:
            ins.name = "i64.load8_u";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x32:
            ins.name = "i64.load16_s";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x33:
            ins.name = "i64.load16_u";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x34:
            ins.name = "i64.load32_s";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x35:
            ins.name = "i64.load32_u";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        // stores
        case 0x36:
            ins.name = "i32.store";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x37:
            ins.name = "i64.store";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x38:
            ins.name = "f32.store";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x39:
            ins.name = "f64.store";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3a:
            ins.name = "i32.store8";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3b:
            ins.name = "i32.store16";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3c:
            ins.name = "i64.store8";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3d:
            ins.name = "i64.store16";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3e:
            ins.name = "i64.store32";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3f:
            ins.name = "memory.size";
            readVarUint32();
            break;

        case 0x40:
            ins.name = "memory.grow";
            readVarUint32();
            break;

        // constants
        case 0x41:
            ins.name = "i32.const";
            ins.value = readVarInt32();
            break;

        case 0x42:
            ins.name = "i64.const";

            // skip signed LEB64
            while (true) {
                const b = u8();
                if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
                    break;
            }

            break;

        case 0x43:
            ins.name = "f32.const";
            p += 4;
            break;

        case 0x44:
            ins.name = "f64.const";
            p += 8;
            break;

        // i32 comparisons
        case 0x45:
            ins.name = "i32.eqz";
            break;

        case 0x46:
            ins.name = "i32.eq";
            break;

        case 0x47:
            ins.name = "i32.ne";
            break;

        case 0x48:
            ins.name = "i32.lt_s";
            break;

        case 0x49:
            ins.name = "i32.lt_u";
            break;

        case 0x4a:
            ins.name = "i32.gt_s";
            break;

        case 0x4b:
            ins.name = "i32.gt_u";
            break;

        case 0x4c:
            ins.name = "i32.le_s";
            break;

        case 0x4d:
            ins.name = "i32.le_u";
            break;

        case 0x4e:
            ins.name = "i32.ge_s";
            break;

        case 0x4f:
            ins.name = "i32.ge_u";
            break;

        // i32 arithmetic
        case 0x67:
            ins.name = "i32.clz";
            break;

        case 0x68:
            ins.name = "i32.ctz";
            break;

        case 0x69:
            ins.name = "i32.popcnt";
            break;

        case 0x6a:
            ins.name = "i32.add";
            break;

        case 0x6b:
            ins.name = "i32.sub";
            break;

        case 0x6c:
            ins.name = "i32.mul";
            break;

        case 0x6d:
            ins.name = "i32.div_s";
            break;

        case 0x6e:
            ins.name = "i32.div_u";
            break;

        case 0x6f:
            ins.name = "i32.rem_s";
            break;

        case 0x70:
            ins.name = "i32.rem_u";
            break;

        case 0x71:
            ins.name = "i32.and";
            break;

        case 0x72:
            ins.name = "i32.or";
            break;

        case 0x73:
            ins.name = "i32.xor";
            break;

        case 0x74:
            ins.name = "i32.shl";
            break;

        case 0x75:
            ins.name = "i32.shr_s";
            break;

        case 0x76:
            ins.name = "i32.shr_u";
            break;

        case 0x77:
            ins.name = "i32.rotl";
            break;

        case 0x78:
            ins.name = "i32.rotr";
            break;

        // i64 arithmetic
        case 0x7c:
            ins.name = "i64.add";
            break;

        case 0x7d:
            ins.name = "i64.sub";
            break;

        case 0x7e:
            ins.name = "i64.mul";
            break;

        case 0x7f:
            ins.name = "i64.div_s";
            break;

        case 0x80:
            ins.name = "i64.div_u";
            break;

        case 0x81:
            ins.name = "i64.rem_s";
            break;

        case 0x82:
            ins.name = "i64.rem_u";
            break;

        case 0x83:
            ins.name = "i64.and";
            break;

        case 0x84:
            ins.name = "i64.or";
            break;

        case 0x85:
            ins.name = "i64.xor";
            break;

        case 0x86:
            ins.name = "i64.shl";
            break;

        case 0x87:
            ins.name = "i64.shr_s";
            break;

        case 0x88:
            ins.name = "i64.shr_u";
            break;

        // conversions
        case 0xa7:
            ins.name = "i32.wrap_i64";
            break;

        case 0xac:
            ins.name = "i64.extend_i32_s";
            break;

        case 0xad:
            ins.name = "i64.extend_i32_u";
            break;

        // ref
        case 0xd0:
            ins.name = "ref.null";
            u8();
            break;

        case 0xd2:
            ins.name = "ref.func";
            ins.functionIndex = readVarUint32();
            break;

        // prefixes
        case 0xfc: {

            ins.prefix = 0xfc;
            ins.subopcode = readVarUint32();
            ins.name = `0xfc.${ins.subopcode}`;

            // memory/table bulk operations

            if (
                ins.subopcode === 8 ||
                ins.subopcode === 9
            ) {
                readVarUint32();
                readVarUint32();
            }

            else if (
                ins.subopcode === 10 ||
                ins.subopcode === 12 ||
                ins.subopcode === 13 ||
                ins.subopcode === 14 ||
                ins.subopcode === 15 ||
                ins.subopcode === 16 ||
                ins.subopcode === 17
            ) {
                readVarUint32();
            }

            break;
        }

        default:

            /*
              Unknown opcode:
              Stop only at this instruction. We do not attempt
              speculative byte consumption because that could corrupt
              the scan.
            */

            ins.unknown = true;
            break;
    }

    ins.end = p;

    return ins;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// BODY LOCAL DECLARATIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function skipLocals() {

    const groups = u32();

    let total = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < groups; i++) {

        const count = u32();

        u8(); // valtype

        total += count;
    }

    return total;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SCAN ALL FUNCTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const allIndirectType4 = [];
const allIndirect = [];

let decoderFailures = 0;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < codeCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const bodySize = u32();

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    const locals = skipLocals();

    while (p < bodyEnd) {

        const insStart = p;

        const ins = decodeInstruction(insStart);

        if (ins.unknown) {

            decoderFailures++;

            // Move to next body rather than corrupting global scan.
            p = bodyEnd;
            break;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x11) {

            const functionIndex =
                importedFunctionCount + bodyIndex;

            const record = {
                bodyIndex,
                functionIndex,
                typeIndex: ins.typeIndex,
                tableIndex: ins.tableIndex,
                address: ins.start,
                functionType: getFunctionType(functionIndex)
            };

            allIndirect.push(record);

            if (ins.typeIndex === 4) {

                allIndirectType4.push(record);
            }
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x0b && p === bodyEnd)
            break;
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

function readU32(buf, p) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function getSections(buf) {
    const sections = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readU32(buf, p);
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size: sizeInfo.value
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseCodeBodies(buf, section) {

    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const bodies = [];

    for (
        let bodyIndex = 0;
        bodyIndex < countInfo.value;
        bodyIndex++
    ) {

        const sizeInfo = readU32(buf, p);
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

        let q = bodyStart;

        // local declaration vector
        const localCountInfo = readU32(buf, q);
        q = localCountInfo.next;

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < localCountInfo.value;
            i++
        ) {

            const n = readU32(buf, q);
            q = n.next;

            // value type
            q++;
        }

        bodies.push({
            bodyIndex,
            functionIndex:
                IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + bodyIndex,
            bodyStart,
            codeStart: q,
            bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function hexByte(b) {
    return b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log(
    "============================================================"
);
console.log(
    "SCAN TYPE 4 DISPATCH SOURCES"
);

    IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS

const codeSection =
    sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!codeSection) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

const bodies =
    parseCodeBodies(
        wasm,

    );

        IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + bodies.length - 1
    }`
);

//
// 0x11
// type index = ULEB128
// table index = ULEB128
//
// For type 4 / table 0:
// 11 04 00
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const hits = [];

for (const body of bodies) {

    for (
        let p = body.codeStart;
        p + 2 < body.bodyEnd;
        p++
    ) {

        if (
            wasm[p] === 0x11 &&
            wasm[p + 1] === 0x04 &&
            wasm[p + 2] === 0x00
        ) {

            hits.push({
                functionIndex: body.functionIndex,
                bodyIndex: body.bodyIndex,
                offset: p,
                codeStart: body.codeStart,
                bodyEnd: body.bodyEnd
            });
        }
    }
}

    );

    console.log(

            hit.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
        }`

        Math.max(

            p === hit.offset
                ? " <<< CALL"
                : "";

        );

    if (!body) {
        console.log(

        );
        continue;
    }

    let found = false;

    for (
        let p = body.codeStart;
        p + 2 < body.bodyEnd;
        p++
    ) {

        if (
            wasm[p] === 0x11 &&
            wasm[p + 1] === 0x04 &&
            wasm[p + 2] === 0x00
        ) {

            found = true;

            console.log();
            console.log(
                `FUNC ${func}`
            );

            console.log(
                `CALL : 0x${
                    p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
                }`
            );

            const start =
                Math.max(
                    body.codeStart,
                    p - 20
                );

            for (
                let q = start;
                q < p + 3;
                q++
            ) {

                console.log(
                    `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                    hexByte(wasm[q])
                );
            }
        }
    }

    if (!found) {
        console.log(
            `FUNC ${func} : TYPE 4 CALL NOT FOUND`
        );
    }
}

console.log();

import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TESTNET_ENDPOINT =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

const ROUTERS = [
    'EQDx--jUU9PUtHltPYZX7wdzIi0SPY3KZ8nvOs0iZvQJd6Ql',
    'EQBQ_UBQvR9ryUjKDwijtoiyyga2Wl-yJm6Y8gl0k-HDh_5x',
    'EQBCtlN7Zy96qx-3yH0Yi4V0SNtQ-8RbhYaNs65MC4Hwfq31',
    'EQDh5oHPvfRwPu2bORBGCoLEO4WQZKL4fk5DD1gydeNG9oEH',
    'EQCiypoBWNIEPlarBp04UePyEj5zH0ZDHxuRNqJ1WQx3FCY-',
    'EQBwpBGEAb-NgjUxpmARAgVl8C4F_5GsXxZ3dpsA1qzQerNl',
    'EQCx0HDJ_DxLxDSQyfsEqHI8Rs65nygvdmeD9Ra7rY15OWN8',
    'EQDAPye7HAPAAl4WXpz5jOCdhf2H9h9QkkzRQ-6K5usiuQeC',
    'EQCDT9dCT52pdfsLNW0e6qP5T3cgq7M4Ug72zkGYgP17tsWD',
    'EQAJG5pyZPWEiQiMVJdf7bDRgRLzg6QR57qKeRsOrMO-ncZN',
    'EQDQ6j53q21HuZtw6oclm7z4LU2cG6S2OKvpSSMH548d7kJT',
    'EQByADL5Ra2dldrMSBctgfSm2X2W1P61NVW2RYDb8eJNJGx6',
    'EQBigMnbY4NU1uwdvzertV5mv_yI7282R-ffW7XZFWPEVRDG',
    'EQD11suHkrO_1Mb5IIdYFx5ZPy38MuHoeHx6dA-QRaD8w0UJ',
    'EQC67o2-2UzR1cJFrUGL5M7OAnLgG8oY_tHaTgGmR63LQNV-',
];

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: TESTNET_ENDPOINT,
    });

    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 ROUTER TESTNET SCAN');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================');

    for (const value of ROUTERS) {
        try {
            const address = Address.parse(value);
            const state = await client.getContractState(address);

            console.log('\n----------------------------------------------');
            console.log('ROUTER:', address.toString());

            console.log('STATE:', state.state);
            console.log(
                'CODE:',
                state.code ? `PRESENT (${state.code.length} bytes)` : 'EMPTY'
            );
            console.log(
                'DATA:',
                state.data ? `PRESENT (${state.data.length} bytes)` : 'EMPTY'
            );
            console.log('BALANCE:', state.balance.toString());

            if (state.state === 'active' && state.code && state.data) {
                console.log('*** ACTIVE CONTRACT FOUND ***');

                try {
                    const result = await client.runMethod(
                        address,
                        'get_router_data',
                        []
                    );

                    console.log('GET_ROUTER_DATA: SUCCESS');
                    console.log('STACK:', result.stack);
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log(
                        'GET_ROUTER_DATA:',
                        e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
                    );
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('\nINVALID ADDRESS:', value);
            console.log(
                e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('\n==============================================');
    console.log(' SCAN COMPLETE');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('==============================================');
}

FILE: scripts\scanWasmKaCalls.ts

import fs from 'fs';

const WASM = './emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const TARGET_DEFINED_INDEX = 5456;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

let p = 8;

function readU32(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const byte = b[p++];

        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readName(): string {
    const len = readU32();
    const s = b.subarray(p, p + len).toString('utf8');
    p += len;
    return s;
}

function readULEB(data: Buffer, pos: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const byte = data[pos++];

        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

let importedFunctions = 0;
let targetBody: Buffer | undefined;

while (p < b.length) {
    const sectionId = b[p++];
    const sectionSize = readU32();

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 2) {
        // Import section
        const count = readU32();

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            readName();
            readName();

            const kind = b[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                readU32();
                importedFunctions++;
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                p++;
                const flags = readU32();
                readU32();
                if (flags & 1) readU32();
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                const flags = readU32();
                readU32();
                if (flags & 1) readU32();
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                p += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        const functionCount = readU32();

        for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {
            const bodySize = readU32();
            const body = b.subarray(p, p + bodySize);

            if (i === TARGET_DEFINED_INDEX) {
                targetBody = Buffer.from(body);
                break;
            }

            p += bodySize;
        }

        break;
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
}

if (!targetBody) {
    throw new Error('Target Ka body not found');
}

console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' WASM Ka CALL SCANNER');
console.log('============================================================');

console.log(`Imported functions : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`Ka defined index   : ${TARGET_DEFINED_INDEX}`);
console.log(`Ka function index  : ${importedFunctions + TARGET_DEFINED_INDEX}`);
console.log(`Ka body size       : ${targetBody.length}`);

console.log('');
console.log('=== CALL INSTRUCTIONS ===');

let found = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < targetBody.length; i++) {

    // 0x10 = call
    if (targetBody[i] !== 0x10) {
        continue;
    }

    const r = readULEB(targetBody, i + 1);

    const target = r.value;

    console.log(
        `offset=${i.toString().padStart(4, '0')}  ` +
        `opcode=10  ` +
        `target=${target}`
    );

    found++;
}

console.log('');
console.log(`Total CALL instructions: ${found}`);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\scanWasmSections.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(offset: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = offset;

    while (true) {
        if (p >= b.length) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB out of range at 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        }

        const x = b[p++];
        value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB too large at 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }
}

const sectionNames: Record<number, string> = {
    0: "custom",
    1: "type",
    2: "import",
    3: "function",
    4: "table",
    5: "memory",
    6: "global",
    7: "export",
    8: "start",
    9: "element",
    10: "code",
    11: "data",
    12: "data-count",
    13: "tag",
};

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" WASM SECTION TABLE SCAN");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log("");

if (
    b[0] !== 0x00 ||
    b[1] !== 0x61 ||
    b[2] !== 0x73 ||
    b[3] !== 0x6d
) {
    throw new Error("Invalid WASM magic");
}

console.log("MAGIC     : OK");
console.log(
    `VERSION   : ${b[4].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} ` +
    `${b[5].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} ` +
    `${b[6].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} ` +
    `${b[7].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
);
console.log("");

let p = 8;
let index = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
    const sectionStart = p;
    const id = b[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const payloadSize = sizeInfo.value;
    const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + payloadSize;

    if (payloadEnd > b.length) {
        throw new Error(
            `Section ${index} exceeds file: ` +
            `end=0x${payloadEnd.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    const name = sectionNames[id] ?? `UNKNOWN(${id})`;

    console.log(
        `#${index.toString().padStart(2, "0")} ` +
        `id=${id.toString().padStart(2, " ")} ` +
        `${name.padEnd(10)} ` +
        `header=0x${sectionStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
        `payload=0x${payloadStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}..` +
        `0x${payloadEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
        `size=${payloadSize}`
    );

    if (id === 10) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(">>> CODE SECTION FOUND <<<");
        console.log(
            `    section header : 0x${sectionStart.toString(16)}`
        );
        console.log(
            `    payload start  : 0x${payloadStart.toString(16)}`
        );
        console.log(
            `    payload end    : 0x${payloadEnd.toString(16)}`
        );
        console.log(`    payload size   : ${payloadSize}`);
        console.log("");
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
    index++;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN COMPLETE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\searchPool7219004800V22.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_NANO = 7219004800n;
const TARGET = '7.219004800';

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL TRANSACTION SEARCH');
    console.log(' TARGET: 7.219004800 ROOSTER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool:', POOL.toString());
    console.log('Target nano ROOSTER:', TARGET_NANO.toString());
    console.log('Target ROOSTER:', TARGET);
    console.log('');

    const url =
        `${TONCENTER}` +
        `?address=${encodeURIComponent(POOL.toString())}` +
        `&limit=100`;

    console.log('Requesting Pool transactions...');
    console.log('');

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`
        );
    }

    const json: any = await response.json();

    if (!json.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter API error: ${JSON.stringify(json)}`
        );
    }

    const transactions = json.result ?? [];

    console.log('Transactions returned:', transactions.length);
    console.log('');

    let found = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < transactions.length; i++) {
        const tx = transactions[i];

        const lt = tx.lt;
        const hash = tx.transaction_id?.hash;
        const utime = tx.utime;

        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log(`#${i + 1}`);
        console.log('LT:', lt);
        console.log('Hash:', hash);
        console.log('UTIME:', utime);

        const inMsg = tx.in_msg;

        if (inMsg) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('IN MESSAGE');
            console.log('Source:', inMsg.source);
            console.log('Destination:', inMsg.destination);
            console.log('Value:', inMsg.value);

            if (inMsg.message) {
                console.log('Message:', inMsg.message);
            }

            if (inMsg.msg_data?.body) {
                console.log(
                    'Body BOC:',
                    inMsg.msg_data.body
                );
            }
        }

        const outMsgs = tx.out_msgs ?? [];

        console.log('');
        console.log('OUT MESSAGES:', outMsgs.length);

        for (let j = 0; j < outMsgs.length; j++) {
            const msg = outMsgs[j];

            console.log('');
            console.log(`OUT #${j + 1}`);
            console.log('Source:', msg.source);
            console.log('Destination:', msg.destination);
            console.log('Value:', msg.value);

            if (msg.message) {
                console.log('Message:', msg.message);
            }

            if (msg.msg_data?.body) {
                console.log(
                    'Body BOC:',
                    msg.msg_data.body
                );
            }

            /*
             * Direct decimal/string search.
             * This catches an exact visible value if returned
             * by the API.
             */
            const serialized = JSON.stringify(msg);

            if (
                serialized.includes(TARGET) ||
                serialized.includes(TARGET_NANO.toString())
            ) {
                found++;

                console.log('');
                console.log('*** TARGET STRING MATCH ***');
                console.log('Target:', TARGET);
                console.log('Target nano:', TARGET_NANO.toString());
            }
        }

        /*
         * Search the complete transaction object as a second pass.
         */
        const fullTx = JSON.stringify(tx);

        if (
            fullTx.includes(TARGET) ||
            fullTx.includes(TARGET_NANO.toString())
        ) {
            found++;

            console.log('');
            console.log('*** TARGET TRANSACTION MATCH ***');
            console.log('Target:', TARGET);
            console.log('Target nano:', TARGET_NANO.toString());
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log('SEARCH COMPLETE');
    console.log('Exact string matches:', found);
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    if (found === 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'No direct decimal match was found in the returned fields.'
        );
        console.log(
            'Next step: decode transaction message bodies / BOCs.'
        );
    }
}

FILE: scripts\sendDirectAddLiquidity.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const AMOUNT_ROOSTER = 500000000000000n;
const AMOUNT_PTON = 500000000n;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' DIRECT ADD LIQUIDITY');
    console.log(' REAL TRANSACTION');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');

    const pool = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(POOL)
    );

    const lpAccount = await pool.getLpAccount({
        ownerAddress: USER
    });

    console.log('LP Account:');
    console.log(lpAccount.address.toString());
    console.log('');

    const contractProvider = provider.provider(
        lpAccount.address
    );

    const data = await lpAccount.getLpAccountData(
        contractProvider
    );

    console.log('===== CURRENT LP ACCOUNT DATA =====');
    console.log('Amount 0:', data.amount0.toString());
    console.log('Amount 1:', data.amount1.toString());
    console.log('');

    if (data.amount0 !== AMOUNT_ROOSTER) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected ROOSTER amount: ${data.amount0}`
        );
    }

    if (data.amount1 !== AMOUNT_PTON) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected pTON amount: ${data.amount1}`
        );
    }

    console.log('Sending Direct Add Liquidity...');
    console.log('ROOSTER: 500,000');
    console.log('pTON: 0.5');
    console.log('Minimum LP: 1');
    console.log('Gas: 0.3 TON');
    console.log('');

    await lpAccount.sendDirectAddLiquidity(
        contractProvider,
        provider.sender(),
        {
            amount0: AMOUNT_ROOSTER,
            amount1: AMOUNT_PTON,
            minimumLpToMint: 1n,
            userWalletAddress: USER,
            refundAddress: USER,
            excessesAddress: USER
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Transaction sent to wallet.');
    console.log('Confirm it in Tonkeeper.');
}

import {
    Address,
    Cell,
    beginCell,
} from '@ton/core';

import { readFileSync } from 'fs';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100_000_000_000n;
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249_908n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 150_109_265n;

const ROOSTER_VALUE = 300_000_000n;
const PTON_VALUE = 310_249_908n;

const JETTON_TRANSFER_OP = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const TON_TRANSFER_OP = 0x01f3835d;
const PROVIDE_LP_OP = 0x37c096df;

function ok(
    label: string,
    value: boolean
) {
    console.log(
        `${label}: ${value ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}`
    );
}

function loadBoc(
    filename: string
): Cell {
    const data = readFileSync(filename);

    const cells = Cell.fromBoc(data);

    if (cells.length !== 1) {
        throw new Error(
            `${filename}: expected exactly one root cell, got ${cells.length}`
        );
    }

    return cells[0];
}

function addressEqual(
    a: Address,
    b: Address
): boolean {
    return a.equals(b);
}

function printEnvelope(
    title: string,
    to: Address,
    value: bigint,
    body: Cell
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '------------------------------------------------------------------'
    );
    console.log(title);
    console.log(
        '------------------------------------------------------------------'
    );

    console.log(
        'TO:',
        to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        Number(value) / 1_000_000_000,
        'TON'
    );

    const boc =
        body.toBoc({
            idx: false,
        });

    console.log(
        'BODY BOC BYTES:',
        boc.length
    );

    console.log(
        'BODY BOC HEX:',
        boc.toString('hex')
    );

    console.log(
        'BODY BOC BASE64:',
        boc.toString('base64')
    );
}

function inspectRoosterBody(
    body: Cell
) {
    const s = body.beginParse();

    const opcode =
        s.loadUint(32);

    const queryId =
        s.loadUintBig(64);

    const amount =
        s.loadCoins();

    const destination =
        s.loadAddress();

    const responseDestination =
        s.loadAddress();

    const hasCustomPayload =
        s.loadBit();

    let customPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
        customPayload = s.loadRef();
    }

    const forwardTon =
        s.loadCoins();

    const hasForwardPayload =
        s.loadBit();

    let forwardPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (hasForwardPayload) {
        forwardPayload = s.loadRef();
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER OUTER MESSAGE');
    console.log(
        'OPCODE:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16)
    );

    console.log(
        'QUERY ID:',
        queryId.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT:',
        amount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'DESTINATION:',
        destination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'RESPONSE:',
        responseDestination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'CUSTOM PAYLOAD:',
        customPayload ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'FORWARD TON:',
        forwardTon.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'FORWARD PAYLOAD:',
        forwardPayload ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    ok(
        'ROOSTER TRANSFER OPCODE',
        opcode === JETTON_TRANSFER_OP
    );

    ok(
        'ROOSTER AMOUNT',
        amount === ROOSTER_AMOUNT
    );

    ok(
        'ROOSTER DESTINATION = ROUTER',
        addressEqual(
            destination,
            ROUTER
        )
    );

    ok(
        'ROOSTER RESPONSE = USER',
        addressEqual(
            responseDestination,
            USER
        )
    );

    ok(
        'ROOSTER CUSTOM PAYLOAD = NO',
        customPayload === null
    );

    ok(
        'ROOSTER FORWARD TON',
        forwardTon === 235_000_000n
    );

    ok(
        'ROOSTER FORWARD PAYLOAD = YES',
        forwardPayload !== null
    );

    if (!forwardPayload) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROOSTER forward payload is missing.'
        );
    }

    inspectProvideLp(
        forwardPayload,
        'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP'
    );

    ok(
        'ROOSTER ROOT REMAINING',
        s.remainingBits === 0 &&
        s.remainingRefs === 0
    );
}

function inspectProvideLp(
    cell: Cell,
    title: string
) {
    const s = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode =
        s.loadUint(32);

    const routerWallet =
        s.loadAddress();

    const refund =
        s.loadAddress();

    const excesses =
        s.loadAddress();

    const deadline =
        s.loadUintBig(64);

    const nested =
        s.loadRef();

    console.log('');
    console.log(title);

    console.log(
        'OPCODE:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16)
    );

    console.log(
        'ROUTER WALLET:',
        routerWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'REFUND:',
        refund.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'EXCESSES:',
        excesses.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'DEADLINE:',
        deadline.toString()
    );

    ok(
        `${title} OPCODE`,
        opcode === PROVIDE_LP_OP
    );

    ok(
        `${title} REFUND = USER`,
        addressEqual(
            refund,
            USER
        )
    );

    ok(
        `${title} EXCESSES = USER`,
        addressEqual(
            excesses,
            USER
        )
    );

    ok(
        `${title} DEADLINE FUTURE`,
        deadline > BigInt(
            Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)
        )
    );

    const n =
        nested.beginParse();

    const minLpOut =
        n.loadCoins();

    const receiver =
        n.loadAddress();

    const bothPositive =
        n.loadBit();

    const customGas =
        n.loadCoins();

    const hasCustomPayload =
        n.loadMaybeRef();

    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'RECEIVER:',
        receiver.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'BOTH POSITIVE:',
        bothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        'CUSTOM GAS:',
        customGas.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'CUSTOM PAYLOAD:',
        hasCustomPayload ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    ok(
        `${title} MIN LP`,
        minLpOut === MIN_LP_OUT
    );

    ok(
        `${title} RECEIVER = USER`,
        addressEqual(
            receiver,
            USER
        )
    );

    ok(
        `${title} BOTH POSITIVE`,
        bothPositive === true
    );

    ok(
        `${title} CUSTOM GAS = 0`,
        customGas === 0n
    );

    ok(
        `${title} CUSTOM PAYLOAD = NO`,
        hasCustomPayload === null
    );

    ok(
        `${title} NESTED REMAINING`,
        n.remainingBits === 0 &&
        n.remainingRefs === 0
    );

    ok(
        `${title} REMAINING`,
        s.remainingBits === 0 &&
        s.remainingRefs === 0
    );
}

export async function run() {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 SEND ENVELOPE PREFLIGHT'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );
    console.log(
        ' NO WALLET SIGNATURE'
    );
    console.log(
        ' NO send() CALL'
    );
    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );

    // ================================================================
    // LOAD BOCs
    // ================================================================

    const roosterBody =
        loadBoc(
            './rooster_v22_generated.boc'
        );

    const ptonBody =
        loadBoc(
            './pton_v22_generated.boc'
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'BOC FILES: LOADED'
    );

    // ================================================================
    // ENVELOPE DEFINITIONS
    // ================================================================

    const roosterTo =
        ROOSTER_WALLET;

    const ptonTo =
        PTON_WALLET;

    const roosterValue =
        ROOSTER_VALUE;

    const ptonValue =
        PTON_VALUE;

    // ================================================================
    // ROOSTER ENVELOPE
    // ================================================================

    printEnvelope(
        'ROOSTER FINAL ENVELOPE',
        roosterTo,
        roosterValue,
        roosterBody
    );

    ok(
        'ROOSTER TO = USER ROOSTER WALLET',
        addressEqual(
            roosterTo,
            ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    );

    ok(
        'ROOSTER VALUE',
        roosterValue ===
            ROOSTER_VALUE
    );

    inspectRoosterBody(
        roosterBody
    );

    // ================================================================
    // pTON ENVELOPE
    // ================================================================

    printEnvelope(
        'pTON FINAL ENVELOPE',
        ptonTo,
        ptonValue,
        ptonBody
    );

    ok(
        'pTON TO = ROUTER pTON WALLET',
        addressEqual(
            ptonTo,
            PTON_WALLET
        )
    );

    ok(
        'pTON VALUE',
        ptonValue ===
            PTON_VALUE
    );

    inspectPtonBody(
        ptonBody
    );

    // ================================================================
    // FINAL
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' SEND ENVELOPE PREFLIGHT RESULT'
    );
    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER MESSAGE: VALIDATED'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON MESSAGE: VALIDATED'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'IMPORTANT:'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION SENT'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO WALLET SIGNATURE'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO send() CALL'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO FUNDS MOVED'
    );

    console.log(
        'POOL NOT MODIFIED'
    );

    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );
}

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

import {
    TonConnect,
    CHAIN,
    SendTransactionRequest
} from '@tonconnect/sdk';


// ============================================================
// STON.FI v2.2
// ROOSTER / pTON
//
// FINAL TONCONNECT SEND
//
// IMPORTANT:
// - Builds BOTH messages freshly with STON.fi SDK
// - Uses the existing TonConnect session
// - Does NOT use private keys
// - Does NOT use sender.send()
// - Does NOT use wallet.send()
// - Requires exact terminal confirmation: SEND
// - Only then calls connector.sendTransaction()
// ============================================================


// ============================================================
// ADDRESSES
// ============================================================

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const ROOSTER_USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const PTON_POOL_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);


// ============================================================

// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100000000000n;
// 100 ROOSTER

const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;
// 0.000249908 pTON

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;


// ============================================================
// EXPECTED OUTER VALUES
// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_TX_VALUE = 300000000n;
// 0.300000000 TON

const PTON_TX_VALUE = 310249908n;
// 0.310249908 TON

const TOTAL_TX_VALUE =
    ROOSTER_TX_VALUE +
    PTON_TX_VALUE;


// ============================================================
// TONCONNECT STORAGE
// ============================================================

function createStorage(
    storagePath: string
) {

    return {

        async setItem(
            key: string,
            value: string
        ): Promise<void> {

            let obj: Record<string, string> = {};

            try {

                obj =
                    JSON.parse(
                        await fs.promises.readFile(
                            storagePath,
                            'utf8'
                        )
                    );

            } catch {

                obj = {};

            }

            obj[key] = value;

            await fs.promises.mkdir(
                path.dirname(storagePath),
                {
                    recursive: true
                }
            );

            await fs.promises.writeFile(
                storagePath,
                JSON.stringify(obj)
            );
        },


        async getItem(
            key: string
        ): Promise<string | null> {

            try {

                const obj =
                    JSON.parse(
                        await fs.promises.readFile(
                            storagePath,
                            'utf8'
                        )
                    );

                return (
                    obj[key] ??
                    null
                );

            } catch {

                return null;

            }
        },


        async removeItem(
            key: string
        ): Promise<void> {

            let obj: Record<string, string> = {};

            try {

                obj =
                    JSON.parse(
                        await fs.promises.readFile(
                            storagePath,
                            'utf8'
                        )
                    );

            } catch {

                return;

            }

            delete obj[key];

            await fs.promises.writeFile(
                storagePath,
                JSON.stringify(obj)
            );
        }

    };

}


// ============================================================
// HELPERS
// ============================================================

function sameAddress(
    a: Address,
    b: Address
): boolean {

    return (
        a.toRawString() ===
        b.toRawString()
    );
}


function nanoToTon(
    value: bigint
): string {

    const whole =
        value /
        1000000000n;

    const fraction =
        (
            value %
            1000000000n
        )
            .toString()
            .padStart(
                9,
                '0'
            );

    return (
        whole.toString() +
        '.' +
        fraction
    );
}


function section(
    title: string
): void {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(title);

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}


// ============================================================
// MAIN
// ============================================================

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    section(
        'STON.FI v2.2 FINAL TWO-MESSAGE SEND'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'NETWORK: TESTNET'
    );

    console.log(
        'MODE: FINAL SEND GATE'
    );

    console.log(
        'PRIVATE KEY: NOT USED'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 1. TONCONNECT
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '1. TONCONNECT CONNECTION'
    );


    const storagePath =
        path.join(
            process.cwd(),
            'temp',
            'testnet',
            'tonconnect.json'
        );


    if (
        !fs.existsSync(
            storagePath
        )
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: TonConnect storage file does not exist'
        );
    }


    const connector =
        new TonConnect({

            storage:
                createStorage(
                    storagePath
                ),

            manifestUrl:
                'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ton-org/blueprint/main/tonconnect/manifest.json'

        });


    await connector.restoreConnection();


    if (
        !connector.wallet
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: TonConnect wallet was not restored'
        );
    }


    const connectedWallet =
        Address.parse(
            connector.wallet.account.address
        );


    console.log(
        'Connected wallet:',
        connectedWallet.toString({
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true
        })
    );


    console.log(
        'Expected wallet:',
        USER.toString({
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true
        })
    );


    if (
        !sameAddress(
            connectedWallet,
            USER
        )
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Connected wallet does not match USER'
        );
    }


    if (
        connector.wallet.account.chain !==
        CHAIN.TESTNET
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Connected wallet is not on TESTNET'
        );
    }


    console.log(
        'Wallet identity: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Network: TESTNET PASS'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 2. BUILD SDK TRANSACTIONS
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '2. BUILD FRESH SDK TRANSACTIONS'
    );


    const router =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        );


    const proxyTon =
        DEX.v2_1.pTON.create(
            PTON_MASTER
        );


    const deadline =
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        ) + 600;


    console.log(
        'Deadline:',
        deadline
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // ROOSTER LEG
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            sendTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            otherTokenAddress:
                PTON_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                ROOSTER_AMOUNT,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,

            deadline

        });


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // pTON LEG
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            otherTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                PTON_AMOUNT,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,

            deadline,

            proxyTon

        });


    console.log(
        'ROOSTER SDK transaction: BUILT'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON SDK transaction: BUILT'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 3. OUTER MESSAGE VALIDATION
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '3. OUTER MESSAGE VALIDATION'
    );


    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterTx.to,
            ROOSTER_USER_WALLET
        )
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: ROOSTER destination mismatch'
        );
    }


    if (
        !sameAddress(
            ptonTx.to,
            PTON_POOL_WALLET
        )
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON destination mismatch'
        );
    }


    if (
        roosterTx.value !==
        ROOSTER_TX_VALUE
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: ROOSTER outer TON value mismatch'
        );
    }


    if (
        ptonTx.value !==
        PTON_TX_VALUE
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON outer TON value mismatch'
        );
    }


    if (
        !roosterTx.body
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: ROOSTER body missing'
        );
    }


    if (
        !ptonTx.body
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON body missing'
        );
    }


    console.log(
        'ROOSTER destination: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON destination: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER value:',
        nanoToTon(
            roosterTx.value
        ),
        'TON - PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON value:',
        nanoToTon(
            ptonTx.value
        ),
        'TON - PASS'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 4. BUILD TONCONNECT PAYLOADS
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '4. BUILD TONCONNECT PAYLOADS'
    );


    const roosterPayload =
        roosterTx.body
            .toBoc()
            .toString('base64');


    const ptonPayload =
        ptonTx.body
            .toBoc()
            .toString('base64');


    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON BOC: PASS'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 5. FINAL REQUEST
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '5. FINAL TONCONNECT REQUEST'
    );


    const request: SendTransactionRequest = {

        validUntil:
            deadline,

        network:
            CHAIN.TESTNET,

        messages: [

            {
                address:
                    roosterTx.to.toString(),

                amount:
                    roosterTx.value.toString(),

                payload:
                    roosterPayload
            },

            {
                address:
                    ptonTx.to.toString(),

                amount:
                    ptonTx.value.toString(),

                payload:
                    ptonPayload
            }

        ]

    };


    // ========================================================
    // 6. FINAL STRUCTURE CHECKS
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '6. FINAL SAFETY CHECKS'
    );


    if (
        request.messages.length !== 2
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Expected exactly 2 messages'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.network !==
        CHAIN.TESTNET
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Network is not TESTNET'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.validUntil !==
        deadline
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: validUntil/deadline mismatch'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.validUntil <=
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        )
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Request deadline has expired'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.messages[0].address !==
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Message 1 destination changed'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.messages[1].address !==
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Message 2 destination changed'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.messages[0].amount !==
        ROOSTER_TX_VALUE.toString()
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Message 1 amount changed'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.messages[1].amount !==
        PTON_TX_VALUE.toString()
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Message 2 amount changed'
        );
    }


    console.log(
        'Message count = 2: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Network TESTNET: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Shared deadline: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 1 destination: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 1 amount: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 1 payload: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 2 destination: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 2 amount: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 2 payload: PASS'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 7. DISPLAY EXACT TRANSACTION
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '7. TRANSACTION TO BE SENT'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        '  Amount:',
        nanoToTon(
            ROOSTER_AMOUNT
        ),
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        '  To:',
        request.messages[0].address
    );

    console.log(
        '  Outer TON:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(
                request.messages[0].amount
            )
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON'
    );

    console.log(
        '  Amount:',
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString(),
        'nano pTON'
    );

    console.log(
        '  To:',
        request.messages[1].address
    );

    console.log(
        '  Outer TON:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(
                request.messages[1].amount
            )
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT:',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'TOTAL OUTER TON:',
        nanoToTon(
            TOTAL_TX_VALUE
        ),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'VALID UNTIL:',
        request.validUntil
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 8. FINAL HUMAN CONFIRMATION
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '8. FINAL CONFIRMATION'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'WARNING: THE NEXT STEP CAN OPEN TONKEEPER'
    );

    console.log(
        'WARNING: TONKEEPER WILL ASK FOR TRANSACTION APPROVAL'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'EXACTLY 2 MESSAGES WILL BE SENT:'
    );

    console.log(
        '1) 100 ROOSTER ->',
        request.messages[0].address
    );

    console.log(
        '   Outer TON:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(
                request.messages[0].amount
            )
        )
    );

    console.log(
        '2) 249908 nano pTON ->',
        request.messages[1].address
    );

    console.log(
        '   Outer TON:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(
                request.messages[1].amount
            )
        )
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TOTAL OUTER TON:',
        nanoToTon(
            TOTAL_TX_VALUE
        )
    );

    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT:',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'No private key will be used.'
    );

    console.log(
        'No sender.send() will be used.'
    );

    console.log(
        'No wallet.send() will be used.'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'TYPE EXACTLY: SEND'
    );

    console.log(
        'Anything else will CANCEL.'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 9. TERMINAL CONFIRMATION
    // ========================================================

    const readline =
        await import('readline');


    const rl =
        readline.createInterface({

            input:
                process.stdin,

            output:
                process.stdout

        });


    const answer =
        await new Promise<string>(
            resolve => {

                rl.question(
                    '\nConfirmation: ',
                    value => {

                        rl.close();

                        resolve(
                            value.trim()
                        );

                    }
                );

            }
        );


    if (
        answer !==
        'SEND'
    ) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'SEND CANCELLED.'
        );

        console.log(
            'connector.sendTransaction(): NOT CALLED'
        );

        return;
    }


    // ========================================================
    // 10. LAST-MOMENT DEADLINE CHECK
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '10. LAST-MOMENT CHECK'
    );


    const now =
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        );


    if (
        request.validUntil <=
        now
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Request expired before send'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.messages.length !== 2
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Message count changed'
        );
    }


    console.log(
        'Deadline still valid: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message count still 2: PASS'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 11. SEND
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '11. TONCONNECT SEND'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Calling connector.sendTransaction()...'
    );

    console.log(
        'TONKEEPER APPROVAL MAY NOW APPEAR.'
    );


    const result =
        await connector.sendTransaction(
            request
        );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TONCONNECT RESPONSE RECEIVED.'
    );


    console.log(
        'Response:',
        result
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 12. FINAL NOTICE
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '12. IMPORTANT'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'sendTransaction() returned.'
    );

    console.log(
        'This does NOT by itself prove on-chain success.'
    );

    console.log(
        'The transaction must be verified on TESTNET.'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'FINAL RESULT: TONCONNECT REQUEST SUBMITTED'
    );

}

FILE: scripts\sendInitialLiquidityV22.ts
