import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
    );

    const roosterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const ptonMasterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
    );

    const userWalletAddress = Address.parse(
        '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
    );

    const router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress);
    const proxyTon = DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(ptonMasterAddress);

    // ================================================
    // REAL LIQUIDITY AMOUNTS
    // ================================================

    const roosterAmount =
        100_000n * 1_000_000_000n;

    const ptonAmount =
        1n * 1_000_000_000n;

    // ================================================
    // GET REAL POOL
    // ================================================

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(routerAddress);

    const poolAddress =
        await router.getPoolAddress(
            routerProvider,
            {
                token0: roosterAddress,
                token1: ptonMasterAddress,
            }
        );

    const pool =
        await router.getPool(
            routerProvider,
            {
                token0: roosterAddress,
                token1: ptonMasterAddress,
            }
        );

    const poolProvider =
        provider.provider(poolAddress);

    const poolData =
        await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    // ================================================
    // CALCULATE EXPECTED LP
    // ================================================

    const roosterRatio =
        roosterAmount * 1_000_000_000n /
        poolData.reserve0;

    const ptonRatio =
        ptonAmount * 1_000_000_000n /
        poolData.reserve1;

    const limitingRatio =
        roosterRatio < ptonRatio
            ? roosterRatio
            : ptonRatio;

    const expectedLp =
        poolData.totalSupplyLP *
        limitingRatio /
        1_000_000_000n;

    // 5% safety margin
    const minLpOut =
        expectedLp * 95n / 100n;

    // Deadline: 1 hour from now
    const deadline =
        Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600;

    // ================================================
    // SHOW REAL POOL DATA
    // ================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('      REAL LIQUIDITY - STON.fi v2.2');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Pool deployed:');
    console.log(
        await provider.isContractDeployed(poolAddress)
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('CURRENT POOL');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER reserve:',
        Number(poolData.reserve0) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON reserve:',
        Number(poolData.reserve1) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'Total LP:',
        poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('LIQUIDITY TO ADD');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER:',
        Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON:',
        Number(ptonAmount) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('LP CALCULATION');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Minimum LP (95%):',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('Deadline:');
    console.log(
        new Date(deadline * 1000).toISOString()
    );

    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // BUILD ROOSTER TRANSACTION
    // ================================================

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                sendTokenAddress: roosterAddress,
                otherTokenAddress: ptonMasterAddress,

                sendAmount: roosterAmount,

                userWalletAddress,

                receiverAddress:
                    userWalletAddress,

                refundAddress:
                    userWalletAddress,

                excessesAddress:
                    userWalletAddress,

                minLpOut,

                deadline,
            }
        );

    // ================================================
    // BUILD pTON TRANSACTION
    // ================================================

    const tonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                proxyTon,

                otherTokenAddress:
                    roosterAddress,

                sendAmount:
                    ptonAmount,

                userWalletAddress,

                receiverAddress:
                    userWalletAddress,

                refundAddress:
                    userWalletAddress,

                excessesAddress:
                    userWalletAddress,

                minLpOut,

                deadline,
            }
        );

    // ================================================
    // SHOW TRANSACTIONS BEFORE SENDING
    // ================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('             TRANSACTIONS READY');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER TRANSACTION');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('Destination:');
    console.log(
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('Value:');
    console.log(
        roosterTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON TRANSACTION');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('Destination:');
    console.log(
        tonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('Value:');
    console.log(
        tonTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('minLpOut:');
    console.log(
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('              REAL SEND STARTING');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // SEND 1
    // ================================================

    console.log(
        'STEP 1/2: Sending 100,000 ROOSTER...'
    );

    await provider.sender().send(roosterTx);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'STEP 1/2: ROOSTER transaction submitted.'
    );
    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // SEND 2
    // ================================================

    console.log(
        'STEP 2/2: Sending 1 pTON...'
    );

    await provider.sender().send(tonTx);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'STEP 2/2: pTON transaction submitted.'
    );
    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // FINAL RESULT
    // ================================================

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('       LIQUIDITY TRANSACTIONS SUBMITTED');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER sent:',
        '100,000 ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON sent:',
        '1 pTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Pool:',
        poolAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Now verify Pool state and LP balance.'
    );

    console.log('');
}

import { Address, internal } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const filePath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        'initial_rooster_leg.boc'
    );

    const bocBase64 = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8').trim();

    const { Cell } = await import('@ton/core');
    const body = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(bocBase64, 'base64')
    )[0];

    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('INITIAL ROOSTER LIQUIDITY - LEG 1');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('Sending: 100,000,000 ROOSTER');
    console.log('Value:   0.3 TON');
    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION HAS BEEN SENT YET.');
    console.log('');

    await provider.sender().send({
        to: Address.parse(
            'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
        ),
        value: 300000000n,
        body,
    });

    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('ROOSTER LEG TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Wait for confirmation before sending pTON.');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\sendInitialRoosterPool.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX, pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const roosterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const pton = pTON.v2_1.create(
        Address.parse(
            'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
        )
    );

    const userWalletAddress = provider.sender().address;

    if (!userWalletAddress) {
        throw new Error('Wallet address not available');
    }

    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress)
    );

    const roosterAmount =
        100_000_000n * 1_000_000_000n;

    const ptonAmount = 100_000_000n;

    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('INITIAL ROOSTER / pTON LIQUIDITY');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('User:', userWalletAddress.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER: 100,000,000');
    console.log('pTON:    0.1');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Two messages will be sent in ONE TonConnect');
    console.log('transaction.');
    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER gas: 0.3 TON');
    console.log('pTON gas:    0.3 TON');
    console.log('');

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({
            userWalletAddress,
            sendTokenAddress: roosterAddress,
            sendAmount: roosterAmount,
            otherTokenAddress: pton.address,
            minLpOut: 1n,
        });

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({
            userWalletAddress,
            sendTokenAddress: pton.address,
            sendAmount: ptonAmount,
            otherTokenAddress: roosterAddress,
            minLpOut: 1n,
        });

    if (!roosterTx.body) {
        throw new Error('ROOSTER transaction body is missing');
    }

    if (!ptonTx.body) {
        throw new Error('pTON transaction body is missing');
    }

    console.log('========== FINAL CHECK ==========');
    console.log('ROOSTER wallet:', roosterTx.to.toString());
    console.log('pTON wallet:', ptonTx.to.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER amount:', roosterAmount.toString());
    console.log('pTON amount:', ptonAmount.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('Sending to Tonkeeper...');
    console.log('==========================================');

    await provider.sender().send({
        to: roosterTx.to,
        value: roosterTx.value,
        body: roosterTx.body,
    });

    await provider.sender().send({
        to: ptonTx.to,
        value: ptonTx.value,
        body: ptonTx.body,
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('BOTH LIQUIDITY LEGS SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
}

import { Address } from "@ton/core";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";

const USER = Address.parse(
    "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix"
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx"
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S"
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3"
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;

const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const GAS_AMOUNT = 300000000n;

const FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT = 240000000n;


function formatRooster(amount: bigint): string {
    return (
        Number(amount) / 1e9
    ).toLocaleString("en-US", {
        minimumFractionDigits: 9,
        maximumFractionDigits: 9,
    });
}


function formatTon(amount: bigint): string {
    return (
        Number(amount) / 1e9
    ).toFixed(9);
}


export async function run(provider: any) {

    console.log("");
    console.log("=================================================");
    console.log("       STON.fi v2.2 REAL LIQUIDITY SEND");
    console.log("=================================================");
    console.log("");
    console.log("REAL TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.");
    console.log("Tonkeeper confirmation is required.");
    console.log("");


    // ====================================================
    // 1. CONNECTED WALLET
    // ====================================================

    const sender = provider.sender();

    const senderAddress = sender.address;

    if (!senderAddress) {
        throw new Error(
            "Connected wallet address is unavailable."
        );
    }

    console.log(
        "===== 1. CONNECTED WALLET ====="
    );

    console.log(
        "Connected wallet:",
        senderAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "Expected wallet:",
        USER.toString()
    );

    if (!senderAddress.equals(USER)) {
        throw new Error(
            [
                "WRONG WALLET.",
                "",
                `Connected: ${senderAddress.toString()}`,
                `Expected:  ${USER.toString()}`,
            ].join("\n")
        );
    }

    console.log(
        "Wallet identity: PASS"
    );

    console.log("");


    // ====================================================
    // 2. FRESH DEADLINE
    // ====================================================

    const now =
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        );

    const deadline =
        now + 900;

    console.log(
        "===== 2. FRESH DEADLINE ====="
    );

    console.log(
        "Current Unix time:",
        now
    );

    console.log(
        "Fresh deadline:",
        deadline
    );

    console.log(
        "Lifetime:",
        "900 seconds"
    );

    console.log(
        "Deadline: PASS"
    );

    console.log("");


    // ====================================================
    // 3. USER ROOSTER WALLET
    // ====================================================

    console.log(
        "===== 3. ROOSTER JETTON WALLET ====="
    );

    /*
     * We intentionally do NOT import JettonMinter
     * from @ston-fi/sdk because SDK 2.7.0 does not
     * export that class.
     *
     * The SDK router itself will derive the wallet
     * when getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams() runs.
     */

    console.log(
        "Expected user ROOSTER wallet:"
    );

    console.log(
        "EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh"
    );

    console.log(
        "ROOSTER wallet reference: PASS"
    );

    console.log("");


    // ====================================================
    // 4. CREATE STON.fi v2.2 ROUTER
    // ====================================================

    console.log(
        "===== 4. STON.fi v2.2 ROUTER ====="
    );

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
            ROUTER
        );

    console.log(
        "Router:",
        ROUTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "Router version:",
        "v2.2 CPI"
    );

    console.log(
        "Router: PASS"
    );

    console.log("");


    // ====================================================
    // 5. GENERATE SDK TRANSACTION
    // ====================================================

    console.log(
        "===== 5. BUILDING SDK TRANSACTION ====="
    );

    /*
     * IMPORTANT:
     *
     * SDK 2.7.0 signature is:
     *
     * getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
     *     provider,
     *     params
     * )
     *
     * pTON MASTER is passed as otherTokenAddress.
     * NOT the router pTON wallet.
     */

    const tx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            provider,
            {
                userWalletAddress:
                    USER,

                sendTokenAddress:
                    ROOSTER_MASTER,

                otherTokenAddress:
                    PTON_MASTER,

                sendAmount:
                    ROOSTER_AMOUNT,

                minLpOut:
                    MIN_LP_OUT,

                deadline:
                    deadline,

                gasAmount:
                    GAS_AMOUNT,

                forwardGasAmount:
                    FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT,

                transferExcessAddress:
                    USER,
            }
        );

    console.log(
        "SDK transaction generated: PASS"
    );

    console.log("");


    // ====================================================
    // 6. VERIFY DESTINATION
    // ====================================================

    console.log(
        "===== 6. TRANSACTION DESTINATION ====="
    );

    console.log(
        "Actual destination:",
        tx.to.toString()
    );

    const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET =
        Address.parse(
            "EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh"
        );

    console.log(
        "Expected destination:",
        EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString()
    );

    if (
        !tx.to.equals(
            EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            [
                "WRONG TRANSACTION DESTINATION.",
                "",
                `Actual:   ${tx.to.toString()}`,
                `Expected: ${EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString()}`,
            ].join("\n")
        );
    }

    console.log(
        "Destination: PASS"
    );

    console.log("");


    // ====================================================
    // 7. VERIFY GAS
    // ====================================================

    console.log(
        "===== 7. TRANSACTION GAS ====="
    );

    const actualValue =
        BigInt(
            tx.value.toString()
        );

    console.log(
        "Expected:",
        GAS_AMOUNT.toString(),
        `(${formatTon(GAS_AMOUNT)} TON)`
    );

    console.log(
        "Actual:",
        actualValue.toString(),
        `(${formatTon(actualValue)} TON)`
    );

    if (
        actualValue !==
        GAS_AMOUNT
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            [
                "WRONG GAS AMOUNT.",
                "",
                `Actual:   ${actualValue.toString()}`,
                `Expected: ${GAS_AMOUNT.toString()}`,
            ].join("\n")
        );
    }

    console.log(
        "Gas: PASS"
    );

    console.log("");


    // ====================================================
    // 8. FINAL SEND SUMMARY
    // ====================================================

    console.log(
        "================================================="
    );

    console.log(
        "              FINAL SEND SUMMARY"
    );

    console.log(
        "================================================="
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "Network:",
        "TON Testnet"
    );

    console.log(
        "Router:",
        ROUTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "User:",
        USER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "ROOSTER master:",
        ROOSTER_MASTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "pTON master:",
        PTON_MASTER.toString()
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "ROOSTER amount:",
        formatRooster(
            ROOSTER_AMOUNT
        )
    );

    console.log(
        "ROOSTER nano:",
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "Minimum LP out:",
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "Gas:",
        formatTon(
            GAS_AMOUNT
        ),
        "TON"
    );

    console.log(
        "Forward gas:",
        formatTon(
            FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT
        ),
        "TON"
    );

    console.log(
        "Deadline:",
        deadline
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "Transaction destination:"
    );

    console.log(
        tx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log("");

    // ====================================================
    // 9. REAL SEND
    // ====================================================

    console.log(
        "================================================="
    );

    console.log(
        "             SENDING TO TONKEEPER"
    );

    console.log(
        "================================================="
    );

    console.log("");

    await sender.send(
        tx
    );

    // ====================================================
    // 10. SUBMITTED
    // ====================================================

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "================================================="
    );

    console.log(
        "          TRANSACTION SUBMITTED"
    );

    console.log(
        "================================================="
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "ROOSTER submitted:",
        formatRooster(
            ROOSTER_AMOUNT
        )
    );

    console.log(
        "Minimum LP out:",
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "TON gas:",
        formatTon(
            GAS_AMOUNT
        )
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "The transaction was submitted."
    );

    console.log(
        "Check Tonkeeper and TON testnet explorer"
    );

    console.log(
        "for final blockchain confirmation."
    );

    console.log("");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\sendPtonLegV22.ts

import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

// ======================================================
// STON.fi v2.2 pTON LEG
// CORRECTED SEMANTIC PREFLIGHT
// READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT
// ======================================================

// ---------- USER ----------
const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

// ---------- TOKENS ----------
const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

// ---------- STON.fi v2.2 ----------
const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const POOL_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

// pTON wallet belonging to the Router
const PTON_POOL_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

// ROOSTER Jetton wallet belonging to the Pool.
// On the pTON leg, this is the "other token wallet"
// stored inside PROVIDE_LP.
const ROOSTER_POOL_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

// ---------- LIQUIDITY ----------
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;

// SDK calculation:
// tonAmount + forwardTonAmount + pTON tonTransfer gas
//
// 249,908
// +300,000,000
// + 10,000,000
// =310,249,908
const EXPECTED_VALUE = 310249908n;

const EXPECTED_FORWARD_TON = 300000000n;
const EXPECTED_PTON_TRANSFER_GAS = 10000000n;

// pTON v2.1 implementation inherited by DEX v2.2
const EXPECTED_TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 32736093;

// provide_lp opcode
const EXPECTED_PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;

// ---------- SAFETY ----------
const SAFETY_RESERVE = toNano('0.1');

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '======================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.fi v2.2 pTON LEG SEMANTIC PREFLIGHT'
    );
    console.log(
        ' CORRECTED pTON BODY DECODER'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );
    console.log(
        '======================================================'
    );

    // ==================================================
    // 1. CONSTANTS
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 1. CONSTANTS =====');

    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER MASTER:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON MASTER:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL_ADDRESS.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON ROUTER WALLET:');
    console.log(PTON_POOL_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER POOL WALLET:');
console.log(ROOSTER_POOL_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON AMOUNT RAW:');
    console.log(PTON_AMOUNT.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON AMOUNT:');
    console.log(Number(PTON_AMOUNT) / 1e9);

    console.log('');
    console.log('MIN LP OUT:');
    console.log(MIN_LP_OUT.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED TON_TRANSFER OPCODE:');
    console.log(
        EXPECTED_TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED TON_TRANSFER OPCODE HEX:');
    console.log(
        '0x' + EXPECTED_TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE.toString(16)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED PROVIDE_LP OPCODE:');
    console.log(
        '0x' + EXPECTED_PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE.toString(16)
    );

    // ==================================================
    // 2. WALLET BALANCE
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 2. WALLET BALANCE =====');

    const openedWallet = provider.sender();

    if (!openedWallet.address) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: connected wallet address is unavailable'
        );
    }

    const connectedWallet = openedWallet.address;

    console.log('Connected wallet:');
    console.log(connectedWallet.toString());

    if (!connectedWallet.equals(USER)) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: connected wallet does not match USER'
        );
    }

    console.log('Wallet identity: PASS');

    const tonBalance = await provider
        .provider(connectedWallet)
        .getState()
        .then((state) => {
            if (!state.balance) {
                return 0n;
            }

            return state.balance;
        });

    console.log('');
    console.log('TON BALANCE RAW:');
    console.log(tonBalance.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TON BALANCE:');
    console.log(Number(tonBalance) / 1e9);

    const requiredMinimum =
        EXPECTED_VALUE + SAFETY_RESERVE;

    console.log('');
    console.log('REQUIRED MINIMUM:');
    console.log(
        Number(requiredMinimum) / 1e9
    );

    if (tonBalance < requiredMinimum) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: insufficient TON balance for pTON leg + safety reserve'
        );
    }

    console.log('TON balance safety: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 3. STON.fi ROUTER
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 3. STON.fi ROUTER =====');

    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER_ADDRESS)
    );

    const routerData = await router.getRouterData();

    console.log('Router address:');
    console.log(ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router ID:');
    console.log(routerData.routerId);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router locked:');
    console.log(routerData.isLocked);

    if (routerData.isLocked) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: STON.fi router is locked'
        );
    }

    console.log('Router unlocked: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 4. POOL
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 4. POOL =====');

    const pool = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(POOL_ADDRESS)
    );

    const poolData = await pool.getPoolData();

    console.log('Pool address:');
    console.log(POOL_ADDRESS.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool router:');
    console.log(poolData.routerAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
console.log('Pool token0:');
console.log(poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
console.log('Pool token1:');
console.log(poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool locked:');
    console.log(poolData.isLocked);

    if (poolData.isLocked) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pool is locked'
        );
    }

    if (!poolData.routerAddress.equals(ROUTER_ADDRESS)) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pool router does not match expected router'
        );
    }

    console.log('Pool router: PASS');
    console.log('Pool unlocked: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 5. BUILD FRESH pTON TRANSACTION
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 5. BUILD FRESH pTON TRANSACTION =====');

    const deadline =
    Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 600;

    console.log('Deadline:');
    console.log(
        new Date(deadline * 1000).toISOString()
    );

   const txParams =
    await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({
        userWalletAddress: USER,
        proxyTon: DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(PTON_MASTER),
        otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
        sendAmount: PTON_AMOUNT,
        minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
        receiverAddress: USER,
        refundAddress: USER,
        excessesAddress: USER,
        deadline,
    });

    // ==================================================
    // 6. TX DESTINATION
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 6. TX DESTINATION =====');

    console.log('TX TO:');
    console.log(txParams.to.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED pTON ROUTER WALLET:');
    console.log(PTON_POOL_WALLET.toString());

    if (!txParams.to.equals(PTON_POOL_WALLET)) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: transaction destination does not match pTON router wallet'
        );
    }

    console.log('TX destination: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 7. TX VALUE
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 7. TX VALUE =====');

    const txValue = BigInt(txParams.value);

    console.log('TX VALUE RAW:');
    console.log(txValue.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TX VALUE TON:');
    console.log(Number(txValue) / 1e9);

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED VALUE RAW:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_VALUE.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED VALUE TON:');
    console.log(Number(EXPECTED_VALUE) / 1e9);

    if (txValue !== EXPECTED_VALUE) {
        throw new Error(
            `STOP: TX VALUE mismatch. Got ${txValue}, expected ${EXPECTED_VALUE}`
        );
    }

    console.log('TX VALUE: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 8. pTON TON_TRANSFER BODY
    //
    // SDK source:
    //
    // storeUint(TON_TRANSFER, 32)
    // storeUint(queryId, 64)
    // storeCoins(tonAmount)
    // storeAddress(refundAddress)
    // storeBit(hasForwardPayload)
    // storeRef(forwardPayload)
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 8. pTON TON_TRANSFER BODY =====');

    const body = txParams.body;

if (!body) {
    throw new Error(
        'STOP: SDK returned an empty transaction body'
    );
}

const slice = body.beginParse();

    const outerOpcode =
        slice.loadUint(32);

    const queryId =
        slice.loadUint(64);

    const ptonAmount =
        slice.loadCoins();

    const refundAddress =
        slice.loadAddress();

    const hasForwardPayload =
        slice.loadBit();

    console.log('');
    console.log('Outer opcode:');
    console.log(
        '0x' + outerOpcode.toString(16)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Expected opcode:');
    console.log(
        '0x' +
        EXPECTED_TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE.toString(16)
    );

    if (
        outerOpcode !==
        EXPECTED_TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            `STOP: unexpected TON_TRANSFER opcode: ${outerOpcode}`
        );
    }

    console.log('TON_TRANSFER opcode: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Query ID:');
    console.log(queryId.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON amount raw:');
    console.log(ptonAmount.toString());

    if (ptonAmount !== PTON_AMOUNT) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON amount mismatch'
        );
    }

    console.log('pTON amount: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Refund address:');
    console.log(refundAddress.toString());

    if (!refundAddress.equals(USER)) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: refund address does not match USER'
        );
    }

    console.log('Refund address: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Has forward payload:');
    console.log(hasForwardPayload);

    if (!hasForwardPayload) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON body has no forward payload'
        );
    }

    console.log('Forward payload flag: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 9. NESTED PROVIDE-LP PAYLOAD
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 9. NESTED PROVIDE-LP PAYLOAD =====');

    const forwardPayload =
        slice.loadRef();

    const nested =
        forwardPayload.beginParse();

    const nestedOpcode =
        nested.loadUint(32);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Nested opcode:');
    console.log(
        '0x' + nestedOpcode.toString(16)
    );

    if (
        nestedOpcode !==
        EXPECTED_PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            `STOP: unexpected provide-liquidity opcode: 0x${nestedOpcode.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    console.log('Nested PROVIDE_LP opcode: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 10. PROVIDE-LP ADDRESSES
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 10. PROVIDE-LP ADDRESSES =====');

    const routerWallet =
        nested.loadAddress();

    const refundAddressNested =
        nested.loadAddress();

    const excessesAddress =
        nested.loadAddress();

    const nestedDeadline =
        nested.loadUint(64);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router wallet:');
    console.log(routerWallet.toString());

   if (
    !routerWallet.equals(
        ROOSTER_POOL_WALLET
    )
) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: PROVIDE_LP other-token wallet does not match ROOSTER Pool wallet'
        );
    }

    console.log('Router wallet: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Refund address:');
    console.log(
        refundAddressNested.toString()
    );

    if (
        !refundAddressNested.equals(USER)
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: nested refund address does not match USER'
        );
    }

    console.log('Nested refund address: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Excesses address:');
    console.log(
        excessesAddress.toString()
    );

    if (
        !excessesAddress.equals(USER)
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: excesses address does not match USER'
        );
    }

    console.log('Excesses address: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 11. DEADLINE
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 11. DEADLINE =====');

    console.log('Nested deadline:');
    console.log(
        new Date(
            Number(nestedDeadline) * 1000
        ).toISOString()
    );

    const deadlineDifference =
        Math.abs(
            Number(nestedDeadline) - deadline
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Deadline difference:');
    console.log(
        deadlineDifference,
        'seconds'
    );

    if (deadlineDifference > 5) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: nested deadline differs from requested deadline'
        );
    }

    console.log('Deadline consistency: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 12. LP PARAMETERS
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 12. LP PARAMETERS =====');

    const lpRef =
        nested.loadRef();

    const lpSlice =
        lpRef.beginParse();

    const minLpOut =
        lpSlice.loadCoins();

    const receiverAddress =
        lpSlice.loadAddress();

    const bothPositive =
        lpSlice.loadBit();

    const customGas =
        lpSlice.loadCoins();

    console.log('');
    console.log('MIN LP OUT:');
    console.log(minLpOut.toString());

    if (minLpOut !== MIN_LP_OUT) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: minLpOut mismatch'
        );
    }

    console.log('MIN LP OUT: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Receiver:');
    console.log(receiverAddress.toString());

    if (!receiverAddress.equals(USER)) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: receiver does not match USER'
        );
    }

    console.log('Receiver: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Both positive:');
    console.log(bothPositive);

    if (!bothPositive) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: bothPositive is not enabled'
        );
    }

    console.log('Both positive: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Custom gas:');
    console.log(customGas.toString());

    if (customGas !== 0n) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: unexpected custom gas amount'
        );
    }

    console.log('Custom gas: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 13. TX VALUE COMPONENTS
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 13. TX VALUE COMPONENTS =====');

    const calculatedValue =
        PTON_AMOUNT +
        EXPECTED_FORWARD_TON +
        EXPECTED_PTON_TRANSFER_GAS;

    console.log('pTON amount:');
    console.log(PTON_AMOUNT.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Forward TON:');
    console.log(
        EXPECTED_FORWARD_TON.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON transfer gas:');
    console.log(
        EXPECTED_PTON_TRANSFER_GAS.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Calculated TX value:');
    console.log(
        calculatedValue.toString()
    );

    if (calculatedValue !== EXPECTED_VALUE) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: internal TX value calculation mismatch'
        );
    }

    console.log('TX value arithmetic: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // 14. FINAL SAFETY CHECKS
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 14. FINAL SAFETY CHECKS =====');

    const remainingAfterTx =
        tonBalance - txValue;

    console.log('Remaining TON after tx:');
    console.log(
        Number(remainingAfterTx) / 1e9
    );

    if (
        remainingAfterTx <
        SAFETY_RESERVE
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: remaining TON would be below safety reserve'
        );
    }

    console.log('Safety reserve: PASS');

    // ==================================================
    // FINAL
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '======================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' ALL SEMANTIC PREFLIGHT CHECKS PASSED'
    );
    console.log(
        '======================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('TX DESTINATION: PASS');
    console.log('TX VALUE: PASS');
    console.log('TON_TRANSFER OPCODE: PASS');
    console.log('pTON AMOUNT: PASS');
    console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD: PASS');
    console.log('PROVIDE_LP OPCODE: PASS');
    console.log('ROUTER WALLET: PASS');
    console.log('REFUND: PASS');
    console.log('EXCESSES: PASS');
    console.log('DEADLINE: PASS');
    console.log('MIN LP OUT: PASS');
    console.log('RECEIVER: PASS');
    console.log('BOTH POSITIVE: PASS');
    console.log('CUSTOM GAS: PASS');
    console.log('TX VALUE ARITHMETIC: PASS');
    console.log('SAFETY RESERVE: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log('sender.send(): NOT CALLED');
    console.log('TRANSACTION SENT: NO');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX, pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const PTON = pTON.v2_1.create(PTON_MASTER);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' PTON LIQUIDITY - SEND SCRIPT');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('This script WILL send a transaction when executed.');
    console.log('');

    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER)
    );

    const params = {
        proxyTon: PTON,
        otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
        sendAmount: 500000000n,
        userWalletAddress: USER,
        receiverAddress: USER,
        refundAddress: USER,
        excessesAddress: USER,
        minLpOut: 0n
    };

    console.log('pTON amount:', '0.5 pTON');
    console.log('Other token:', ROOSTER.toString());
    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('Receiver:', USER.toString());
    console.log('minLpOut:', '0');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Sending pTON liquidity transaction...');

    await router.sendProvideLiquidityTon(
        provider.sender(),
        params
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON liquidity transaction submitted.');
    console.log('==============================================');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { JettonMaster } from '@ton/ton';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';


// ============================================================
// STON.fi v2.2
// ROOSTER / pTON
//
// REAL TESTNET SEND
// ROOSTER LEG ONLY
//
// IMPORTANT:
// - This script CAN send a real Testnet transaction.
// - pTON leg is NOT sent by this script.
// - The wallet confirmation is the final authorization.
// ============================================================


// ============================================================
// ADDRESSES
// ============================================================

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);


// ============================================================
// EXACT CURRENT INPUTS
// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100000000000n;
// 100 ROOSTER

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;

const GAS_AMOUNT = 300000000n;
// 0.3 TON

const EXPECTED_FORWARD_GAS = 235000000n;
// 0.235 TON


// ============================================================
// OPCODES
// ============================================================

const JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x0f8a7ea5;

const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;


// ============================================================
// HELPERS
// ============================================================

function format9(value: bigint): string {

    const whole =
        value / 1000000000n;

    const fraction =
        (value % 1000000000n)
            .toString()
            .padStart(9, '0')
            .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction.length > 0
        ? `${whole}.${fraction}`
        : `${whole}`;
}


function assertEqual(
    label: string,
    actual: string,
    expected: string
) {

    if (actual !== expected) {

        throw new Error(
            `${label} FAILED\n` +
            `Expected: ${expected}\n` +
            `Actual:   ${actual}`
        );
    }

    console.log(`PASS  ${label}`);
}


function assertBigInt(
    label: string,
    actual: bigint,
    expected: bigint
) {

    if (actual !== expected) {

        throw new Error(
            `${label} FAILED\n` +
            `Expected: ${expected}\n` +
            `Actual:   ${actual}`
        );
    }

    console.log(`PASS  ${label}`);
}


function section(
    title: string
) {

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ` ${title}`
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}


// ============================================================
// MAIN
// ============================================================

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    section(
        'STON.fi v2.2 REAL ROOSTER SEND'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'REAL TESTNET TRANSACTION'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER LEG ONLY'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON LEG WILL NOT BE SENT'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 1. WALLET
    // ========================================================

    section('1. CONNECTED WALLET');

    const sender =
        provider.sender();


    if (!sender.address) {

        throw new Error(
            'Connected wallet address is unavailable.'
        );
    }


    const senderAddress =
        sender.address;


    console.log(
        'Connected wallet:',
        senderAddress.toString()
    );


    assertEqual(
        'Connected wallet',
        senderAddress.toRawString(),
        USER.toRawString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 2. ROUTER
    // ========================================================

    section('2. ROUTER VERIFICATION');


    const router =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        );


    const routerData =
        await router.getRouterData();


    const routerVersion =
        await router.getRouterVersion();


    console.log(
        'Router:',
        ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Router ID:',
        routerData.routerId.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'DEX type:',
        routerData.dexType
    );

    console.log(
        'Locked:',
        routerData.isLocked
    );

    console.log(
        'Version:',
        routerVersion
    );


    assertEqual(
        'Router v2.2',
        `${routerVersion.major}.${routerVersion.minor}`,
        '2.2'
    );


    if (routerData.isLocked) {

        throw new Error(
            'Router is LOCKED. Aborting before send.'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS  Router unlocked'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 3. POOL
    // ========================================================

    section('3. POOL VERIFICATION');


    const pool =
        await router.getPool({

            token0:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            token1:
                PTON_MASTER
        });


    const poolContract =
        provider.open(pool);


    const poolData =
        await poolContract.getPoolData();


    console.log(
        'Resolved Pool:',
        pool.address.toString()
    );


    assertEqual(
        'Pool address',
        pool.address.toRawString(),
        EXPECTED_POOL.toRawString()
    );


    if (poolData.isLocked) {

        throw new Error(
            'Pool is LOCKED. Aborting before send.'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS  Pool unlocked'
    );


    assertEqual(
        'Pool Router',
        poolData.routerAddress.toRawString(),
        ROUTER_ADDRESS.toRawString()
    );


    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER reserve:',
        format9(poolData.reserve0)
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON reserve:',
        format9(poolData.reserve1)
    );

    console.log(
        'LP total supply:',
        poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 4. USER ROOSTER WALLET
    // ========================================================

    section('4. USER ROOSTER WALLET');


    const roosterMaster =
        provider.open(
            JettonMaster.create(
                ROOSTER_MASTER
            )
        );


    const roosterWalletAddress =
        await roosterMaster.getWalletAddress(
            USER
        );


    console.log(
        'Derived ROOSTER wallet:',
        roosterWalletAddress.toString()
    );


    assertEqual(
        'ROOSTER wallet',
        roosterWalletAddress.toRawString(),
        EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET.toRawString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 5. ROOSTER BALANCE
    // ========================================================

    section('5. ROOSTER BALANCE');


    const walletResult =
        await provider
            .provider(roosterWalletAddress)
            .get(
                'get_wallet_data',
                []
            );


    const roosterBalance =
        walletResult.stack.readBigNumber();


    console.log(
        'Current ROOSTER:',
        format9(roosterBalance)
    );

    console.log(
        'Required ROOSTER:',
        format9(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)
    );


    if (
        roosterBalance <
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'Insufficient ROOSTER balance. ' +
            'Aborting before send.'
        );
    }


    console.log(
        'PASS  ROOSTER balance sufficient'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 6. TON BALANCE
    // ========================================================

    section('6. TON BALANCE');


    const userState =
        await provider
            .provider(USER)
            .getState();


    const tonBalance =
        userState.balance;


    console.log(
        'Current TON:',
        format9(tonBalance)
    );

    console.log(
        'Required message value:',
        format9(GAS_AMOUNT)
    );


    if (
        tonBalance <
        GAS_AMOUNT
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'Insufficient TON balance. ' +
            'Aborting before send.'
        );
    }


    console.log(
        'PASS  TON balance sufficient'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 7. FRESH DEADLINE
    // ========================================================

    section('7. FRESH DEADLINE');


    const now =
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        );


    const deadline =

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { RoosterCoin } from '../wrappers/RoosterCoin';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    if (provider.network() !== 'testnet') {
        throw new Error('STOP: This script is only for TON Testnet.');
    }

    const masterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const master = provider.open(
        RoosterCoin.createFromAddress(masterAddress)
    );

    const metadataUrl =
        'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hosseinkhanihamid/Rooster-Coin/main/metadata.json';

    // TEP-64 off-chain metadata:
    // 0x01 + UTF-8 URL
    const content = beginCell()
        .storeUint(1, 8)
        .storeStringTail(metadataUrl)
        .endCell();

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== SET ROOSTER METADATA =====');
    console.log('Network: TESTNET');
    console.log('Master:', masterAddress.toString());
    console.log('Metadata URL:', metadataUrl);
    console.log('Content Cell:', content.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    console.log('');

    console.log('Sending ChangeMinterContent...');
    console.log('IMPORTANT: Tonkeeper must show TESTNET.');

    await master.sendChangeContent(
        provider.sender(),
        content
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Transaction sent.');
    console.log('Wait for Testnet confirmation.');
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function clean(s: string): string {
    return s
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();
}

type Arg =
    | { kind: "mem"; text: string }
    | { kind: "shift"; text: string };

type CallInfo = {
    no: number;
    pc: number;
    slot: number;
    A: string;
    B: string;
};

 * infer or guess any call destination.
 */

    { no: 28, pc: 0x126d4, slot: 22, A: "MEM64[local1+8]", B: "MEM64[local1+8]" },
    { no: 29, pc: 0x126e4, slot: 7,  A: "MEM64[FRAME+488]", B: "MEM64[FRAME+488]" },

];

function simplify(A: string, B: string) {
    const a0 = `(${A} & 0xffffffff)`;
    const a1 = `(${A} >>u 32)`;
    const b0 = `(${B} & 0xffffffff)`;
    const b1 = `(${B} >>u 32)`;

    const p = `(a0 * b0)`;

    const c0 = `(p >>u 32)`;

    const m = `(c0 + (a0 * b1))`;

    const q = `((b0 * a1) + (m & 0xffffffff))`;

    const low =
        `((p & 0xffffffff) | ((q & 0xffffffff) << 32))`;

    const high =
        `((a1 * b1) + (m >>u 32) + (q >>u 32))`;

    return {
        a0,
        a1,
        b0,
        b1,
        p,
        c0,
        m,
        q,
        low,
        high,
    };
}

function normalize128(A: string, B: string) {
    const x = simplify(A, B);

    return {
        A,
        B,
        low:
            x.low
                .replace(/\bp\b/g, x.p)
                .replace(/\bq\b/g, x.q),
        high:
            x.high
                .replace(/\ba1\b/g, x.a1)
                .replace(/\bb1\b/g, x.b1)
                .replace(/\bm\b/g, x.m)
                .replace(/\bq\b/g, x.q)
                .replace(/\bc0\b/g, x.c0)
                .replace(/\bp\b/g, x.p),
    };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 INSTANCE SIMPLIFICATION v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" EXACT SSA -> CANONICAL 128-BIT PRODUCT");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");
console.log("WASM size :", fs.statSync(WASM).size.toLocaleString());
console.log("Calls     :", calls.length);
console.log("");

for (const c of calls) {
    const r = normalize128(c.A, c.B);

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
        `CALL #${c.no.toString().padStart(2, "0")} @ ${hex(c.pc)} -> S${c.slot}`
    );

    console.log(`A = ${r.A}`);
    console.log(`B = ${r.B}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("CANONICAL LIMBS");
    console.log(`  a0 = (${r.A} & 0xffffffff)`);
    console.log(`  a1 = (${r.A} >>u 32)`);
    console.log(`  b0 = (${r.B} & 0xffffffff)`);
    console.log(`  b1 = (${r.B} >>u 32)`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("INTERMEDIATE");
    console.log("  p  = a0 * b0");
    console.log("  c0 = p >>u 32");
    console.log("  m  = c0 + a0*b1");
    console.log("  q  = b0*a1 + (m & 0xffffffff)");

    console.log("");
    console.log("RESULT");
    console.log("  S" + c.slot + "+0 = LOW");
    console.log("    = (p & 0xffffffff) | ((q & 0xffffffff) << 32)");

    console.log("  S" + c.slot + "+8 = HIGH");
    console.log("    = a1*b1 + (m >>u 32) + (q >>u 32)");
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" COVERAGE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALLs        :", calls.length);
console.log("Unique slots :", new Set(calls.map(x => x.slot)).size);
console.log(
    "Missing slots:",
    [...Array(30).keys()].filter(x => !calls.some(c => c.slot === x))
);

FILE: scripts\slice656Args.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const CALLS = [
  { func: 473,  addr: 0x00020087 },
  { func: 599,  addr: 0x000284fb },
  { func: 1249, addr: 0x00057842 },
  { func: 1601, addr: 0x00071954 },
  { func: 2161, addr: 0x0009b0b8 },
  { func: 2161, addr: 0x0009b0fe },
  { func: 3490, addr: 0x0010da53 },
  { func: 6665, addr: 0x0022fa9e },
  { func: 7020, addr: 0x0023d95e },
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function section(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (sid === id) return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`section ${id} not found`);
}

function getBodies() {
  const sec = section(10);

  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;

  const bodies: {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    code: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    const localCount = uleb(p);
    p = localCount.next;

    for (let j = 0; j < localCount.value; j++) {
      const cnt = uleb(p);
      p = cnt.next;
      p++;
    }

    bodies.push({
      start,
      end,
      code: p,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Lightweight instruction decoder.
 *
 * Returns:
 *   opcode
 *   next
 *
 * It is intentionally conservative.
 */
function decode(p: number) {
  const start = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  switch (op) {
    case 0x20: // local.get
    case 0x21: // local.set
    case 0x22: // local.tee
    case 0x0c: // br
    case 0x0d: // br_if
    case 0x10: { // call
      const r = uleb(p);
      return {
        start,
        op,
        next: r.next,
        imm: r.value,
      };
    }

    case 0x41: { // i32.const
      const r = uleb(p);
      return {
        start,
        op,
        next: r.next,
        imm: r.value,
      };
    }

    case 0x28: { // i32.load
      const a = uleb(p);
      const b = uleb(a.next);

      return {
        start,
        op,
        next: b.next,
        align: a.value,
        offset: b.value,
      };
    }

    case 0x36: { // i32.store
      const a = uleb(p);
      const b = uleb(a.next);

      return {
        start,
        op,
        next: b.next,
        align: a.value,
        offset: b.value,
      };
    }

    case 0x04: // if
    case 0x02: // block
    case 0x03: { // loop
      return {
        start,
        op,
        next: p + 1,
        blockType: wasm[p],
      };
    }

    case 0x05:
    case 0x0b:
    case 0x0f:
    case 0x1a:
    case 0x45:
    case 0x47:
    case 0x4a:
    case 0x4f:
    case 0x6a:
    case 0x6b:
    case 0x6c:
    case 0x6d:
    case 0x6e:
    case 0x71:
    case 0x74:
    case 0x76:
    case 0x7f:
      return {
        start,
        op,
        next: p,
      };

    default:
      return {
        start,
        op,
        next: p,
        unknown: true,
      };
  }
}

function findLastLocalGet(
  codeStart: number,
  before: number,
  localIndex: number
) {
  let p = codeStart;
  let found: number | null = null;

  while (p < before) {
    const d = decode(p);

    if (d.op === 0x20 && d.imm === localIndex) {
      found = d.start;
    }

    if (d.next <= p) break;

    p = d.next;
  }

  return found;
}

/*
 * Print a compact instruction list immediately preceding CALL.
 */
function dumpWindow(code: number, addr: number) {
  const start = Math.max(code, addr - 64);

  console.log(
    `WINDOW ${hex(start)} .. ${hex(addr)}`
  );

  let p = start;

  while (p < addr) {
    const d = decode(p);

    const raw: string[] = [];

    for (let q = d.start; q < Math.min(d.next, addr); q++) {
      raw.push(
        wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
      );
    }

    let extra = "";

    if (d.imm !== undefined) {
      extra = ` imm=${d.imm}`;
    }

    if (d.offset !== undefined) {
      extra += ` offset=${d.offset}`;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(d.start)}  ${raw.join(" ")}${extra}`
    );

    if (d.next <= p) break;

    p = d.next;
  }
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("SLICE ARGUMENTS OF DIRECT CALL 656");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const c of CALLS) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`FUNC ${c.func}`);
  console.log(`CALL 656 @ ${hex(c.addr)}`);

  const body = bodies.find(
    b => c.addr >= b.code && c.addr < b.end
  );

  if (!body) {
    console.log("BODY NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `CODE = ${hex(body.code)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
  );

  console.log("");

  dumpWindow(body.code, c.addr);

  /*
   * The immediate CALL 656 is:
   *
   *   10 90 05
   *
   * We now inspect the last locals referenced immediately
   * before the call. This is deliberately diagnostic only.
   */

  console.log("");
  console.log("LOCAL GETS IN PRE-CALL REGION");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const start = Math.max(body.code, c.addr - 64);

  const seen = new Set<number>();

  let p = start;

  while (p < c.addr) {
    const d = decode(p);

    if (d.op === 0x20 && d.imm !== undefined) {
      if (!seen.has(d.imm)) {
        console.log(
          `local.get ${d.imm} @ ${hex(d.start)}`
        );

        seen.add(d.imm);
      }
    }

    if (d.next <= p) break;

    p = d.next;
  }

  /*
   * Show all local.get instructions in the larger body
   * that occur immediately before the call.
   */
  console.log("");
  console.log("LAST LOCAL.GET BEFORE CALL");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (let l = 0; l < 16; l++) {
    const at = findLastLocalGet(
      body.code,
      c.addr,
      l
    );

    if (at !== null) {
      console.log(
        `local[${l}] <- GET @ ${hex(at)}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

FILE: scripts\stack113Exact.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = [
  [6464, 0x22838e],
  [6611, 0x22c948],
  [6611, 0x22c9ef],
  [6613, 0x22cb34],
  [6615, 0x22cc55],
  [6615, 0x22cc5e],
  [6618, 0x22d083],
  [7467, 0x25093d],
  [7579, 0x2562b8],
] as const;

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }
  return { v: v >>> 0, p };
}

function bodyOf(target: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.p;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.v;

    if (id === 10 && target >= start && target < end)
      return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function decodeInstr(p: number) {
  const op = wasm[p];

  // local.get/set/tee
  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    return {
      text:
        op === 0x20 ? `local.get ${wasm[p+1]}` :
        op === 0x21 ? `local.set ${wasm[p+1]}` :
        `local.tee ${wasm[p+1]}`,
      next: p + 2
    };
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    return {
      text: `i32.const ${x.v}`,
      next: x.p
    };
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    return {
      text: `call ${x.v}`,
      next: x.p
    };
  }

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    const y = uleb(x.p);
    return {
      text: `call_indirect type=${x.v} table=${y.v}`,
      next: y.p
    };
  }

  // memory load/store
  if (
    op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2d || op === 0x2f ||
    op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b
  ) {
    const a = uleb(p + 1);
    const o = uleb(a.p);
    return {
      text: `memop 0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.v} offset=${o.v}`,
      next: o.p
    };
  }

  // common numeric ops
  const one = new Map<number,string>([
    [0x6a,"i32.add"],
    [0x6b,"i32.sub"],
    [0x6c,"i32.mul"],
    [0x47,"i32.lt_u"],
    [0x46,"i32.eq"],
    [0x45,"i32.eqz"],
    [0x71,"i32.and"],
    [0x72,"i32.or"],
    [0x73,"i32.xor"],
    [0x74,"i32.shl"],
    [0x76,"i32.shr_u"],
    [0x1a,"drop"],
    [0x0b,"end"],
  ]);

  return {
    text: one.get(op) ?? `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`,
    next: p + 1
  };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT CALL113 STACK CONTEXT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const [func, target] of targets) {

  const body = bodyOf(target);

  console.log(`\nFUNC ${func} @ ${hex(target)}`);
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  if (!body) {
    console.log("BODY NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  let p = body.start;
  const list: { at:number,text:string }[] = [];

  while (p < target) {
    const at = p;
    const d = decodeInstr(p);
    list.push({ at, text:d.text });
    p = d.next;
  }

  const recent = list.slice(-35);

  for (const x of recent)
    console.log(`${hex(x.at)}  ${x.text}`);

  console.log(`${hex(target)}  >>> CALL 113`);
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
