import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const start = 0x7672;
const end   = 0x76e7;

const locals: string[] = [
  "p0",
  "p1",
  "p2",
  "p3",
  "p4",
  "l5"
];

const localTypes = [
  "i32",
  "i64",
  "i64",
  "i64",
  "i64",
  "i64"
];

let stack: string[] = [];

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function pop(): string {
  return stack.pop() ?? "???";
}

function bin(op: string) {
  const b = pop();
  const a = pop();
  stack.push(`(${a} ${op} ${b})`);
}

console.log("FUNC 87 EXACT SYMBOLIC STACK");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = start;

while (p < end) {

  const addr = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  if (op === 0x01) {
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} nop`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    const v = locals[r.value];
    stack.push(v);

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)} local.get ${r.value} -> ${v}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    const v = stack.at(-1) ?? "???";
    locals[r.value] = v;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)} local.tee ${r.value} = ${v}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const r = leb(p);
    p = r.next;

    const v = String(r.value);
    stack.push(v);

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)} i64.const ${v}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x7c) {
    bin("+");
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} i64.add -> ${stack.at(-1)}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x7e) {
    bin("*");
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} i64.mul -> ${stack.at(-1)}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x83) {
    bin("&");
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} i64.and -> ${stack.at(-1)}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x84) {
    bin("|");
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} i64.or -> ${stack.at(-1)}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x86) {
    bin("<<");
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} i64.shl -> ${stack.at(-1)}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x88) {
    bin(">>u");
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} i64.shr_u -> ${stack.at(-1)}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x37) {
    const align = leb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const off = leb(p);
    p = off.next;

    const value = pop();
    const address = pop();

    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} i64.store offset=${off.value}`);
    console.log(`  ADDRESS = ${address}`);
    console.log(`  VALUE   = ${value}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} end`);
    break;
  }

  console.log(
    `0x${addr.toString(16)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("FINAL LOCALS:");
locals.forEach((v, i) => {
  console.log(`  local${i} = ${v}`);
});

console.log("");
console.log("FINAL STACK:");
console.log(stack);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\summarizeImport23.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 23;
const IMPORTED = 83;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32leb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function s32leb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p,
    };
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function section(idWanted: number) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p];

        const s = u32leb(p + 1);

        const start = s.next;
        const end = start + s.value;

        if (id === idWanted) {
            return {
                start,
                end,
            };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${idWanted} NOT FOUND`);
}

function instrLen(pos: number) {
    const op = buf[pos];
    let p = pos + 1;

    if (
        op === 0x20 ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 ||
        op === 0x24 ||
        op === 0x10 ||
        op === 0x0c ||
        op === 0x0d
    ) {
        return u32leb(p).next - pos;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        return s32leb(p).next - pos;
    }

    if (
        op >= 0x28 &&
        op <= 0x3e
    ) {
        const a = u32leb(p);
        const b = u32leb(a.next);
        return b.next - pos;
    }

    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
        return u32leb(p).next - pos;
    }

    if (op === 0xfc || op === 0xfd || op === 0xfe) {
        return u32leb(p).next - pos;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const a = u32leb(p);
        const b = u32leb(a.next);
        return b.next - pos;
    }

    return 1;
}

function decode(pos: number) {
    const op = buf[pos];

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
        return `CALL ${r.value}`;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
        return `GETL ${r.value}`;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
        return `SETL ${r.value}`;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
        return `TEEL ${r.value}`;
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
        const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
        return `GETG ${r.value}`;
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {
        const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
        return `SETG ${r.value}`;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = s32leb(pos + 1);
        return `CONST ${hex(r.value)}`;
    }

    if (
        op >= 0x28 &&
        op <= 0x35
    ) {
        const a = u32leb(pos + 1);
        const o = u32leb(a.next);

        const names: Record<number, string> = {
            0x28: "LOAD32",
            0x29: "LOAD64",
            0x2a: "LOADF32",
            0x2b: "LOADF64",
            0x2c: "LOAD8S",
            0x2d: "LOAD8U",
            0x2e: "LOAD16S",
            0x2f: "LOAD16U",
            0x30: "LOAD8",
            0x31: "LOAD8",
            0x32: "LOAD16",
            0x33: "LOAD16",
            0x34: "LOAD32",
            0x35: "LOAD32",
        };

        return `${names[op] ?? "LOAD"} off=${hex(o.value)}`;
    }

    if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
        const a = u32leb(pos + 1);
        const o = u32leb(a.next);

        return `STORE off=${hex(o.value)}`;
    }

    if (op === 0x04) return "IF";
    if (op === 0x05) return "ELSE";
    if (op === 0x0b) return "END";
    if (op === 0x0f) return "RETURN";
    if (op === 0x1a) return "DROP";

    if (op === 0x02) return "BLOCK";
    if (op === 0x03) return "LOOP";

    if (op === 0x45) return "EQZ";
    if (op === 0x46) return "EQ";
    if (op === 0x47) return "NE";
    if (op === 0x49) return "LT_U";
    if (op === 0x4f) return "GE_U";
    if (op === 0x6a) return "ADD";
    if (op === 0x6b) return "SUB";
    if (op === 0x71) return "AND";
    if (op === 0x72) return "OR";
    if (op === 0x73) return "XOR";

    if (op === 0xfc) {
        const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
        return `EXT ${r.value}`;
    }

    return `OP 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

const functionSection = section(3);
const codeSection = section(10);

let fp = functionSection.start;

const functionCount = u32leb(fp);
fp = functionCount.next;

const definedCount = functionCount.value;

const types: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
    const r = u32leb(fp);
    fp = r.next;

    types.push(r.value);
}

let cp = codeSection.start;

const codeCount = u32leb(cp);
cp = codeCount.next;

const bodies: {
    func: number;
    type: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < codeCount.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = u32leb(cp);

    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + ordinal,
        type: types[ordinal],
        start,
        end,
    });

    cp = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("IMPORT FUNC23 CALL SUMMARY");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log('TARGET = FUNC23 = "a"."x"');
console.log("TYPE   = (i32) -> i32");
console.log("SCRATCH = 0x255a6c");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {
    let pos = body.start;

    const localGroups = u32leb(pos);
    pos = localGroups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
        const count = u32leb(pos);
        pos = count.next + 1;
    }

    const instructions: {
        at: number;
        len: number;
        text: string;

    while (pos < body.end) {
        const at = pos;
        const len = Math.max(1, instrLen(pos));

        instructions.push({
            at,
            len,
            text: decode(pos),
        });

        pos += len;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const ins = instructions[i];

        if (ins.text !== "CALL 23") continue;

        total++;

        console.log("");
        console.log(`CALL #${total}`);
        console.log(`  FUNC  = ${body.func}`);
        console.log(`  TYPE  = ${body.type}`);
        console.log(`  CALL  = ${hex(ins.at)}`);

        const before = instructions.slice(
            Math.max(0, i - 5),
            i
        );

        const after = instructions.slice(
            i + 1,
            Math.min(instructions.length, i + 9)
        );

        console.log("  BEFORE:");

        for (const x of before) {
            console.log(
                `    ${hex(x.at)}  ${x.text}`
            );
        }

        console.log("  CALL:");

        console.log(
            `    ${hex(ins.at)}  CALL 23`
        );

        console.log("  AFTER:");

        for (const x of after) {
            console.log(
                `    ${hex(x.at)}  ${x.text}`
            );
        }

        const previous = before.map(x => x.text);

        const next = after.map(x => x.text);

        const hasScratchClear =
            previous.some(
                x => x === `CONST ${hex(0x255a6c)}`
            );

        const readsScratch = next.some(
            x => x.includes("LOAD32 off=0x00000000")
        );

        console.log("  FLAGS:");

        console.log(
            `    previous CONST 0x255a6c = ${
                hasScratchClear ? "PRESENT" : "NO"
            }`
        );

        console.log(
            `    following LOAD = ${
                readsScratch ? "POSSIBLE" : "NO"
            }`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TOTAL CALL SITES = " + total);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\symbolicFunc810Calls.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const FUNC = 810;
const TARGET = 445;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sec = {};

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const z = uleb(p);
    p = z.next;

    sec[id] = {
      start: p,
      end: p + z.value
    };

    p += z.value;
  }

  return sec;
}

function getFunctions() {

  const sec = readSections();

  let p = sec[3].start;
  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;

  const types = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    types.push(x.value);
  }

  p = sec[10].start;
  const c = uleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {

    const z = uleb(p);
    const size = z.value;
    p = z.next;

    bodies.push({
      func: IMPORTED + i,
      type: types[i],
      start: p,
      end: p + size
    });

    p += size;
  }

  return { sec, types, bodies };
}

const { sec, types, bodies } = getFunctions();

const f = bodies.find(x => x.func === FUNC);

if (!f) {
  console.log("FUNC810 NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(1);
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------- */
/* Resolve type signatures                                    */
/* --------------------------------------------------------- */

function getType(index) {

  let p = sec[1].start;
  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

    const form = b[p++];

    const pc = uleb(p);
    p = pc.next;

    const params = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
      params.push(b[p++]);

    const rc = uleb(p);
    p = rc.next;

    const results = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
      results.push(b[p++]);

    if (i === index)
      return { params, results };
  }

  return null;
}

function typeName(x) {

  const m = {
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64"
  };

  return m[x] ?? `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
}

const funcType = getType(f.type);
const target = bodies.find(x => x.func === TARGET);
const targetType = getType(target.type);

/* --------------------------------------------------------- */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 810 SYMBOLIC STACK -> FUNC 445");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  `FUNC810 TYPE : ${f.type}  (${funcType.params.map(typeName).join(",")}) -> (${funcType.results.map(typeName).join(",")})`
);

console.log(
  `FUNC445 TYPE : ${target.type}  (${targetType.params.map(typeName).join(",")}) -> (${targetType.results.map(typeName).join(",")})`
);

/*
FUNC810 relevant region:

0x38499:
    local.get 2
    local.get 0
    call 445
    local.tee 0

0x384e0:
    local.get 2
    call 445
    local.tee 0
*/

/*
We know from the raw bytes that the first call is:

    local.get 2
    local.get 0
    call 445

Therefore only TWO values are explicitly pushed immediately
before CALL445 at 0x3849b.

The second call is:

    local.get 2
    call 445

Therefore only ONE value is explicitly pushed immediately
before CALL445 at 0x384e2.

This is significant: FUNC445 may NOT have three parameters.
The previous script assumed a 3-argument signature and was wrong.
*/

FILE: scripts\symbolicStackFunc810.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x38447;
const END   = 0x38509;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);
      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }
  }
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readInstr(p) {

  const op = b[p];
  let q = p + 1;

  switch (op) {

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(q);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
        push: `local${x.value}`
      };
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(q);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
        set: x.value
      };
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(q);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
        tee: x.value
      };
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(q);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
        push: `${x.value}`
      };
    }

    case 0x1a:
      return {
        next: q,
        text: "drop",
        drop: true
      };

    case 0x6a:
      return {
        next: q,
        text: "i32.add",
        binary: "+"
      };

    case 0x6b:
      return {
        next: q,
        text: "i32.sub",
        binary: "-"
      };

    case 0x71:
      return {
        next: q,
        text: "i32.and",
        binary: "&"
      };

    case 0x45:
      return {
        next: q,
        text: "i32.eqz",
        unary: "eqz"
      };

    case 0x46:
      return {
        next: q,
        text: "i32.eq",
        binary: "=="
      };

    case 0x47:
      return {
        next: q,
        text: "i32.ne",
        binary: "!="
      };

    case 0x28: {
      const align = uleb(q);
      const off = uleb(align.next);

      return {
        next: off.next,
        text: `i32.load offset=${off.value}`,
        load: off.value
      };
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const align = uleb(q);
      const off = uleb(align.next);

      return {
        next: off.next,
        text: `i32.store offset=${off.value}`,
        store: off.value
      };
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(q);

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `call ${x.value}`,
        call: x.value
      };
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(q);

      return {
        next: t.next,
        text: `call_indirect type=${t.value}`,
        indirect: t.value
      };
    }

    case 0x0b:
      return {
        next: q,
        text: "end"
      };

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(q);

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `br ${x.value}`
      };
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(q);

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `br_if ${x.value}`
      };
    }

    case 0x02:
      return {
        next: q + 1,
        text: "block"
      };

    case 0x03:
      return {
        next: q + 1,
        text: "loop"
      };

    default:
      return {
        next: q,
        text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      };
  }
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------- */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 810 FULL SYMBOLIC STACK TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`RANGE : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("");

const stack = [];

function showStack() {

  if (!stack.length) {
    console.log("STACK = []");
    return;
  }

  console.log(
    "STACK = [" +
    stack.map((x, i) => `${i}:${x}`).join(" | ") +
    "]"
  );
}

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

  const ins = readInstr(p);

  console.log(
    `${hex(p)}  ${ins.text}`
  );

  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* local.get                                                */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (ins.push !== undefined) {
    stack.push(ins.push);
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* local.set                                                 */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (ins.set !== undefined) {

    const value =
      stack.length
        ? stack.pop()
        : "<EMPTY>";

    console.log(
      `          local${ins.set} = ${value}`
    );
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* local.tee                                                 */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (ins.tee !== undefined) {

    const value =
      stack.length
        ? stack[stack.length - 1]
        : "<EMPTY>";

    console.log(
      `          local${ins.tee} = ${value}`
    );
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* binary operation                                          */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (ins.binary) {

    const b2 = stack.pop() ?? "<EMPTY>";
    const b1 = stack.pop() ?? "<EMPTY>";

    stack.push(`(${b1} ${ins.binary} ${b2})`);
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* unary operation                                           */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (ins.unary) {

    const a = stack.pop() ?? "<EMPTY>";

    stack.push(`${ins.unary}(${a})`);
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* load                                                      */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (ins.load !== undefined) {

    const addr = stack.pop() ?? "<EMPTY>";

    stack.push(`MEM[${addr}+${ins.load}]`);
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* store                                                     */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (ins.store !== undefined) {

    const value = stack.pop() ?? "<EMPTY>";
    const addr  = stack.pop() ?? "<EMPTY>";

    console.log(
      `          STORE MEM[${addr}+${ins.store}] = ${value}`
    );
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* drop                                                      */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (ins.drop) {
    stack.pop();
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* FUNC CALL                                                 */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (ins.call !== undefined) {

    if (ins.call === 445) {

      console.log("");
      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log(`CALL FUNC445 @ ${hex(p)}`);
      console.log("############################################################");

      showStack();

      console.log("");
      console.log("FUNC445 TYPE = (i32, i32) -> i32");

      console.log(
        "Therefore the TOP TWO stack values are the arguments:"
      );

      const a1 = stack[stack.length - 2] ?? "<MISSING>";
      const a2 = stack[stack.length - 1] ?? "<MISSING>";

      console.log(`  arg0 = ${a1}`);
      console.log(`  arg1 = ${a2}`);

      console.log("");

      stack.pop();
      stack.pop();

      stack.push("FUNC445_RESULT");

    } else {

      /*
       * For unknown calls we do not guess parameter counts.
       * Keep the symbolic stack intact.
       */

      console.log(
        `          CALL FUNC${ins.call} [parameter count not resolved here]`
      );
    }
  }

  p = ins.next;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL SYMBOLIC STACK");
console.log("============================================================");

showStack();

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\testAdnlConnectionV22.ts

import { ADNLClientTCP } from 'adnl';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TESTNET_CONFIG_URL =
    'https://ton-blockchain.github.io/testnet-global.config.json';

function intToIp(ip: number): string {
    return [
        ip & 255,
        (ip >>> 8) & 255,
        (ip >>> 16) & 255,
        (ip >>> 24) & 255,
    ].join('.');
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' ADNL CONNECTION TEST');
    console.log(' TESTNET GLOBAL CONFIG');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log();

    const response = await fetch(TESTNET_CONFIG_URL);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Failed to download config: ${response.status}`
        );
    }

    const config: any = await response.json();

    if (!config.liteservers || config.liteservers.length === 0) {
        throw new Error('No LiteServers found in testnet config');
    }

    const ls = config.liteservers[0];

    const ip = intToIp(ls.ip);
    const port = ls.port;
    const host = `tcp://${ip}:${port}`;
    const publicKey = Buffer.from(ls.id.key, 'base64');

    console.log(`LiteServer: 1/${config.liteservers.length}`);
    console.log(`IP:         ${ip}`);
    console.log(`Port:       ${port}`);
    console.log(`Host:       ${host}`);
    console.log(`Public key: ${ls.id.key}`);
    console.log(`Key bytes:  ${publicKey.length}`);
    console.log();

    let client: ADNLClientTCP;

    try {
        client = new ADNLClientTCP(host, publicKey);
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('[CONSTRUCTOR ERROR]');
        console.error(err);
        return;
    }

    let finished = false;

    client.on('connect', () => {
        console.log('[CONNECT] TCP connection established');
    });

    client.on('ready', () => {
        if (finished) {
            return;
        }

        finished = true;

        console.log('[READY] ADNL handshake completed');
        console.log();
        console.log('RESULT: ADNL connection is READY');

        setTimeout(() => {
            try {
                client.end();
            } catch {}
        }, 500);
    });

    client.on('close', () => {
        console.log('[CLOSE] Socket closed');
    });

    client.on('error', (err) => {
        console.log();
        console.log('[ERROR] ADNL error:');
        console.log(err);
    });

    try {
        console.log('Connecting...');
        await client.connect();
        console.log('connect() returned');
    } catch (err) {
        console.error();
        console.error('[CONNECT EXCEPTION]');
        console.error(err);
    }

    await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
        setTimeout(() => {
            if (!finished) {
                console.log();
                console.log('RESULT: Timeout waiting for READY');

                try {
                    client.end();
                } catch {}
            }

            resolve();
        }, 15000);
    });
}

import { ADNLClientTCP } from 'adnl';

const SERVER = 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088';
const PUBLIC_KEY = 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=';

async function main() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' DIRECT ADNL CONNECTION TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO WALLET CONNECTION REQUIRED.');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Server:', SERVER);
    console.log('Public key:', PUBLIC_KEY);
    console.log('');

    const client = new ADNLClientTCP(SERVER, PUBLIC_KEY);

    client.on('connect', () => {
        console.log('[EVENT] connect');
    });

    client.on('ready', () => {
        console.log('[EVENT] ready');
    });

    client.on('data', (data: Buffer) => {
        console.log('[EVENT] data:', data.length, 'bytes');
    });

    client.on('close', () => {
        console.log('[EVENT] close');
    });

    client.on('error', (error: Error) => {
        console.log('');
        console.log('===== ADNL ERROR =====');
        console.log('Message:', error?.message);
        console.log('Name:', error?.name);
        console.log('Code:', (error as any)?.code);
        console.log('Stack:', error?.stack);
    });

    console.log('===== CONNECT =====');

    try {
        await client.connect();

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== CONNECT() COMPLETED =====');
        console.log('ADNL connection established successfully.');

    } catch (error: any) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('===== CONNECT FAILED =====');
        console.log('Message:', error?.message);
        console.log('Name:', error?.name);
        console.log('Code:', error?.code);
        console.log('Stack:', error?.stack);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== TEST FINISHED =====');

    setTimeout(() => {
        try {
            client.end();
        } catch {}

        process.exit(0);
    }, 2000);
}

main().catch((error: any) => {
    console.log('');
    console.log('===== UNHANDLED ERROR =====');
    console.log('Message:', error?.message);
    console.log('Stack:', error?.stack);
    process.exit(1);
});

FILE: scripts\testAllAdnlConnectionsV22.ts

import { ADNLClientTCP } from 'adnl';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TESTNET_CONFIG_URL =
    'https://ton-blockchain.github.io/testnet-global.config.json';

function intToIp(ip: number): string {
    return [
        (ip >>> 24) & 255,
        (ip >>> 16) & 255,
        (ip >>> 8) & 255,
        ip & 255,
    ].join('.');
}

function testOne(
    index: number,
    total: number,
    ip: string,
    port: number,
    keyBase64: string
): Promise<string> {
    return new Promise(async (resolve) => {
        const host = `tcp://${ip}:${port}`;
        const publicKey = Buffer.from(keyBase64, 'base64');

        let done = false;

        const finish = (result: string) => {
            if (done) return;
            done = true;

            try {
                client?.end();
            } catch {}

            resolve(result);
        };

        let client: ADNLClientTCP;

        try {
            client = new ADNLClientTCP(host, publicKey);
        } catch (err: any) {
            finish(`CONSTRUCTOR ERROR: ${err?.message ?? String(err)}`);
            return;
        }

        const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            finish('TIMEOUT');
        }, 8000);

        client.on('connect', () => {
            console.log(`  [${index}/${total}] TCP CONNECT`);
        });

        client.on('ready', () => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            console.log(`  [${index}/${total}] ADNL READY`);
            finish('READY');
        });

        client.on('error', (err: any) => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            console.log(
                `  [${index}/${total}] ERROR: ${err?.message ?? String(err)}`
            );
            finish('ERROR');
        });

        client.on('close', () => {
            if (!done) {
                clearTimeout(timeout);
                console.log(`  [${index}/${total}] CLOSE`);
                finish('CLOSE');
            }
        });

        try {
            await client.connect();
        } catch (err: any) {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            finish(`CONNECT THROW: ${err?.message ?? String(err)}`);
        }
    });
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' ALL TESTNET ADNL CONNECTIONS');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    const response = await fetch(TESTNET_CONFIG_URL);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Config HTTP ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`
        );
    }

    const config: any = await response.json();

    console.log(`LiteServers found: ${config.liteservers.length}`);
    console.log();

    const results: string[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < config.liteservers.length; i++) {
        const ls = config.liteservers[i];

        const ip = intToIp(ls.ip);
        const port = ls.port;

        console.log(
            `[${i + 1}/${config.liteservers.length}] ${ip}:${port}`
        );

        const result = await testOne(
            i + 1,
            config.liteservers.length,
            ip,
            port,
            ls.id.key
        );

        console.log(`  RESULT: ${result}`);
        console.log();

        results.push(`${i + 1}. ${ip}:${port} -> ${result}`);
    }

    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' SUMMARY');
    console.log('========================================================================');

    for (const result of results) {
        console.log(result);
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('No blockchain transaction was sent.');
}

async function getJettonWallet(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
    master: Address,
    owner: Address,
): Promise<Address> {
    const result = await provider
        .provider(master)
        .get('get_wallet_address', [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(owner)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ]);

    return result.stack.readAddress();
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('===== TEST ALL V2.2 POOL-CREATION ROUTERS =====');
    console.log('ROOSTER MASTER:', ROOSTER_MASTER);
    console.log('ROUTERS:', ROUTER_CANDIDATES.length);
    console.log('');

    const roosterMaster = Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);

    for (let i = 0; i < ROUTER_CANDIDATES.length; i++) {
        const routerAddress = ROUTER_CANDIDATES[i];
        const router = Address.parse(routerAddress);

        console.log('==================================================');
        console.log(`ROUTER #${i + 1}`);
        console.log('==================================================');
        console.log('Router:', routerAddress);

        try {
            // -------------------------------------------------
            // 1. Router state
            // -------------------------------------------------
            const routerState = await provider.provider(router).getState();

            console.log(
                'Router State:',
                routerState.state.type,
            );

            console.log(
                'Router Balance:',
                routerState.balance.toString(),
            );

            // -------------------------------------------------
            // 2. ROOSTER Router Wallet
            // -------------------------------------------------
            const roosterWallet = await getJettonWallet(
                provider,
                roosterMaster,
                router,
            );

            console.log(
                'ROOSTER Router Wallet:',
                roosterWallet.toString(),
            );

            // -------------------------------------------------
            // 3. pTON master from API
            // -------------------------------------------------
            const ptonMaster =
                Address.parse(
                    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S',
                );

            const ptonWallet = await getJettonWallet(
                provider,
                ptonMaster,
                router,
            );

            console.log(
                'pTON Router Wallet:',
                ptonWallet.toString(),
            );

            // -------------------------------------------------
            // 4. Direct get_pool_address
            // -------------------------------------------------
            try {
                const result = await provider
                    .provider(router)
                    .get('get_pool_address', [
                        {
                            type: 'slice',
                            cell: beginCell()
                                .storeAddress(roosterWallet)
                                .endCell(),
                        },
                        {
                            type: 'slice',
                            cell: beginCell()
                                .storeAddress(ptonWallet)
                                .endCell(),
                        },
                    ]);

                const poolAddress = result.stack.readAddress();

                console.log(
                    'POOL ADDRESS:',
                    poolAddress.toString(),
                );

                console.log('GET_POOL_ADDRESS: SUCCESS');
            } catch (e) {
                console.log(
                    'GET_POOL_ADDRESS: ERROR',
                    e instanceof Error
                        ? e.message
                        : String(e),
                );
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                'ROUTER TEST ERROR:',
                e instanceof Error
                    ? e.message
                    : String(e),
            );
        }

        console.log('');
    }

    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log('ALL ROUTERS TEST COMPLETE');
    console.log('==================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\testGetLibraryV22.ts

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const LITESERVER_HOST =
    'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842';

const LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY =
    'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=';

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LIBRARY GET TEST');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    console.log(`Host:       ${LITESERVER_HOST}`);
    console.log(`Public key: ${LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY}`);
    console.log();

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: LITESERVER_HOST,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY, 'base64'),
    });

    try {
        console.log('Waiting for ADNL READY...');

        const started = Date.now();

        while (!engine.isReady()) {
            if (Date.now() - started > 15000) {
                throw new Error(
                    'LiteServer did not become ready within 15 seconds'
                );
            }

            await sleep(250);
        }

        console.log('ADNL READY');
        console.log();

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
        });

        const hash = Buffer.from(LIBRARY_HASH_HEX, 'hex');

        console.log('Library hash:');
        console.log(LIBRARY_HASH_HEX);
        console.log();

        console.log('Calling getLibraries()...');

        const result = await client.getLibraries([hash]);

        console.log();
        console.log('getLibraries() returned.');
        console.log();

        console.log(`Result kind: ${result.kind}`);
        console.log(`Entries: ${result.result.length}`);

        for (let i = 0; i < result.result.length; i++) {
            const entry = result.result[i];

            console.log();
            console.log(`--- Entry ${i + 1} ---`);
            console.log(`Kind: ${entry.kind}`);
            console.log(
                `Hash: ${Buffer.from(entry.hash)
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase()}`
            );
            console.log(`Data bytes: ${entry.data.length}`);

            try {
                const cell = Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(entry.data))[0];

                console.log('Cell BOC: VALID');
                console.log(
                    `Cell hash: ${cell.hash()
                        .toString('hex')
                        .toUpperCase()}`
                );
                console.log(`Cell bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
                console.log(`Cell refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);
            } catch (err: any) {
                console.log('Cell BOC: INVALID');
                console.log(
                    `Decode error: ${err?.message ?? String(err)}`
                );
            }
        }

        console.log();
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log(' TEST COMPLETE');
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log('No blockchain transaction was sent.');
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

function intToIP(ip: number): string {
    const unsigned = ip >>> 0;

    return [
        unsigned & 255,
        (unsigned >>> 8) & 255,
        (unsigned >>> 16) & 255,
        (unsigned >>> 24) & 255,
    ].join('.');
}

async function main() {
    console.log('===== DIRECT LIBRARY LOOKUP TEST =====');

    const servers = [
        {
            name: 'Server 1',
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            name: 'Server 2',
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const libraryHash = Buffer.from(
        '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86',
        'hex'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Library hash:');
    console.log(libraryHash.toString('hex'));
    console.log('Hash bytes:', libraryHash.length);

    for (const server of servers) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(server.name);
        console.log('========================================');

        const publicKey = Buffer.from(
            server.key,
            'base64'
        );

        const host =
            `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`;

        console.log('Host:', host);
        console.log('Key bytes:', publicKey.length);

        const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
            host,
            publicKey,
            reconnectTimeout: 5000,
        });

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
            batchSize: 1,
        });

        try {
            console.log('Testing masterchain...');

            const master =
                await client.getMasterchainInfo();

            console.log(
                'Masterchain OK',
                'seqno =',
                master.last.seqno
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('Calling getLibraries...');

            const result =
                await client.getLibraries([
                    libraryHash,
                ]);

            console.log('');
            console.log('Result:');

            console.dir(result, {
                depth: 5,
            });

            if (
                result.result &&
                result.result.length > 0
            ) {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '>>> LIBRARY FOUND ON',
                    server.name
                );
                console.log(
                    'Count:',
                    result.result.length
                );
            } else {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '>>> LIBRARY NOT FOUND ON',
                    server.name
                );
            }

        } catch (error) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                '>>> SERVER ERROR ON',
                server.name
            );
            console.error(
                error instanceof Error
                    ? error.message
                    : error
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('TEST COMPLETE');
    console.log('========================================');
}

export async function run() {
    await main();
}

const boc = 'te6cckEBAQEAIwAIQgJaNMMzvNyscNQfmvynmqbngvHICl5LLviW7F4g6Rgshrb7NBQ=';

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

async function main() {

    const servers = [
        {
            name: 'Server 1',
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            name: 'Server 2',
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const LIBRARY_HASH =
        '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86';

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' TON LITE LIBRARY TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO WALLET');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Target Library Hash:');
    console.log(LIBRARY_HASH);
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        const host =
            `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`;

        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log(server.name);
        console.log('Host:', host);

        const key =
            Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64');

        console.log('Key bytes:', key.length);

        const single =
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host,
                publicKey: key,
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            });

        engines.push(single);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('Creating RoundRobin engine...');

    const engine =
        new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client =
        new LiteClient({
            engine,
            batchSize: 1,
        });

    try {

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('MASTERCHAIN TEST');

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('');
        console.log('SUCCESS');

        console.log(
            'Masterchain seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        console.log(
            'Root hash:',
            Buffer.from(
                master.last.rootHash
            ).toString('hex')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('RoundRobin connection is working.');

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('LIBRARY TEST');

        const hash =
            Buffer.from(
                LIBRARY_HASH,
                'hex'
            );

        console.log('Hash bytes:', hash.length);

        const result =
            await client.getLibraries([hash]);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Raw result:');

        console.dir(
            result,
            { depth: 5 }
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Result count:',
            result.result.length
        );

        if (result.result.length > 0) {

            console.log('');
            console.log('LIBRARY RETURNED');

            for (
                let i = 0;
                i < result.result.length;
                i++
            ) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('Item', i, ':');

                console.dir(
                    result.result[i],
                    { depth: 5 }
                );
            }

        } else {

            console.log('');
            console.log('LIBRARY NOT RETURNED');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(' TEST FINISHED');
        console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
        console.log('========================================');

    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ERROR');

        console.error(error);

    } finally {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Closing engines...');

        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }

        console.log('Done.');
    }

    // Force exit only after all test logic and engine cleanup.
    process.exit(0);
}

function intToIP(int: number) {

    const part1 =
        int & 255;

    const part2 =
        (int >> 8) & 255;

    const part3 =
        (int >> 16) & 255;

    const part4 =
        (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch((error) => {

    console.error('');
    console.error('FATAL ERROR');
    console.error(error);

    process.exit(1);
});

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
    LiteEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

function intToIP(int: number) {
    const part1 = int & 255;
    const part2 = (int >> 8) & 255;
    const part3 = (int >> 16) & 255;
    const part4 = (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

async function main() {
    const servers = [
        {
            ip: 822907680,
            port: 27842,
            key: 'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    // فقط برای تست عملکرد getLibraries
    const testHash = Buffer.from(
        '8F452D7A4DFD74066B682365177259ED05734435BE76B5FD4BD5D8AF2B7C3D68',
        'hex'
    );

    console.log('===== LITESERVER LIBRARY TEST =====');
    console.log('Test hash:', testHash.toString('hex'));
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {
        const host = `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`;
        console.log('Adding:', host);

        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            })
        );
    }

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);
    const client = new LiteClient({ engine });

    try {
        console.log('');
        console.log('Getting masterchain info...');

        const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('Masterchain seqno:', master.last.seqno);
        console.log('');

        console.log('Calling getLibraries()...');
        
        const result = await client.getLibraries([testHash]);

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== RESULT =====');
        console.dir(result, { depth: 5 });
        console.log('');

        console.log('Returned libraries:', result.result.length);
    } finally {
        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }
}

main().catch((e) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== ERROR =====');
    console.error(e);
    process.exit(1);
});

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

async function main() {

    const servers = [
        {
            name: 'Server 1',
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            name: 'Server 2',
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' TON LITE ROUND ROBIN CONNECTION TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO WALLET');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        const host =
            `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`;

        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log(server.name);
        console.log('Host:', host);

        const key =
            Buffer.from(
                server.key,
                'base64'
            );

        console.log(
            'Key bytes:',
            key.length
        );

        const engine =
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host,
                publicKey: key,
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            });

        engines.push(engine);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('Creating RoundRobin engine...');

    const engine =
        new LiteRoundRobinEngine(
            engines
        );

    const client =
        new LiteClient({
            engine,
            batchSize: 1,
        });

    try {

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('MASTERCHAIN TEST');

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('');
        console.log('SUCCESS');
        console.log(
            'Masterchain seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        console.log(
            'Root hash:',
            Buffer.from(
                master.last.rootHash
            ).toString('hex')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('RoundRobin connection is working.');

    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ERROR');

        console.error(error);

    } finally {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Closing engines...');

        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }

        console.log('Done.');
    }
}

function intToIP(int: number) {

    const part1 =
        int & 255;

    const part2 =
        (int >> 8) & 255;

    const part3 =
        (int >> 16) & 255;

    const part4 =
        (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch((error) => {

    console.error('');
    console.error('FATAL ERROR');
    console.error(error);

    process.exit(1);
});

FILE: scripts\testMissingLibrary.ts
====================================================================================================
import { LiteClient } from "ton-lite-client";
import { LiteSingleEngine } from "ton-lite-client/dist/engines/single";

const MISSING_HASH = Buffer.from(
    "631cf98bbb3ee540dacc48256975d8e2c3080c6342e3ff4a9edaac6db73730041",
    "hex"
);

const SERVERS = [
    {
        name: "SERVER 1",
        host: "tcp://168.119.95.207:27787",
        key: "Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=",
    },
    {
        name: "SERVER 2",
        host: "tcp://168.119.82.221:51088",
        key: "Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=",
    },
];

async function waitUntilReady(
    engine: LiteSingleEngine,
    timeoutMs = 15000
) {
    const start = Date.now();

    while (Date.now() - start < timeoutMs) {
        if (engine.isReady()) {
            return true;
        }

        if (engine.isClosed()) {
            // Give the engine a little time to establish the connection.
            await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 250));
        } else {
            await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 250));
        }
    }

    return false;
}

async function testServer(
    name: string,
    host: string,
    key: string
) {
    console.log(`\n===== ${name} =====`);
    console.log(`Server: ${host}`);
    console.log(`Requested hash: ${MISSING_HASH.toString("hex")}`);

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(key, "base64"),
        client: "tcp",
        reconnectTimeout: 3000,
    });

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
    });

    try {
        console.log("Waiting for LiteServer connection...");

        const ready = await waitUntilReady(engine, 15000);

        console.log("Engine ready:", ready);
        console.log("Engine closed:", engine.isClosed());

        if (!ready) {
            console.log("RESULT: CONNECTION NOT READY");
            return;
        }

        const result = await client.getLibraries([
            MISSING_HASH
        ]);

        console.log("Result kind:", result.kind);
        console.log("Result count:", result.result.length);

        if (result.result.length === 0) {
            console.log("RESULT: NOT FOUND");
        } else {
            console.log("RESULT: FOUND");

            for (const entry of result.result) {
                console.log(
                    "Returned hash:",
                    entry.hash.toString("hex")
                );

                console.log(
                    "Data bytes:",
                    entry.data.length
                );
            }
        }
    } catch (e: any) {
        console.log(
            "ERROR:",
            e?.message ?? e
        );
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("========================================");
    console.log(" MISSING ROUTER LIBRARY CROSS-SERVER TEST");
    console.log("========================================");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.");

    for (const server of SERVERS) {
        await testServer(
            server.name,
            server.host,
            server.key
        );
    }

    console.log("\n========================================");
    console.log("TEST FINISHED");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION");
    console.log("========================================");
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQBsGx9ArADUrREB34W-ghgsCgBShvfUr4Jvlu-0KGc33Rbt'
    );

    console.log('===== OFFICIAL STON TESTNET ROUTER =====');
    console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());

    try {
        const state = await provider.provider(routerAddress).getState();

        console.log('State:', state.state.type);
        console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());

        if (state.state.type !== 'active') {
            console.log('Router is NOT ACTIVE');
            return;
        }

        const tests = [
            'get_router_version',
            'get_router_data',
            'get_router_id',
        ];

        for (const method of tests) {
            try {
                const result =
                    await provider.provider(routerAddress).get(method, []);

                console.log(`\n${method}: SUCCESS`);
                console.log(result.stack);
            } catch (error) {
                console.log(`\n${method}: FAILED`);

                const message =
                    error instanceof Error
                        ? error.message
                        : String(error);

                console.log(message);
            }
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('===== ROUTER INSPECTION FAILED =====');

        const message =
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error);

        console.log(message);
    }
}

import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

const servers = [
    {
        host: 'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842',
        key: 'sU7QavX2F964iI9oP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM='

    },

        key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek='

    },

    },

        key: 'Mf/JGvcWAvcrN3oheze8RF/ps6p7oL6ifrIzFmGQFQ8='
    }

export async function run() {

    console.log('===== OFFICIAL TESTNET LITESERVERS =====');

    for (const server of servers) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('SERVER:', server.host);

        const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: server.host,
            publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
            reconnectTimeout: 3000
        });

        let ready = false;

        for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
            if (engine.isReady()) {
                ready = true;
                break;
            }

            await sleep(500);
        }

        if (!ready) {
            console.log('ENGINE NOT READY');
            engine.close();
            continue;
        }

        console.log('ENGINE READY');

        try {
            const client = new LiteClient({ engine });

            const info = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

            console.log(
                'Masterchain seqno:',
                info.last.seqno
            );

            console.log('RESULT: OK');

        } catch (e) {

            console.log(
                'ERROR:',
                e instanceof Error
                    ? e.message
                    : String(e)
            );

        } finally {
            engine.close();
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== DONE =====');
}

FILE: scripts\testPoolCreationRouters.ts
====================================================================================================
import { StonApiClient } from '@ston-fi/api';
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('===== DIRECT V2.2 POOL ADDRESS TEST =====');

    const api = new StonApiClient({
        baseUrl: 'https://api.ston.fi',
    });

    const routers = await api.getRouters({
        dexV2: true,
    });

    const candidates = routers.filter((r: any) =>
        r.majorVersion === 2 &&
        r.minorVersion === 2 &&
        r.routerType === 'ConstantProduct' &&
        r.poolCreationEnabled === true
    );

    console.log('CANDIDATES:', candidates.length);

    const r = candidates[0];

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(r.address);
    const roosterMaster = Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);
    const ptonMaster = Address.parse(r.ptonMasterAddress);

    console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER master:', roosterMaster.toString());
    console.log('pTON master:', ptonMaster.toString());

    try {
        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 1. Get ROOSTER wallet belonging to Router
        // --------------------------------------------------
        const roosterWalletResult =
            await provider.provider(roosterMaster).get(
                'get_wallet_address',
                [
                    {
                        type: 'slice',
                        cell: beginAddressCell(routerAddress),
                    },
                ]
            );

        const roosterRouterWallet =
            roosterWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

        console.log(
            'ROOSTER Router Wallet:',
            roosterRouterWallet.toString()
        );

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 2. Get pTON wallet belonging to Router
        // --------------------------------------------------
        const ptonWalletResult =
            await provider.provider(ptonMaster).get(
                'get_wallet_address',
                [
                    {
                        type: 'slice',
                        cell: beginAddressCell(routerAddress),
                    },
                ]
            );

        const ptonRouterWallet =
            ptonWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

        console.log(
            'pTON Router Wallet:',
            ptonRouterWallet.toString()
        );

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 3. Call Router get_pool_address directly
        // --------------------------------------------------
        const poolResult =
            await provider.provider(routerAddress).get(
                'get_pool_address',
                [
                    {
                        type: 'slice',
                        cell: beginAddressCell(roosterRouterWallet),
                    },
                    {
                        type: 'slice',
                        cell: beginAddressCell(ptonRouterWallet),
                    },
                ]
            );

        const poolAddress =
            poolResult.stack.readAddress();

        console.log(
            'POOL:',
            poolAddress.toString()
        );

    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        );
    }
}

function beginAddressCell(address: Address) {
    const { beginCell } = require('@ton/core');

    return beginCell()
        .storeAddress(address)
        .endCell();
}

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const poolAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
    );

    const ownerAddress = Address.parse(
        '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
    );

    const roosterAmount = 100_000n * 1_000_000_000n;
    const ptonAmount = 1n * 1_000_000_000n;

    const lpAmount = 1_000_000_000n;

    const pool = provider.provider(poolAddress);

    console.log('===== V2.2 POOL GETTER TEST =====');
    console.log('Pool:', poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('Owner:', ownerAddress.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // get_pool_data
    // ------------------------------------------------
    try {
        const result = await pool.get('get_pool_data', []);

        console.log('\nget_pool_data: SUCCESS');
        console.log(result.stack);
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.log('\nget_pool_data: FAILED');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // get_lp_account_address
    // ------------------------------------------------
    try {
        const result = await pool.get('get_lp_account_address', [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(ownerAddress)
                    .endCell()
            }
        ]);

        console.log('\nget_lp_account_address: SUCCESS');
        console.log('LP Account:', result.stack.readAddress().toString());
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.log('\nget_lp_account_address: FAILED');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // get_expected_tokens
    // ------------------------------------------------
    try {
        const result = await pool.get('get_expected_tokens', [
            {
                type: 'int',
                value: roosterAmount
            },
            {
                type: 'int',
                value: ptonAmount
            }
        ]);

        console.log('\nget_expected_tokens: SUCCESS');
        console.log('Expected LP:', result.stack.readBigNumber().toString());
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.log('\nget_expected_tokens: FAILED');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // get_expected_liquidity
    // ------------------------------------------------
    try {
        const result = await pool.get('get_expected_liquidity', [
            {
                type: 'int',
                value: lpAmount
            }
        ]);

        console.log('\nget_expected_liquidity: SUCCESS');

        console.log(
            'Amount0:',
            result.stack.readBigNumber().toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Amount1:',
            result.stack.readBigNumber().toString()
        );
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.log('\nget_expected_liquidity: FAILED');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // get_expected_outputs
    // ------------------------------------------------
    try {
        const result = await pool.get('get_expected_outputs', [
            {
                type: 'int',
                value: roosterAmount
            },
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(ownerAddress)
                    .endCell()
            }
        ]);

        console.log('\nget_expected_outputs: SUCCESS');

        console.log(
            'Jetton to receive:',
            result.stack.readBigNumber().toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Protocol fee:',
            result.stack.readBigNumber().toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Ref fee:',
            result.stack.readBigNumber().toString()
        );
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.log('\nget_expected_outputs: FAILED');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
    }

    console.log('\n===== TEST FINISHED =====');
}

import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const pton = pTON.v1.create(PTON_MASTER);

    const ptonProvider = provider.provider(PTON_MASTER);

    console.log('\n===== pTON DEPLOY GAS TEST =====');

    console.log('pTON master:', PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('User:', USER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log(
        'SDK default:',
        Number(pton.gasConstants.deployWallet) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    const gasValues = [
        '1.05',
        '0.50',
        '0.30',
        '0.20',
        '0.10',
        '0.05',
    ];

    for (const gas of gasValues) {

        console.log('\n----------------------------------------');
        console.log('Testing gas:', gas, 'TON');

        try {

            const tx = await pton.getDeployWalletTxParams(
                ptonProvider,
                {
                    ownerAddress: USER_WALLET,
                    gasAmount: toNano(gas),
                }
            );

            console.log(
                'TX value:',
                Number(tx.value) / 1e9,
                'TON'
            );

            if (tx.body) {
                console.log(
                    'BOC:',
                    tx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
                );
            }

        } catch (error) {

            console.log('ERROR:', error);

        }
    }

    console.log('\n===== END: NO TRANSACTION SENT =====\n');
}

FILE: scripts\testRouterGetters.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';
import { Functions } from 'ton-lite-client/dist/schema';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

// get_router_data
const GET_ROUTER_DATA = '128085';

// Common TON getter IDs to test.
// These are diagnostic only.
const TEST_METHODS = [
    {
        name: 'get_router_data',
        id: GET_ROUTER_DATA,
    },
];

async function main() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER GETTER EXECUTION TEST');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================');

    const engines = [
        new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
            publicKey: Buffer.from(
                'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
                'base64'
            ),
        }),

        new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
            publicKey: Buffer.from(
                'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
                'base64'
            ),
        }),
    ];

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine: new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines),
    });

    const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

    console.log('\nMASTERCHAIN');
    console.log('seqno:', master.last.seqno);

    const baseRequest = {
        kind: 'liteServer.runSmcMethod' as const,

        id: {
            kind: 'tonNode.blockIdExt' as const,
            seqno: master.last.seqno,
            shard: master.last.shard,
            workchain: master.last.workchain,
            fileHash: master.last.fileHash,
            rootHash: master.last.rootHash,
        },

        account: {
            kind: 'liteServer.accountId' as const,
            workchain: ROUTER.workChain,
            id: Buffer.from(ROUTER.hash),
        },

        params: Buffer.alloc(0),
    };

    for (const method of TEST_METHODS) {
        console.log('\n----------------------------------------');
        console.log('METHOD:', method.name);
        console.log('METHOD ID:', method.id);
        console.log('----------------------------------------');

        const request = {
            ...baseRequest,
            mode: 4,
            methodId: method.id,
        };

        const result = await client.engine.query(
            Functions.liteServer_runSmcMethod,
            request
        );

        console.log('exit code:', result.exitCode);

        console.log(
            'result bytes:',
            result.result ? result.result.length : 0
        );

        if (result.result) {
            const boc = Buffer.from(result.result);

            console.log(
                'result BOC:',
                boc.toString('base64')
            );

            try {
                const cells = Cell.fromBoc(boc);

                console.log(
                    'decoded root count:',
                    cells.length
                );

                if (cells.length > 0) {
                    console.log(
                        'root bits:',
                        cells[0].bits.length
                    );

                    console.log(
                        'root refs:',
                        cells[0].refs.length
                    );
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.log(
                    'result BOC decode error:',
                    e instanceof Error ? e.message : e
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('\n========================================');
    console.log('END');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('========================================');
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('\nFATAL ERROR');
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
});
