FILE: scripts\testRouterLibExtras.ts
====================================================================================================
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import {
    Address,
} from '@ton/core';

import {
    Functions,
} from 'ton-lite-client/dist/schema';

import {
    crc16,
} from 'ton-lite-client/dist/utils/crc16';

async function main() {

    const servers = [
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const ROUTER =
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval';

    const METHOD =
        'get_router_data';

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER LIB EXTRAS TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO WALLET');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        const host =
            `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`;

        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('Server');
        console.log('Host:', host);

        const engine =
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(
                    server.key,
                    'base64'
                ),
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            });

        engines.push(engine);
    }

    const engine =
        new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client =
        new LiteClient({
            engine,
            batchSize: 1,
        });

    try {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Getting masterchain info...');

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            'Masterchain seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        const router =
            Address.parse(ROUTER);

        const methodId =
            ((crc16(METHOD) & 0xffff) | 0x10000);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Method:', METHOD);
        console.log(
            'Method ID:',
            methodId
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Running direct liteServer.runSmcMethod...');
        console.log('Mode: 20');
        console.log('4  = result');
        console.log('16 = libExtras');
        console.log('');

        const request = {
            kind: 'liteServer.runSmcMethod' as const,

            mode: 20,

            id: {
                kind: 'tonNode.blockIdExt' as const,
                seqno: master.last.seqno,
                shard: master.last.shard,
                workchain: master.last.workchain,
                rootHash: master.last.rootHash,
                fileHash: master.last.fileHash,
            },

            account: {
                kind: 'liteServer.accountId' as const,
                workchain: router.workChain,
                id: router.hash,
            },

            methodId:
                methodId.toString(),

            params:
                Buffer.alloc(0),
        };

        const result =
            await client.engine.query(
                Functions.liteServer_runSmcMethod,
                request
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(' RAW RUN SMC METHOD RESULT');
        console.log('========================================');

        console.log('');
        console.log('Exit code:');
        console.log(result.exitCode);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Mode returned:');
        console.log(result.mode);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Result bytes:');
        console.log(
            result.result
                ? result.result.length
                : null
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Result BOC:');
        console.log(
            result.result
                ? result.result.toString('base64')
                : null
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('libExtras bytes:');
        console.log(
            result.libExtras
                ? result.libExtras.length
                : null
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('libExtras BOC:');
        console.log(
            result.libExtras
                ? result.libExtras.toString('base64')
                : null
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(' TEST FINISHED');
        console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
        console.log('========================================');

    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('ERROR');
        console.error(error);

    } finally {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Closing engines...');

        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }

        console.log('Done.');
    }

    process.exit(0);
}

function intToIP(int: number) {

    const part1 =
        int & 255;

    const part2 =
        (int >> 8) & 255;

    const part3 =
        (int >> 16) & 255;

    const part4 =
        (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch((error) => {

    console.error('');
    console.error('FATAL ERROR');
    console.error(error);

    process.exit(1);
});

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    const roosterWallet = Address.parse(
        'EQAmO8BvKx4ta9jkCCDx197VqrUrsaC42Dv6yhDAyKzFpi2S'
    );

    const ptonWallet = Address.parse(
        'EQBbJjnahBMGbMUJwhAXLn8BiigcGXMJhSC0l7DBhdYABqox'
    );

    console.log('===== EXACT STON v2.1 POOL GETTER =====');
    console.log('Router:', router.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('Token0 / ROOSTER Router Wallet:');
    console.log(roosterWallet.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('Token1 / pTON Router Wallet:');
    console.log(ptonWallet.toString());
    console.log('');

    const token0Cell = beginCell()
        .storeAddress(roosterWallet)
        .endCell();

    const token1Cell = beginCell()
        .storeAddress(ptonWallet)
        .endCell();

    console.log('Token0 slice BOC:');
    console.log(token0Cell.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    console.log('');

    console.log('Token1 slice BOC:');
    console.log(token1Cell.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    console.log('');

    try {
        const result = await provider.provider(router).get(
            'get_pool_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: token0Cell,
                },
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: token1Cell,
                },
            ]
        );

        console.log('===== GETTER SUCCESS =====');
        console.log('Stack:', result.stack);

        try {
            console.log('Pool Address:', result.stack.readAddress().toString());
        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                'Could not read address:',
                e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
            );
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('===== GETTER ERROR =====');
        console.log(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
    }
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import { Address } from '@ton/core';

async function main() {

    const servers = [
        {
            name: 'Server 1',
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            name: 'Server 2',
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const ROUTER =
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval';

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER RUN METHOD TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO WALLET');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        const host =
            `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`;

        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log(server.name);
        console.log('Host:', host);

        const engine =
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(
                    server.key,
                    'base64'
                ),
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            });

        engines.push(engine);
    }

    const engine =
        new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client =
        new LiteClient({
            engine,
            batchSize: 1,
        });

    try {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Getting masterchain info...');

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('');
        console.log('SUCCESS');
        console.log(
            'Masterchain seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        console.log(
            'Root hash:',
            Buffer.from(
                master.last.rootHash
            ).toString('hex')
        );

        const router =
            Address.parse(ROUTER);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Router:');
        console.log(router.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('Running get_router_data...');

        const params =
            Buffer.alloc(0);

        const result =
            await client.runMethod(
                router,
                'get_router_data',
                params,
                master.last
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(' RUN METHOD RESULT');
        console.log('========================================');

        console.log('');
        console.log('Exit code:');
        console.log(result.exitCode);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Result:');
        console.log(result.result);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Block:');
        console.dir(
            result.block,
            { depth: 5 }
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Shard block:');
        console.dir(
            result.shardBlock,
            { depth: 5 }
        );

    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ERROR');
        console.error(error);

    } finally {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Closing engines...');

        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }

        console.log('Done.');
    }

    process.exit(0);
}

function intToIP(int: number) {

    const part1 =
        int & 255;

    const part2 =
        (int >> 8) & 255;

    const part3 =
        (int >> 16) & 255;

    const part4 =
        (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    const pton = Address.parse(
        'EQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT1h7'
    );

    console.log('===== ROUTER vs JETTON GETTER TEST =====');
    console.log('Router:', router.toString());
    console.log('pTON:', pton.toString());

    // -----------------------------
    // TEST 1: Router getter
    // -----------------------------
    console.log('\n[1] Router get_router_version');

    try {
        const r = await provider.provider(router).get(
            'get_router_version',
            []
        );

        console.log('SUCCESS');
        console.log('Stack:', r.stack);
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('FAILED');
        console.log(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
    }

    // -----------------------------
    // TEST 2: pTON getter
    // -----------------------------
    console.log('\n[2] pTON get_wallet_address');

    try {
        const r = await provider.provider(pton).get(
            'get_wallet_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: provider
                        .provider(pton)
                        ? undefined as never
                        : undefined as never
                }
            ]
        );

        console.log('SUCCESS');
        console.log('Stack:', r.stack);
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('FAILED');
        console.log(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
    }
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const { CPIRouterV2_2 } = require('@ston-fi/sdk/dex/v2_2');

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const KNOWN_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 SDK GETPOOL TEST');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('\nRouter:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('\nToken0 ROOSTER wallet:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('\nToken1 pTON wallet:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());

    const routerProvider = provider.provider(ROUTER);

    const router = CPIRouterV2_2.create(ROUTER);

    const pool = await router.getPool(routerProvider, {
    token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
    token1: PTON_MASTER,
});

    const poolAddress = pool.address;

    console.log('\n===== SDK RESULT =====');
    console.log('SDK Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());

    console.log('\nKnown Pool:');
    console.log(KNOWN_POOL.toString());

    console.log(
        '\nPool address MATCH:',
        poolAddress.equals(KNOWN_POOL) ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    const poolProvider = provider.provider(poolAddress);

    console.log('\n===== POOL DATA =====');

    const poolData = await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    console.log('isLocked:', poolData.isLocked);
    console.log('routerAddress:', poolData.routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('totalSupplyLP:', poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString());
    console.log('reserve0:', poolData.reserve0.toString());
    console.log('reserve1:', poolData.reserve1.toString());

    console.log(
        'token0WalletAddress:',
        poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'token1WalletAddress:',
        poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log('lpFee:', poolData.lpFee.toString());
    console.log('protocolFee:', poolData.protocolFee.toString());

    console.log('\n==============================================');
    console.log(' END - READ-ONLY');
    console.log('==============================================');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 SDK GET POOL TEST');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router:        ', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER Master:', ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('pTON Master:   ', PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('Expected Pool: ', EXPECTED_POOL.toString());

    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Calling SDK router.getPool(params)...');

    const pool = await router.getPool({
        token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
        token1: PTON_MASTER,
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('SDK-derived Pool address:');
    console.log(pool.address.toString());

    const matches = pool.address.equals(EXPECTED_POOL);

    console.log('');
    console.log('MATCH EXPECTED:', matches);

    if (!matches) {
        throw new Error(
            `SDK returned unexpected pool: ${pool.address.toString()}`
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('SUCCESS: SDK Pool address matches the real on-chain Pool.');
    console.log('READ-ONLY TEST COMPLETE.');
}

    console.log('ROOSTER:', ROOSTER);

    const api = new StonApiClient({
        baseUrl: 'https://api.ston.fi',
    });

    const routers = await api.getRouters({
        dexV2: true,
    });

    const candidates = routers.filter((r: any) =>
        r.majorVersion === 2 &&
        r.minorVersion === 2 &&
        r.routerType === 'ConstantProduct' &&
        r.poolCreationEnabled === true
    );

    console.log('CANDIDATES:', candidates.length);

    for (const r of candidates) {
        console.log('\n----------------------------------------');

        console.log('pTON MASTER:', r.ptonMasterAddress);
        console.log('pTON WALLET:', r.ptonWalletAddress);

            const router = dex.Router.create(r.address);

            console.log('SDK Router:', router.constructor.name);
            console.log('SDK Pool:', dex.Pool.name);
            console.log('SDK pTON:', dex.pTON.name);

            const contractProvider = tonClient.provider(
                router.address,

                    type: 'TonClient4',

            );

}

FILE: scripts\trace113Arg0Origins.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = [
  0x22838e, // 6464
  0x22c948, // 6611
  0x22c9ef, // 6611
  0x22cb34, // 6613
  0x22cc55, // 6615
  0x22cc5e, // 6615
  0x22d083, // 6618
  0x25093d, // 7467
  0x2562b8, // 7579
];

function readU32(p: number) {
  return wasm.readUInt32LE(p);
}

function findCallers(): Map<number, number[]> {
  const m = new Map<number, number[]>();

  for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 1; p++) {
    if (wasm[p] === 0x10) {
      let x = p + 1, v = 0, sh = 0;
      while (x < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[x++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << sh;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        sh += 7;
      }
      if (v === 113) {
        if (!m.has(p)) m.set(p, []);
      }
    }
  }
  return m;
}

// Locate function bodies from the code section.
// Simple scanner based on known function bodies is enough for local context.
function findFuncContaining(off: number): number {
  // Function body ranges are extracted from code section.
  // We use the standard wasm code section parser.
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    let size = 0, sh = 0;
    while (p < wasm.length) {
      const b = wasm[p++];
      size |= (b & 0x7f) << sh;
      if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
      sh += 7;
    }

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (id === 10 && off >= start && off < end) {
      return start;
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return -1;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL113 ARG0 ORIGIN SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of targets) {
  const base = target - 32;

  console.log(`\nTARGET CALL113 @ ${hex(target)}`);
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  for (let p = base; p <= target; p++) {
    const b = wasm[p];

    if (b === 0x20) {
      const local = wasm[p + 1];
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  local.get ${local}`);
    }
    else if (b === 0x21) {
      const local = wasm[p + 1];
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  local.set ${local}`);
    }
    else if (b === 0x22) {
      const local = wasm[p + 1];
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  local.tee ${local}`);
    }
    else if (b === 0x41) {
      let x = p + 1, v = 0, sh = 0;
      while (x < wasm.length) {
        const q = wasm[x++];
        v |= (q & 0x7f) << sh;
        if (!(q & 0x80)) break;
        sh += 7;
      }
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i32.const ${v} (${hex(v)})`);
    }
    else if (b === 0x28) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i32.load`);
    }
    else if (b === 0x6a) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i32.add`);
    }
    else if (b === 0x10 && wasm[p + 1] === 113) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  CALL 113`);
    }
  }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace113Arg0Producer.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGETS = [
  [6464, 0x22838e],
  [6611, 0x22c948],
  [6611, 0x22c9ef],
  [6613, 0x22cb34],
  [6615, 0x22cc55],
  [6615, 0x22cc5e],
  [6618, 0x22d083],
  [7467, 0x25093d],
  [7579, 0x2562b8],
] as const;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    n++;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }
  return { v: v >>> 0, n };
}

function i32(pos: number) {
  return uleb(pos);
}

function opcodeLen(pos: number): number {
  const op = wasm[pos];

  // locals
  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) return 1 + uleb(pos + 1).n;

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) return 1 + uleb(pos + 1).n;

  // i32 load/store family
  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const a = uleb(pos + 1);
    const b = uleb(pos + 1 + a.n);
    return 1 + a.n + b.n;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) return 1 + uleb(pos + 1).n;

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const a = uleb(pos + 1);
    return 1 + a.n + 1;
  }

  // br / br_if
  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d)
    return 1 + uleb(pos + 1).n;

  // block / loop / if
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04)
    return 2;

  // numeric unary/binary
  if (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0xbf) return 1;

  return 1;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL113 ARG0 PRODUCER TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const [func, callPos] of TARGETS) {
  console.log(`\nFUNC ${func} @ 0x${callPos.toString(16)}`);
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  const start = Math.max(0, callPos - 80);
  let p = start;

  while (p < callPos) {
    const op = wasm[p];

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16)} local.get ${x.v}`
      );
      p += 1 + x.n;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16)} i32.const ${x.v} (0x${x.v.toString(16)})`
      );
      p += 1 + x.n;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16)} local.tee ${x.v}`
      );
      p += 1 + x.n;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x2d || op === 0x2f) {
      const a = uleb(p + 1);
      const b = uleb(p + 1 + a.n);
      console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16)} LOAD op=0x${op.toString(16)} offset=${b.v}`
      );
      p += 1 + a.n + b.n;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      console.log(`0x${p.toString(16)} i32.add`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
      console.log(`0x${p.toString(16)} i32.sub`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6c) {
      console.log(`0x${p.toString(16)} i32.mul`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(`0x${p.toString(16)} call ${x.v}`);
      p += 1 + x.n;
      continue;
    }

    p += opcodeLen(p);
  }

  console.log(`0x${callPos.toString(16)} CALL 113`);
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace113Args.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos, x = 0;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);
  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function readString(pos: number) {
  const n = uleb(pos);
  return { next: n.next + n.value };
}

type Type = { params: number[]; results: number[] };

const types: Type[] = [];
const definedTypes: number[] = [];
let importedFuncs = 0;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 1) {
    let q = start;
    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      if (b[q++] !== 0x60) throw new Error("bad functype");

      const np = uleb(q); q = np.next;
      const params: number[] = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < np.value; j++)
        params.push(b[q++]);

      const nr = uleb(q); q = nr.next;
      const results: number[] = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < nr.value; j++)
        results.push(b[q++]);

      types.push({ params, results });
    }
  }

  if (id === 2) {
    let q = start;
    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const a = readString(q); q = a.next;
      const c = readString(q); q = c.next;

      const kind = b[q++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        const t = uleb(q);
        q = t.next;
        importedFuncs++;
      } else if (kind === 1) {
        q++;
        const fl = uleb(q); q = fl.next;
        const mn = uleb(q); q = mn.next;
        if (fl.value & 1) q = uleb(q).next;
      } else if (kind === 2) {
        q = uleb(q).next;
      } else if (kind === 3) {
        q += 2;
      }
    }
  }

  if (id === 3) {
    let q = start;
    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const t = uleb(q);
      definedTypes.push(t.value);
      q = t.next;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

function funcType(fn: number): Type | null {
  const d = fn - importedFuncs;
  if (d < 0 || d >= definedTypes.length) return null;
  return types[definedTypes[d]];
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
type FuncBody = {
  fn: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  codeStart: number;
  codeEnd: number;
  locals: number[];
};

const funcs: FuncBody[] = [];

p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    let q = start;

    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const bodySize = uleb(q);
      const bodyStart = bodySize.next;
      const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

      let z = bodyStart;

      const localDecls = uleb(z);
      z = localDecls.next;

      const locals: number[] = [];

      for (let k = 0; k < localDecls.value; k++) {
        const cnt = uleb(z);
        z = cnt.next;

        const type = b[z++];

        for (let j = 0; j < cnt.value; j++)
          locals.push(type);
      }

      funcs.push({
        fn: importedFuncs + i,
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd,
        codeStart: z,
        codeEnd: bodyEnd - 1,
        locals
      });

      q = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// helpers
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function fmtConst(v: number) {
  if (v >= 0 && v < 100000)
    return String(v);

  return `${v} (0x${(v >>> 0).toString(16)})`;
}

function pop(stack: string[]): string {
  return stack.length ? stack.pop()! : "<EMPTY>";
}

function resultCount(fn: number): number {
  const t = funcType(fn);
  return t ? t.results.length : 0;
}

function argCount(fn: number): number {
  const t = funcType(fn);
  return t ? t.params.length : 0;
}

function decodeBody(f: FuncBody) {
  const t = funcType(f.fn);
  if (!t) return;

  const localCount = t.params.length + f.locals.length;

  const stack: string[] = [];
  const out: {
    off: number;
    text: string;
    before: string[];
    after: string[];
  }[] = [];

  let q = f.codeStart;

  while (q < f.codeEnd) {
    const off = q;
    const op = b[q++];

    const before = [...stack];

    try {
      if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(q); q = x.next;
        stack.push(`L${x.value}`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(q); q = x.next;
        pop(stack);
      }

      else if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(q); q = x.next;
        const v = pop(stack);
        stack.push(v);
        // tee leaves value on stack
      }

      else if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = sleb(q); q = x.next;
        stack.push(fmtConst(x.value));
      }

      else if (op === 0x28) {
        const al = uleb(q); q = al.next;
        const of = uleb(q); q = of.next;
        const base = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`MEM[${base}+${of.value}]`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x29) {
        const al = uleb(q); q = al.next;
        const of = uleb(q); q = of.next;
        const base = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`MEM64[${base}+${of.value}]`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x36) {
        const al = uleb(q); q = al.next;
        const of = uleb(q); q = of.next;
        const value = pop(stack);
        const base = pop(stack);
      }

      else if (op === 0x37) {
        const al = uleb(q); q = al.next;
        const of = uleb(q); q = of.next;
        const value = pop(stack);
        const base = pop(stack);
      }

      else if (op === 0x6a) {
        const b2 = pop(stack);
        const a2 = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`(${a2}+${b2})`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x6b) {
        const b2 = pop(stack);
        const a2 = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`(${a2}-${b2})`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x6c) {
        const b2 = pop(stack);
        const a2 = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`(${a2}*${b2})`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x45) {
        const a2 = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`(${a2}==0)`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x46) {
        const bb = pop(stack);
        const aa = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`(${aa}==${bb})`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x47) {
        const bb = pop(stack);
        const aa = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`(${aa}<u${bb})`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x84) {
        const bb = pop(stack);
        const aa = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`(${aa}*${bb})`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x50) {
        const aa = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`i64(${aa})`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(q); q = x.next;
        const fn = x.value;

        const n = argCount(fn);
        const args: string[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
          args.unshift(pop(stack));

        const rc = resultCount(fn);

        if (fn === 113) {
          out.push({
            off,
            text: `CALL113(${args.join(", ")})`,
            before,
            after: [...stack]
          });
        }

        if (rc === 1)
          stack.push(`F${fn}(${args.join(",")})`);
        else if (rc > 1)
          for (let i = 0; i < rc; i++)
            stack.push(`F${fn}[${i}]`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x11) {
        const ti = uleb(q); q = ti.next;
        const table = uleb(q); q = table.next;

        const tinfo = types[ti.value];
        const n = tinfo ? tinfo.params.length : 0;

        const args: string[] = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
          args.unshift(pop(stack));

        const selector = pop(stack);

        if (tinfo?.results.length === 1)
          stack.push(`INDIRECT[${selector}](${args.join(",")})`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x1a) {
        pop(stack);
      }

      else if (op === 0x1b) {
        const c = pop(stack);
        const bb = pop(stack);
        const aa = pop(stack);
        stack.push(`select(${aa},${bb},${c})`);
      }

      else if (op === 0x0b) {
        break;
      }

      else if (op === 0x0d) {
        q = uleb(q).next;
      }

      else if (op === 0x0c) {
        q = uleb(q).next;
      }

      else if (op === 0x0e) {
        const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;
        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
          q = uleb(q).next;
        q = uleb(q).next;
      }

      else if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        q = uleb(q).next;
      }

      else if (op === 0x05) {
      }

      else if (op === 0x0f) {
        break;
      }

      else {
        // For unknown instructions, stop symbolic tracing.
        break;
      }
    } catch {
      break;
    }

    const after = [...stack];

    if (off >= f.codeStart && off < f.codeEnd) {
      out.push({
        off,
        text: `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`,
        before,
        after
      });
    }
  }

  return out;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// target functions
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const targets = new Set([6464, 6611, 6613, 6615, 6618, 7467, 7579]);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 113 ARGUMENT SYMBOLIC TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const f of funcs) {

  const events = decodeBody(f);

  for (const e of events) {
    if (!e.text.startsWith("CALL113")) continue;

    console.log("");

    console.log(`  ${e.text}`);

    console.log("  STACK BEFORE CALL:");
    for (const x of e.before)
      console.log(`    ${x}`);

    console.log("  NOTE:");
    console.log("    ARG0 = second-from-top before CALL113");
    console.log("    ARG1 = top before CALL113");
  }
}

FILE: scripts\trace118To122To87.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [118, 122, 87];

function readU32Leb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function loadBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  let codeStart = -1;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const s = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      codeStart = p;
      break;
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error("Code section not found");

  let q = codeStart;
  const count = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
  q = count.next;

  const bodies = new Map<number, Buffer>();

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const s = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
    const size = s.value;
    q = s.next;

    bodies.set(i, wasm.subarray(q, q + size));
    q += size;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = loadBodies();

for (const func of TARGETS) {
  const body = bodies.get(func);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}: NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log(`BODY SIZE : ${body.length}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
    if (body[i] !== 0x10) continue;

    const r = readU32Leb(body, i + 1);

    console.log(
      `CALL @ 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(4, "0")} -> FUNC ${r.value}`
    );

    i = r.next - 1;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 98;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0;
  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    n++;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };
    s += 7;
  }
  return null;
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    n++;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v | 0, len: n };
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/* locate code section */
let p = 8;
let funcs = [];

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  if (!sz) break;

  const start = p + sz.len;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    let q = start;
    const count = uleb(q);
    q += count.len;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
      const z = uleb(q);
      q += z.len;

      funcs.push({
        id: i,
        start: q,
        end: q + z.value
      });

      q += z.value;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

const f = funcs[TARGET];

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 119 TARGET TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`BODY : ${hx(f.start)} .. ${hx(f.end)}`);
console.log(`SIZE : ${f.end - f.start}`);
console.log("");

for (let q = f.start; q < f.end; q++) {

  const op = b[q];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(q + 1);
    if (r)
      console.log(`${hx(q)} i32.const ${r.value} (${hx(r.value)})`);

  } else if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = uleb(q + 1);
    if (r)
      console.log(`${hx(q)} local.get ${r.value}`);

  } else if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = uleb(q + 1);
    if (r)
      console.log(`${hx(q)} local.set ${r.value}`);

  } else if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = uleb(q + 1);
    if (r)
      console.log(`${hx(q)} local.tee ${r.value}`);

  } else if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = uleb(q + 1);
    if (a) {
      const o = uleb(q + 1 + a.len);
      if (o)
        console.log(`${hx(q)} i32.load offset=${o.value}`);
    }

  } else if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = uleb(q + 1);
    if (a) {
      const o = uleb(q + 1 + a.len);
      if (o)
        console.log(`${hx(q)} i32.store offset=${o.value}`);
    }

  } else if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(q + 1);
    if (r)
      console.log(`${hx(q)} call ${r.value}`);

  } else if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = uleb(q + 1);
    if (t)
      console.log(`${hx(q)} call_indirect type=${t.value}`);

  } else if (op === 0x6a) {
    console.log(`${hx(q)} i32.add`);

  } else if (op === 0x6b) {
    console.log(`${hx(q)} i32.sub`);
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGETS = new Set([122, 2057]);

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, end: pos };

    s += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findBodies() {

  let p = 8;
  const bodies = [];

  while (p < b.length) {

    const id = b[p++];

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;

    const end = p + r.value;

    if (id === 10) {

      const c = uleb(p);
      let q = c.end;

      for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {

        const z = uleb(q);
        q = z.end;

        bodies.push({
          start: q,
          end: q + z.value
        });

        q += z.value;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = findBodies();

for (let i = 0; i < bodies.length; i++) {

  const body = bodies[i];

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    if (b[p] !== 0x10)
      continue;

    const r = uleb(p + 1);

    if (!TARGETS.has(r.value))
      continue;

    console.log("");

    const to   = Math.min(body.end, p + 48);

    console.log("CONTEXT:");

    for (let q = from; q < to; q++) {

      console.log(
        `${hex(q)}  ${b[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }
  }
}

FILE: scripts\trace122ArgFlow.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 122;
const CALL_OPCODE = 0x10;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  let imported = 0;
  const bodies: { start: number; end: number }[] = [];

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const section = wasm[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    const start = s.next;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (section === 2) {
      let q = start;
      const n = uleb(q);
      q = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const ml = uleb(q);
        q = ml.next + ml.value;

        const nl = uleb(q);
        q = nl.next + nl.value;

        const kind = wasm[q++];

        if (kind === 0) {
          imported++;
          const t = uleb(q);
          q = t.next;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
          q++;
          const lim = wasm[q++];
          const mn = uleb(q);
          q = mn.next;
          if (lim & 1) {
            const mx = uleb(q);
            q = mx.next;
          }
        } else if (kind === 2) {
          const lim = wasm[q++];
          const mn = uleb(q);
          q = mn.next;
          if (lim & 1) {
            const mx = uleb(q);
            q = mx.next;
          }
        } else if (kind === 3) {
          q += 2;
        }
      }
    }

    if (section === 10) {
      let q = start;
      const n = uleb(q);
      q = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const bs = uleb(q);
        const bodyStart = bs.next;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bs.value;

        bodies.push({
          start: bodyStart,
          end: bodyEnd,
        });

        q = bodyEnd;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return { imported, bodies };
}

const { imported, bodies } = findBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("REAL FUNC 122 — CALLER ARGUMENT FLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS : ${imported}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS  : ${bodies.length}`);
console.log(`TARGET FUNC        : ${TARGET}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodies.length; bodyIndex++) {

  const { start, end } = bodies[bodyIndex];
  const funcIndex = imported + bodyIndex;

  for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {

    if (wasm[p] !== CALL_OPCODE)
      continue;

    const r = uleb(p + 1);

    if (r.value !== TARGET)
      continue;

    hits++;

    const from = Math.max(start, p - 48);
    const to = Math.min(end, r.next + 32);

    console.log("");
    console.log("############################################################");
    console.log(`CALLER FUNC : ${funcIndex}`);
    console.log(`BODY INDEX  : ${bodyIndex}`);
    console.log(`CALL @      : 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let q = from; q < to; q++) {

      const b = wasm[q];

      let name = `0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      let extra = "";

      if (b === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(q + 1);
        name = "call";
        extra = ` ${x.value}`;
      }
      else if (b === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(q + 1);
        name = "local.get";
        extra = ` ${x.value}`;
      }
      else if (b === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(q + 1);
        name = "local.set";
        extra = ` ${x.value}`;
      }
      else if (b === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(q + 1);
        name = "local.tee";
        extra = ` ${x.value}`;
      }
      else if (b === 0x41) {
        const x = uleb(q + 1);
        name = "i32.const";
        extra = ` ${x.value}`;
      }
      else if (b === 0x28) {
        const a = uleb(q + 1);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        name = "i32.load";
        extra = ` align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      }
      else if (b === 0x6a) {
        name = "i32.add";
      }
      else if (b === 0x6b) {
        name = "i32.sub";
      }
      else if (b === 0x45) {
        name = "i32.eqz";
      }
      else if (b === 0x46) {
        name = "i32.eq";
      }
      else if (b === 0x47) {
        name = "i32.ne";
      }
      else if (b === 0x72) {
        name = "i32.or";
      }

      console.log(
        `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${name}${extra}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL CALLS TO FUNC 122 : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 122;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0, shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  let imported = 0;
  const bodies: { start: number; end: number }[] = [];

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const section = wasm[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    const start = s.next;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (section === 2) {
      let q = start;
      const n = uleb(q);
      q = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const ml = uleb(q);
        q = ml.next + ml.value;

        const nl = uleb(q);
        q = nl.next + nl.value;

        const kind = wasm[q++];

        if (kind === 0) {
          imported++;
          const t = uleb(q);
          q = t.next;
        } else {
          // Skip remaining import descriptor.
          if (kind === 1) {
            q++;
            const flags = uleb(q);
            q = flags.next;
            if (flags.value & 1) {
              const x = uleb(q);
              q = x.next;
            }
            const y = uleb(q);
            q = y.next;
          } else if (kind === 2) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            const y = uleb(q);
            q = y.next;
          } else if (kind === 3) {
            q += 2;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (section === 10) {
      let q = start;
      const n = uleb(q);
      q = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const bs = uleb(q);
        const bodyStart = bs.next;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bs.value;

        bodies.push({ start: bodyStart, end: bodyEnd });
        q = bodyEnd;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return { imported, bodies };
}

const { imported, bodies } = findBodies();

function decodeAt(p: number) {
  const op = wasm[p];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    return `i32.const ${x.value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    return `local.get ${x.value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    return `local.set ${x.value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    return `local.tee ${x.value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    return `call ${x.value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x1a) return "drop";
  if (op === 0x46) return "i32.eq";
  if (op === 0x45) return "i32.eqz";
  if (op === 0x6a) return "i32.add";
  if (op === 0x6b) return "i32.sub";
  if (op === 0x71) return "i32.and";
  if (op === 0x72) return "i32.or";
  if (op === 0x73) return "i32.xor";

  return `OP 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 — EXACT STACK PREDECESSOR TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let bi = 0; bi < bodies.length; bi++) {
  const { start, end } = bodies[bi];
  const func = imported + bi;

  for (let p = start; p < end - 1; p++) {

    if (wasm[p] !== 0x10) continue;

    const c = uleb(p + 1);
    if (c.value !== TARGET) continue;

    hits++;

    console.log("");
    console.log(`############################################################`);
    console.log(`FUNC ${func} @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);

    // Show raw instruction neighborhood.
    let q = Math.max(start, p - 24);

    while (q < p) {
      const op = wasm[q];

      let next = q + 1;

      if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(q + 1);
        next = x.next;
      } else if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(q + 1);
        next = x.next;
      }

      console.log(
        `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${decodeAt(q)}`
      );

      q = next;
    }

    console.log(
      `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  call 122`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL CALLS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

function leb(pos: number) {

    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8, code=0;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;
  if(id===10){ code=p; break; }
  p+=n;
}

p=code;
const [count,q0]=u(p); p=q0;

for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const [size,q]=u(p); p=q;

  const func=83+i;
  const start=p, end=p+size;

  for(let x=start;x<end;x++){

    if(b[x]!==0x10) continue;

    const [target,qx]=u(x+1);
    if(target!==122) continue;

    console.log(`\nFUNC ${func} CALL122 @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`);

    // Show preceding instructions, enough to identify stack arguments.
    console.log(
      [...b.subarray(
        Math.max(start,x-100),
        Math.min(end,x+12)
      )]
      .map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0"))
      .join(" ")
    );

    console.log("--------------------------------------------");
  }

  p=end;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace122ConstFamilies.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = [
  { a: 141606, b: [309, 310, 143, 202, 168] },
  { a: 144293, b: [545, 336, 725, 138] },
  { a: 149912, b: [687, 328, 329, 1178] },
  { a: 149932, b: [424, 193] },
];

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = wasm[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readConst(pos: number) {
  if (wasm[pos] !== 0x41) return null;
  return uleb(pos + 1);
}

function hex(p: number) {
  return "0x" + p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 — CONST FAMILY TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of targets) {
  console.log("");
  console.log(`############ CONST1 ${t.a} = 0x${t.a.toString(16)} ############`);

  let shown = 0;

  for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 1; p++) {
    const c1 = readConst(p);
    if (!c1 || c1.value !== t.a) continue;

    // Search next ~20 bytes for CONST2 followed by CALL 122 (0x7a)
    for (let q = c1.next; q < Math.min(wasm.length, c1.next + 30); q++) {
      const c2 = readConst(q);
      if (!c2 || !t.b.includes(c2.value)) continue;

      let call122 = false;

      for (let r = c2.next; r < Math.min(wasm.length, c2.next + 12); r++) {
        if (wasm[r] === 0x10) {
          const f = uleb(r + 1);
          if (f.value === 122) {
            call122 = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      if (!call122) continue;

      console.log("");
      console.log(`MATCH @ ${hex(p)}`);
      console.log(`  CONST1 : ${t.a}`);
      console.log(`  CONST2 : ${c2.value}`);
      console.log(`  CALL122: YES`);

      const start = Math.max(0, p - 18);
      const end = Math.min(wasm.length, c2.next + 8);

      let line = "";
      for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
        line += wasm[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
      }

      console.log(`  BYTES  : ${line.trim()}`);

      shown++;
      break;
    }

    if (shown >= 20) break;
  }

  console.log(`  MATCHES SHOWN : ${shown}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace122ControlFlow.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function bodyOf(target) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const [size,q] = u(p);
    p = q;

    const end = p + size;

    if (id === 10) {
      const [count,q0] = u(p);
      p = q0;

      for (let i=0;i<count;i++) {
        const [bs,q1] = u(p);
        p = q1;

        const s = p;
        const e = p + bs;

        if (83+i === target)
          return {start:s,end:e};

        p=e;
      }
    }

    p=end;
  }

  return null;
}

function read(p) {

  const op=b[p];

  if(op===0x20){
    const [x,q]=u(p+1);
    return [`local.get L${x}`,q,"value"];
  }

  if(op===0x21){
    const [x,q]=u(p+1);
    return [`local.set L${x}`,q,"other"];
  }

  if(op===0x22){
    const [x,q]=u(p+1);
    return [`local.tee L${x}`,q,"value"];
  }

  if(op===0x41){
    const [x,q]=u(p+1);
    return [`i32.const ${x} (0x${x.toString(16)})`,q,"value"];
  }

  if(op===0x28){
    const [a,q1]=u(p+1);
    const [o,q2]=u(q1);
    return [`i32.load align=${a} offset=${o}`,q2,"value"];
  }

  if(op===0x36){
    const [a,q1]=u(p+1);
    const [o,q2]=u(q1);
    return [`i32.store align=${a} offset=${o}`,q2,"other"];
  }

  if(op===0x10){
    const [f,q]=u(p+1);
    return [`CALL ${f}`,q,"call"];
  }

  if(op===0x11){
    const [t,q1]=u(p+1);
    const [tab,q2]=u(q1);
    return [`CALL_INDIRECT type=${t} table=${tab}`,q2,"call"];
  }

  if(op===0x04) return ["IF",p+1,"block"];
  if(op===0x05) return ["ELSE",p+1,"else"];
  if(op===0x0b) return ["END",p+1,"end"];

  if(op===0x02) return ["BLOCK",p+1,"block"];
  if(op===0x03) return ["LOOP",p+1,"loop"];

  if(op===0x0c){
    const [x,q]=u(p+1);
    return [`BR ${x}`,q,"branch"];
  }

  if(op===0x0d){
    const [x,q]=u(p+1);
    return [`BR_IF ${x}`,q,"branch"];
  }

  if(op===0x0f)
    return ["RETURN",p+1,"return"];

  if(op===0x45)
    return ["i32.eqz",p+1,"other"];

  if(op===0x4f)
    return ["i32.lt_u",p+1,"other"];

  if(op===0x47)
    return ["i32.ne",p+1,"other"];

  return [`opcode=0x${op.toString(16)}`,p+1,"other"];
}

const body=bodyOf(122);

if(!body) throw new Error("FUNC122 not found");

let p=body.start;

const [groups,q0]=u(p);
p=q0;

for(let i=0;i<groups;i++){
  const [,q1]=u(p);
  p=q1;
  p++;
}

let depth=0;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 CONTROL FLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

while(p<body.end){

  const pos=p;
  const [text,q,kind]=read(p);

  if(kind==="block" || kind==="loop"){
    console.log(
      `${"  ".repeat(depth)}0x${pos.toString(16)}  ${text}  [DEPTH ${depth}]`
    );
    depth++;
  }
  else if(text==="END"){
    depth=Math.max(0,depth-1);
    console.log(
      `${"  ".repeat(depth)}0x${pos.toString(16)}  END  [DEPTH ${depth}]`
    );
  }
  else if(text==="ELSE"){
    console.log(
      `${"  ".repeat(depth-1)}0x${pos.toString(16)}  ELSE`
    );
  }
  else if(
    kind==="branch" ||
    text.includes("CALL") ||
    text.includes("19534c") ||
    text.includes("4788") ||
    text.includes("i32.load") ||
    text.includes("i32.store") ||
    kind==="return"
  ){
    console.log(
      `${"  ".repeat(depth)}0x${pos.toString(16)}  ${text}`
    );
  }

  p=q;
}

console.log("");
console.log("FINAL DEPTH:",depth);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace122From104Args.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 122;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= ~0 << shift;
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const sections: {
  id: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  sections.push({
    id,
    start: p,
    end: p + size.value,
  });

  p += size.value;
}

const importSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);
const funcSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Missing function/code section");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let importedCount = 0;

if (importSec) {
  p = importSec.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const ml = readULEB(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readULEB(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0x00) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      importedCount++;
    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
      p++;

      const flags = wasm[p++];

      p = readULEB(p).next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        p = readULEB(p).next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
      const flags = wasm[p++];

      p = readULEB(p).next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        p = readULEB(p).next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
      p += 2;
    } else if (kind === 0x04) {
      p++;

      p = readULEB(p).next;
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

p = funcSec.start;

{
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    functionTypes.push(r.value);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// LOCATE FUNC 122
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSec.start;

const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

let found = false;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

  const localDecls = readULEB(p);
  p = localDecls.next;

  const locals: {
    count: number;
    type: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;

    const type = wasm[p++];

    locals.push({
      count: n.value,
      type,
    });
  }

  const codeStart = p;

  const globalFunc = importedCount + ordinal;

  if (globalFunc !== TARGET_FUNC) {
    p = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  found = true;

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("TRACE FUNC 122 FROM FUNC 104");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${globalFunc}`);
  console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${ordinal}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${functionTypes[ordinal]}`);
  console.log(`BODY = ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
  console.log(`SIZE = ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("KNOWN CALL FROM FUNC 104:");
  console.log("  arg0 = 0x00000020");
  console.log("  arg1 = 0x000242d1");
  console.log("  arg2 = 0x00000386");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("TARGET VALUES:");
  console.log("  0x00000020");
  console.log("  0x000242d1");
  console.log("  0x00000386");
  console.log("============================================================");

  let q = codeStart;

  while (q < bodyEnd) {
    const addr = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    let line = "";

    switch (op) {
      case 0x02: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line = `block type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x03: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line = `loop type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x04: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line = `if type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        line = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        line = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line = `br ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line = `br_if ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {
        const n = readULEB(q);
        q = n.next;

        const labels: number[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
          const r = readULEB(q);
          q = r.next;
          labels.push(r.value);
        }

        const def = readULEB(q);
        q = def.next;

        line =
          `br_table [${labels.join(", ")}] default=${def.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        line = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line = `call func=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const t = readULEB(q);
        q = t.next;

        const table = readULEB(q);
        q = table.next;

        line =
          `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        line = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;

        line = `local.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;

        line = `local.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;

        line = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;

        line = `global.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;

        line = `global.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        const m = memarg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line =
          `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x29: {
        const m = memarg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line =
          `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x2c: {
        const m = memarg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line =
          `i32.load8_s align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x2d: {
        const m = memarg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line =
          `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x2e: {
        const m = memarg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line =
          `i32.load16_s align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x2f: {
        const m = memarg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line =
          `i32.load16_u align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        const m = memarg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line =
          `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x3a: {
        const m = memarg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line =
          `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x3b: {
        const m = memarg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line =
          `i32.store16 align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;

        const value = r.value >>> 0;

        const marker =
          value === 0x20 ||
          value === 0x242d1 ||
          value === 0x386
            ? "  <<< ARGUMENT CONSTANT"
            : "";

        line =
          `i32.const ${hex(value)}${marker}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;

        line = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        line = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        line = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        line = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        line = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        line = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        line = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        line = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        line = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        line = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        line = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        line = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x50:
        line = "i64.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        line = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        line = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x6c:
        line = "i32.mul";

      case 0x6d:

      default:

    console.log(`${hex(addr)}  ${line}`);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace122Recursion.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0xeec5;
const END   = 0xef44;

function readU32(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readS32(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);
      return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
    }
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 RECURSION / SELF-DISPATCH TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("TABLE[4788] = FUNC 122");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = buf[p++];

  let text = `0x${at.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}  opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  switch (op) {

    case 0x20: {
      const x = readU32(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += ` local.get ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = readU32(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += ` local.set ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = readU32(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += ` local.tee ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = readU32(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += ` global.get ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = readU32(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += ` global.set ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const align = readU32(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readU32(p);
      p = off.next;

      text += ` i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const align = readU32(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readU32(p);
      p = off.next;

      text += ` i32.load8_u align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const align = readU32(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readU32(p);
      p = off.next;

      text += ` i32.store align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3a: {
      const align = readU32(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readU32(p);
      p = off.next;

      text += ` i32.store8 align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = readS32(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += ` i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value >>> 0)})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      // i64.const — فقط برای نمایش
      let value = 0n;
      let shift = 0n;

      while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 7n;
      }

      text += ` i64.const ${value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      text += " i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      text += " i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x4c:
      text += " i32.le_s";
      break;

    case 0x4d:
      text += " i32.gt_s";
      break;

    case 0x4f:
      text += " i32.ge_u";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      text += " i32.and";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      text += " i32.or";
      break;

    case 0x73:
      text += " i32.xor";
      break;

    case 0x74:
      text += " i32.shl";
      break;

    case 0x79:
      text += " i32.rotl";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const x = readU32(p);
      p = x.next;

      text += ` call FUNC_${x.value}`;

      if (x.value === 122)
        text += "  <<< SELF CALL";

      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const type = readU32(p);
      p = type.next;

      const table = readU32(p);
      p = table.next;

      text += ` call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = readU32(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += ` br ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = readU32(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += ` br_if ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const x = readU32(p);
      p = x.next;

      text += ` br_table count=${x.value}`;

      for (let i = 0; i <= x.value; i++) {
        const y = readU32(p);
        p = y.next;
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      text += " return";
      break;

    case 0x1a:
      text += " drop";
      break;

    case 0x04:
      text += " if";
      break;

    case 0x05:
      text += " else";
      break;

    case 0x02:
      text += " block";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text += " end";
      break;

    case 0x00:
      text += " unreachable";
      break;

    case 0x01:
      text += " nop";
      break;

    default:
      text += " UNKNOWN";
      break;
  }

  console.log(text);
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("SELF-DISPATCH SEARCH");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// جستجوی مستقیم call_indirect و call FUNC_122
for (let i = START; i < END; i++) {

  // call_indirect
  if (buf[i] === 0x11) {
    const t = readU32(i + 1);

    if (t.value === 3) {
      console.log(
        `call_indirect type=3 @ ${hex(i)}`
      );
    }
  }

  // call 122 = 10 7a
  if (buf[i] === 0x10 && buf[i + 1] === 0x7a) {
    console.log(
      `DIRECT CALL FUNC_122 @ ${hex(i)}`
    );
  }
}

FILE: scripts\trace12B4.ts
