import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 0x12b4;

function readU8(p: number): number {
  return wasm[p];
}

function readULEB(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = readU8(p++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = readU8(p++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= ~0 << shift;

  return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findCodeSection(): {
  start: number;
  end: number;
} {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

const code = findCodeSection();

let p = code.start;

const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

const funcs: {
  func: number;
  ordinal: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const bodySize = readULEB(p);
  p = bodySize.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + bodySize.value;

  funcs.push({
    func: 83 + ordinal,
    ordinal,
    start,
    end,
  });

  p = end;
}

console.log("TRACE ALL i32.CONST 0x12B4");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CODE SECTION = ${hex(code.start)} .. ${hex(code.end)}`);
console.log(`TARGET VALUE = ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${funcs.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;

for (const f of funcs) {
  let pos = f.start;

  // local declarations
  const localGroups = readULEB(pos);
  pos = localGroups.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(pos);
    pos = n.next;
    pos += 1;
  }

  const hits: number[] = [];

  while (pos < f.end) {
    const at = pos;
    const op = readU8(pos++);

    switch (op) {
      // i32.const
      case 0x41: {
        const x = readSLEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;

        if ((x.value >>> 0) === TARGET) {
          hits.push(at);
        }
        break;
      }

      // i64.const
      case 0x42: {
        const x = readSLEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;
        break;
      }

      // local.get/set/tee
      case 0x20:
      case 0x21:
      case 0x22: {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;
        break;
      }

      // global.get/set
      case 0x23:
      case 0x24: {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;
        break;
      }

      // br / br_if / br_table
      case 0x0c:
      case 0x0d: {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {
        const n = readULEB(pos);
        pos = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
          const x = readULEB(pos);
          pos = x.next;
        }

        const def = readULEB(pos);
        pos = def.next;
        break;
      }

      // call
      case 0x10: {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;
        break;
      }

      // call_indirect
      case 0x11: {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;

        const y = readULEB(pos);
        pos = y.next;
        break;
      }

      // block / loop / if
      case 0x02:
      case 0x03:
      case 0x04:
        pos += 1;
        break;

      // memory load/store family
      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2a:
      case 0x2b:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x38:
      case 0x39:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e: {
        const a = readULEB(pos);
        pos = a.next;

        const o = readULEB(pos);
        pos = o.next;
        break;
      }

      // memory.size / memory.grow
      case 0x3f:
      case 0x40:
        pos += 1;
        break;

      // prefixed opcode
      case 0xfc: {
        const sub = readULEB(pos);
        pos = sub.next;

        // Most bulk-memory/table instructions have additional immediates.
        // We avoid pretending to decode them here.
        break;
      }

      // ref.null / ref.func
      case 0xd0: {
        pos += 1;
        break;
      }

      case 0xd2: {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;
        break;
      }

      default:
        break;
    }
  }

  if (hits.length === 0)
    continue;

  total += hits.length;

  for (const hit of hits) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNC ${f.func} | CONST @ ${hex(hit)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    const from = Math.max(f.start, hit - 48);
    const to = Math.min(f.end, hit + 96);

    console.log(`RANGE ${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let q = from; q < to; q += 16) {
      const end = Math.min(q + 16, to);

      const bytes: string[] = [];

      for (let r = q; r < end; r++) {
        bytes.push(readU8(r).toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
      }

      console.log(
        `${hex(q)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
      );
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    // Second lightweight pass:
    // Decode instructions after the target and identify nearby
    // stores/calls/constants.
    let q = hit;

    for (let n = 0; n < 40 && q < f.end; n++) {
      const at = q;
      const op = readU8(q++);

      if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSLEB(q);
        q = x.next;

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  i32.const ${hex(x.value)}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = readULEB(q);
        q = x.next;

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  CALL func=${x.value}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (
        op >= 0x28 &&
        op <= 0x2d
      ) {
        const a = readULEB(q);
        q = a.next;

        const o = readULEB(q);
        q = o.next;

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  LOAD opcode=0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")} align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (
        op >= 0x36 &&
        op <= 0x3e
      ) {
        const a = readULEB(q);
        q = a.next;

        const o = readULEB(q);
        q = o.next;

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  STORE opcode=0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")} align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        const x = readULEB(q);
        q = x.next;

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  LOCAL opcode=0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")} index=${x.value}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
        const x = readULEB(q);
        q = x.next;

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  GLOBAL opcode=0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")} index=${x.value}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        const bt = readU8(q++);
        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  CONTROL opcode=0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")} type=0x${bt
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x0b) {
        console.log(`${hex(at)}  END`);
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const x = readULEB(q);
        q = x.next;

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  BR opcode=0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")} depth=${x.value}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x45 || op === 0x46 || op === 0x47) {
        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  COMPARE opcode=0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b || op === 0x72) {
        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  ARITH opcode=0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
        continue;
      }
    }
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 1533;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    n++;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };

    s += 7;
  }
  return null;
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0, x;

  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    n++;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v | 0, len: n };
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/* Parse code section */
let p = 8;
let funcBodies = [];

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  if (!sz) break;

  const start = p + sz.len;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    let q = start;
    const count = uleb(q);
    q += count.len;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
      const bodySize = uleb(q);
      q += bodySize.len;

      const bodyStart = q;
      const bodyEnd = q + bodySize.value;

      funcBodies.push({
        func: i,
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd
      });

      q = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

const f = funcBodies[TARGET];

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 1533 EXACT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

console.log(`BODY : ${hx(f.start)} .. ${hx(f.end)}`);
console.log(`SIZE : ${f.end - f.start}`);
console.log("");

/*
 * Print raw instructions approximately, but specifically highlight:
 *
 * - stores
 * - loads
 * - local get/set/tee
 * - calls
 * - constants
 */

for (let q = f.start; q < f.end; q++) {

  const op = b[q];
  let s = `${hx(q)}  ${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(q + 1);
    if (r)
      s += `  i32.const ${r.value} (${hx(r.value)})`;

  } else if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = uleb(q + 1);
    if (r)
      s += `  local.get ${r.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = uleb(q + 1);
    if (r)
      s += `  local.set ${r.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = uleb(q + 1);
    if (r)
      s += `  local.tee ${r.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = uleb(q + 1);
    if (a) {
      const o = uleb(q + 1 + a.len);
      if (o)
        s += `  i32.load offset=${o.value}`;
    }

  } else if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = uleb(q + 1);
    if (a) {
      const o = uleb(q + 1 + a.len);
      if (o)
        s += `  i32.store offset=${o.value}`;
    }

  } else if (op === 0x3a) {
    const a = uleb(q + 1);
    if (a) {
      const o = uleb(q + 1 + a.len);
      if (o)
        s += `  i32.store8 offset=${o.value}`;
    }

  } else if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(q + 1);
    if (r)
      s += `  call ${r.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x6a) {
    s += "  i32.add";

  } else if (op === 0x6b) {
    s += "  i32.sub";

  } else if (op === 0x45) {
    s += "  i32.eqz";

  } else if (op === 0x0b) {
    s += "  end";
  }

  console.log(s);
}

console.log("");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = [0x195350, 0x195354];

console.log("FIND MEMORY ADDRESS REFERENCES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of targets) {
    console.log(`\nTARGET ADDRESS = 0x${target.toString(16)} (${target})`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let count = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i + 5 <= wasm.length; i++) {
        if (
            wasm[i] === 0x41 &&
            wasm[i + 1] === (
                target & 0x7f
            )
        ) {
            // handled below by exact ULEB decode
        }

        if (wasm[i] !== 0x41) continue;

        let p = i + 1;
        let value = 0;
        let shift = 0;

        while (p < wasm.length) {
            const b = wasm[p++];
            value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

            if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
            shift += 7;
            if (shift > 35) break;
        }

        if ((value >>> 0) !== target) continue;

        const end = p;

        console.log(
            `CONST @ 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
            `  VALUE=${value >>> 0}`
        );

        const from = Math.max(0, i - 12);
        const to = Math.min(wasm.length, end + 12);

        let hex = "";

        for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {
            hex += wasm[j].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
        }

        console.log(
            `  BYTES 0x${from.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
            ` .. 0x${to.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
        console.log(`  ${hex}`);

        count++;
    }

    console.log(`TOTAL CONSTANT REFERENCES = ${count}`);
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET_FUNC = 1795;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result -= 2 ** shift;
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const idPos = p;
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const sz = readULEB(p);
    p = sz.next;

    if (id === 10) return p;

    p = p + sz.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function getBody(funcIndex) {
  let p = findCodeSection();

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const sz = readULEB(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sz.value;

    if (i === funcIndex) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("Function not found");
}

function decodeBody(start, end) {
  let p = start;

  const localDecl = readULEB(p);
  p = localDecl.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;
    p++;
  }

  const out = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text:
          (op === 0x20 ? "local.get " :
           op === 0x21 ? "local.set " :
           "local.tee ") + x.value
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        value: x.value,
        text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        target: x.value,
        text: `call ${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = readULEB(p);
      p = t.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2d || op === 0x2c ||
        op === 0x2e || op === 0x2f) {

      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        offset: o.value,
        text: `LOAD offset=${o.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    const simple = {
      0x6a: "i32.add",
      0x6b: "i32.sub",
      0x6c: "i32.mul",
      0x71: "i32.and",
      0x72: "i32.or",
      0x73: "i32.xor",
      0x74: "i32.shl",
      0x75: "i32.shr_s",
      0x76: "i32.shr_u",
      0x45: "i32.eqz",
      0x46: "i32.eq",
      0x47: "i32.ne",
      0x1a: "drop",
      0x04: "if",
      0x05: "else",
      0x0b: "end",
      0x0c: "br",
      0x0d: "br_if"
    };

    out.push({
      at,
      op,
      text: simple[op] || `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    });

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      out[out.length - 1].text += ` ${x.value}`;
    }
  }

  return out;
}

const body = getBody(TARGET_FUNC);
const ins = decodeBody(body.start, body.end);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace195348BaseArithmetic.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const BASE = 0x195348;
const TARGETS = new Set([
  0x195348,
  0x19534c,
  0x195350,
  0x195354,
  0x195358,
]);

const IMPORTED = 83;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function decode(pos: number): { text: string; next: number } {
  const op = buf[pos++];

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00: return { text: "unreachable", next: pos };
    case 0x01: return { text: "nop", next: pos };

    case 0x02: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `block ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x03: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `loop ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x04: return { text: "if", next: pos };
    case 0x05: return { text: "else", next: pos };
    case 0x0b: return { text: "end", next: pos };

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `br ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `br_if ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x0f:
      return { text: "return", next: pos };

    case 0x10: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `call ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      const y = readULEB(x.next);
      return {
        text: `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`,
        next: y.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x1a:
      return { text: "drop", next: pos };

    case 0x20: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.get ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.set ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.tee ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `global.get ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `global.set ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);

      return {
        text: `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);

      return {
        text: `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x37: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);

      return {
        text: `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);
      return {
        text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
        next: x.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);
      return {
        text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
        next: x.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x6a: return { text: "i32.add", next: pos };
    case 0x6b: return { text: "i32.sub", next: pos };
    case 0x6c: return { text: "i32.mul", next: pos };

    case 0x71: return { text: "i32.and", next: pos };
    case 0x72: return { text: "i32.or", next: pos };
    case 0x73: return { text: "i32.xor", next: pos };

    case 0x74: return { text: "i32.shl", next: pos };
    case 0x75: return { text: "i32.shr_s", next: pos };
    case 0x76: return { text: "i32.shr_u", next: pos };

    case 0xa7:
      return { text: "i32.wrap_i64", next: pos };

    case 0xad:
      return { text: "i64.extend_i32_u", next: pos };

    default:
      return {
        text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        next: pos,
      };
  }
}

const code = findCodeSection();

let p = code.start;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log("TRACE 0x195348 BASE ARITHMETIC");

console.log(`BASE    = 0x${BASE.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`TARGETS = 0x195348 / 0x19534c / 0x195350 / 0x195354 / 0x195358`);
console.log("");

let total = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < functionCount; ordinal++) {
  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sizeInfo.value;
  p = end;

  const ins: { pos: number; text: string }[] = [];

  let q = start;

  while (q < end) {
    const pos = q;
    const d = decode(q);

    ins.push({
      pos,
      text: d.text,
    });

    if (d.next <= q) break;
    q = d.next;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const m = ins[i].text.match(/^i32\.const (-?\d+)$/);

    if (!m) continue;

    const value = Number(m[1]);

    if (value !== BASE) continue;

    total++;

    const globalFunc = ordinal + IMPORTED;

    console.log(
      `FUNC ${globalFunc} (ordinal ${ordinal}) @0x${ins[i].pos
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
      `  BODY = 0x${start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${end
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    const from = Math.max(0, i - 4);
    const to = Math.min(ins.length, i + 18);

    for (let k = from; k < to; k++) {
      console.log(
        `  0x${ins[k].pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins[k].text}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL EXACT BASE LITERALS = ${total}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
    [0x195348, "TARGET_195348"],
    [0x19534c, "TARGET_19534C"],
    [0x195350, "TARGET_195350"],
    [0x195354, "TARGET_195354"],
    [0x195358, "TARGET_195358"],
]);

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function sections() {
    let p = 8;

    const out: {
        id: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (p < buf.length) {

        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        out.push({ id, start, end });

        p = end;
    }

    return out;
}

const secs = sections();

const funcSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec)
    throw new Error("Missing function/code section");

let p = funcSec.start;

const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.next;

const definedCount = fc.value;

p = codeSec.start;

const cc = uleb(p);
p = cc.next;

if (cc.value !== definedCount)
    throw new Error("Function/code mismatch");

const funcs: {
    global: number;
    ordinal: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {

    const bs = uleb(p);
    p = bs.next;

    funcs.push({
        global: 83 + i,
        ordinal: i,
        start: p,
        end: p + bs.value,
    });

    p += bs.value;
}

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function decodeConst(pos: number) {

    if (buf[pos] !== 0x41)
        return null;

    const x = sleb(pos + 1);

    return {
        value: x.value >>> 0,
        next: x.next,
    };
}

function isStore(op: number) {
    return op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e;
}

function isLoad(op: number) {
    return op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35;
}

console.log("TRACE 0x195348 BLOCK");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGETS:");

for (const [addr, name] of TARGETS) {
    console.log(`  ${hex(addr)} = ${name}`);
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (const fn of funcs) {

    for (let pos = fn.start; pos < fn.end; pos++) {

        if (buf[pos] !== 0x41)
            continue;

        const c = decodeConst(pos);

        if (!c)
            continue;

        const name = TARGETS.get(c.value);

        if (!name)
            continue;

        hits++;

        console.log();
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`FUNC ${fn.global}`);
        console.log(`CONST @ ${hex(pos)}`);
        console.log(`VALUE = ${hex(c.value)}`);
        console.log(`NAME  = ${name}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        const contextStart = Math.max(fn.start, pos - 24);
        const contextEnd = Math.min(fn.end, pos + 48);

        for (
            let q = contextStart;
            q < contextEnd;
            q += 16
        ) {

            const end = Math.min(q + 16, contextEnd);

            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let k = q; k < end; k++) {
                bytes.push(buf[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
            }

            console.log(
                `${hex(q)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
        }

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        // Inspect the next ~48 bytes instruction-opcode-wise.
        let q = c.next;

        while (q < Math.min(fn.end, c.next + 48)) {

            const opPos = q;
            const op = buf[q];

            let annotation = "";

            if (isLoad(op))
                annotation = " <LOAD>";

            if (isStore(op))
                annotation = " <STORE>";

            if (op === 0x6a)
                annotation = " <I32.ADD>";

            if (op === 0x6b)
                annotation = " <I32.SUB>";

            if (op === 0x22)
                annotation = " <LOCAL.TEE>";

            if (op === 0x21)
                annotation = " <LOCAL.SET>";

            console.log(
                `  ${hex(opPos)}  ${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}${annotation}`
            );

            // Minimal operand skipping for common instructions.
            if (
                op === 0x41 ||
                op === 0x21 ||
                op === 0x22 ||
                op === 0x23 ||
                op === 0x24 ||
                op === 0x10 ||
                op === 0x11 ||
                op === 0x0c ||
                op === 0x0d
            ) {
                const x = uleb(q + 1);
                q = x.next;
                continue;
            }

            if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

                const a = uleb(q + 1);
                const o = uleb(a.next);

                q = o.next;
                continue;
            }

            q++;
        }
    }
}

FILE: scripts\trace195348CalleeDataflow.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const BASE = 0x195348;
const TARGETS = new Set([
    0x195348,
    0x19534c,
    0x195350,
    0x195354,
    0x195358,
]);

const CALLEES = [
    2034,
    3314,
    3315,
    3316,
    3319,
    813,
    1079,
    1533,
];

function readU32(p: number): number {
    return (
        buf[p] |
        (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

const bodies: {
    globalFunc: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

let importedFunctions = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const sectionId = buf[p++];

    const sz = readULEB(p);
    const sectionSize = sz.value;
    p = sz.next;

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

    // Import section
    if (sectionId === 2) {
        let q = p;

        const count = readULEB(q);
        q = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
            const modLen = readULEB(q);
            q = modLen.next + modLen.value;

            const nameLen = readULEB(q);
            q = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

            const kind = buf[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                importedFunctions++;

                const typeIdx = readULEB(q);
                q = typeIdx.next;
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                q += 4;
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                const flags = buf[q++];
                const min = readULEB(q);
                q = min.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    const max = readULEB(q);
                    q = max.next;
                }
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    // Code section
    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = sectionStart;
        codeEnd = sectionEnd;

        let q = sectionStart;

        const count = readULEB(q);
        q = count.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
            const bodySize = readULEB(q);
            q = bodySize.next;

            const bodyStart = q;
            const bodyEnd = q + bodySize.value;

            bodies.push({
                globalFunc: importedFunctions + ordinal,
                start: bodyStart,
                end: bodyEnd,
            });

            q = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
}

function findBody(globalFunc: number) {
    return bodies.find(x => x.globalFunc === globalFunc);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Lightweight instruction decoder
// ------------------------------------------------------------

type Ins = {
    off: number;
    op: number;
    imm?: number;
    text: string;
};

function decodeBody(body: { start: number; end: number }): Ins[] {
    const out: Ins[] = [];

    let q = body.start;

    // local declarations
    const localCount = readULEB(q);
    q = localCount.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(q);
        q = n.next;

        q += 1; // valtype
    }

    while (q < body.end - 1) {
        const off = q;
        const op = buf[q++];

        let text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
        let imm: number | undefined;

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readSLEB(q);
            q = r.next;
            imm = r.value >>> 0;
            text = `i32.const ${imm} (0x${imm.toString(16)})`;
        } else if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = readULEB(q);
            q = r.next;
            imm = r.value;
            text = `local.get ${imm}`;
        } else if (op === 0x21) {
            const r = readULEB(q);
            q = r.next;
            imm = r.value;
            text = `local.set ${imm}`;
        } else if (op === 0x22) {
            const r = readULEB(q);
            q = r.next;
            imm = r.value;
            text = `local.tee ${imm}`;
        } else if (op === 0x28) {
            const align = readULEB(q);
            q = align.next;

            const offv = readULEB(q);
            q = offv.next;

            text = `i32.load offset=${offv.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x36) {
            const align = readULEB(q);
            q = align.next;

            const offv = readULEB(q);
            q = offv.next;

            text = `i32.store offset=${offv.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(q);
            q = r.next;
            imm = r.value;
            text = `call ${imm}`;
        } else if (op === 0x11) {
            const type = readULEB(q);
            q = type.next;

            const table = buf[q++];

            text = `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table}`;
        } else if (op === 0x6a) {
            text = "i32.add";
        } else if (op === 0x6b) {
            text = "i32.sub";
        } else if (op === 0x45) {
            text = "i32.eqz";
        } else if (op === 0x46) {
            text = "i32.eq";
        } else if (op === 0x47) {
            text = "i32.ne";
        } else if (op === 0x4a) {
            text = "i32.gt_u";
        } else if (op === 0x4b) {
            text = "i32.lt_u";
        } else if (op === 0x4c) {
            text = "i32.le_u";
        } else if (op === 0x4e) {
            text = "i32.ge_u";
        } else if (op === 0x1a) {
            text = "drop";
        } else if (op === 0x0f) {
            text = "return";
        } else if (op === 0x0b) {
            text = "end";
        } else if (op === 0xfc) {
            // Bulk instruction: keep scanning conservatively.
            const sub = readULEB(q);
            q = sub.next;
            text = `0xfc subop=${sub.value}`;
        } else {
            // For unknown opcodes, stop decoding this body rather than
            // inventing operand lengths.
            text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
        }

        out.push({ off, op, imm, text });

        // Avoid walking into garbage after unsupported complex opcodes.
        if (
            op !== 0x41 &&
            op !== 0x20 &&
            op !== 0x21 &&
            op !== 0x22 &&
            op !== 0x28 &&
            op !== 0x36 &&
            op !== 0x10 &&
            op !== 0x11 &&
            op !== 0x6a &&
            op !== 0x6b &&
            op !== 0x45 &&
            op !== 0x46 &&
            op !== 0x47 &&
            op !== 0x4a &&
            op !== 0x4b &&
            op !== 0x4c &&
            op !== 0x4e &&
            op !== 0x1a &&
            op !== 0x0f &&
            op !== 0x0b &&
            op !== 0xfc
        ) {
            // Continue only a limited amount; this scanner is intentionally
            // conservative.
        }
    }

    return out;
}

// Main analysis
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("TRACE 0x195348 CALLEE DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BASE = 0x${BASE.toString(16)}`);
console.log(
    `TARGETS = ${[...TARGETS]
        .map(x => "0x" + x.toString(16))
        .join(" / ")}`
);
console.log("");

for (const func of CALLEES) {
    const body = findBody(func);

    if (!body) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${func}: BODY NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    let ins: Ins[];

    try {
        ins = decodeBody(body);
    } catch {
        console.log(`FUNC ${func}: decode stopped`);
        continue;
    }

    const hits: { i: number; reason: string }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
        const x = ins[i];

        // Direct base literal.
        if (x.op === 0x41 && x.imm === BASE) {
            hits.push({
                i,
                reason: "BASE_LITERAL",
            });
        }

        // Exact target literals.
        if (
            x.op === 0x41 &&
            x.imm !== undefined &&
            TARGETS.has(x.imm)
        ) {
            hits.push({
                i,
                reason: "TARGET_LITERAL",
            });
        }

        // Calls to interesting helpers.
        if (
            x.op === 0x10 &&
            x.imm !== undefined &&
            CALLEES.includes(x.imm)
        ) {
            hits.push({
                i,
                reason: `CALL_${x.imm}`,
            });
        }
    }

    if (hits.length === 0)
        continue;

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${func} @0x${body.start.toString(16)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  BODY = 0x${body.start
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${(body.end - 1)
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    const shown = new Set<number>();

    for (const h of hits) {
        if (shown.has(h.i))
            continue;

        shown.add(h.i);

        const a = Math.max(0, h.i - 5);
        const b = Math.min(ins.length, h.i + 8);

        console.log(`  --- ${h.reason} ---`);

        for (let j = a; j < b; j++) {
            const z = ins[j];

            console.log(
                `  0x${z.off
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, "0")}  ${z.text}`
            );
        }

        console.log("");
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace195348ComputedStores.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const BASE = 0x195348;
const TARGETS = new Set([
    0x195348,
    0x19534c,
    0x195350,
    0x195354,
    0x195358,
]);

const TARGET_OFFSETS = new Set([0, 4, 8, 12, 16]);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos,
    };
}

type Ins = {
    off: number;
    op: number;
    imm?: number;
    memOffset?: number;
    text: string;
};

function decodeBody(
    start: number,
    end: number
): Ins[] {
    const out: Ins[] = [];

    let p = start;

    // Local declaration vector.
    const locals = readULEB(p);
    p = locals.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
        const count = readULEB(p);
        p = count.next;

        // valtype
        p += 1;
    }

    while (p < end) {
        const off = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
        let imm: number | undefined;
        let memOffset: number | undefined;

        try {
            switch (op) {
                case 0x41: {
                    const x = readSLEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    imm = x.value >>> 0;
                    text =
                        `i32.const ${imm} (0x${imm.toString(16)})`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x20: {
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    imm = x.value;
                    text = `local.get ${imm}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x21: {
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    imm = x.value;
                    text = `local.set ${imm}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x22: {
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    imm = x.value;
                    text = `local.tee ${imm}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x28: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;

                    memOffset = o.value;
                    text =
                        `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x29: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;

                    memOffset = o.value;
                    text =
                        `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x2a: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;

                    memOffset = o.value;
                    text =
                        `f32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x2b: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;

                    memOffset = o.value;
                    text =
                        `f64.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x36: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;

                    memOffset = o.value;
                    text =
                        `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x37: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;

                    memOffset = o.value;
                    text =
                        `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x3a: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;

                    memOffset = o.value;
                    text =
                        `i32.store8 align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x3b: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;

                    memOffset = o.value;
                    text =
                        `i32.store16 align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x10: {
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    imm = x.value;
                    text = `call ${imm}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x11: {
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;

                    // table index
                    p += 1;

                    imm = x.value;
                    text = `call_indirect type=${x.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0c: {
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    imm = x.value;
                    text = `br ${x.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0d: {
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    imm = x.value;
                    text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x02:
                    text = "block";
                    break;

                case 0x03:
                    text = "loop";
                    break;

                case 0x04:
                    text = "if";
                    break;

                case 0x05:
                    text = "else";
                    break;

                case 0x0b:
                    text = "end";
                    break;

                case 0x0f:
                    text = "return";
                    break;

                case 0x1a:
                    text = "drop";
                    break;

                case 0x45:
                    text = "i32.eqz";
                    break;

                case 0x46:
                    text = "i32.eq";
                    break;

                case 0x47:
                    text = "i32.ne";
                    break;

                case 0x4a:
                    text = "i32.gt_s";
                    break;

                case 0x4b:
                    text = "i32.gt_u";
                    break;

                case 0x4c:
                    text = "i32.lt_s";
                    break;

                case 0x4d:
                    text = "i32.lt_u";
                    break;

                case 0x6a:
                    text = "i32.add";
                    break;

                case 0x6b:
                    text = "i32.sub";
                    break;

                case 0x71:
                    text = "i32.and";
                    break;

                case 0x72:
                    text = "i32.or";
                    break;

                case 0x74:
                    text = "i32.shl";
                    break;

                case 0x75:
                    text = "i32.shr_s";
                    break;

                case 0x76:
                    text = "i32.shr_u";
                    break;

                case 0x77:
                    text = "i32.rotl";
                    break;

                case 0x78:
                    text = "i32.rotr";
                    break;

                case 0x40:
                    text = "blocktype/empty";
                    break;

                default:
                    // Unknown opcode: don't attempt to invent
                    // operand lengths.
                    break;
            }
        } catch {
            break;
        }

        out.push({
            off,
            op,
            imm,
            memOffset,
            text,
        });
    }

    return out;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section / function bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let importedFunctions = 0;

const bodies = new Map<
    number,
    { start: number; end: number }
>();

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + size;

    if (sectionId === 2) {
        let q = sectionStart;

        const c = readULEB(q);
        q = c.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
            const ml = readULEB(q);
            q = ml.next + ml.value;

            const nl = readULEB(q);
            q = nl.next + nl.value;

            const kind = wasm[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                importedFunctions++;

                const t = readULEB(q);
                q = t.next;
            }
            else if (kind === 1) {
                q += 4;
            }
            else if (kind === 2) {
                const flags = wasm[q++];

                const min = readULEB(q);
                q = min.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    const max = readULEB(q);
                    q = max.next;
                }
            }
            else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        let q = sectionStart;

        const c = readULEB(q);
        q = c.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < c.value; ordinal++) {
            const bs = readULEB(q);
            q = bs.next;

            const bodyStart = q;
            const bodyEnd = q + bs.value;

            const globalFunc =
                importedFunctions + ordinal;

            bodies.set(globalFunc, {
                start: bodyStart,
                end: bodyEnd,
            });

            q = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
}

console.log("TRACE 0x195348 COMPUTED STORES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
    "TARGET RANGE = 0x195348 .. 0x195358"
);
console.log(
    "TARGET OFFSETS = +0 / +4 / +8 / +12 / +16"
);
console.log("");

let candidateCount = 0;

for (const [func, body] of bodies) {
    let ins: Ins[];

    try {
        ins = decodeBody(body.start, body.end);
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            x.op !== 0x36 &&
            x.op !== 0x37 &&
            x.op !== 0x3a &&
            x.op !== 0x3b
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        // Look backward for a possible address construction.
        const a = Math.max(0, i - 16);

        let baseIndex = -1;
        let offsetIndex = -1;
        let addIndex = -1;

        for (let j = i - 1; j >= a; j--) {
            if (
                ins[j].op === 0x41 &&
                ins[j].imm === BASE
            ) {
                baseIndex = j;
                break;
            }
        }

        // Direct target literal in the same expression.
        let directTarget = -1;

        for (let j = i - 1; j >= a; j--) {
            if (
                ins[j].op === 0x41 &&
                ins[j].imm !== undefined &&
                TARGETS.has(ins[j].imm)
            ) {
                directTarget = j;
                break;
            }
        }

        // Search for an add immediately before / near the store.
        for (let j = i - 1; j >= a; j--) {
            if (ins[j].op === 0x6a) {
                addIndex = j;
                break;
            }
        }

        // Look for small constants.
        if (addIndex >= 0) {
            for (
                let j = Math.max(a, addIndex - 5);
                j < addIndex;
                j++
            ) {
                if (
                    ins[j].op === 0x41 &&
                    ins[j].imm !== undefined
                ) {
                    const value = ins[j].imm >>> 0;

                    if (TARGET_OFFSETS.has(value)) {
                        offsetIndex = j;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        const interesting =
            baseIndex >= 0 ||
            directTarget >= 0 ||
            (
                offsetIndex >= 0 &&
                addIndex >= 0
            );

        if (!interesting)
            continue;

        candidateCount++;

        console.log(
            `FUNC ${func} @0x${body.start
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  STORE @0x${x.off
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}  ${x.text}`
        );

        if (baseIndex >= 0)
            console.log(
                `  BASE FOUND @0x${ins[baseIndex].off
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, "0")}`
            );

        if (directTarget >= 0)
            console.log(
                `  TARGET LITERAL @0x${ins[directTarget].off
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, "0")} = 0x${(
                    ins[directTarget].imm!
                ).toString(16)}`
            );

        if (offsetIndex >= 0)
            console.log(
                `  SMALL OFFSET @0x${ins[offsetIndex].off
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, "0")} = +${ins[offsetIndex].imm}`
            );

        if (addIndex >= 0)
            console.log(
                `  ADD @0x${ins[addIndex].off
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, "0")}`
            );

        console.log("  CONTEXT:");

        const c0 = Math.max(0, i - 10);
        const c1 = Math.min(ins.length, i + 2);

        for (let j = c0; j < c1; j++) {
            console.log(
                `    0x${ins[j].off
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins[j].text}`
            );
        }

        console.log("");
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL COMPUTED-STORE CANDIDATES = ${candidateCount}`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace195348DataSegments.ts
