import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_START = 0x195348;
const TARGET_END   = 0x195364;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readU32LE(pos: number): number {
    return (
        buf[pos] |
        (buf[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DATA SEGMENTS OVERLAPPING 0x195348..0x195364");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;
let segmentIndex = 0;
let matches = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {

    const sectionId = buf[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

    //
    // Data section = 11
    //
    if (sectionId !== 11) {
        p = sectionEnd;
        continue;
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`DATA SECTION @ ${hex(sectionStart)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE = ${sectionSize}`);

    let q = sectionStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(q);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    q = countInfo.next;

    console.log(`DATA SEGMENT COUNT = ${count}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        //
        // flags
        //
        const flagsInfo = readULEB(q);
        const flags = flagsInfo.value;
        q = flagsInfo.next;

        let offset: number | null = null;

        //
        // Active segment:
        // flags 0 = memory 0 + offset expression
        // flags 2 = explicit memory index + offset expression
        //
        if (flags === 0) {

            const op = buf[q++];

            if (op !== 0x41) {
                console.log(
                    `SEGMENT ${i}: unexpected offset opcode ${hex(op)}`
                );
                break;
            }

            const offInfo = readULEB(q);
            offset = offInfo.value;
            q = offInfo.next;

            const endOp = buf[q++];

            if (endOp !== 0x0b) {
                console.log(
                    `SEGMENT ${i}: unexpected offset end ${hex(endOp)}`
                );
                break;
            }

        } else if (flags === 2) {

            const memInfo = readULEB(q);
            q = memInfo.next;

            const op = buf[q++];

            if (op !== 0x41) {
                console.log(
                    `SEGMENT ${i}: unexpected offset opcode ${hex(op)}`
                );
                break;
            }

            const offInfo = readULEB(q);
            offset = offInfo.value;
            q = offInfo.next;

            const endOp = buf[q++];

            if (endOp !== 0x0b) {
                console.log(
                    `SEGMENT ${i}: unexpected offset end ${hex(endOp)}`
                );
                break;
            }

        } else if (flags === 1 || flags === 3) {

            //
            // Passive data segment.
            //
            offset = null;

        } else {

            console.log(
                `SEGMENT ${i}: unknown flags=${flags}`
            );
            break;
        }

        const sizeInfo2 = readULEB(q);
        const dataSize = sizeInfo2.value;
        q = sizeInfo2.next;

        const dataStart = q;
        const dataEnd = q + dataSize;

        if (
            offset !== null &&
            offset < TARGET_END &&
            offset + dataSize > TARGET_START
        ) {

            matches++;

            const overlapStart =
                Math.max(offset, TARGET_START);

            const overlapEnd =
                Math.min(offset + dataSize, TARGET_END);

            console.log("");
            console.log("############################################################");
            console.log(`MATCH #${matches}`);
            console.log(`SEGMENT       = ${i}`);
            console.log(`FLAGS         = ${flags}`);
            console.log(`OFFSET        = ${hex(offset)}`);
            console.log(`SIZE          = ${dataSize}`);
            console.log(`DATA RANGE    = ${hex(offset)} .. ${hex(offset + dataSize)}`);
            console.log(
                `OVERLAP       = ${hex(overlapStart)} .. ${hex(overlapEnd)}`
            );
            console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

            for (
                let a = overlapStart;
                a < overlapEnd;
                a += 4
            ) {
                const relative = a - offset;
                const available = Math.min(4, overlapEnd - a);

                let bytes = "";

                for (let k = 0; k < available; k++) {
                    bytes +=
                        buf[dataStart + relative + k]
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0") + " ";
                }

                while (bytes.length < 12) {
                    bytes += "   ";
                }

                console.log(
                    `${hex(a)}  ${bytes}`
                );
            }

            console.log("############################################################");
        }

        q = dataEnd;
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
    segmentIndex++;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`OVERLAPPING SEGMENTS = ${matches}`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace195348StoreDataflow.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([
  0x00195348,
  0x0019534c,
  0x00195350,
  0x00195354,
  0x00195358,
]);

const TARGET_FUNCS = new Set([
  3336,
  6991,
  7006,
]);

const data = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32LEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = data[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readVecU32(pos: number) {
  return readU32LEB(pos);
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decodeSignedLEB32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = data[pos++];
    result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: result | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function readULEBAt(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findCodeSection(): { start: number; end: number; payload: number } {
  if (
    data[0] !== 0x00 ||
    data[1] !== 0x61 ||
    data[2] !== 0x73 ||
    data[3] !== 0x6d
  ) {
    throw new Error("Not a WASM binary");
  }

  let p = 8;

  while (p < data.length) {
    const id = data[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readU32LEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payload = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (id === 10) {
      return {
        start: payload,
        end,
        payload,
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function getDefinedFunctionBodies() {
  const sec = findCodeSection();

  let p = sec.payload;

  const countInfo = readU32LEB(p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  const bodies = new Map<number, {
    globalFunc: number;
    ordinal: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    body: Buffer;
  }>();

  const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count; ordinal++) {
    const sizeInfo = readU32LEB(p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    if (TARGET_FUNCS.has(IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal)) {
      bodies.set(IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal, {
        globalFunc: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal,
        ordinal,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        body: data.subarray(bodyStart, bodyEnd),
      });
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function decodeInstrs(body: Buffer, bodyStart: number) {
  const out: {
    off: number;
    opcode: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
  }[] = [];

  let p = 0;

  // locals declaration
  const localCount = readULEBAt(body, p);
  p = localCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEBAt(body, p);
    p = n.next;
    p += 1; // valtype
  }

  while (p < body.length) {
    const off = bodyStart + p;
    const opcode = body[p++];

    let text = "opcode " + hex(opcode);
    let imm: number | undefined;

    switch (opcode) {
      case 0x41: {
        const x = readULEBAt(body, p);
        imm = x.value;
        p = x.next;
        text = `i32.const ${hex(imm)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        const x = readULEBAt(body, p);
        imm = x.value;
        p = x.next;
        text = `i64.const ${hex(imm)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x20:
        imm = body[p++];
        text = `local.get ${imm}`;
        break;

      case 0x21:
        imm = body[p++];
        text = `local.set ${imm}`;
        break;

      case 0x22:
        imm = body[p++];
        text = `local.tee ${imm}`;
        break;

      case 0x23:
        imm = body[p++];
        text = `global.get ${imm}`;
        break;

      case 0x24:
        imm = body[p++];
        text = `global.set ${imm}`;
        break;

      case 0x28: {
        const a = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = o.next;

        text = `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x29: {
        const a = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = o.next;

        text = `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f: {
        const a = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = o.next;

        text =
          `${opcode === 0x2c ? "i32.load8_s" :
            opcode === 0x2d ? "i32.load8_u" :
            opcode === 0x2e ? "i32.load16_s" :
            "i32.load16_u"} align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x38:
      case 0x39:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e: {
        const a = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = o.next;

        const names: Record<number, string> = {
          0x36: "i32.store",
          0x37: "i64.store",
          0x38: "f32.store",
          0x39: "f64.store",
          0x3a: "i32.store8",
          0x3b: "i32.store16",
          0x3c: "i64.store8",
          0x3d: "i64.store16",
          0x3e: "i64.store32",
        };

        text = `${names[opcode]} align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x6a:
        text = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        text = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x71:
        text = "i32.and";
        break;

      case 0x72:
        text = "i32.or";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        text = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x75:
        text = "i32.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x76:
        text = "i32.shr_u";
        break;

      case 0x45:
        text = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        text = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        text = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        text = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        text = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        text = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        text = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const x = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `call func=${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const x = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = x.next;

        const y = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = y.next;

        text = `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0b:
        text = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const x = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `br ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const x = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {
        const n = readULEBAt(body, p);
        p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
          const x = readULEBAt(body, p);
          p = x.next;
        }

        text = "br_table";
        break;
      }

      case 0x02:
      case 0x03:
      case 0x04:
      case 0x05:
      case 0x06:
      case 0x07:
      case 0x08:
      case 0x09:
      case 0x0f:
      case 0x1a:
      case 0x1b:
        text = `control opcode ${hex(opcode)}`;
        break;

      default:
        break;
    }

    out.push({ off, opcode, text, imm });
  }

  return out;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE 0x195348 STORE DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGETS:");
for (const t of TARGETS) {
  console.log(`  ${hex(t)}`);
}
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const bodies = getDefinedFunctionBodies();

for (const func of TARGET_FUNCS) {
  const f = bodies.get(func);

  if (!f) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func} NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY ${hex(f.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(f.bodyEnd)} SIZE=${f.body.length}`
  );
  console.log("============================================================");

  const instrs = decodeInstrs(f.body, f.bodyStart);

  for (let i = 0; i < instrs.length; i++) {
    const ins = instrs[i];

    if (
      ins.opcode === 0x36 ||
      ins.opcode === 0x37 ||
      ins.opcode === 0x38 ||
      ins.opcode === 0x39 ||
      ins.opcode === 0x3a ||
      ins.opcode === 0x3b ||
      ins.opcode === 0x3c ||
      ins.opcode === 0x3d ||
      ins.opcode === 0x3e
    ) {
      console.log("");
      console.log(`STORE @ ${hex(ins.off)}`);
      console.log(`  ${ins.text}`);
      console.log("  PREVIOUS INSTRUCTIONS:");

      const start = Math.max(0, i - 12);

      for (let j = start; j < i; j++) {
        const x = instrs[j];
        console.log(
          `    ${hex(x.off)}  ${x.text}`
        );
      }

      console.log("  FOLLOWING INSTRUCTIONS:");

      const end = Math.min(instrs.length, i + 5);

      for (let j = i + 1; j < end; j++) {
        const x = instrs[j];
        console.log(
          `    ${hex(x.off)}  ${x.text}`
        );
      }

      // Check nearby constants.
      console.log("  NEARBY TARGET CONSTANTS:");

      for (let j = start; j <= i; j++) {
        const x = instrs[j];

        if (
          x.opcode === 0x41 &&
          x.imm !== undefined &&
          TARGETS.has(x.imm >>> 0)
        ) {
          console.log(
            `    ${hex(x.off)} -> ${hex(x.imm >>> 0)}`
          );
        }
      }
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("TARGET CONSTANT READS:");

  for (const ins of instrs) {
    if (
      ins.opcode === 0x41 &&
      ins.imm !== undefined &&
      TARGETS.has(ins.imm >>> 0)
    ) {
      console.log(
        `  CONST @ ${hex(ins.off)} = ${hex(ins.imm >>> 0)}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace195348StrictStoreChain.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 0x195348;
const OFFSETS = new Set([0, 4, 8, 12, 16]);

function readU32LE(p: number): number {
    return (
        buf[p] |
        (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
    switch (op) {
        case 0x20: return "local.get";
        case 0x21: return "local.set";
        case 0x22: return "local.tee";
        case 0x41: return "i32.const";
        case 0x6a: return "i32.add";
        case 0x36: return "i32.store";
        case 0x3a: return "i32.store8";
        case 0x3b: return "i32.store16";
        case 0x37: return "i64.store";
        default: return "opcode";
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STRICT 0x195348 STORE CHAIN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET BASE = ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log("OFFSETS     = 0,4,8,12,16");
console.log("============================================================");

//
// Locate code section (id 10)
//
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];
    const s = readULEB(p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const payloadStart = p;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = payloadStart;
        codeEnd = payloadStart + size;
        break;
    }

    p += size;
}

if (!codeStart) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

let total = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(q);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    q = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = q;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    //
    // Skip local declarations
    //
    let x = bodyStart;

    const localGroups = readULEB(x);
    x = localGroups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(x);
        x = n.next;
        x += 1; // valtype
    }

    const instrStart = x;

    //
    // Track locals that received exact TARGET.
    //
    const targetLocals = new Set<number>();

    //
    // Keep a short decoded history.
    //
    const hist: {
        pos: number;
        text: string;
    }[] = [];

    function pushHist(pos: number, text: string) {
        hist.push({ pos, text });
        if (hist.length > 12) hist.shift();
    }

    while (x < bodyEnd) {
        const opPos = x;
        const op = buf[x++];

        //
        // End of function body
        //
        if (op === 0x0b) {
            pushHist(opPos, "end");
            break;
        }

        //
        // local.get
        //
        if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            const local = r.value;

            pushHist(opPos, `local.get ${local}`);

            //
            // Nothing else needed here.
            //
            continue;
        }

        //
        // local.set / local.tee
        //
        if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            const local = r.value;

            //
            // If immediately preceded by i32.const TARGET,
            // this local becomes a target-base local.
            //
            const prev = hist[hist.length - 1];

            if (
                prev &&
                prev.text === `i32.const ${TARGET}`
            ) {
                targetLocals.add(local);

                console.log("");
                console.log(`FUNC ${ordinal}`);
                console.log(`  TARGET LOCAL = ${local}`);
                console.log(`  ${hex(prev.pos)}  i32.const ${hex(TARGET)}`);
                console.log(`  ${hex(opPos)}  ${opcodeName(op)} ${local}`);
            }

            pushHist(
                opPos,
                `${opcodeName(op)} ${local}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // i32.const
        //
        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readSLEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            const value = r.value >>> 0;

            pushHist(
                opPos,
                `i32.const ${value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // i32.add
        //
        if (op === 0x6a) {
            pushHist(opPos, "i32.add");

            //
            // Look for:
            //
            // local.get X
            // i32.const OFFSET
            // i32.add
            //
            // where X is a target local.
            //
            if (hist.length >= 3) {
                const a = hist[hist.length - 3];
                const b = hist[hist.length - 2];

                const m = a.text.match(/^local\.get (\d+)$/);
                const n = b.text.match(/^i32\.const (\d+)$/);

                if (m && n) {
                    const local = Number(m[1]);
                    const offset = Number(n[1]);

                    if (
                        targetLocals.has(local) &&
                        OFFSETS.has(offset)
                    ) {
                        console.log(
                            `  ${hex(opPos)}  TARGET ADDRESS = ${hex(
                                TARGET + offset
                            )}`
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `  CHAIN: local ${local} + ${offset}`
                        );
                    }
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        //
        // Stores
        //
        if (
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b
        ) {
            //
            // Check whether the preceding expression contained
            // our target-local + small offset chain.
            //
            let targetChain = false;
            let chainOffset = 0;

            if (hist.length >= 3) {
                const a = hist[hist.length - 3];
                const b = hist[hist.length - 2];
                const c = hist[hist.length - 1];

                const m = a.text.match(/^local\.get (\d+)$/);
                const n = b.text.match(/^i32\.const (\d+)$/);

                if (
                    m &&
                    n &&
                    c.text === "i32.add"
                ) {
                    const local = Number(m[1]);
                    const offset = Number(n[1]);

                    if (
                        targetLocals.has(local) &&
                        OFFSETS.has(offset)
                    ) {
                        targetChain = true;
                        chainOffset = offset;
                    }
                }
            }

            //
            // Decode memarg.
            //
            const align = readULEB(x);
            x = align.next;

            const offset = readULEB(x);
            x = offset.next;

            if (targetChain) {
                total++;

                console.log("");
                console.log("############################################################");
                console.log(`MATCH #${total}`);
                console.log(`FUNC ${ordinal}`);
                console.log(`STORE @${hex(opPos)}`);
                console.log(`TARGET ADDRESS = ${hex(TARGET + chainOffset)}`);
                console.log(`OFFSET = +${chainOffset}`);
                console.log(`STORE = ${opcodeName(op)}`);
                console.log(`MEMARG OFFSET = ${offset.value}`);
                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

                for (const h of hist) {
                    console.log(
                        `  ${hex(h.pos)}  ${h.text}`
                    );
                }

                console.log("############################################################");
            }

            pushHist(
                opPos,
                `${opcodeName(op)} offset=${offset.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // Memory load
        //
        if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d
        ) {
            const align = readULEB(x);
            x = align.next;

            const offset = readULEB(x);
            x = offset.next;

            pushHist(
                opPos,
                `load offset=${offset.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // Simple control-flow instructions.
        //
        if (
            op === 0x02 ||
            op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x04
        ) {
            //
            // block type
            //
            if (x < bodyEnd) {
                x++;
            }

            pushHist(opPos, opcodeName(op));
            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            pushHist(
                opPos,
                `${opcodeName(op)} ${r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            pushHist(
                opPos,
                `call ${r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // Conservative fallback:
        // record opcode only and continue.
        //
        pushHist(
            opPos,
            `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`STRICT MATCHES = ${total}`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace19534CAliasWriter.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 0x0019534c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8): string {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function section(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];

    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    if (sid === id) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`section ${id} not found`);
}

function codeBodies() {
  const sec = section(10);

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const result: {
    func: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    result.push({
      func: 83 + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + size.value,
    });

    p += size.value;
  }

  return result;
}

type Ins = {
  addr: number;
  kind: string;
  value?: number;
  index?: number;
  offset?: number;
  func?: number;
};

function decode(pos: number): Ins {
  const addr = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "const",
      value: x.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "local.get",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "local.set",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "local.tee",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x23) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "global.get",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x24) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "global.set",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = readUleb(pos);
    const o = readUleb(a.next);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "load",
      offset: o.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = readUleb(pos);
    const o = readUleb(a.next);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "store",
      offset: o.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x3a) {
    const a = readUleb(pos);
    const o = readUleb(a.next);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "store8",
      offset: o.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x3b) {
    const a = readUleb(pos);
    const o = readUleb(a.next);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "store16",
      offset: o.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x6a) {
    return { addr, kind: "add" };
  }

  if (op === 0x6b) {
    return { addr, kind: "sub" };
  }

  if (op === 0x45) {
    return { addr, kind: "eqz" };
  }

  if (op === 0x46) {
    return { addr, kind: "eq" };
  }

  if (op === 0x47) {
    return { addr, kind: "ne" };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "call",
      func: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0c) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "br",
      value: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0d) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "br_if",
      value: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x02) {
    const x = readSleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "block",
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x03) {
    const x = readSleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "loop",
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x04) {
    const x = readSleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      kind: "if",
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    return {
      addr,
      kind: "end",
    };
  }

  /*
   * Unknown instruction.
   *
   * We intentionally stop decoding this function at unknown
   * instructions to avoid producing false address proofs.
   */
  return {
    addr,
    kind: `unknown_${hex(op, 2)}`,
  };
}

function printInstruction(x: Ins) {
  let s = `${hex(x.addr)} ${x.kind}`;

  if (x.value !== undefined) {
    s += ` ${hex(x.value)}`;
  }

  if (x.index !== undefined) {
    s += ` index=${x.index}`;
  }

  if (x.offset !== undefined) {
    s += ` offset=${hex(x.offset)}`;
  }

  if (x.func !== undefined) {
    s += ` func=${x.func}`;
  }

  return s;
}

console.log("TRACE 0x19534C ALIAS WRITERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET = ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log("MODE   = CONST -> LOCAL -> STORE");
console.log("============================================================");

const bodies = codeBodies();

let totalCandidates = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {
  let p = body.start;
  const ins: Ins[] = [];

  while (p < body.end) {
    const x = decode(p);
    ins.push(x);

    /*
     * Recover instruction length from next decoded address.
     * For unknown opcodes we stop because the following position
     * cannot safely be inferred.
     */
    let next = p + 1;

    if (x.kind === "const") {
      const q = readSleb(p + 1);
      next = q.next;
    } else if (
      x.kind === "local.get" ||
      x.kind === "local.set" ||
      x.kind === "local.tee" ||
      x.kind === "global.get" ||
      x.kind === "global.set" ||
      x.kind === "call" ||
      x.kind === "br" ||
      x.kind === "br_if"
    ) {
      const q = readUleb(p + 1);
      next = q.next;
    } else if (
      x.kind === "load" ||
      x.kind === "store" ||
      x.kind === "store8" ||
      x.kind === "store16"
    ) {
      const a = readUleb(p + 1);
      const o = readUleb(a.next);
      next = o.next;
    } else if (
      x.kind === "block" ||
      x.kind === "loop" ||
      x.kind === "if"
    ) {
      const q = readSleb(p + 1);
      next = q.next;
    }

    if (x.kind.startsWith("unknown_")) {
      break;
    }

    p = next;
  }

  /*
   * Look for:
   *
   *   i32.const 0x19534C
   *   local.set N
   *
   * or
   *
   *   i32.const 0x19534C
   *   local.tee N
   *
   * and then a store using local.get N.
   */
  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const a = ins[i];

    if (a.kind !== "const" || a.value !== TARGET) {
      continue;
    }

    let aliasLocal: number | undefined;
    let aliasAt = -1;

    if (
      i + 1 < ins.length &&
      (ins[i + 1].kind === "local.set" ||
        ins[i + 1].kind === "local.tee")
    ) {
      aliasLocal = ins[i + 1].index;
      aliasAt = i + 1;
    }

    /*
     * Direct address:
     *
     * const TARGET
     * store
     */
    if (
      i + 1 < ins.length &&
      (ins[i + 1].kind === "store" ||
        ins[i + 1].kind === "store8" ||
        ins[i + 1].kind === "store16")
    ) {
      console.log("");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log(
        `DIRECT WRITER CANDIDATE FUNC ${body.func} @ ${hex(a.addr)}`
      );
      console.log("============================================================");

      for (const x of ins.slice(i, i + 4)) {
        console.log(printInstruction(x));
      }

      totalCandidates++;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Alias through local.
     */
    if (aliasLocal !== undefined) {
      for (
        let j = aliasAt + 1;
        j < Math.min(aliasAt + 40, ins.length);
        j++
      ) {
        const x = ins[j];

        if (
          x.kind === "store" ||
          x.kind === "store8" ||
          x.kind === "store16"
        ) {
          /*
           * Check whether the store's address stack contains
           * local.get aliasLocal.
           *
           * We use a bounded backwards scan because full WASM
           * stack simulation is not necessary for this first pass.
           */
          let localUse = -1;

          for (
            let k = Math.max(aliasAt + 1, j - 8);
            k < j;
            k++
          ) {
            if (
              ins[k].kind === "local.get" &&
              ins[k].index === aliasLocal
            ) {
              localUse = k;
            }
          }

          if (localUse >= 0) {
            console.log("");
            console.log("============================================================");
            console.log(
              `ALIAS WRITER CANDIDATE FUNC ${body.func}`
            );
            console.log("============================================================");
            console.log(
              `TARGET CONST @ ${hex(a.addr)}`
            );
            console.log(
              `ALIAS LOCAL = ${aliasLocal}`
            );
            console.log(
              `LOCAL SET  @ ${hex(ins[aliasAt].addr)}`
            );
            console.log(
              `LOCAL USE  @ ${hex(ins[localUse].addr)}`
            );
            console.log(
              `STORE      @ ${hex(x.addr)}`
            );
            console.log(
              `STORE TYPE = ${x.kind}`
            );
            console.log(
              `STORE OFF  = ${hex(x.offset ?? 0)}`
            );
            console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

            for (
              let k = Math.max(i, localUse - 6);
              k <= Math.min(j, localUse + 4);
              k++
            ) {
              console.log(printInstruction(ins[k]));
            }

            totalCandidates++;
          }

          break;
        }

        /*
         * If another local.set to the same local occurs,
         * the old alias is no longer reliable.
         */
        if (
          x.kind === "local.set" &&
          x.index === aliasLocal
        ) {
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WRITER CANDIDATES = ${totalCandidates}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("IMPORTANT:");
console.log("Candidates are address-alias based, not WASM execution.");
console.log("Unknown opcodes are not interpreted.");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace19534cAllRefs.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 0x19534c;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function findCodeSection(): { start: number; end: number } {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sizeInfo.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function decode(pos: number): { text: string; next: number } {
  const op = buf[pos++];

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00:
      return { text: "unreachable", next: pos };

    case 0x01:
      return { text: "nop", next: pos };

    case 0x02: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `block ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x03: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `loop ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x04:
      return { text: "if", next: pos };

    case 0x05:
      return { text: "else", next: pos };

    case 0x0b:
      return { text: "end", next: pos };

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `br ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `br_if ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      let q = x.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < x.value; i++) {
        const y = readULEB(q);
        q = y.next;
      }

      const def = readULEB(q);

      return {
        text: `br_table ${x.value}`,
        next: def.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x0f:
      return { text: "return", next: pos };

    case 0x10: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `call ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      const y = readULEB(x.next);

      return {
        text: `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`,
        next: y.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x1a:
      return { text: "drop", next: pos };

    case 0x20: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.get ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.set ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.tee ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `global.get ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `global.set ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);

      return {
        text: `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x2c: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);

      return {
        text: `i32.load8_s align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);

      return {
        text: `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);

      return {
        text: `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x37: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);

      return {
        text: `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x3f:
      return {
        text: "memory.size",
        next: pos + 1,
      };

    case 0x40:
      return {
        text: "memory.grow",
        next: pos + 1,
      };

    case 0x41: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);

      return {
        text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
        next: x.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);

      return {
        text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
        next: x.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x45:
      return { text: "i32.eqz", next: pos };

    case 0x46:
      return { text: "i32.eq", next: pos };

    case 0x47:
      return { text: "i32.ne", next: pos };

    case 0x4b:
      return { text: "i32.gt_u", next: pos };

    case 0x4f:
      return { text: "i32.ge_u", next: pos };

    case 0x50:
      return { text: "i64.eqz", next: pos };

    case 0x51:
      return { text: "i64.eq", next: pos };

    case 0x52:
      return { text: "i64.ne", next: pos };

    case 0x54:
      return { text: "i64.lt_s", next: pos };

    case 0x55:
      return { text: "i64.lt_u", next: pos };

    case 0x56:
      return { text: "i64.gt_s", next: pos };

    case 0x57:
      return { text: "i64.gt_u", next: pos };

    case 0x58:
      return { text: "i64.le_s", next: pos };

    case 0x59:
      return { text: "i64.le_u", next: pos };

    case 0x5a:
      return { text: "i64.ge_s", next: pos };

    case 0x5b:
      return { text: "i64.ge_u", next: pos };

    case 0x67:
      return { text: "i32.clz", next: pos };

    case 0x68:
      return { text: "i32.ctz", next: pos };

    case 0x69:
      return { text: "i32.popcnt", next: pos };

    case 0x6a:
      return { text: "i32.add", next: pos };

    case 0x6b:
      return { text: "i32.sub", next: pos };

    case 0x6c:
      return { text: "i32.mul", next: pos };

    case 0x6d:
      return { text: "i32.div_s", next: pos };

    case 0x6e:
      return { text: "i32.div_u", next: pos };

    case 0x6f:
      return { text: "i32.rem_s", next: pos };

    case 0x70:
      return { text: "i32.rem_u", next: pos };

    case 0x71:
      return { text: "i32.and", next: pos };

    case 0x72:
      return { text: "i32.or", next: pos };

    case 0x73:
      return { text: "i32.xor", next: pos };

    case 0x74:
      return { text: "i32.shl", next: pos };

    case 0x75:
      return { text: "i32.shr_s", next: pos };

    case 0x76:
      return { text: "i32.shr_u", next: pos };

    case 0x77:
      return { text: "i32.rotl", next: pos };

    case 0x78:
      return { text: "i32.rotr", next: pos };

    case 0x79:
      return { text: "i64.clz", next: pos };

    case 0x7a:
      return { text: "i64.ctz", next: pos };

    case 0x7b:
      return { text: "i64.popcnt", next: pos };

    case 0x7c:
      return { text: "i64.add", next: pos };

    case 0x7d:
      return { text: "i64.sub", next: pos };

    case 0x7e:
      return { text: "i64.mul", next: pos };

    case 0x86:
      return { text: "i64.shl", next: pos };

    case 0x87:
      return { text: "i64.shr_s", next: pos };

    case 0x88:
      return { text: "i64.shr_u", next: pos };

    case 0xa7:
      return { text: "i32.wrap_i64", next: pos };

    case 0xad:
      return { text: "i64.extend_i32_u", next: pos };

    default:
      return {
        text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        next: pos,
      };
  }
}

const code = findCodeSection();

let p = code.start;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const functions: {
  ordinal: number;
  globalFunc: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < functionCount; ordinal++) {
  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sizeInfo.value;

  functions.push({
    ordinal,
    globalFunc: ordinal + IMPORTED,
    start,
    end,
  });

  p = end;
}

console.log("TRACE ALL 0x19534C REFERENCES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET = 0x${TARGET.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`FUNCTIONS = ${functionCount}`);
console.log("");

let total = 0;

for (const f of functions) {
  let q = f.start;

  const ins: {
    pos: number;
    text: string;
  }[] = [];

  while (q < f.end) {
    const pos = q;
    const d = decode(q);

    ins.push({
      pos,
      text: d.text,
    });

    if (d.next <= q) break;

    q = d.next;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const m = ins[i].text.match(/^i32\.const (-?\d+)$/);

    if (!m) continue;

    const value = Number(m[1]) >>> 0;

    if (value !== TARGET) continue;

    total++;

    console.log(
      `FUNC ${f.globalFunc} ` +
      `(ordinal ${f.ordinal}) ` +
      `@0x${ins[i].pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
      `  BODY = 0x${f.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. ` +
      `0x${f.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log("  CONTEXT:");

    const from = Math.max(0, i - 3);
    const to = Math.min(ins.length, i + 9);

    for (let k = from; k < to; k++) {
      console.log(
        `    0x${ins[k].pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins[k].text}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL EXACT 0x19534C LITERALS = ${total}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (total === 0) {
  console.log("NO EXACT REFERENCES FOUND");
}

console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

FILE: scripts\trace19534CFunctions.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const FUNCS = [
  { id: 91,   start: 0x00007c00, end: 0x00007d00 },
  { id: 113,  start: 0x0000d800, end: 0x0000d900 },
  { id: 119,  start: 0x0000ec00, end: 0x0000ee50 },
  { id: 122,  start: 0x0000ed00, end: 0x0000f000 },
  { id: 656,  start: 0x0002c000, end: 0x0002d000 },
  { id: 2057, start: 0x00093d00, end: 0x00095000 },
];

const TARGET = 0x0019534c;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let len = 0;

  while (pos + len < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos + len];
    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    len++;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: value >>> 0, len };

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("TRACE 0x19534C WRITERS / REFERENCES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`TARGET : ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`VALUE  : 4788 (0x12b4)`);
console.log();

for (const f of FUNCS) {
  console.log("-".repeat(72));
  console.log(`FUNC ${f.id}`);
  console.log(`BODY RANGE : ${hex(f.start)} .. ${hex(f.end)}`);

  let found = false;

  for (let p = f.start; p < f.end; p++) {

    // i32.const
    if (b[p] === 0x41) {
      const r = readULEB(p + 1);

      if (r && r.value === TARGET) {
        found = true;

        console.log(
          `CONST TARGET @ ${hex(p)}`
        );

        const s = Math.max(f.start, p - 8);
        const e = Math.min(f.end, p + 24);

        console.log(
          "  " +
          Array.from(b.slice(s, e))
            .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ")
        );
      }
    }

    // i32.store
    if (b[p] === 0x36) {
      const align = b[p + 1];
      const r = readULEB(p + 2);

      if (!r) continue;

      // Show stores with small offsets.
      if (r.value <= 128) {
        const s = Math.max(f.start, p - 12);
        const e = Math.min(f.end, p + 12);

        console.log(
          `STORE @ ${hex(p)} ` +
          `align=${align} offset=${r.value}`
        );

        console.log(
          "  " +
          Array.from(b.slice(s, e))
            .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ")
        );
      }
    }

    // local.set / local.tee
    if (b[p] === 0x21 || b[p] === 0x22) {
      const op = b[p] === 0x21 ? "local.set" : "local.tee";
      const r = readULEB(p + 1);

      if (r) {
        console.log(
          `${op} @ ${hex(p)} local=${r.value}`
        );
      }
    }

    // call
    if (b[p] === 0x10) {
      const r = readULEB(p + 1);

      if (r) {
        console.log(
          `CALL @ ${hex(p)} FUNC=${r.value}`
        );
      }
    }
  }

  if (!found) {
    console.log("TARGET CONST : NONE");
  }

  console.log();
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

FILE: scripts\trace19534CPointers.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8, code=0;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;
  if(id===10){ code=p; break; }
  p+=n;
}

p=code;
const [count,q0]=u(p); p=q0;

const TARGET=0x19534c;

for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const [size,q]=u(p); p=q;

  const func=83+i;
  const start=p, end=p+size;

  for(let x=start;x<end;x++){
    if(b[x]!==0x41) continue;

    const [v,qv]=u(x+1);
    if(v!==TARGET) continue;

    console.log(`\nFUNC ${func} CONST 0x19534c @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`);

    console.log(
      [...b.subarray(Math.max(start,x-30),Math.min(end,x+50))]
      .map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join(" ")
    );

    // classify immediate next meaningful opcode
    let y=qv;
    while(y<end && b[y]===0x1a) y++;

    if(y<end){
      console.log(`NEXT OPCODE = 0x${b[y].toString(16)}`);
    }

    console.log("--------------------------------------------");
  }

  p=end;
}

FILE: scripts\trace19534CProducers.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE 0x19534C PRODUCERS");
console.log("FUNC 122 -> FUNC 87");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET : 0x${TARGET.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`WASM   : ${wasm.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function uleb(buf: Buffer, off: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    const b = buf[off + i];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, size: i + 1 };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(off: number, n = 24) {
  return wasm.subarray(off, off + n).toString("hex");
}

/*
 * Search arithmetic/immediate construction patterns involving
 * bytes that could contribute to 0x19534C.
 *
 * We deliberately do NOT execute the WASM.
 */

const targetParts = [
  TARGET & 0xff,
  (TARGET >>> 8) & 0xff,
  (TARGET >>> 16) & 0xff,
  (TARGET >>> 24) & 0xff,
];

console.log("TARGET BYTES:");
console.log(
  targetParts
    .map((x, i) => `  byte${i}: 0x${x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`)
    .join("\n")
);

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("SEARCHING IMMEDIATE CONSTANTS NEAR TARGET COMPONENTS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < wasm.length; i++) {
  const r = uleb(wasm, i);
  if (!r) continue;

  /*
   * Report useful constants around the numeric decomposition of
   * 0x19534C rather than requiring the complete target literal.
   */
  if (
    r.value === 0x19 ||
    r.value === 0x53 ||
    r.value === 0x4c ||
    r.value === 0x1953 ||
    r.value === 0x534c ||
    r.value === 0x19534
  ) {
    hits++;

    console.log("");
    console.log(`HIT #${hits}`);
    console.log(`OFFSET : 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
    console.log(`VALUE  : 0x${r.value.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`ULEB   : ${wasm.subarray(i, i + r.size).toString("hex")}`);
    console.log(`RAW    : ${hex(Math.max(0, i - 12), 36)}`);
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`COMPONENT HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\trace19534CStores.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSections() {
    let p = 8;

    const sections: {
        id: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        sections.push({ id, start, end });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

const sections = readSections();

const funcSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
    throw new Error("Missing function/code section");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = funcSec.start;

const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.next;

const definedCount = fc.value;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSec.start;

const cc = uleb(p);
p = cc.next;

if (cc.value !== definedCount) {
    throw new Error("Function/code count mismatch");
}

const functions: {
    globalFunc: number;
    ordinal: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < cc.value; ordinal++) {

    const bs = uleb(p);
    p = bs.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + bs.value;

    functions.push({
        globalFunc: 83 + ordinal,
        ordinal,
        start,
        end,
    });

    p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Store opcode set
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const storeOpcodes = new Set([
    0x36, // i32.store
    0x37, // i64.store
    0x38, // f32.store
    0x39, // f64.store
    0x3a, // i32.store8
    0x3b, // i32.store16
    0x3c, // i64.store8
    0x3d, // i64.store16
    0x3e, // i64.store32
]);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE WRITES TO 0x0019534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
    `CODE SECTION = 0x${codeSec.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. ` +
    `0x${codeSec.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log("TARGET = 0x0019534C");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// First pass:
// find all occurrences of i32.const 0x19534C
// ------------------------------------------------------------

type Hit = {
    func: number;
    off: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
};

const hits: Hit[] = [];

for (const fn of functions) {

    for (let off = fn.start; off < fn.end - 1; off++) {

        if (buf[off] !== 0x41)
            continue;

        const x = uleb(off + 1);

        if (x.value !== TARGET)
            continue;

        hits.push({
            func: fn.globalFunc,
            off,
            start: fn.start,
            end: fn.end,
        });
    }
}

console.log(`CONST HITS = ${hits.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// For every target constant, inspect:
//   previous 48 bytes
//   next 96 bytes
//
// And identify stores nearby.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (const hit of hits) {

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(
        `FUNC ${hit.func} | CONST @ 0x${hit.off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
    console.log("============================================================");

    const c0 = Math.max(hit.start, hit.off - 48);
    const c1 = Math.min(hit.end, hit.off + 112);

    for (let off = c0; off < c1; off += 16) {

        const e = Math.min(off + 16, c1);

        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let i = off; i < e; i++) {
            bytes.push(
                buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("NEARBY STORES:");

    for (let off = Math.max(hit.start, hit.off - 96);
         off < Math.min(hit.end, hit.off + 112);
         off++) {

        if (!storeOpcodes.has(buf[off]))
            continue;

        const a = uleb(off + 1);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        console.log(
            `STORE @ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} | ` +
            `opcode=0x${buf[off].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} | ` +
            `align=${a.value} | ` +
            `offset=0x${o.value.toString(16)} | ` +
            `delta=${off - hit.off}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Second pass:
// specifically search for common absolute-address store form:
//
// i32.const 0x19534C
// ... stack operations ...
// i32.store*
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ABSOLUTE-ADDRESS STORE CANDIDATES");
console.log("============================================================");

let candidates = 0;

for (const hit of hits) {

    // Search forward until next control boundary / 128 bytes.
    const limit = Math.min(hit.end, hit.off + 128);

    for (let off = hit.off + 1; off < limit; off++) {

        if (!storeOpcodes.has(buf[off]))
            continue;

        candidates++;

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `CANDIDATE #${candidates} | FUNC ${hit.func}`
        );
        console.log(
            `CONST = 0x${hit.off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
        console.log(
            `STORE = 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
        console.log(
            `DELTA = ${off - hit.off}`
        );

        const c0 = Math.max(hit.start, hit.off - 16);
        const c1 = Math.min(hit.end, off + 24);

        for (let c = c0; c < c1; c += 16) {

            const e = Math.min(c + 16, c1);

            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let i = c; i < e; i++) {
                bytes.push(
                    buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `  0x${c.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
        }

        // only first store after each constant
        break;
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CONST HITS = ${hits.length}`);
console.log(`STORE CANDIDATES = ${candidates}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace19534CWriter.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 0x0019534c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32(p: number): number {
  return (
    (wasm[p] |
      (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
      (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
      (wasm[p + 3] << 24)) >>> 0
  );
}

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (-1 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];
    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (sectionId === id) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`section ${id} not found`);
}

function findCodeBodies() {
  const sec = findSection(10);
  let p = sec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodies: { func: number; start: number; end: number }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    bodies.push({
      func: 83 + i,
      start,
      end,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number) {
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSleb(pos);
    return {
      op,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "const",
      value: x.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  // i32.store
  if (op === 0x36) {
    const align = readUleb(pos);
    const off = readUleb(align.next);

    return {
      op,
      next: off.next,
      kind: "store",
      align: align.value,
      offset: off.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  // i32.store8
  if (op === 0x3a) {
    const align = readUleb(pos);
    const off = readUleb(align.next);

    return {
      op,
      next: off.next,
      kind: "store8",
      align: align.value,
      offset: off.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  // i32.store16
  if (op === 0x3b) {
    const align = readUleb(pos);
    const off = readUleb(align.next);

    return {
      op,
      next: off.next,
      kind: "store16",
      align: align.value,
      offset: off.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      op,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "local.get",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  // local.set
  if (op === 0x21) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      op,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "local.set",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  // local.tee
  if (op === 0x22) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      op,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "local.tee",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  // global.get
  if (op === 0x23) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      op,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "global.get",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  // global.set
  if (op === 0x24) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      op,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "global.set",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  // i32.add
  if (op === 0x6a) {
    return {
      op,
      next: pos,
      kind: "add",
    };
  }

  // i32.sub
  if (op === 0x6b) {
    return {
      op,
      next: pos,
      kind: "sub",
    };
  }

  // i32.load
  if (op === 0x28) {
    const align = readUleb(pos);
    const off = readUleb(align.next);

    return {
      op,
      next: off.next,
      kind: "load",
      align: align.value,
      offset: off.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  // i32.load8_u
  if (op === 0x2d) {
    const align = readUleb(pos);
    const off = readUleb(align.next);

    return {
      op,
      next: off.next,
      kind: "load8",
      align: align.value,
      offset: off.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  // i32.eqz
  if (op === 0x45) {
    return { op, next: pos, kind: "eqz" };
  }

  // i32.eq
  if (op === 0x46) {
    return { op, next: pos, kind: "eq" };
  }

  // i32.ne
  if (op === 0x47) {
    return { op, next: pos, kind: "ne" };
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      op,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "call",
      func: x.value,
    };
  }

  // br
  if (op === 0x0c) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      op,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "br",
      depth: x.value,
    };
  }

  // br_if
  if (op === 0x0d) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);

    return {
      op,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "br_if",
      depth: x.value,
    };
  }

  // block / loop / if
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    const x = readSleb(pos);

    return {
      op,
      next: x.next,
      kind: op === 0x02 ? "block" : op === 0x03 ? "loop" : "if",
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    return { op, next: pos, kind: "end" };
  }

  return {
    op,
    next: pos,
    kind: "other",
  };
}

console.log("TRACE 0x19534C WRITERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET = ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log("SEARCH = STATIC ADDRESS PROOF");
console.log("============================================================");

const bodies = findCodeBodies();

let hits = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {
  let p = body.start;

  const insns: any[] = [];

  while (p < body.end) {
    const ins = decodeInstruction(p);
    ins.addr = p;
    insns.push(ins);

    if (ins.kind === "end" && p > body.start) {
      // Do not stop here: nested blocks/functions can contain ends.
    }

    p = ins.next;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < insns.length; i++) {
    const a = insns[i];

    /*
     * Direct pattern:
     *
     *   i32.const 0x19534C
     *   ...
     *   i32.store
     *
     * We first report the immediate store case.
     */
    if (a.kind !== "const" || a.value !== TARGET) continue;

    const nearby = insns.slice(i, Math.min(i + 12, insns.length));

    let storeIndex = -1;

    for (let j = 1; j < nearby.length; j++) {
      if (
        nearby[j].kind === "store" ||
        nearby[j].kind === "store8" ||
        nearby[j].kind === "store16"
      ) {
        storeIndex = j;
        break;
      }

      // Stop at another obvious address-producing call.
      if (
        nearby[j].kind === "call" ||
        nearby[j].kind === "block" ||
        nearby[j].kind === "loop" ||
        nearby[j].kind === "if"
      ) {
        // Keep scanning only a short distance.
      }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNC ${body.func} | CONST @ ${hex(a.addr)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    if (storeIndex >= 0) {
      const s = nearby[storeIndex];

      console.log(
        `DIRECT STORE CANDIDATE @ ${hex(s.addr)} ` +
        `kind=${s.kind} offset=${hex(s.offset, 8)}`
      );

      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      for (const x of nearby.slice(0, storeIndex + 1)) {
        let extra = "";

        if (x.kind === "const") {
          extra = ` value=${hex(x.value)}`;
        } else if (
          x.kind === "store" ||
          x.kind === "store8" ||
          x.kind === "store16"
        ) {
          extra = ` offset=${hex(x.offset)}`;
        } else if (x.kind === "call") {
          extra = ` func=${x.func}`;
        } else if (
          x.kind === "local.get" ||
          x.kind === "local.set" ||
          x.kind === "local.tee"
        ) {
          extra = ` index=${x.index}`;
        }

        console.log(`${hex(x.addr)} ${x.kind}${extra}`);
      }
    } else {
      console.log("NO NEARBY STORE CANDIDATE");
    }

    hits++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CONST 0x19534C HITS = ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("IMPORTANT:");
console.log("This pass proves only direct/static candidates.");
console.log("Computed aliases through locals/globals require a second pass.");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace195354.ts
