import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_ADDR = 0x195354;
const TARGET_VALUE = 4790;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32Leb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error(`ULEB too large @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readS32Leb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    b = buf[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (-1 << shift);
      }

      return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p,
      };
    }

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error(`SLEB too large @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionStart = p;
    const sid = wasm[p++];

    const s = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size;

    if (sid === id) {
      return {
        sectionStart,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd,
        size,
      };
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${id} not found`);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const importSection = findSection(2);
const functionSection = findSection(3);
const codeSection = findSection(10);

console.log("TRACE 0x195354 / VALUE 4790");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET MEMORY ADDRESS = 0x${hex(TARGET_ADDR)}`);
console.log(`TARGET VALUE          = ${TARGET_VALUE}`);
console.log(`TARGET VALUE HEX      = 0x${TARGET_VALUE.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = importSection.payloadStart;

let r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
const importCount = r.value;
p = r.next;

let importedFunctions = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
  r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
  const moduleLen = r.value;
  p = r.next + moduleLen;

  r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
  const fieldLen = r.value;
  p = r.next + fieldLen;

  const kind = wasm[p++];

  if (kind === 0) {
    importedFunctions++;

    r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = r.next;
  } else if (kind === 1) {
    p++;

    r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = r.next;

    const flags = wasm[p - 1];

    r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = r.next;

    if (flags & 1) {
      r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
      p = r.next;
    }
  } else if (kind === 2) {
    r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    const flags = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = r.next;

    if (flags & 1) {
      r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
      p = r.next;
    }
  } else if (kind === 3) {
    p += 2;
  }
}

console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION TYPE INDICES
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = functionSection.payloadStart;

r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
const definedCount = r.value;
p = r.next;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
  r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
  typeIndices.push(r.value);
  p = r.next;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE BODIES
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSection.payloadStart;

r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
const bodyCount = r.value;
p = r.next;

if (bodyCount !== definedCount) {
  throw new Error(`BODY COUNT mismatch`);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function printContext(
  body: Buffer,
  bodyStart: number,
  pos: number,
  radius = 24
) {
  const from = Math.max(0, pos - radius);
  const to = Math.min(body.length, pos + radius + 1);

  for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
    const mark = i === pos ? " <-- TARGET" : "";

    console.log(
      `    +0x${i.toString(16).padStart(5, "0")} ` +
      `0x${hex(bodyStart + i)} ` +
      `${body[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}${mark}`
    );
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let addressHits = 0;
let valueHits = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount; ordinal++) {
  r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);

  const bodySize = r.value;
  p = r.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  const body = wasm.subarray(bodyStart, bodyEnd);

  const globalFunc = importedFunctions + ordinal;
  const typeIndex = typeIndices[ordinal];

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Scan for:
  //
  //   i32.const 0x195354
  //   i32.const 4790
  //
  // We intentionally do NOT decode the entire function.
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
    const opcode = body[i];

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32.const = 0x41
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (opcode !== 0x41) continue;

    try {
      const s = readS32Leb(body, i + 1);

      if (s.value === TARGET_ADDR) {
        addressHits++;

        console.log();
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`MEMORY ADDRESS HIT #${addressHits}`);
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC   = ${globalFunc}`);
        console.log(`DEFINED ORD   = ${ordinal}`);
        console.log(`TYPE INDEX    = ${typeIndex}`);
        console.log(`ABS ADDRESS   = 0x${hex(bodyStart + i)}`);
        console.log(`CONST VALUE   = 0x${hex(TARGET_ADDR)}`);
        console.log();
        console.log("CONTEXT");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        printContext(body, bodyStart, i);
      }

      if (s.value === TARGET_VALUE) {
        valueHits++;

        console.log();
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`VALUE 4790 HIT #${valueHits}`);
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC   = ${globalFunc}`);
        console.log(`DEFINED ORD   = ${ordinal}`);
        console.log(`TYPE INDEX    = ${typeIndex}`);
        console.log(`ABS ADDRESS   = 0x${hex(bodyStart + i)}`);
        console.log(`CONST VALUE   = ${s.value}`);
        console.log(`CONST HEX     = 0x${s.value.toString(16)}`);
        console.log();
        console.log("CONTEXT");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        printContext(body, bodyStart, i);
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore malformed candidates.
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`0x${hex(TARGET_ADDR)} CONST HITS = ${addressHits}`);
console.log(`4790 CONST HITS        = ${valueHits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace195354Dataflow.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGETS = [91, 119, 656];

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v: number) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function section(id: number) {
  let p = 8;
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];
    const z = uleb(p);
    p = z.next;
    const start = p, end = p + z.value;
    if (sid === id) return { start, end };
    p = end;
  }
  throw new Error(`section ${id} not found`);
}

const fsx = section(3);
const csx = section(10);

let p = fsx.start;
const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.next;

const types: number[] = [];
for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
  const x = uleb(p);
  types.push(x.value);
  p = x.next;
}

let cp = csx.start;
const cc = uleb(cp);
cp = cc.next;

const bodies = new Map<number, {
  start: number;
  end: number;
  type: number;
}>();

for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
  const z = uleb(cp);
  cp = z.next;

  const start = cp;
  const end = start + z.value;
  const func = IMPORTS + i;

  bodies.set(func, {
    start,
    end,
    type: types[i]
  });

  cp = end;
}

function dump(func: number) {
  const b = bodies.get(func);
  if (!b) return;

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${func}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX  = ${b.type}`);
  console.log(`BODY        = ${hex(b.start)} .. ${hex(b.end - 1)}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (let p = b.start; p < b.end; p++) {
    const op = wasm[p];

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = uleb(p + 1);

      if (x.value === 0x195354 || x.value === 4790) {
        console.log(
          `${hex(p)}  i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`
        );
      }

      p = x.next - 1;
      continue;
    }

    // local.get
    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  local.get ${x.value}`);
      p = x.next - 1;
      continue;
    }

    // local.set
    if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  local.set ${x.value}`);
      p = x.next - 1;
      continue;
    }

    // local.tee
    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  local.tee ${x.value}`);
      p = x.next - 1;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.load
    if (op === 0x28) {
      const a = uleb(p + 1);
      const o = uleb(a.next);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)}  i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
      );

      p = o.next - 1;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.ne
    if (op === 0x47) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i32.ne`);
      continue;
    }

    // if
    if (op === 0x04) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  if`);
      continue;
    }

    // call_indirect
    if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = uleb(p + 1);
      const table = uleb(t.next);

      console.log();
      console.log(`>>> CALL_INDIRECT @ ${hex(p)}`);
      console.log(`    TYPE  = ${t.value}`);
      console.log(`    TABLE = ${table.value}`);

      const from = Math.max(b.start, p - 24);

      console.log("    STACK-PRECEDING BYTES:");

      for (let q = from; q < p; q++) {
        console.log(
          `    ${hex(q)}  ${wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
      }

      console.log("<<< END CALL_INDIRECT");
      console.log();

      p = table.next - 1;
      continue;
    }
  }
}

console.log("TRACE 0x195354 DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const f of TARGETS) dump(f);

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\trace195354Indirect.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCS = [91, 119, 656];
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < bytes.length) {
    const b = bytes[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readU32LE(pos: number) {
  return (
    bytes[pos] |
    (bytes[pos + 1] << 8) |
    (bytes[pos + 2] << 16) |
    (bytes[pos + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < bytes.length) {
    const sectionId = bytes[p++];

    const sizeInfo = uleb(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + size;

    if (sectionId === id) {
      return { start, end, size };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

console.log("TRACE 0x195354 -> INDIRECT CALLS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`TARGET MEMORY ADDRESS = ${hex(0x195354)}`);
console.log(`TARGET VALUE          = 4790`);
console.log(`TARGET VALUE HEX      = ${hex(4790, 4)}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS    = ${IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS}`);
console.log();

const functionSection = findSection(3);
const codeSection = findSection(10);

if (!functionSection) {
  throw new Error("Function section not found");
}

if (!codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

console.log("FUNCTION SECTION");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`START = ${hex(functionSection.start)}`);
console.log(`END   = ${hex(functionSection.end)}`);
console.log(`SIZE  = ${functionSection.size}`);
console.log();

console.log("CODE SECTION");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`START = ${hex(codeSection.start)}`);
console.log(`END   = ${hex(codeSection.end)}`);
console.log(`SIZE  = ${codeSection.size}`);
console.log();

//
// Parse function section.
// Entries are type indices for defined functions.
// Global function index = imported count + defined ordinal.
//

let p = functionSection.start;
const functionCountInfo = uleb(p);
const functionCount = functionCountInfo.value;
p = functionCountInfo.next;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {
  const x = uleb(p);
  typeIndices.push(x.value);
  p = x.next;
}

//
// Parse code section.
//
let cp = codeSection.start;
const codeCountInfo = uleb(cp);
const codeCount = codeCountInfo.value;
cp = codeCountInfo.next;

if (codeCount !== functionCount) {
  console.log(
    `WARNING: code count ${codeCount} != function count ${functionCount}`
  );
  console.log();
}

const bodies = new Map<number, {
  ordinal: number;
  typeIndex: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  bodySize: number;
}>();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < codeCount; ordinal++) {
  const sizeInfo = uleb(cp);
  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  cp = sizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = cp;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  const globalFunc = IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal;

  bodies.set(globalFunc, {
    ordinal,
    typeIndex: typeIndices[ordinal] ?? -1,
    bodyStart,
    bodyEnd,
    bodySize,
  });

  cp = bodyEnd;
}

function dumpBody(globalFunc: number) {
  const body = bodies.get(globalFunc);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${globalFunc}: BODY NOT FOUND`);
    return;
  }

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC   = ${globalFunc}`);
  console.log(`DEFINED ORD   = ${body.ordinal}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX    = ${body.typeIndex}`);
  console.log(`BODY          = ${hex(body.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd - 1)}`);
  console.log(`SIZE          = ${body.bodySize}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  //
  // Search for:
  //   i32.const 0x195354
  //   i32.const 4790
  //   call_indirect (0x11)
  //
  const data = bytes.subarray(body.bodyStart, body.bodyEnd);

  const hits195354: number[] = [];
  const hits4790: number[] = [];
  const indirects: { abs: number; typeIndex: number; tableIndex: number }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    const abs = body.bodyStart + i;

    if (data[i] === 0x41) {
      try {
        const x = uleb(abs + 1);

        if (x.value === 0x195354) {
          hits195354.push(abs);
        }

        if (x.value === 4790) {
          hits4790.push(abs);
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    if (data[i] === 0x11) {
      try {
        const typeInfo = uleb(abs + 1);
        const tableInfo = uleb(typeInfo.next);

        indirects.push({
          abs,
          typeIndex: typeInfo.value,
          tableIndex: tableInfo.value,
        });
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("CONST 0x195354 HITS");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (const h of hits195354) {
    console.log(`${hex(h)}  i32.const 0x195354`);
  }

  if (!hits195354.length) {
    console.log("NONE");
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("CONST 4790 HITS");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (const h of hits4790) {
    console.log(`${hex(h)}  i32.const 4790 (0x12b6)`);
  }

  if (!hits4790.length) {
    console.log("NONE");
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("CALL_INDIRECT HITS");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (const c of indirects) {
    console.log(
      `${hex(c.abs)}  call_indirect type=${c.typeIndex} table=${c.tableIndex}`
    );
  }

  if (!indirects.length) {
    console.log("NONE");
  }

  //
  // Dump a compact byte window around each relevant constant.
  //
  const interesting = [
    ...hits195354.map(x => ({ pos: x, label: "ADDR" })),
    ...hits4790.map(x => ({ pos: x, label: "VALUE" })),
    ...indirects.map(x => ({ pos: x.abs, label: "CALL_INDIRECT" })),
  ];

  console.log();
  console.log("RAW WINDOWS");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (const item of interesting) {
    const from = Math.max(body.bodyStart, item.pos - 12);
    const to = Math.min(body.bodyEnd, item.pos + 32);

    console.log();
    console.log(`${item.label} @ ${hex(item.pos)}`);

    for (let q = from; q < to; q++) {
      const mark = q === item.pos ? " <--" : "";
      console.log(
        `${hex(q)}  ${bytes[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}${mark}`
      );
    }
  }
}

for (const f of TARGET_FUNCS) {
  dumpBody(f);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace1A8180Consumers.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const TARGET = 0x1A8180;

let p = 8;

  if (id === 10) {

  p += size;

  [size, q] = u(q);

  const start = q;
  const end = q + size;

  for (let x = start; x < end - 5; x++) {

      b[x + 1] === 0x80 &&

    ) {

      const to = Math.min(end, x + 48);

      console.log("RAW:");
      console.log(

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace2057Arg1.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function s32(v) {
  return v >>> 0;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16);
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0, codeEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    codeEnd = p + n;
    break;
  }

  p += n;
}

p = codeStart;
const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

const targets = new Set([3328, 3331, 3332]);

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (!targets.has(func)) {
    p = end;
    continue;
  }

  console.log("\n============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  const locals = new Map();
  const stack = [];

  function L(n) {
    return locals.get(n) ?? `L${n}`;
  }

  function push(x) {
    stack.push(x);
  }

  function pop() {
    return stack.length ? stack.pop() : "?";
  }

  let x = start;

  while (x < end) {
    const op = b[x];
    const at = x;

    // local.get
    if (op === 0x20) {
      const [n, q2] = u(x + 1);
      x = q2;
      push(L(n));
      continue;
    }

    // local.set
    if (op === 0x21) {
      const [n, q2] = u(x + 1);
      x = q2;
      locals.set(n, pop());
      continue;
    }

    // local.tee
    if (op === 0x22) {
      const [n, q2] = u(x + 1);
      x = q2;
      const v = stack.length ? stack[stack.length - 1] : "?";
      locals.set(n, v);
      continue;
    }

    // global.get
    if (op === 0x23) {
      const [n, q2] = u(x + 1);
      x = q2;
      push(`G${n}`);
      continue;
    }

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
      const [v, q2] = u(x + 1);
      x = q2;
      push(hex(v));
      continue;
    }

    // i32.add
    if (op === 0x6a) {
      x++;
      const b1 = pop();
      const a1 = pop();
      push(`(${a1}+${b1})`);
      continue;
    }

    // i32.sub
    if (op === 0x6b) {
      x++;
      const b1 = pop();
      const a1 = pop();
      push(`(${a1}-${b1})`);
      continue;
    }

    // i32.shl
    if (op === 0x74) {
      x++;
      const b1 = pop();
      const a1 = pop();
      push(`(${a1}<<${b1})`);
      continue;
    }

    // i32.load
    if (op === 0x28) {
      const [align, q2] = u(x + 1);
      const [off, q3] = u(q2);
      x = q3;

      const base = pop();
      push(`MEM[${base}+${hex(off)}]`);
      continue;
    }

    // i32.store
    if (op === 0x36) {
      const [align, q2] = u(x + 1);
      const [off, q3] = u(q2);
      x = q3;

      const value = pop();
      const addr = pop();

      // stack representation is normally value then address,
      // but print both so we can inspect the exact state.
      if (at > start && at < end) {
        console.log(
          `STORE @ 0x${at.toString(16)} ` +
          `ADDR=${addr} VALUE=${value} OFFSET=${off}`
        );
      }
      continue;
    }

    // direct call
    if (op === 0x10) {
      const [callee, q2] = u(x + 1);
      x = q2;

      if (callee === 2057) {
        const arg1 = pop();
        const arg0 = pop();

        console.log("");
        console.log(`CALL2057 @ 0x${at.toString(16)}`);
        console.log(`ARG0 = ${arg0}`);
        console.log(`ARG1 = ${arg1}`);

        if (arg1.includes("19534c"))
          console.log(">>> POSSIBLE 0x19534C PATH <<<");

        console.log("--------------------------------------------");
      } else {
        // Unknown return value: preserve symbolic call result.
        push(`CALL${callee}(...)`);
      }

      continue;
    }

    // i32.eqz
    if (op === 0x45) {
      x++;
      const a1 = pop();
      push(`eqz(${a1})`);
      continue;
    }

    // Drop
    if (op === 0x1a) {
      x++;
      pop();
      continue;
    }

    // Everything else: advance conservatively.
    if (op === 0xfc) {
      const [, q2] = u(x + 1);
      x = q2;
      continue;
    }

    x++;
  }

  p = end;
}

FILE: scripts\trace2057Callers.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2057;
const IMPORTS = 83;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

let p = 8;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND ALL CALLERS OF FUNC 2057");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET FUNC : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const si = readU32(p);
  p = si.next;

  const sectionEnd = p + si.value;

  if (sectionId !== 10) {
    p = sectionEnd;
    continue;
  }

  const ci = readU32(p);
  const bodyCount = ci.value;
  p = ci.next;

  for (let body = 0; body < bodyCount; body++) {

    const bi = readU32(p);
    const bodySize = bi.value;
    p = bi.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const li = readU32(p);
    const localDecls = li.value;
    p = li.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls; i++) {
      p = readU32(p).next;
      p++;
    }

    const codeStart = p;

    for (let q = codeStart; q < bodyEnd; q++) {

      if (buf[q] !== 0x10)
        continue;

      const call = readU32(q + 1);

      if (call.value !== TARGET_FUNC)
        continue;

      total++;

      const realFunc = body + IMPORTS;

      console.log("");
      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log(`CALLER #${total}`);
      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log(`CALLER FUNC : ${realFunc}`);
      console.log(`BODY INDEX  : ${body}`);
      console.log(`BODY START  : ${hex(bodyStart)}`);
      console.log(`CALL OFFSET : ${hex(q)}`);
      console.log(`TARGET      : FUNC ${call.value}`);
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      const from = Math.max(codeStart, q - 80);
      const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, q + 100);

      for (let x = from; x < to; x += 16) {

        const e = Math.min(to, x + 16);
        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let k = x; k < e; k++)
          bytes.push(buf[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));

        console.log(`${hex(x)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
      }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL CALLS TO FUNC 2057 : ${total}`);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace2101Indirect.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCS = new Set<number>();

for (let i = 4057; i <= 4188; i++) {
    TARGET_FUNCS.add(i);
}

const TARGET_TABLE_VALUES = new Set<number>([
    0x91,
    0x93,
    0x94,
    0x95,
    0x96,
    0x97,
    0x98,
    0x99,
    0xfd9,
    0xfda,
]);

function readU32LE(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    return (
        buf[p] |
        (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
    const sections: {
        id: number;
        start: number;
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const size = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = size.next;

        const payloadStart = p;
        const payloadEnd = p + size.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start: payloadStart,
            payloadStart,
            payloadEnd,
        });

        p = payloadEnd;
    }

    return sections;
}

function getSection(
    sections: ReturnType<typeof parseSections>,
    id: number
) {
    return sections.find((s) => s.id === id);
}

/*
 * Parse Element Section (id=9)
 *
 * We are interested in active element segments containing
 * function indexes. We intentionally keep this parser conservative.
 */
function parseElements(buf: Buffer, sec: any) {
    const results: {
        segment: number;
        mode: string;
        tableIndex: number;
        offset?: number;
        functions: number[];
    }[] = [];

    let p = sec.payloadStart;

    const count = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = count.next;

    for (let seg = 0; seg < count.value; seg++) {
        const flags = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = flags.next;

        let tableIndex = 0;
        let offset: number | undefined;

        /*
         * MVP active segment:
         * flags 0:
         *   offset expr
         *   elemkind
         *   vec(funcidx)
         *
         * flags 2:
         *   tableidx
         *   offset expr
         *   elemkind
         *   vec(funcidx)
         */

        if (flags.value === 0) {
            // offset expression
            if (buf[p] !== 0x41) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unexpected offset opcode at element ${seg}: ${hex(
                        buf[p]
                    )}`
                );
            }

            p++;

            const off = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = off.next;
            offset = off.value;

            if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error("Bad element offset end");
            }

            // elemkind
            p++;
        } else if (flags.value === 2) {
            const ti = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = ti.next;
            tableIndex = ti.value;

            if (buf[p] !== 0x41) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unexpected offset opcode at element ${seg}: ${hex(
                        buf[p]
                    )}`
                );
            }

            p++;

            const off = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = off.next;
            offset = off.value;

            if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error("Bad element offset end");
            }

            p++;
        } else {
            /*
             * Other element modes are intentionally skipped using
             * a conservative error rather than guessing.
             */
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported element flags=${flags.value} segment=${seg}`
            );
        }

        const n = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = n.next;

        const functions: number[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
            const f = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = f.next;
            functions.push(f.value);
        }

        results.push({
            segment: seg,
            mode: flags.value === 0 ? "active" : "active-table",
            tableIndex,
            offset,
            functions,
        });
    }

    return results;
}

/*
 * Parse code section and locate REAL instructions.
 *
 * We do not scan raw bytes for 0x11.
 * We walk the instruction stream and recognize:
 *
 *   0x11 call_indirect
 *
 * Then decode its type index and table index.
 *
 * This is still a focused decoder. Unknown instructions are skipped
 * conservatively according to their immediate formats.
 */

function skipULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    return readULEB(buf, p).next;
}

function skipSLEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) return p;
    }
}

function skipMemArg(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    p = skipULEB(buf, p); // align
    p = skipULEB(buf, p); // offset
    return p;
}

function skipInstruction(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    const op = buf[p++];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x00: // unreachable
        case 0x01: // nop
        case 0x05: // else
        case 0x0b: // end
        case 0x0f: // return
        case 0x1a: // drop
        case 0x1b: // select
            return p;

        case 0x02: // block
        case 0x03: // loop
        case 0x04: // if
            return p + 1;

        case 0x0c: // br
        case 0x0d: // br_if
        case 0x10: // call
        case 0x11: // call_indirect -- handled below
        case 0x20: // local.get
        case 0x21: // local.set
        case 0x22: // local.tee
        case 0x23: // global.get
        case 0x24: // global.set
        case 0xd2: // ref.func
            return skipULEB(buf, p);

        case 0x0e: {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = x.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < x.value; i++) {
                p = skipULEB(buf, p);
            }

            return skipULEB(buf, p);
        }

        case 0x41: // i32.const
            return skipSLEB(buf, p);

        case 0x42: // i64.const
            return skipSLEB(buf, p);

        case 0x43: // f32.const
            return p + 4;

        case 0x44: // f64.const
            return p + 8;

        case 0x28: // i32.load
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e:
            return skipMemArg(buf, p);

        case 0x3f: // memory.size
        case 0x40: // memory.grow
            return p + 1;

        default:
            /*
             * Most numeric/simple opcodes have no immediates.
             * Prefix instructions need special handling.
             */
            if (op === 0xfc) {
                const sub = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = sub.next;

                switch (sub.value) {
                    case 8:  // memory.init
                        p = skipULEB(buf, p);
                        return p;

                    case 9:  // data.drop
                    case 13: // elem.drop
                        return skipULEB(buf, p);

                    case 10: // memory.copy
                        return p + 2;

                    case 12: // table.init
                        p = skipULEB(buf, p);
                        p = skipULEB(buf, p);
                        return p;

                    case 14: // table.copy
                        return p + 2;

                    case 15: // table.grow
                    case 16: // table.size
                    case 17: // table.fill
                        return p + (sub.value === 17 ? 1 : 1);

                    default:
                        return p;
                }
            }

            if (op === 0xfd) {
                const sub = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = sub.next;

                /*
                 * SIMD instructions are numerous. For this pass,
                 * don't guess their lengths. Returning the current
                 * position is unsafe, so abort the function parser.
                 */
                throw new Error(
                    `SIMD opcode encountered: ${hex(sub.value)}`
                );
            }

            return p;
    }
}

function decodeCallIndirect(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
) {
    let p = start;
    const hits: {
        offset: number;
        typeIndex: number;
        tableIndex: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (p < end) {
        const at = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        if (op === 0x11) {
            const type = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = type.next;

            const table = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = table.next;

            hits.push({
                offset: at,
                typeIndex: type.value,
                tableIndex: table.value,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0xfd) {
            /*
             * Stop rather than risking desynchronization.
             */
            break;
        }

        /*
         * Rewind and use generic instruction skipper.
         */
        p = at;

        try {
            p = skipInstruction(buf, p);
        } catch {
            break;
        }

        if (p <= at || p > end) break;
    }

    return hits;
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" 2101 → SEGMENT 413 → CALL_INDIRECT TRACE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log("");
    console.log("WASM size :", buf.length.toLocaleString());

    const sections = parseSections(buf);

    const elementSec = getSection(sections, 9);
    const codeSec = getSection(sections, 10);
    const functionSec = getSection(sections, 3);

    if (!elementSec || !codeSec || !functionSec) {
        throw new Error("Required WASM sections missing");
    }

    console.log("Element section :", elementSec.payloadStart.toString(16));
    console.log("Code section    :", codeSec.payloadStart.toString(16));

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" ELEMENT / TABLE MAP");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const elements = parseElements(buf, elementSec);

    let candidateElementHits = 0;

    for (const e of elements) {
        const matched: {
            tableIndex: number;
            functionIndex: number;
        }[] = [];

        if (e.offset !== undefined) {
            for (let i = 0; i < e.functions.length; i++) {
                const tableIndex = e.offset + i;
                const fn = e.functions[i];

                if (
                    TARGET_TABLE_VALUES.has(tableIndex) ||
                    TARGET_FUNCS.has(fn)
                ) {
                    matched.push({
                        tableIndex,
                        functionIndex: fn,
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        if (matched.length > 0) {
            candidateElementHits++;

            console.log("");
            console.log(
                `ELEMENT ${e.segment} table=${e.tableIndex} offset=${e.offset}`
            );

            for (const m of matched) {
                console.log(
                    `  table[${m.tableIndex}] -> function ${m.functionIndex}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "Candidate element segments :",
        candidateElementHits
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" CALL_INDIRECT SCAN");
    console.log("============================================================");

    /*
     * Parse code bodies.
     *
     * Function section gives type indexes for defined functions.
     * Code bodies start after the body vector count.
     */

    let cp = codeSec.payloadStart;

    const bodyCount = readULEB(buf, cp);
    cp = bodyCount.next;

    console.log("Code bodies :", bodyCount.value);

    let totalIndirect = 0;
    let targetIndirect = 0;

    for (let body = 0; body < bodyCount.value; body++) {
        const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, cp);
        cp = sizeInfo.next;

        const bodyStart = cp;
        const bodyEnd = cp + sizeInfo.value;

        const localCountInfo = readULEB(buf, cp);
        cp = localCountInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < localCountInfo.value; i++) {
            cp = skipULEB(buf, cp); // count
            cp += 1;                // value type
        }

        const instructionStart = cp;
        const hits = decodeCallIndirect(
            buf,
            instructionStart,
            bodyEnd
        );

        for (const h of hits) {
            totalIndirect++;

            /*
             * We are interested in:
             *   - table indexes seen in Segment 413
             *   - function indexes reached by known element mappings
             */
            let mappedFunction: number | undefined;

            for (const e of elements) {
                if (e.offset === undefined) continue;

                const idx = h.tableIndex - e.offset;

                if (
                    idx >= 0 &&
                    idx < e.functions.length
                ) {
                    mappedFunction = e.functions[idx];
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (
                TARGET_TABLE_VALUES.has(h.tableIndex) ||
                (
                    mappedFunction !== undefined &&
                    TARGET_FUNCS.has(mappedFunction)
                )
            ) {
                targetIndirect++;

                const functionIndex =
                    83 + body;

                console.log("");
                console.log(
                    `MATCH caller=${functionIndex}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  caller code : ${hex(instructionStart)}..${hex(
                        bodyEnd
                    )}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  call_indirect @ ${hex(h.offset)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  type index   : ${h.typeIndex}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  table index  : ${h.tableIndex}`
                );

                if (mappedFunction !== undefined) {
                    console.log(
                        `  table target : function ${mappedFunction}`
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        cp = bodyEnd;
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(
        "Element segments           :",
        elements.length
    );
    console.log(
        "Total call_indirect        :",
        totalIndirect
    );
    console.log(
        "Relevant indirect matches  :",
        targetIndirect
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("Focus:");
    console.log("  2101 → 0x0018f0d8 → Segment 413");
    console.log("  Segment 413 values: 0x91, 0x93..0x99, 0xfd9, 0xfda");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const SEG412_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG412_END   = 0x0018ab8c;

const SEG413_START = 0x0018ab98;
const SEG413_END   = 0x0018f511;

const SEG413_FILE_START = 0x004a9de0;

const ROOT = 0x0018f0d8;

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function runtimeToFile(addr: number): number | null {
    if (addr >= SEG412_START && addr <= SEG412_END) {
        return 0x004a6695 + (addr - SEG412_START);
    }

    if (addr >= SEG413_START && addr <= SEG413_END) {
        return SEG413_FILE_START + (addr - SEG413_START);
    }

    return null;
}

function readU32File(file: number): number {
    return wasm.readUInt32LE(file);
}

function classify(v: number): string {
    if (v === 0)
        return "ZERO";

    if (v >= SEG412_START && v <= SEG412_END)
        return "SEG412_PTR";

    if (v >= SEG413_START && v <= SEG413_END)
        return "SEG413_PTR";

    if (v < 0x10000)
        return "INDEX";

    return "VALUE";
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("2101 → SEGMENT 413 CHAIN TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`ROOT      : ${hex(ROOT)}`);

const rootFile = runtimeToFile(ROOT);

if (rootFile === null) {
    throw new Error("ROOT is not inside known segment");
}

console.log(`ROOT file : ${hex(rootFile)}`);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ROOT STRUCTURE");
console.log("============================================================");

const rootValues: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
    const p = rootFile + i * 4;

    if (p + 4 > wasm.length)
        break;

    const v = readU32File(p);
    rootValues.push(v);

    console.log(
        `+${String(i * 4).padStart(3, " ")} ` +
        `${hex(ROOT + i * 4)} ` +
        `file=${hex(p)} ` +
        `${hex(v)} ${classify(v)}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("============================================================");

const firstLevel: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < rootValues.length; i++) {
    const v = rootValues[i];

    if (v >= SEG413_START && v <= SEG413_END) {
        if (!firstLevel.includes(v))
            firstLevel.push(v);

        console.log(
            `field +${String(i * 4).padStart(3, " ")} ` +
            `→ ${hex(v)}`
        );
    }

    firstLevel.length);

const visited = new Set<number>();
const queue: number[] = [...firstLevel];

let nodeCount = 0;
let pointerCount = 0;
let indexCount = 0;

while (queue.length > 0 && nodeCount < 100) {

    const addr = queue.shift()!;

    if (visited.has(addr))
        continue;

    visited.add(addr);
    nodeCount++;

    const file = runtimeToFile(addr);

    if (file === null)
        continue;

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `NODE #${nodeCount} ${hex(addr)} file=${hex(file)}`
    );
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) {

        const p = file + i * 4;

        if (p + 4 > wasm.length)
            break;

        const v = readU32File(p);

        let tag = classify(v);

        if (tag === "INDEX")
            indexCount++;

        if (tag === "SEG413_PTR") {

            pointerCount++;

            if (!visited.has(v) && queue.length < 200)
                queue.push(v);
        }

        console.log(
            `+${String(i * 4).padStart(3, " ")} ` +
            `${hex(v)} ${tag}`
        );
    }
}

console.log(`Segment 413 pointers      : ${pointerCount}`);
console.log(`Index candidates          : ${indexCount}`);

console.log("============================================================");

for (const addr of [...visited].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
    const file = runtimeToFile(addr);

    console.log(
        `${hex(addr)} file=${file !== null ? hex(file) : "n/a"}`
    );
}

console.log("");

for (const addr of visited) {

    if (file === null)
        continue;

    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {

        const p = file + i * 4;

        if (p + 4 > wasm.length)

        const v = readU32File(p);

        if (
            v > 0 &&

            v !== 0x91 &&

            candidates.add(v);

FILE: scripts\trace248Arg0.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 248;

function readULEB(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function skipBlockType(pos) {
    const b = wasm[pos];

    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c ||
        b === 0x7b ||
        b === 0x70 ||
        b === 0x6f
    ) {
        return pos + 1;
    }

    return readSLEB(pos).next;
}

function memArg(pos) {
    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next
    };
}

function decode(body) {

    const out = [];

    let p = body.start;

    while (p < body.end) {

        const at = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = "";

        try {

            switch (op) {

                case 0x02:
                    p = skipBlockType(p);
                    text = "block";
                    break;

                case 0x03:
                    p = skipBlockType(p);
                    text = "loop";
                    break;

                case 0x04:
                    p = skipBlockType(p);
                    text = "if";
                    break;

                case 0x05:
                    text = "else";
                    break;

                case 0x0b:
                    text = "end";
                    break;

                case 0x0c: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `br ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0d: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0e: {
                    const n = readULEB(p);
                    p = n.next;

                    const labels = [];

                    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                        const r = readULEB(p);
                        p = r.next;
                        labels.push(r.value);
                    }

                    const d = readULEB(p);
                    p = d.next;

                    text =
                        `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${d.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x0f:
                    text = "return";
                    break;

                case 0x10: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `call ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x11: {
                    const t = readULEB(p);
                    p = t.next;

                    const table = readULEB(p);
                    p = table.next;

                    text =
                        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x1a:
                    text = "drop";
                    break;

                case 0x1b:
                    text = "select";
                    break;

                case 0x20: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x21: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x22: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x23: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x24: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x28:
                case 0x29:
                case 0x2a:
                case 0x2b:
                case 0x2c:
                case 0x2d:
                case 0x2e:
                case 0x2f:
                case 0x30:
                case 0x31:
                case 0x32:
                case 0x33:
                case 0x34:
                case 0x35:
                case 0x36:
                case 0x37:
                case 0x38:
                case 0x39:
                case 0x3a:
                case 0x3b:
                case 0x3c:
                case 0x3d:
                case 0x3e: {

                    const m = memArg(p);
                    p = m.next;

                    const names = {
                        0x28: "i32.load",
                        0x29: "i64.load",
                        0x2a: "f32.load",
                        0x2b: "f64.load",
                        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
                        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
                        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
                        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
                        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
                        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
                        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
                        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
                        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
                        0x35: "i64.load32_u",
                        0x36: "i32.store",
                        0x37: "i64.store",
                        0x38: "f32.store",
                        0x39: "f64.store",
                        0x3a: "i32.store8",
                        0x3b: "i32.store16",
                        0x3c: "i64.store8",
                        0x3d: "i64.store16",
                        0x3e: "i64.store32"
                    };

                    text =
                        `${names[op]} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x41: {
                    const r = readSLEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x42: {
                    const r = readSLEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                default:

                    text =
                        `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

                    break;
            }

        } catch {

            text = "DECODE ERROR";
        }

        out.push({
            pos: at,
            op,
            text
        });
    }

    return out;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
const bodies = [];

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const section = wasm[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(pos);
    pos = s.next;

    const end = pos + s.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        const c = readULEB(pos);
        pos = c.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {

            const b = readULEB(pos);
            pos = b.next;

            const start = pos;
            const finish = start + b.value;

            bodies.push({
                index: i,
                start,
                end: finish
            });

            pos = finish;
        }

        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

const targets = new Set([
    1795,
    2717,
    3015,
    4725

for (const body of bodies) {

        );

        console.log(

        );

        const start = Math.max(0, i - 18);
        const end = Math.min(ins.length, i + 3);

        for (let j = start; j < end; j++) {

            const marker =
                j === i ? ">>> " : "    ";

            console.log(
                marker +
                "0x" +
                ins[j].pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
                "  " +
                ins[j].text
            );
        }

        console.log("");
        console.log("ARGUMENT STACK CANDIDATE:");

        /*
         * FUNC 248 has 3 parameters.
         *
         * Immediately before:
         *
         * call 248
         *
         * stack is:
         *
         *   arg0
         *   arg1
         *   arg2
         *
         * We therefore inspect the final producer sequence.
         */

        const before = ins.slice(
            Math.max(0, i - 8),
            i
        );

        for (const x of before) {
            console.log(
                "    " + x.text
            );
        }

        console.log(
            "    >>> CALL 248"
        );
    }
}

FILE: scripts\trace248DispatchObject.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNC 248 DISPATCH OBJECT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const TARGET_FUNC = 248;
const TARGET_OFFSET = 4;
const CALL446 = 446;

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return null;
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << (shift + 7));
            }

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return null;
}

function hx(v) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = readULEB(p);
        if (!s) break;

        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = start + s.value;

        if (id === 10) {
            return {
                start,
                end
            };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

const code = readCodeSection();

let p = code.start;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
p = countInfo.next;

const functionCount = countInfo.value;

const functions = [];

for (let func = 0; func < functionCount; func++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    if (!sizeInfo) break;

    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

    functions.push({
        func,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("FUNCTION COUNT :", functions.length);
console.log("TARGET FUNC    :", TARGET_FUNC);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*
 * Decode a compact instruction stream.
 *
 * We are primarily interested in:
 *
 *   local.get
 *   local.set
 *   local.tee
 *   i32.const
 *   i32.load
 *   i32.store
 *   i32.add
 *   call
 *
 * The output is intentionally local around CALL 248.
 */

function decodeFunction(fn) {

    let p = fn.bodyStart;

    const locals = [];

    const localGroup = readULEB(p);
    if (!localGroup) return null;

    p = localGroup.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroup.value; i++) {

        const n = readULEB(p);
        if (!n) return null;

        p = n.next;

        const type = buf[p++];

        for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++) {
            locals.push({
                type,
                kind: "local"
            });
        }
    }

    const instructions = [];

    while (p < fn.bodyEnd) {

        const pos = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        let ins = {
            pos,
            op,
            text: ""
        };

        /*
         * local.get
         */
        if (op === 0x20) {

            const r = readULEB(p);
            if (!r) break;

            p = r.next;

            ins.local = r.value;
            ins.text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
        }

        /*
         * local.set
         */
        else if (op === 0x21) {

            const r = readULEB(p);
            if (!r) break;

            p = r.next;

            ins.local = r.value;
            ins.text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
        }

        /*
         * local.tee
         */
        else if (op === 0x22) {

            const r = readULEB(p);
            if (!r) break;

            p = r.next;

            ins.local = r.value;
            ins.text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        }

        /*
         * i32.const
         */
        else if (op === 0x41) {

            const r = readSLEB(p);
            if (!r) break;

            p = r.next;

            ins.value = r.value;
            ins.text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
        }

        /*
         * call
         */
        else if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = readULEB(p);
            if (!r) break;

            p = r.next;

            ins.target = r.value;
            ins.text = `call ${r.value}`;
        }

        /*
         * call_indirect
         */
        else if (op === 0x11) {

            const a = readULEB(p);
            if (!a) break;

            p = a.next;

            const b = readULEB(p);
            if (!b) break;

            p = b.next;

            ins.type = a.value;
            ins.table = b.value;
            ins.text =
                `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;
        }

        /*
         * i32.load / load8 / etc.
         */
        else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {

            const align = readULEB(p);
            if (!align) break;

            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            if (!off) break;

            p = off.next;

            ins.offset = off.value;
            ins.align = align.value;

            ins.text =
                `memory.load offset=${off.value}`;
        }

        /*
         * i32.store / store8 etc.
         */
        else if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {

            const align = readULEB(p);
            if (!align) break;

            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            if (!off) break;

            p = off.next;

            ins.offset = off.value;
            ins.align = align.value;

            ins.text =
                `memory.store offset=${off.value}`;
        }

        /*
         * i32.add
         */
        else if (op === 0x6a) {
            ins.text = "i32.add";
        }

        /*
         * i32.sub
         */
        else if (op === 0x6b) {
            ins.text = "i32.sub";
        }

        /*
         * i32.const-related / comparison / misc:
         * keep opcode but don't attempt full decode.
         */
        else {
            ins.text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
        }

        instructions.push(ins);
    }

    return instructions;
}

const callers = [];

for (const fn of functions) {

    let ins;

    try {
        ins = decodeFunction(fn);
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    if (!ins) continue;

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

        /*
         * Direct CALL 248
         */
        if (
            ins[i].text === `call ${TARGET_FUNC}`
        ) {

            callers.push({
                fn,
                index: i,
                instructions: ins
            });
        }
    }
}

console.log("DIRECT CALL 248 :", callers.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const c of callers) {

    const ins = c.instructions;
    const i = c.index;

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("FUNC :", c.fn.func);
    console.log("CALL :", hx(ins[i].pos));
    console.log("============================================================");

    /*
     * Print 30 instructions before CALL 248.
     */
    const start = Math.max(0, i - 30);

    for (let j = start; j < i; j++) {

        const x = ins[j];

        console.log(
            hx(x.pos),
            x.text
        );
    }

    console.log(
        hx(ins[i].pos),
        "CALL 248"
    );

    /*
     * Heuristic reconstruction of the top stack item.
     *
     * We walk backwards from CALL 248 looking for the
     * last expression that can plausibly supply param0.
     *
     * Important special case:
     *
     *   local.get N
     *   call 446
     *
     * means param0 is the result of FUNC 446.
     */

    console.log("");
    console.log("PARAM0 ORIGIN SEARCH");
    console.log("----------------------------------------");

    let found = false;

    for (let j = i - 1; j >= Math.max(0, i - 20); j--) {

        const x = ins[j];

        if (x.text.startsWith("local.get")) {

            const local = x.local;

            console.log(
                "  possible PARAM0 : local",
                local,
                "at",
                hx(x.pos)
            );

            /*
             * Search backwards for the nearest definition
             * of this local.
             */
            for (let k = j - 1; k >= 0; k--) {

                const d = ins[k];

                if (
                    (d.text === `local.set ${local}`) ||
                    (d.text === `local.tee ${local}`)
                ) {

                    console.log(
                        "  local definition :",
                        hx(d.pos),
                        d.text
                    );

                    /*
                     * Check the expression immediately before
                     * the local definition.
                     */
                    const a = Math.max(0, k - 8);

                    for (let z = a; z < k; z++) {

                        if (
                            ins[z].text === `call ${CALL446}`
                        ) {

                            console.log(
                                "  *** PARAM0 DERIVES FROM FUNC 446 ***",
                                hx(ins[z].pos)
                            );

                            found = true;
                        }
                    }

                    break;
                }
            }

            if (found) break;
        }
    }

    if (!found) {
        console.log(
            "  No direct 446-origin detected in local window."
        );
    }

    /*
     * Look for STORE +4 in same function.
     */
    console.log("");
    console.log("OFFSET +4 STORES IN CALLER");
    console.log("----------------------------------------");

    let stores4 = 0;

    for (let j = 0; j < i; j++) {

        if (
            ins[j].text === "memory.store offset=4"
        ) {

            stores4++;

            console.log(
                "  STORE +4 @",
                hx(ins[j].pos)
            );

            const a = Math.max(0, j - 6);

            for (let z = a; z < j; z++) {
                console.log(
                    "   ",
                    hx(ins[z].pos),
                    ins[z].text
                );
            }
        }
    }

    if (stores4 === 0) {
        console.log("  NONE");
    }

    /*
     * Look for constant 4275 or equivalent nearby.
     */
    console.log("");
    console.log("4275 / 0x10B3 CHECK");
    console.log("----------------------------------------");

    let targetHits = 0;

    for (
        let j = Math.max(0, i - 100);
        j < Math.min(ins.length, i + 20);
        j++
    ) {

        if (
            ins[j].text === "i32.const 4275"
        ) {

            console.log(
                "  *** DIRECT 4275 *** @",
                hx(ins[j].pos)
            );

            targetHits++;
        }
    }

    if (!targetHits) {
        console.log("  No literal 4275 near CALL 248.");
    }
}
