const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_CALL = 248;

const CALLERS = [
  1795,
  2717,
  3015,
  4725
];

function uleb(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: result, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        result |= (~0 << shift);

      return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decode(pos, end) {

  const start = pos;

  if (pos >= end)
    return null;

  const op = buf[pos++];

  const ins = {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
  };

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {

    const a = uleb(pos);

    if (!a)
      return null;

    ins.end = a.next;

    const name =
      op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
      op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
      "local.tee";

    ins.local = a.value;
    ins.text = `${name} local=${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {

    const a = sleb(pos);

    if (!a)
      return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.value = a.value;
    ins.text = `i32.const ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const a = uleb(pos);

    if (!a)
      return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.callTarget = a.value;
    ins.text = `call ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {

    const a = uleb(pos);

    if (!a)
      return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);

    if (!b)
      return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.text =
      `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2d) {

    const a = uleb(pos);

    if (!a)
      return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);

    if (!b)
      return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.isLoad = true;

    const name =
      op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" :
      op === 0x29 ? "i64.load" :
      "i32.load8_u";

    ins.text =
      `${name} align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {

    const a = uleb(pos);

    if (!a)
      return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);

    if (!b)
      return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.isStore = true;

    ins.text =
      `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x6a) {
    ins.text = "i32.add";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x6b) {
    ins.text = "i32.sub";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x45) {
    ins.text = "i32.eqz";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x46) {
    ins.text = "i32.eq";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x47) {
    ins.text = "i32.ne";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    ins.text = "end";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x05) {
    ins.text = "else";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

    const a = uleb(pos);

    if (!a)
      return null;

    ins.end = a.next;

    ins.text =
      `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04) {

    if (pos >= end)
      return null;

    const bt = buf[pos++];

    ins.end = pos;

    ins.text =
      op === 0x02 ? "block" :
      op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
      "if";

    return ins;
  }

  return ins;
}

/*
 * Locate code section.
 */

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);

  if (!s)
    throw new Error("Bad section");

  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("Code section not found");

/*
 * Locate function bodies.
 */

let q = codeStart;

const countInfo = uleb(q);
q = countInfo.next;

const bodyCount = countInfo.value;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let func = 0; func < bodyCount; func++) {

  const s = uleb(q);

  if (!s)
    throw new Error("Bad body");

  const bodySize = s.value;
  const bodyStart = s.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  if (CALLERS.includes(func))
    bodies.set(func, {
      func,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd
    });

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNC 248 OBJECT ORIGINS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("BODY COUNT:", bodyCount);
console.log("TARGET CALL:", TARGET_CALL);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*
 * Decode callers.
 */

for (const func of CALLERS) {

  const body = bodies.get(func);

  if (!body)
    continue;

  let x = body.bodyStart;

  const localInfo = uleb(x);

  if (!localInfo)
    continue;

  x = localInfo.next;

  for (let g = 0; g < localInfo.value; g++) {

    const n = uleb(x);

    if (!n)
      break;

    x = n.next + 1;
  }

  const ins = [];

  while (x < body.bodyEnd) {

    const z = decode(x, body.bodyEnd);

    if (!z)
      break;

    ins.push(z);
    x = z.end;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("CALLER FUNC", func);
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

    if (ins[i].callTarget !== TARGET_CALL)
      continue;

    console.log("");
    console.log("CALL 248 @", hex(ins[i].start));

    /*
     * Show preceding stack-building instructions.
     */
    const from = Math.max(0, i - 20);

    console.log("PRE-CALL STACK:");

    for (let k = from; k < i; k++) {

      console.log(
        " ",
        hex(ins[k].start),
        ins[k].text
      );
    }

    /*
     * Look for the first local.get that is likely param0.
     *
     * We print every local.get in the final 10 instructions
     * so we don't make an unsafe assumption about stack order.
     */
    console.log("");
    console.log("LAST LOCAL GETS:");

    for (
      let k = Math.max(from, i - 10);
      k < i;
      k++
    ) {

      if (
        ins[k].text.startsWith("local.get")
      ) {
        console.log(
          " ",
          hex(ins[k].start),
          ins[k].text
        );
      }
    }

    /*
     * Show memory loads in pre-call window.
     */
    console.log("");
    console.log("LAST MEMORY LOADS:");

    for (
      let k = Math.max(from, i - 15);
      k < i;
      k++
    ) {

      if (ins[k].isLoad) {

        console.log(
          " ",
          hex(ins[k].start),
          ins[k].text
        );
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\trace248Param0Defs.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGETS = {
    1795: [
        { addr: 0x0008671f, local: 2 },
        { addr: 0x00086758, local: 8 },
        { addr: 0x0008677e, local: 8 }
    ],

    2717: [
        { addr: 0x000db217, local: 1 },
        { addr: 0x000db226, local: 1 },
        { addr: 0x000db23c, local: 1 },
        { addr: 0x000db253, local: 1 },
        { addr: 0x000db26a, local: 1 },
        { addr: 0x000db29a, local: 1 }
    ],

    3015: [
        { addr: 0x000f0862, local: 13 },
        { addr: 0x000f08a8, local: 13 },
        { addr: 0x000f0a8f, local: 13 }
    ],

    4725: [
        { addr: 0x001eb215, local: 0 }
    ]
};

function readULEB(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function skipBlockType(pos) {
    const b = wasm[pos];

    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c ||
        b === 0x7b ||
        b === 0x70 ||
        b === 0x6f
    ) {
        return pos + 1;
    }

    return readSLEB(pos).next;
}

function memArg(pos) {
    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next
    };
}

function decode(body) {

    const out = [];

    let p = body.start;

    while (p < body.end) {

        const at = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = "";

        try {

            switch (op) {

                case 0x02:
                    p = skipBlockType(p);
                    text = "block";
                    break;

                case 0x03:
                    p = skipBlockType(p);
                    text = "loop";
                    break;

                case 0x04:
                    p = skipBlockType(p);
                    text = "if";
                    break;

                case 0x05:
                    text = "else";
                    break;

                case 0x0b:
                    text = "end";
                    break;

                case 0x0c: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `br ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0d: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0e: {
                    const n = readULEB(p);
                    p = n.next;

                    const labels = [];

                    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                        const r = readULEB(p);
                        p = r.next;
                        labels.push(r.value);
                    }

                    const d = readULEB(p);
                    p = d.next;

                    text =
                        `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${d.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x0f:
                    text = "return";
                    break;

                case 0x10: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `call ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x11: {
                    const t = readULEB(p);
                    p = t.next;

                    const table = readULEB(p);
                    p = table.next;

                    text =
                        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x1a:
                    text = "drop";
                    break;

                case 0x1b:
                    text = "select";
                    break;

                case 0x20: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x21: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x22: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x23: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x24: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x28:
                case 0x29:
                case 0x2a:
                case 0x2b:
                case 0x2c:
                case 0x2d:
                case 0x2e:
                case 0x2f:
                case 0x30:
                case 0x31:
                case 0x32:
                case 0x33:
                case 0x34:
                case 0x35:
                case 0x36:
                case 0x37:
                case 0x38:
                case 0x39:
                case 0x3a:
                case 0x3b:
                case 0x3c:
                case 0x3d:
                case 0x3e: {

                    const m = memArg(p);
                    p = m.next;

                    const names = {
                        0x28: "i32.load",
                        0x29: "i64.load",
                        0x2a: "f32.load",
                        0x2b: "f64.load",
                        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
                        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
                        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
                        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
                        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
                        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
                        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
                        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
                        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
                        0x35: "i64.load32_u",
                        0x36: "i32.store",
                        0x37: "i64.store",
                        0x38: "f32.store",
                        0x39: "f64.store",
                        0x3a: "i32.store8",
                        0x3b: "i32.store16",
                        0x3c: "i64.store8",
                        0x3d: "i64.store16",
                        0x3e: "i64.store32"
                    };

                    text =
                        `${names[op]} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x41: {
                    const r = readSLEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x42: {
                    const r = readSLEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                default:
                    text =
                        `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
                    break;
            }

        } catch {
            text = "DECODE ERROR";
        }

        out.push({
            pos: at,
            op,
            text
        });
    }

    return out;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
const bodies = [];

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const section = wasm[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(pos);
    pos = s.next;

    const end = pos + s.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        const count = readULEB(pos);
        pos = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

            const b = readULEB(pos);
            pos = b.next;

            const start = pos;
            const finish = start + b.value;

            bodies.push({
                index: i,
                start,
                end: finish
            });

            pos = finish;
        }

        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TRACE LOCAL DEFINITIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE PARAM0 LOCAL DEFINITIONS OF FUNC 248 CALLS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const [funcText, targets] of Object.entries(TARGETS)) {

    const func = Number(funcText);
    const body = bodies[func];

    if (!body) continue;

    const ins = decode(body);

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`CALLER FUNC ${func}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const target of targets) {

        const callIndex =
            ins.findIndex(x => x.pos === target.addr);

        if (callIndex < 0) {
            console.log(
                `CALL NOT FOUND @ 0x${target.addr.toString(16)}`
            );
            continue;
        }

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `CALL 248 @ 0x${target.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
        console.log(`PARAM0 = local ${target.local}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        let found = 0;

        // Look backwards for assignments to param0 local.
        for (let i = callIndex - 1; i >= 0 && found < 8; i--) {

            const t = ins[i].text;

            if (
                t === `local.set ${target.local}` ||
                t === `local.tee ${target.local}`
            ) {

                console.log(
                    `DEF ${found + 1} @ 0x${ins[i].pos
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(8, "0")}`
                );

                const start = Math.max(0, i - 10);

                for (let j = start; j <= i; j++) {

                    const marker =
                        j === i ? ">>> " : "    ";

                    console.log(
                        marker +
                        "0x" +
                        ins[j].pos
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(8, "0") +
                        "  " +
                        ins[j].text
                    );
                }

                console.log("");

                found++;
            }
        }

        if (found === 0) {
            console.log(
                "NO LOCAL SET/TEE DEFINITION FOUND BEFORE CALL"
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace252d20Store8.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 0x00252d20;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readI32Const(pos: number): {
  value: number;
  next: number;
} {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      break;
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: result | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function readMemArg(pos: number): {
  align: number;
  offset: number;
  next: number;
} {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

function readString(pos: number): {
  value: string;
  next: number;
} {
  const r = readULEB(pos);
  const end = r.next + r.value;

  return {
    value: wasm.subarray(r.next, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end,
  };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const sections: {
  id: number;
  payloadStart: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const end = p + size.value;

  sections.push({
    id,
    payloadStart,
    end,
  });

  p = end;
}

const importSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 2);
const funcSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Missing FUNCTION or CODE section");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let importedCount = 0;

if (importSec) {
  p = importSec.payloadStart;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const mod = readString(p);
    p = mod.next;

    const name = readString(p);
    p = name.next;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    switch (kind) {
      case 0x00: {
        const type = readULEB(p);
        p = type.next;
        importedCount++;
        break;
      }

      case 0x01: {
        p++;

        const flags = wasm[p++];

        p = readULEB(p).next;

        if (flags & 1) {
          p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        break;
      }

      case 0x02: {
        const flags = wasm[p++];

        p = readULEB(p).next;

        if (flags & 1) {
          p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        break;
      }

      case 0x03:
        p += 2;
        break;

      case 0x04:
        p++;
        p = readULEB(p).next;
        break;

      default:
        throw new Error(
          `Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`
        );
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

p = funcSec.payloadStart;

{
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    functionTypes.push(r.value);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

interface Body {
  globalFunc: number;
  ordinal: number;
  typeIndex: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  codeStart: number;
  codeEnd: number;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

p = codeSec.payloadStart;

{
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

    const localDecls = readULEB(p);
    p = localDecls.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      p++;
    }

    const codeStart = p;

    bodies.push({
      globalFunc: importedCount + ordinal,
      ordinal,
      typeIndex: functionTypes[ordinal],
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      codeStart,
      codeEnd: bodyEnd,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// INSTRUCTION DECODER
// ------------------------------------------------------------

interface Instr {
  addr: number;
  op: number;
  text: string;
  next: number;
  constValue?: number;
  memOffset?: number;
}

function decodeOne(pos: number): Instr {
  const addr = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  let text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  let constValue: number | undefined;
  let memOffset: number | undefined;

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `block type=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x03: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `loop type=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x04: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `if type=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x05:
      text = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text = "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const count = readULEB(pos);
      pos = count.next;

      const labels: number[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        labels.push(r.value);
      }

      const def = readULEB(pos);
      pos = def.next;

      text =
        `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`;

      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      text = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `call func=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = readULEB(pos);
      pos = t.next;

      const table = readULEB(pos);
      pos = table.next;

      text =
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;

      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      text = "drop";
      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      memOffset = m.offset;
      text =
        `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      memOffset = m.offset;
      text =
        `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2c: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      memOffset = m.offset;
      text =
        `i32.load8_s align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      memOffset = m.offset;
      text =
        `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      memOffset = m.offset;
      text =
        `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3a: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      memOffset = m.offset;
      text =
        `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readI32Const(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      constValue = r.value;
      text = `i32.const ${hex(r.value)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      text = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      text = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      text = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x48:
      text = "i32.lt_s";
      break;

    case 0x49:
      text = "i32.lt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4a:
      text = "i32.gt_s";
      break;

    case 0x4b:
      text = "i32.gt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4c:
      text = "i32.le_s";
      break;

    case 0x4d:
      text = "i32.le_u";
      break;

    case 0x4e:
      text = "i32.ge_s";
      break;

    case 0x4f:
      text = "i32.ge_u";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      text = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text = "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x6c:
      text = "i32.mul";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      text = "i32.and";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      text = "i32.or";
      break;

    case 0x73:
      text = "i32.xor";
      break;

    case 0x74:
      text = "i32.shl";
      break;

    case 0x75:
      text = "i32.shr_s";
      break;

    case 0x76:
      text = "i32.shr_u";
      break;
  }

  return {
    addr,
    op,
    text,
    next: pos,
    constValue,
    memOffset,
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SCAN
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE 0x252D20 STORE8");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET TABLE = ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${importedCount}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${bodies.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let totalCandidates = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {
  const instructions: Instr[] = [];

  let q = body.codeStart;

  while (q < body.codeEnd) {
    const ins = decodeOne(q);
    instructions.push(ins);

    if (ins.next <= q) {
      break;
    }

    q = ins.next;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const store = instructions[i];

    if (store.op !== 0x3a) {
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Look backwards up to 16 instructions for:
     *
     *   i32.const 0x252d20
     *
     * or an equivalent nearby address-building sequence.
     */
    let targetConstIndex = -1;

    for (
      let j = Math.max(0, i - 16);
      j < i;
      j++
    ) {
      if (
        instructions[j].op === 0x41 &&
        instructions[j].constValue === TARGET
      ) {
        targetConstIndex = j;
      }
    }

    /*
     * Also detect the common pattern:
     *
     *   i32.const 0x252d20
     *   ...
     *   i32.add
     *   ...
     *   i32.store8
     */
    if (targetConstIndex < 0) {
      continue;
    }

    totalCandidates++;

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNC ${body.globalFunc}`);
    console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${body.ordinal}`);
    console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${body.typeIndex}`);
    console.log(
      `BODY = ${hex(body.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd)}`
    );
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const start = Math.max(0, targetConstIndex - 4);
    const end = Math.min(
      instructions.length,
      i + 4
    );

    for (let k = start; k < end; k++) {
      const x = instructions[k];

      const marker =
        k === targetConstIndex
          ? "TARGET"
          : k === i
          ? "STORE8"
          : "      ";

      console.log(
        `${marker} ${hex(x.addr)}  ${x.text}`
      );
    }

    /*
     * Try to identify an immediate value immediately before
     * store8. In the usual pattern:
     *
     *   ... address
     *   i32.const VALUE
     *   i32.store8
     */
    let valueConst: number | null = null;

    for (
      let j = i - 1;
      j >= Math.max(0, i - 5);
      j--
    ) {
      if (instructions[j].op === 0x41) {
        valueConst = instructions[j].constValue ?? null;
        break;
      }

      /*
       * Stop if we hit another store/load/call that likely
       * changes the stack relationship.
       */
      if (
        instructions[j].op === 0x10 ||
        instructions[j].op === 0x11 ||
        instructions[j].op === 0x28 ||
        instructions[j].op === 0x2c ||
        instructions[j].op === 0x2d ||
        instructions[j].op === 0x36 ||
        instructions[j].op === 0x3a
      ) {
        break;
      }
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    if (valueConst !== null) {
      console.log(
        `POSSIBLE STORED VALUE = ${hex(valueConst)} (${valueConst & 0xff})`
      );

      if ((valueConst & 0xff) === 0xff) {
        console.log(
          ">>> IMPORTANT: STORE8 VALUE = 0xFF"
        );
      }

      if ((valueConst & 0xff) === 0x01) {
        console.log(
          ">>> TABLE ACTIVATION VALUE = 0x01"
        );
      }

      if ((valueConst & 0xff) === 0x00) {
        console.log(
          ">>> TABLE CLEAR VALUE = 0x00"
        );
      }
    } else {
      console.log(
        "POSSIBLE STORED VALUE = DYNAMIC / NOT RESOLVED"
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL STORE8 CANDIDATES = ${totalCandidates}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET = 0x00252d20");
console.log();
console.log("LOOK FOR:");
console.log("  • writes of 0xFF");
console.log("  • writes of 0x01");
console.log("  • writes of 0x00");
console.log("  • address construction using 0x252d20");
console.log("  • calls immediately before/after STORE8");
console.log();
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace252d20Table.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 0x00252d20;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readString(pos: number): { value: string; next: number } {
  const r = readULEB(pos);
  const end = r.next + r.value;

  return {
    value: wasm.subarray(r.next, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end,
  };
}

function readSections() {
  const sections: {
    id: number;
    payloadStart: number;
    end: number;
  }[] = [];

  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    sections.push({
      id,
      payloadStart,
      end,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return sections;
}

const sections = readSections();

const importSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 2);
const funcSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Missing FUNCTION or CODE section");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let importedCount = 0;

if (importSec) {
  let p = importSec.payloadStart;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const mod = readString(p);
    p = mod.next;

    const name = readString(p);
    p = name.next;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    switch (kind) {
      case 0x00: {
        const type = readULEB(p);
        p = type.next;
        importedCount++;
        break;
      }

      case 0x01: {
        p++; // elem type

        const flags = wasm[p++];

        p = readULEB(p).next;

        if (flags & 1) {
          p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        break;
      }

      case 0x02: {
        const flags = wasm[p++];

        p = readULEB(p).next;

        if (flags & 1) {
          p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        break;
      }

      case 0x03:
        p += 2;
        break;

      case 0x04:
        p++;
        p = readULEB(p).next;
        break;

      default:
        throw new Error(`Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`);
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

{
  let p = funcSec.payloadStart;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    functionTypes.push(r.value);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

interface Body {
  globalFunc: number;
  ordinal: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  codeStart: number;
  codeEnd: number;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

{
  let p = codeSec.payloadStart;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

    const localCount = readULEB(p);
    p = localCount.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      p++;
    }

    const codeStart = p;

    bodies.push({
      globalFunc: importedCount + ordinal,
      ordinal,
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd,
      codeStart,
      codeEnd: bodyEnd,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CONSTANT / MEMARG DECODERS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function readI32Const(pos: number): {
  value: number;
  next: number;
} {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      break;
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: result | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function readMemArg(pos: number): {
  align: number;
  offset: number;
  next: number;
} {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TRACK SIMPLE STACK CONSTANTS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function getConstBefore(
  start: number,
  targetOffset: number
): number | null {
  let p = start;
  let lastConst: number | null = null;

  while (p < targetOffset) {
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = readI32Const(p);
      p = r.next;
      lastConst = r.value;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2a ||
      op === 0x2b ||
      op === 0x2c ||
      op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x2e ||
      op === 0x2f ||
      op === 0x30 ||
      op === 0x31 ||
      op === 0x32 ||
      op === 0x33 ||
      op === 0x34 ||
      op === 0x35 ||
      op === 0x36 ||
      op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x38 ||
      op === 0x39 ||
      op === 0x3a ||
      op === 0x3b ||
      op === 0x3c ||
      op === 0x3d ||
      op === 0x3e
    ) {
      p = readMemArg(p).next;
      lastConst = null;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d || op === 0x10) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      lastConst = null;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      lastConst = null;
      continue;
    }
  }

  return lastConst;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// MAIN SCAN
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE 0x252D20 TABLE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET TABLE = ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${importedCount}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${bodies.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log();

let totalHits = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {
  let p = body.codeStart;

  const hits: {
    addr: number;
    kind: "LOAD8" | "STORE8" | "LOAD32" | "STORE32";
    offset: number;
    align: number;
    baseHint: number | null;
  }[] = [];

  while (p < body.codeEnd) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    // i32.load
    if (op === 0x28) {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;

      if (m.offset === TARGET) {
        hits.push({
          addr,
          kind: "LOAD32",
          offset: m.offset,
          align: m.align,
          baseHint: getConstBefore(body.codeStart, addr),
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    // i32.load8_s
    if (op === 0x2c) {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;

      if (m.offset === TARGET) {
        hits.push({
          addr,
          kind: "LOAD8",
          offset: m.offset,
          align: m.align,
          baseHint: getConstBefore(body.codeStart, addr),
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    // i32.load8_u
    if (op === 0x2d) {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;

      if (m.offset === TARGET) {
        hits.push({
          addr,
          kind: "LOAD8",
          offset: m.offset,
          align: m.align,
          baseHint: getConstBefore(body.codeStart, addr),
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    // i32.store
    if (op === 0x36) {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;

      if (m.offset === TARGET) {
        hits.push({
          addr,
          kind: "STORE32",
          offset: m.offset,
          align: m.align,
          baseHint: getConstBefore(body.codeStart, addr),
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    // i32.store8
    if (op === 0x3a) {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;

      if (m.offset === TARGET) {
        hits.push({
          addr,
          kind: "STORE8",
          offset: m.offset,
          align: m.align,
          baseHint: getConstBefore(body.codeStart, addr),
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    // instructions with immediates
    if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04
    ) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d ||
      op === 0x10
    ) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23 ||
      op === 0x24
    ) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      p = readI32Const(p).next;
      continue;
    }
  }

  if (hits.length === 0) {
    continue;
  }

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${body.globalFunc}`);
  console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${body.ordinal}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
  );
  console.log(`HITS = ${hits.length}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (const h of hits) {
    console.log(
      `${hex(h.addr)}  ${h.kind.padEnd(7)} ` +
      `offset=${hex(h.offset)} align=${h.align}`
    );

    if (h.baseHint !== null) {
      console.log(
        `             previous i32.const = ${hex(h.baseHint)}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log();
  totalHits += hits.length;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL HITS = ${totalHits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NOTE:");
console.log("This scan matches direct memory offsets equal to 0x252D20.");
console.log("Indexed accesses using another base + dynamic offset are");
console.log("reported only when their encoded memarg offset is exactly");
console.log("0x252D20.");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\trace2559acTableV30.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_BASE = 0x2559ac;
const TARGET_MASK = 0x255880;

const TARGETS = [
    0x25587c,
    0x255880,
    0x255884,
    0x255888,
    0x25588c,
    0x255890,
    0x255894,
    0x255898,
    0x25589c,
    0x2558a0,
    0x2558a4,
    0x2559ac,
    0x255a5c,
];

const FUNCTIONS = [
    { index: 259, body: 176 },
    { index: 1021, body: 938 },
    { index: 1423, body: 1340 },
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB too long");
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("SLEB too long");
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function u32(pos: number) {
    return wasm.readUInt32LE(pos);
}

interface Instr {
    pos: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
    memOffset?: number;
    memAlign?: number;
}

function decodeBody(start: number, end: number): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];
    let p = start;

    // locals
    const localGroups = wasm[p++];
    let localCount = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        localCount += r.value;

        // value type
        p++;
    }

    // Conservative decoder.
    // We decode the instructions relevant to this investigation.
    while (p < end) {
        const pos = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        try {
            switch (op) {
                case 0x0b:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "end" });
                    break;

                case 0x02:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "block" });
                    p++; // blocktype
                    break;

                case 0x03:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "loop" });
                    p++;
                    break;

                case 0x04:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "if" });
                    p++;
                    break;

                case 0x05:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "else" });
                    break;

                case 0x0c: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `br ${r.value}`,
                        imm: r.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0d: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `br_if ${r.value}`,
                        imm: r.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x10: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `call ${r.value}`,
                        imm: r.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x20: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `local.get ${r.value}`,
                        imm: r.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x21: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `local.set ${r.value}`,
                        imm: r.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x22: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
                        imm: r.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x23: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `global.get ${r.value}`,
                        imm: r.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x24: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `global.set ${r.value}`,
                        imm: r.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x28:
                case 0x29:
                case 0x2a:
                case 0x2b:
                case 0x2c:
                case 0x2d:
                case 0x2e:
                case 0x2f: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;

                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `memory-op 0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`,
                        memOffset: o.value,
                        memAlign: a.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x36:
                case 0x37:
                case 0x38:
                case 0x39:
                case 0x3a:
                case 0x3b: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;

                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `memory-op 0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`,
                        memOffset: o.value,
                        memAlign: a.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x41: {
                    const r = readSLEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`,
                        imm: r.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                case 0x42: {
                    const r = readSLEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `i64.const ${r.value}`,
                        imm: r.value
                    });
                    break;
                }

                // comparison / numeric operations
                case 0x45:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.eqz" });
                    break;

                case 0x46:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.eq" });
                    break;

                case 0x47:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.ne" });
                    break;

                case 0x4a:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.lt_s" });
                    break;

                case 0x4b:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.lt_u" });
                    break;

                case 0x4c:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.le_s" });
                    break;

                case 0x4d:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.le_u" });
                    break;

                case 0x4e:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.gt_s" });
                    break;

                case 0x4f:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.gt_u" });
                    break;

                case 0x50:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.ge_s" });
                    break;

                case 0x51:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.ge_u" });
                    break;

                case 0x67:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.clz" });
                    break;

                case 0x68:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.ctz" });
                    break;

                case 0x69:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.popcnt" });
                    break;

                case 0x6a:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.add" });
                    break;

                case 0x6b:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.sub" });
                    break;

                case 0x6c:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.mul" });
                    break;

                case 0x71:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.and" });
                    break;

                case 0x72:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.or" });
                    break;

                case 0x73:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.xor" });
                    break;

                case 0x74:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.shl" });
                    break;

                case 0x75:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.shr_s" });
                    break;

                case 0x76:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.shr_u" });
                    break;

                case 0x77:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.rotl" });
                    break;

                case 0x78:
                    out.push({ pos, op, text: "i32.rotr" });
                    break;

                default:
                    out.push({
                        pos,
                        op,
                        text: `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
                    });

                    // For unknown opcodes we cannot safely skip.
                    // Stop decoding rather than fabricate instruction boundaries.
                    return out;
            }
        } catch {
            out.push({
                pos,
                op,
                text: `decode-error-opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            });
            return out;
        }
    }

    return out;
}

function locateCodeSection(): { start: number; end: number } {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[p++];

        const r = readULEB(p);
        const size = r.value;
        p = r.next;

        const payloadStart = p;
        const payloadEnd = p + size;

        if (sectionId === 10) {
            return {
                start: payloadStart,
                end: payloadEnd
            };
        }

        p = payloadEnd;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function getFunctionBodies(): { start: number; end: number }[] {
    const sec = locateCodeSection();

    let p = sec.start;

    const countR = readULEB(p);
    const count = countR.value;
    p = countR.next;

    const bodies: { start: number; end: number }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        const bodySize = r.value;
        p = r.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        bodies.push({
            start: bodyStart,
            end: bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function isTargetOffset(offset: number | undefined) {
    if (offset === undefined) return false;

    return TARGETS.includes(offset >>> 0);
}

function printWindow(instrs: Instr[], hitIndex: number, radius = 12) {
    const from = Math.max(0, hitIndex - radius);
    const to = Math.min(instrs.length, hitIndex + radius + 1);

    for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
        const x = instrs[i];
        const mark = i === hitIndex ? " >>> " : "     ";

        console.log(
            `${mark}${hex(x.pos)}  ${x.text}`
        );
    }
}

function describeWriter(instrs: Instr[], hitIndex: number) {
    const hit = instrs[hitIndex];

    if (hit.op < 0x36 || hit.op > 0x3b) {
        return;
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("    DATA-FLOW PREVIEW:");

    const before = instrs.slice(Math.max(0, hitIndex - 10), hitIndex);

    for (const x of before) {
        console.log(`      ${hex(x.pos)}  ${x.text}`);
    }

    console.log(`      >>> STORE at ${hex(hit.pos)}`);
}

function analyzeFunction(
    functionIndex: number,
    body: { start: number; end: number }
) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNCTION ${functionIndex}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(`body start : ${hex(body.start)}`);
    console.log(`body end   : ${hex(body.end)}`);

    const instrs = decodeBody(body.start, body.end);

    console.log(`decoded instructions : ${instrs.length}`);

    const hits: { i: number; kind: string; offset: number }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < instrs.length; i++) {
        const x = instrs[i];

        if (x.memOffset === undefined) continue;

        if (!isTargetOffset(x.memOffset)) continue;

        const isLoad = x.op >= 0x28 && x.op <= 0x2f;
        const isStore = x.op >= 0x36 && x.op <= 0x3b;

        hits.push({
            i,
            kind: isLoad ? "LOAD" : isStore ? "STORE" : "MEM",
            offset: x.memOffset
        });
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`TARGET MEMORY ACCESSES : ${hits.length}`);

    for (const h of hits) {
        const x = instrs[h.i];

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `${h.kind.padEnd(6)} @ ${hex(x.pos)}  offset=${hex(h.offset)}`
        );

        printWindow(instrs, h.i, 8);

        if (h.kind === "STORE") {
            describeWriter(instrs, h.i);
        }
    }

    // Special scan:
    // detect the characteristic:
    //
    // local.get X
    // i32.const 2
    // i32.shl
    // memory-op offset=0x2559ac
    //
    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("INDEXED 0x2559AC PATTERNS");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let indexed = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i + 3 < instrs.length; i++) {
        const a = instrs[i];
        const b = instrs[i + 1];
        const c = instrs[i + 2];
        const d = instrs[i + 3];

        if (
            a.text.startsWith("local.get ") &&
            b.text === "i32.const 2 (0x00000002)" &&
            c.text === "i32.shl" &&
            d.memOffset === TARGET_BASE
        ) {
            indexed++;

            console.log("");
            console.log(`PATTERN #${indexed}`);
            console.log(`  ${hex(a.pos)}  ${a.text}`);
            console.log(`  ${hex(b.pos)}  ${b.text}`);
            console.log(`  ${hex(c.pos)}  ${c.text}`);
            console.log(`  ${hex(d.pos)}  ${d.text}`);

            const local = a.imm;

            if (local !== undefined) {
                console.log(`  INDEX LOCAL : ${local}`);
                console.log(
                    `  ADDRESS     : 0x2559ac + (local${local} << 2)`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`INDEXED PATTERN COUNT : ${indexed}`);
}

function main() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("  0x2559AC TABLE / 0x255880 BITMAP ANALYSIS V30");
    console.log("  READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("");
    console.log(`WASM : ${WASM}`);
    console.log(`SIZE : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

    const bodies = getFunctionBodies();

    console.log("");
    console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES : ${bodies.length}`);

    for (const f of FUNCTIONS) {
        if (!bodies[f.body]) {
            console.log(`Function ${f.index}: body ${f.body} NOT FOUND`);
            continue;
        }

        analyzeFunction(f.index, bodies[f.body]);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("FINAL TARGETS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const t of TARGETS) {
        console.log(`  ${hex(t)}`);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
}

main();

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace2774Callers.ts
