import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 8620;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

type Instr = {
    offset: number;
    opcode: number;
    text: string;
    local?: number;
    target?: number;
};

function readU32Leb(pos: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32Leb(pos: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= ~0 << shift;
            }

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }
    }
}

function readU64Leb(pos: number): {
    value: bigint;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function skipMemArg(pos: number): number {
    const a = readU32Leb(pos);
    return readU32Leb(a.next).next;
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number): {
    instr: Instr;
    next: number;
} {
    const start = pos;
    const op = wasm[pos++];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x00:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "unreachable",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x01:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "nop",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const blockType = readS32Leb(pos);
            pos = blockType.next;

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text:
                        op === 0x02
                            ? `block ${blockType.value}`
                            : op === 0x03
                              ? `loop ${blockType.value}`
                              : `if ${blockType.value}`,
                },
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        case 0x05:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "else",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x0b:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "end",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `br ${x.value}`,
                    target: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `br_if ${x.value}`,
                    target: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const n = readU32Leb(pos);
            pos = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                pos = readU32Leb(pos).next;
            }

            pos = readU32Leb(pos).next;

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "br_table",
                },
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        case 0x0f:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "return",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `call ${x.value}`,
                    target: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const type = readU32Leb(pos);
            const table = readU32Leb(type.next);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text:
                        `call_indirect type=${type.value} ` +
                        `table=${table.value}`,
                },
                next: table.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
                    local: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
                    local: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
                    local: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e:
            pos = skipMemArg(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text:
                        `memory-op 0x${op
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0")}`,
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x3f:
        case 0x40:
            pos++;

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text:
                        `memory-op 0x${op
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0")}`,
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readS32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = readU64Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x43:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "f32.const",
                },
                next: pos + 4,
            };

        case 0x44:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "f64.const",
                },
                next: pos + 8,
            };

        default:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text:
                        `op=0x${op
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0")}`,
                },
                next: pos,
            };
    }
}

function findCodeSection(): {
    start: number;
    size: number;
} {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = readU32Leb(p);
        p = s.next;

        if (id === 10) {
            return {
                start: p,
                size: s.value,
            };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function getBodies(): {
    funcIndex: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] {
    const sec = findCodeSection();

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = readU32Leb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const bodies: {
        funcIndex: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const size = readU32Leb(p);
        p = size.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + size.value;

        bodies.push({
            funcIndex: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + i,
            start,
            end,
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function decodeBody(
    start: number,
    end: number,
): Instr[] {
    let p = start;

    const localDecls = readU32Leb(p);
    p = localDecls.next;

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < localDecls.value;
        i++
    ) {
        const n = readU32Leb(p);
        p = n.next;

        p++;
    }

    const result: Instr[] = [];

    while (p < end) {
        const d = decodeInstruction(p);

        if (d.next <= p) {
            throw new Error(
                `Decoder stalled at 0x${p.toString(16)}`,
            );
        }

        result.push(d.instr);
        p = d.next;
    }

    return result;
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return (
        "0x" +
        n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
    );
}

export async function run() {
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " FUNCTION 8620 - FIRST LOCAL9 ASSIGNMENT",
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log(`Target    : Function ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log();

    const bodies = getBodies();

    console.log(
        `Function bodies : ${bodies.length}`,
    );
    console.log();

    const targetBody = bodies.find(
        b => b.funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC,
    );

    if (!targetBody) {
        console.log(
            `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} not found.`,
        );
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        `Function range : ${hex(targetBody.start)} .. ${hex(targetBody.end)}`,
    );
    console.log();

    let instructions: Instr[];

    try {
        instructions = decodeBody(
            targetBody.start,
            targetBody.end,
        );
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            "DECODE ERROR",
        );

        console.log(
            e instanceof Error
                ? e.message
                : String(e),
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(
        `Decoded instructions : ${instructions.length}`,
    );
    console.log();

    const local9Assignments: {
        index: number;
        instr: Instr;
    }[] = [];

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < instructions.length;
        i++
    ) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        if (
            (
                x.opcode === 0x21 ||
                x.opcode === 0x22
            ) &&
            x.local === 9
        ) {
            local9Assignments.push({
                index: i,
                instr: x,
            });
        }
    }

    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " LOCAL9 ASSIGNMENTS",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(
        `Total local9 assignments : ${local9Assignments.length}`,
    );
    console.log();

    if (local9Assignments.length === 0) {
        console.log(
            "No local.set 9 / local.tee 9 found.",
        );

        console.log();

        console.log(
            "============================================================",
        );
        console.log(
            " END",
        );
        console.log(
            "============================================================",
        );

        return;
    }

    for (
        let n = 0;
        n < local9Assignments.length;
        n++
    ) {
        const a = local9Assignments[n];

        console.log(
            `LOCAL9 #${n + 1} @ ${hex(a.instr.offset)}`,
        );

        console.log(
            `  ${a.instr.text}`,
        );

        console.log();

        const from = Math.max(
            0,
            a.index - 40,
        );

        const to = Math.min(
            instructions.length,
            a.index + 13,
        );

        console.log(
            "  --- surrounding instructions ---",
        );

        for (
            let i = from;
            i < to;
            i++
        ) {
            const prefix =
                i === a.index
                    ? ">>>"
                    : "   ";

            console.log(
                `${prefix} ${hex(
                    instructions[i].offset,
                )} ${instructions[i].text}`,
            );
        }

        console.log();

        console.log(
            "------------------------------------------------------------",
        );
        console.log();
    }

    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " LOCAL9 USES",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    let useCount = 0;

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < instructions.length;
        i++
    ) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        if (
            x.opcode === 0x20 &&
            x.local === 9
        ) {
            useCount++;

            console.log(
                `USE #${useCount} @ ${hex(x.offset)}`,
            );

            console.log(
                `  ${x.text}`,
            );

            const from = Math.max(
                0,
                i - 8,
            );

            const to = Math.min(
                instructions.length,
                i + 6,
            );

            for (
                let j = from;
                j < to;
                j++
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `  ${hex(
                        instructions[j].offset,
                    )} ${instructions[j].text}`,
                );
            }

            console.log();
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `Total local9 uses : ${useCount}`,
    );

    console.log();

    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " TARGET MEMORY WRITE CONTEXT",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    const targetMemory =
        1837608;

    console.log(
        `Target memory address : ${targetMemory}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `Target memory hex     : ${hex(targetMemory)}`,
    );

    console.log();

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < instructions.length;
        i++
    ) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        if (
            x.opcode === 0x41 &&
            x.text ===
                `i32.const ${targetMemory}`
        ) {
            console.log(
                `CONST target @ ${hex(x.offset)}`,
            );

            const from = Math.max(
                0,
                i - 6,
            );

            const to = Math.min(
                instructions.length,
                i + 8,
            );

            for (
                let j = from;
                j < to;
                j++
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `  ${hex(
                        instructions[j].offset,
                    )} ${instructions[j].text}`,
                );
            }

            console.log();
        }
    }

    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " SUMMARY",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(
        `Function        : ${TARGET_FUNC}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `Range           : ${hex(targetBody.start)} .. ${hex(targetBody.end)}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `Instructions    : ${instructions.length}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `local9 assigns  : ${local9Assignments.length}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `local9 uses     : ${useCount}`,
    );

    console.log();

    console.log(
        "NEXT:",
    );

    console.log(
        "Trace the first local9 assignment backward.",
    );

    console.log(
        "We need to identify the exact stack value that",
    );

    console.log(
        "becomes local9 before Function 8620 writes it",
    );

    console.log(
        "to memory[1837608].",
    );

    console.log();
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2774;
const TARGET_LOCAL = 2;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function readS32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << shift);
            }

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function readU64Leb(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error("ULEB64 overflow");
        }
    }
}

function readName(pos: number): { value: string; next: number } {
    const n = readU32Leb(pos);
    const end = n.next + n.value;
    return {
        value: Buffer.from(wasm.subarray(n.next, end)).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

type Instr = {
    offset: number;
    opcode: number;
    text: string;
    local?: number;
    memOffset?: number;
    memAlign?: number;
    target?: number;
};

function parseMemArg(pos: number): {
    next: number;
    align: number;
    offset: number;
} {
    const a = readU32Leb(pos);
    const o = readU32Leb(a.next);

    return {
        next: o.next,
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
    };
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number): Instr {
    const start = pos;
    const op = wasm[pos++];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x0b:
            return { offset: start, opcode: op, text: "end" };

        case 0x00:
            return { offset: start, opcode: op, text: "unreachable" };

        case 0x01:
            return { offset: start, opcode: op, text: "nop" };

        case 0x02: {
            const b = wasm[pos++];
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `block type=0x${b.toString(16)}`,
            };
        }

        case 0x03: {
            const b = wasm[pos++];
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `loop type=0x${b.toString(16)}`,
            };
        }

        case 0x04: {
            const b = wasm[pos++];
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `if type=0x${b.toString(16)}`,
            };
        }

        case 0x05:
            return { offset: start, opcode: op, text: "else" };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `br ${x.value}`,
                target: x.value,
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `br_if ${x.value}`,
                target: x.value,
            };
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const n = readU32Leb(pos);
            pos = n.next;

            const labels: number[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                const x = readU32Leb(pos);
                pos = x.next;
                labels.push(x.value);
            }

            const def = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`,
            };
        }

        case 0x0f:
            return { offset: start, opcode: op, text: "return" };

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `call ${x.value}`,
                target: x.value,
            };
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const type = readU32Leb(pos);
            const table = readU32Leb(type.next);

            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`,
            };
        }

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
                local: x.value,
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
                local: x.value,
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
                local: x.value,
            };
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
            };
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
            };
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const m = parseMemArg(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i32.load offset=${m.offset}`,
                memOffset: m.offset,
                memAlign: m.align,
            };
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const m = parseMemArg(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i64.load offset=${m.offset}`,
                memOffset: m.offset,
                memAlign: m.align,
            };
        }

        case 0x2c: {
            const m = parseMemArg(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i32.load8_s offset=${m.offset}`,
                memOffset: m.offset,
                memAlign: m.align,
            };
        }

        case 0x2d: {
            const m = parseMemArg(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i32.load8_u offset=${m.offset}`,
                memOffset: m.offset,
                memAlign: m.align,
            };
        }

        case 0x2e: {
            const m = parseMemArg(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i32.load16_s offset=${m.offset}`,
                memOffset: m.offset,
                memAlign: m.align,
            };
        }

        case 0x2f: {
            const m = parseMemArg(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i32.load16_u offset=${m.offset}`,
                memOffset: m.offset,
                memAlign: m.align,
            };
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const m = parseMemArg(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i32.store offset=${m.offset}`,
                memOffset: m.offset,
                memAlign: m.align,
            };
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const m = parseMemArg(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i64.store offset=${m.offset}`,
                memOffset: m.offset,
                memAlign: m.align,
            };
        }

        case 0x3a: {
            const m = parseMemArg(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i32.store8 offset=${m.offset}`,
                memOffset: m.offset,
                memAlign: m.align,
            };
        }

        case 0x3b: {
            const m = parseMemArg(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i32.store16 offset=${m.offset}`,
                memOffset: m.offset,
                memAlign: m.align,
            };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readS32Leb(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = readU64Leb(pos);
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            };
        }

        default:
            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
            };
    }
}

function findCodeSection(): { start: number; size: number } {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];
        const s = readU32Leb(p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;

        if (id === 10) {
            return {
                start,
                size: s.value,
            };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function getFunctionBodies(): {
    funcIndex: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] {
    const sec = findCodeSection();

    let p = sec.start;
    const count = readU32Leb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const bodies: {
        funcIndex: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    const importedFunctions = 83;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const sz = readU32Leb(p);
        p = sz.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + sz.value;

        bodies.push({
            funcIndex: importedFunctions + i,
            start: bodyStart,
            end: bodyEnd,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function decodeBody(
    bodyStart: number,
    bodyEnd: number,
): { locals: number; instructions: Instr[] } {
    let p = bodyStart;

    const localDeclCount = readU32Leb(p);
    p = localDeclCount.next;

    let totalLocals = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount.value; i++) {
        const n = readU32Leb(p);
        p = n.next;

        // valtype
        p++;

        totalLocals += n.value;
    }

    const instructions: Instr[] = [];

    while (p < bodyEnd) {
        const ins = decodeInstruction(p);

        /*
         * Determine next instruction by decoding opcode operands again.
         * This avoids depending on text length.
         */
        const before = p;
        p++;

        switch (ins.opcode) {
            case 0x02:
            case 0x03:
            case 0x04:
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x0c:
            case 0x0d:
            case 0x10:
            case 0x20:
            case 0x21:
            case 0x22:
            case 0x23:
            case 0x24:
                p = readU32Leb(p).next;
                break;

            case 0x0e: {
                const n = readU32Leb(p);
                p = n.next;

                for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                    p = readU32Leb(p).next;
                }

                p = readU32Leb(p).next;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                p = readU32Leb(p).next;
                p = readU32Leb(p).next;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28:
            case 0x29:
            case 0x2c:
            case 0x2d:
            case 0x2e:
            case 0x2f:
            case 0x36:
            case 0x37:
            case 0x3a:
            case 0x3b:
                p = readU32Leb(p).next;
                p = readU32Leb(p).next;
                break;

            case 0x41:
                p = readS32Leb(p).next;
                break;

            case 0x42:
                p = readU64Leb(p).next;
                break;

            default:
                break;
        }

        if (p <= before) {
            throw new Error(
                `Decoder did not advance at 0x${before.toString(16)}`,
            );
        }

        instructions.push(ins);

        if (ins.opcode === 0x0b && p >= bodyEnd) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return {
        locals: totalLocals,
        instructions,
    };
}

function hex(x: number): string {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const bodies = getFunctionBodies();

if (!body) {
    throw new Error(`Function ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`);
}

const decoded = decodeBody(body.start, body.end);

const fs = require("fs");

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 3317;

const b = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function leb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[pos++];
        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(x & 0x80)) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(x) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (pos < b.length) {
    const sectionId = b[pos++];

    const sr = leb(pos);
    const size = sr.value;
    pos = sr.next;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = pos;
        break;
    }

    pos += size;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");

let p = codeStart;

const countR = leb(p);
const bodyCount = countR.value;
p = countR.next;

let found = false;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const sizeR = leb(p);
    const bodySize = sizeR.value;
    p = sizeR.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const funcIndex = bodyIndex + 83;

    if (funcIndex !== TARGET) {
        p = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    found = true;

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("FUNC 3317 CALL_REF ANALYSIS");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("BODY INDEX :", bodyIndex);
    console.log("BODY START :", hex(bodyStart));
    console.log("BODY END   :", hex(bodyEnd));
    console.log("SIZE       :", bodySize);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let q = bodyStart;

    const localsR = leb(q);
    const localDeclCount = localsR.value;
    q = localsR.next;

    console.log("LOCAL DECLS :", localDeclCount);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
        const n = leb(q);
        q = n.next;

        const type = b[q++];

        console.log(
            "local group",
            i,
            "count",
            n.value,
            "type",
            hex(type)
        );
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    while (q < bodyEnd) {

        const at = q;
        const op = b[q++];

        let extra = "";

        if (op === 0x14) {
            extra = "  <<< CALL_REF";
            console.log(hex(at), "call_ref", extra);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            const table = b[q++];

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "call_indirect type=" + r.value,
                "table=" + table
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "call",
                r.value
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "local.get",
                r.value
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x21) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "local.set",
                r.value
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x22) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "local.tee",
                r.value
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x23) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "global.get",
                r.value
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x24) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "global.set",
                r.value
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "i32.const",
                r.value,
                "(" + hex(r.value) + ")"
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "i64.const",
                r.value
            );
            continue;
        }

        // Loads / stores
        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

            const alignR = leb(q);
            q = alignR.next;

            const offsetR = leb(q);
            q = offsetR.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "MEMORY",
                "opcode=" + hex(op),
                "align=" + alignR.value,
                "offset=" + offsetR.value
            );

            continue;
        }

        // Block / loop / if
        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {

            const blockType = b[q++];

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                op === 0x02 ? "block" :
                op === 0x03 ? "loop" : "if",
                "type=" + hex(blockType)
            );

            continue;
        }

        // br / br_if
        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if",
                r.value
            );

            continue;
        }

        // br_table
        if (op === 0x0e) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            const count = r.value;

            const labels = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const x = leb(q);
                q = x.next;
                labels.push(x.value);
            }

            const def = leb(q);
            q = def.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "br_table",
                "count=" + count,
                "default=" + def.value,
                labels.length ? "labels=" + labels.join(",") : ""
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0b) {
            console.log(hex(at), "end");
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0f) {
            console.log(hex(at), "return");
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x1a) {
            console.log(hex(at), "drop");
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x1b) {
            console.log(hex(at), "select");
            continue;
        }

        // ref.null
        if (op === 0xd0) {
            const type = b[q++];

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "ref.null type=" + hex(type)
            );

            continue;
        }

        // ref.func
        if (op === 0xd2) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            console.log(
                hex(at),
                "ref.func",
                r.value,
                "<<< FUNCTION REFERENCE"
            );

            continue;
        }

        // ref.is_null
        if (op === 0xd1) {
            console.log(hex(at), "ref.is_null");
            continue;
        }

        // Everything else
        console.log(
            hex(at),
            "opcode",
            hex(op)
        );
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("END FUNC 3317");
    console.log("============================================================");

    break;
}

if (!found) {
    console.log("FUNC 3317 NOT FOUND");
}

];

function hx(n, w = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function dump(addr) {
  const start = Math.max(0, addr - RADIUS);
  const end = Math.min(d.length, addr + RADIUS + 16);

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`TARGET : ${hx(addr)}`);
  console.log(`RANGE  : ${hx(start)} .. ${hx(end - 1)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
    const q = Math.min(end, p + 16);
    const bytes = [];

    for (let i = p; i < q; i++) {
      bytes.push(d[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    console.log(`${hx(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("BYTE POSITIONS AROUND TARGET");

  for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
    const mark = p === addr ? "<<< TARGET" : "";
    console.log(
      `${hx(p)}  ${d[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} ${mark}`
    );
  }
}

FILE: scripts\trace3317InputProvenance.js

const fs = require("fs");

const file =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGETS = [6993, 6995];

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(pos);
    const size = s.value;
    pos = s.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const count = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + i,
        start: p,
        end: p + size,
        size
    });

    p += size;
}

function getBody(func) {
    return bodies.find(x => x.func === func);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Dump bytes
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function dump(start, end) {

    start = Math.max(0, start);
    end = Math.min(buf.length, end);

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {

        const e = Math.min(end, p + 16);
        const bytes = [];

        for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
            bytes.push(
                buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode selected simple instructions
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function decodeInstruction(p) {

    const op = buf[p];

    // local.get
    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);
        return {
            name: "local.get",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    // local.set
    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);
        return {
            name: "local.set",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    // local.tee
    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);
        return {
            name: "local.tee",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(p + 1);
        return {
            name: "i32.const",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    // i32.load / load8 / load16
    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x2c || op === 0x2d) {

        const align = readULEB(p + 1);
        const offset = readULEB(align.next);

        let name = "i32.load";

        if (op === 0x2c)
            name = "i32.load8_s";

        if (op === 0x2d)
            name = "i32.load8_u";

        return {
            name,
            align: align.value,
            offset: offset.value,
            next: offset.next
        };
    }

    // i32.add
    if (op === 0x6a) {
        return {
            name: "i32.add",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    // call
    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "call",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    return {
        name: "opcode " + hex(op, 2),
        next: p + 1
    };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find CALL 3317
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function findCall(body) {

    const hits = [];

    for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

        if (buf[p] !== 0x10)
            continue;

        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        if (r.value === 3317) {

            hits.push({
                addr: p,
                next: r.next
            });
        }
    }

    return hits;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Analyze backwards from call
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function analyzeCall(body, call) {

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(`CALL 3317 @ ${hex(call.addr)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("\nRAW CONTEXT:");
    dump(
        Math.max(body.start, call.addr - 100),
        Math.min(body.end, call.next + 30)
    );

    console.log("\n------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("INSTRUCTIONS BEFORE CALL");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    // Decode every byte in a limited window.
    // We are primarily interested in the last ~40 bytes.
    const start = Math.max(body.start, call.addr - 50);

    for (let p = start; p < call.addr; ) {

        const d = decodeInstruction(p);

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${d.name}` +
            (d.value !== undefined
                ? ` ${d.value}`
                : "") +
            (d.offset !== undefined
                ? ` offset=${d.offset}`
                : "")
        );

        if (d.next <= p)
            p++;

        else
            p = d.next;
    }

    console.log("\n------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("IMMEDIATE STACK PRODUCER");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    // Look backwards for the most recent instructions that can
    // plausibly produce the single i32 argument.
    //
    // Important:
    // We do NOT assume a complete stack decode here.
    // We report candidates only.

    let candidates = [];

    const start2 = Math.max(body.start, call.addr - 25);

    for (let p = start2; p < call.addr; ) {

        const d = decodeInstruction(p);

        if (
            d.name === "i32.const" ||
            d.name === "i32.load" ||
            d.name === "i32.load8_s" ||
            d.name === "i32.load8_u" ||
            d.name === "local.get" ||
            d.name === "local.tee"
        ) {
            candidates.push({
                addr: p,
                instruction: d
            });
        }

        if (d.next <= p)
            p++;

        else
            p = d.next;
    }

    for (const c of candidates) {

        console.log(
            `${hex(c.addr)}  ${c.instruction.name}` +
            (
                c.instruction.value !== undefined
                    ? ` ${c.instruction.value}`
                    : ""
            ) +
            (
                c.instruction.offset !== undefined
                    ? ` offset=${c.instruction.offset}`
                    : ""
            )
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Main
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 3317 INPUT PROVENANCE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET CALLERS : FUNC 6993 / FUNC 6995");
console.log("TARGET         : FUNC 3317");
console.log("SIGNATURE      : (i32) -> (i32)");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const func of TARGETS) {

    const body = getBody(func);

    console.log("\n############################################################");
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
    console.log("############################################################");

    if (!body) {
        console.log("BODY NOT FOUND");
        continue;
    }

    console.log(
        `BODY : ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
    );

    const calls = findCall(body);

    console.log(
        `CALL 3317 COUNT : ${calls.length}`
    );

    for (const call of calls)
        analyzeCall(body, call);
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const file =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET = 3317;

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(pos);
    const size = s.value;
    pos = s.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const count = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + i,
        start: p,
        end: p + size,
        size
    });

    p += size;
}

const body = bodies.find(x => x.func === TARGET);

if (!body)
    throw new Error("FUNC 3317 not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find local.set / local.tee local12
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function decodeLocal(pos) {

    const op = buf[pos];

    if (op !== 0x21 && op !== 0x22)
        return null;

    const r = readULEB(pos + 1);

    return {
        op,
        local: r.value,
        next: r.next
    };
}

function opName(op) {
    return op === 0x21 ? "local.set" : "local.tee";
}

function dump(start, end) {

    start = Math.max(body.start, start);
    end = Math.min(body.end, end);

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {

        const e = Math.min(end, p + 16);
        const bytes = [];

        for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
            bytes.push(
                buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 3317 - LOCAL12 PROVENANCE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
console.log(`SIZE : ${body.size}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let count12 = 0;

for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    const d = decodeLocal(p);

    if (!d)
        continue;

    if (d.local !== 12)
        continue;

    count12++;

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(`${opName(d.op)} local=12 @ ${hex(p)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("CONTEXT BEFORE:");

    dump(
        p - 120,
        p + 20
    );
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL LOCAL12 ASSIGNMENTS : ${count12}`);
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("ALL local.get 12");
console.log("============================================================");

let get12 = 0;

for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    if (buf[p] !== 0x20)
        continue;

    const r = readULEB(p + 1);

    if (r.value !== 12)
        continue;

    get12++;

    console.log(
        `local.get 12 @ ${hex(p)}`
    );
}

console.log(`TOTAL local.get 12 : ${get12}`);

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p) {
  return buf[p];
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(p++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGET = 3317;

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const id = u8(p++);

  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!codeStart) {
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let q = codeStart;

const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

let bodyStart = null;
let bodyEnd = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const sz = readULEB(q);
  q = sz.next;

  const start = q;
  const end = q + sz.value;

  const func = IMPORTS + i;

  if (func === TARGET) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  q = end;
}

if (bodyStart === null) {
  throw new Error("FUNC 3317 NOT FOUND");
}

function dump(pos, before = 24, after = 32) {
  const s = Math.max(bodyStart, pos - before);
  const e = Math.min(bodyEnd, pos + after);

  let a = [];

  for (let i = s; i < e; i++) {
    a.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
  }

  return a.join(" ");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNC 3317 LOCAL 1");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("BODY :", hex(bodyStart), "..", hex(bodyEnd));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// local1 references
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const refs = [];

for (let x = bodyStart; x < bodyEnd - 1; x++) {
  const op = u8(x);
  const imm = u8(x + 1);

  if (op === 0x20 && imm === 0x01) {
    refs.push({ addr: x, kind: "local.get 1" });
  }

  if (op === 0x21 && imm === 0x01) {
    refs.push({ addr: x, kind: "local.set 1" });
  }

  if (op === 0x22 && imm === 0x01) {
    refs.push({ addr: x, kind: "local.tee 1" });
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("LOCAL 1 REFERENCES");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("COUNT :", refs.length);

for (const r of refs) {
  console.log("");
  console.log(hex(r.addr), r.kind);
  console.log(dump(r.addr));
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Explicit memory accesses using local1.
// We look for:
//   local.get 1
//   i32.load / i64.load
//   i32.store / i64.store
//
// and especially offset 0.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("LOCAL1 + OFFSET 0 CANDIDATES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let x = bodyStart; x < bodyEnd - 2; x++) {

  // local.get 1
  if (u8(x) !== 0x20 || u8(x + 1) !== 0x01) {
    continue;
  }

  const op = u8(x + 2);

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.load
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = readULEB(x + 3);
    const off = readULEB(a.next);

    if (off.value === 0) {
      console.log("");
      console.log(
        "LOAD32 local1+0 @",
        hex(x)
      );
      console.log(dump(x));
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i64.load
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x29) {
    const a = readULEB(x + 3);
    const off = readULEB(a.next);

    if (off.value === 0) {
      console.log("");
      console.log(
        "LOAD64 local1+0 @",
        hex(x)
      );
      console.log(dump(x));
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i32.store
  //
  // Here the address must be local1 directly.
  // Stack semantics depend on preceding value.
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = readULEB(x + 3);
    const off = readULEB(a.next);

    if (off.value === 0) {
      console.log("");
      console.log(
        "STORE32 local1+0 CANDIDATE @",
        hex(x)
      );
      console.log(dump(x));
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // i64.store
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x37) {
    const a = readULEB(x + 3);
    const off = readULEB(a.next);

    if (off.value === 0) {
      console.log("");
      console.log(
        "STORE64 local1+0 CANDIDATE @",
        hex(x)
      );
      console.log(dump(x));
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Atomic accesses.
//
// Pattern:
// local.get 1
// fe <subopcode> <memarg>
//
// We specifically want offset=0.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ATOMIC LOCAL1 + OFFSET 0");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let x = bodyStart; x < bodyEnd - 3; x++) {

  if (
    u8(x) === 0x20 &&
    u8(x + 1) === 0x01 &&
    u8(x + 2) === 0xfe
  ) {
    const sub = readULEB(x + 3);
    const align = readULEB(sub.next);
    const off = readULEB(align.next);

    if (off.value === 0) {
      console.log("");
      console.log(
        "ATOMIC @",
        hex(x),
        "SUBOP =",
        hex(sub.value, 2),
        "ALIGN =",
        align.value,
        "OFFSET =",
        off.value
      );

      console.log(dump(x));
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CALL_REF sites and exact preceding stack construction.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL_REF + PREDECESSOR");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let x = bodyStart; x < bodyEnd; x++) {

  if (u8(x) !== 0x14) {
    continue;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("CALL_REF @", hex(x));

  const s = Math.max(bodyStart, x - 40);

  let a = [];

  for (let i = s; i < Math.min(bodyEnd, x + 12); i++) {
    a.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
  }

  console.log(a.join(" "));
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const file =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET = 3317;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ULEB
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(pos);
    const size = s.value;
    pos = s.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const count = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + size;

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + i,
        start,
        end,
        size
    });

    p = end;
}

const body = bodies.find(x => x.func === TARGET);

if (!body)
    throw new Error("FUNC 3317 not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Opcode helpers
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function isLocalGet(b) {
    return b === 0x20;
}

function isLocalSet(b) {
    return b === 0x21;
}

function isLocalTee(b) {
    return b === 0x22;
}

// Decode local instruction only.
// Returns {local,next} or null.
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function decodeLocal(pos) {

    const op = buf[pos];

    if (
        op !== 0x20 &&
        op !== 0x21 &&
        op !== 0x22
    )
        return null;

    const r = readULEB(pos + 1);

    return {
        op,
        local: r.value,
        next: r.next
    };
}

function localOpName(op) {
    if (op === 0x20) return "local.get";
    if (op === 0x21) return "local.set";
    if (op === 0x22) return "local.tee";
    return "?";
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find all local1 events
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const events = [];

for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    const d = decodeLocal(p);

    if (!d)
        continue;

    if (d.local !== 1)
        continue;

    events.push({
        addr: p,
        op: d.op,
        next: d.next
    });
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Dump bytes around an event
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function dump(start, end) {

    start = Math.max(body.start, start);
    end = Math.min(body.end, end);

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {

        const e = Math.min(end, p + 16);

        const bytes = [];

        for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
            bytes.push(
                buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Main output
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 3317 - LOCAL1 PROVENANCE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
console.log(`SIZE : ${body.size}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log(`LOCAL1 EVENTS : ${events.length}`);

for (const e of events) {

    console.log("\n------------------------------------------------------------");

    console.log(
        `${hex(e.addr)}  ${localOpName(e.op)} local=1`
    );

    console.log(
        `NEXT : ${hex(e.next)}`
    );

    console.log("\nCONTEXT -80 .. +120:");

    dump(
        e.addr - 80,
        e.next + 120
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Special: CALL_REF sites
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("CALL_REF SITES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    if (buf[p] !== 0x14)
        continue;

    console.log("\n------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`CALL_REF @ ${hex(p)}`);

    dump(p - 100, p + 40);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Special: local1 + atomic load candidates
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("LOCAL1 + ATOMIC / LOAD CANDIDATES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    if (buf[p] !== 0x20)
        continue;

    const r = readULEB(p + 1);

    if (r.value !== 1)
        continue;

    // Search next 20 bytes for atomic prefix FE
    for (
        let q = r.next;
        q < Math.min(body.end, r.next + 20);
        q++
    ) {

        if (buf[q] !== 0xfe)
            continue;

        console.log(
            `LOCAL1 @ ${hex(p)} -> ATOMIC PREFIX @ ${hex(q)}`
        );

        dump(p - 20, q + 20);

        break;
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p) {
  return buf[p];
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(p++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGET = 3317;

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const id = u8(p++);

  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!codeStart) throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

let q = codeStart;

const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

let bodyStart = null;
let bodyEnd = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const sz = readULEB(q);
  q = sz.next;

  const start = q;
  const end = q + sz.value;

  const func = IMPORTS + i;

  if (func === TARGET) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  q = end;
}

if (bodyStart === null) {
  throw new Error("FUNC 3317 NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNC 3317 LOCAL 2");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("BODY :", hex(bodyStart), "..", hex(bodyEnd));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function bytesAt(pos, count = 20) {
  let a = [];

  for (let i = pos; i < Math.min(bodyEnd, pos + count); i++) {
    a.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
  }

  return a.join(" ");
}

let hits = [];

for (let x = bodyStart; x < bodyEnd; x++) {
  const op = u8(x);

  // local.get 2
  // 20 02
  if (op === 0x20 && u8(x + 1) === 0x02) {
    hits.push({
      addr: x,
      kind: "local.get 2"
    });
  }

  // local.set 2
  // 21 02
  if (op === 0x21 && u8(x + 1) === 0x02) {
    hits.push({
      addr: x,
      kind: "local.set 2"
    });
  }

  // local.tee 2
  // 22 02
  if (op === 0x22 && u8(x + 1) === 0x02) {
    hits.push({
      addr: x,
      kind: "local.tee 2"
    });
  }
}

console.log("LOCAL 2 REFERENCES :", hits.length);
console.log("");

for (const h of hits) {
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(hex(h.addr), h.kind);
  console.log(bytesAt(h.addr, 32));
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Special: show every call_ref and preceding 16 bytes.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL_REF SITES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let x = bodyStart; x < bodyEnd; x++) {
  if (u8(x) === 0x14) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("CALL_REF @", hex(x));
    console.log(bytesAt(Math.max(bodyStart, x - 20), 40));
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Special: show stores offset 0 and preceding bytes.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("OFFSET-0 STORES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let x = bodyStart; x < bodyEnd; x++) {
  if (u8(x) === 0x36) {
    const a = readULEB(x + 1);
    const off = readULEB(a.next);

    if (off.value === 0) {
      console.log("");
      console.log("STORE @", hex(x));
      console.log(bytesAt(Math.max(bodyStart, x - 20), 40));
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");
